`components/ThemeToggle.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
   2 |
   3 | type ThemeMode = "light" | "dark" | "auto";
   4 |
   5 | function getInitialMode(): ThemeMode {
   6 | 	if (typeof window === "undefined") {
   7 | 		return "auto";
   8 | 	}
   9 |
  10 | 	const stored = window.localStorage.getItem("theme");
  11 | 	if (stored === "light" || stored === "dark" || stored === "auto") {
  12 | 		return stored;
  13 | 	}
  14 |
  15 | 	return "auto";
  16 | }
  17 |
  18 | function applyThemeMode(mode: ThemeMode) {
  19 | 	const prefersDark = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
  20 | 	const resolved = mode === "auto" ? (prefersDark ? "dark" : "light") : mode;
  21 |
  22 | 	document.documentElement.classList.remove("light", "dark");
  23 | 	document.documentElement.classList.add(resolved);
  24 |
  25 | 	if (mode === "auto") {
  26 | 		document.documentElement.removeAttribute("data-theme");
  27 | 	} else {
  28 | 		document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", mode);
  29 | 	}
  30 |
  31 | 	document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = resolved;
  32 | }
  33 |
  34 | export default function ThemeToggle() {
  35 | 	const [mode, setMode] = useState<ThemeMode>("auto");
  36 |
  37 | 	useEffect(() => {
  38 | 		const initialMode = getInitialMode();
  39 | 		setMode(initialMode);
  40 | 		applyThemeMode(initialMode);
  41 | 	}, []);
  42 |
  43 | 	useEffect(() => {
  44 | 		if (mode !== "auto") {
  45 | 			return;
  46 | 		}
  47 |
  48 | 		const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
  49 | 		const onChange = () => applyThemeMode("auto");
  50 |
  51 | 		media.addEventListener("change", onChange);
  52 | 		return () => {
  53 | 			media.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  54 | 		};
  55 | 	}, [mode]);
  56 |
  57 | 	function toggleMode() {
  58 | 		const nextMode: ThemeMode = mode === "light" ? "dark" : mode === "dark" ? "auto" : "light";
  59 | 		setMode(nextMode);
  60 | 		applyThemeMode(nextMode);
  61 | 		window.localStorage.setItem("theme", nextMode);
  62 | 	}
  63 |
  64 | 	const label =
  65 | 		mode === "auto"
  66 | 			? "Theme mode: auto (system). Click to switch to light mode."
  67 | 			: `Theme mode: ${mode}. Click to switch mode.`;
  68 |
  69 | 	return (
  70 | 		<button
  71 | 			aria-label={label}
  72 | 			className='rounded-full border border-[var(--chip-line)] bg-[var(--chip-bg)] px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold text-[var(--sea-ink)] text-sm shadow-[0_8px_22px_rgba(30,90,72,0.08)] transition hover:-translate-y-0.5'
  73 | 			onClick={toggleMode}
  74 | 			title={label}
  75 | 			type='button'
  76 | 		>
  77 | 			{mode === "auto" ? "Auto" : mode === "dark" ? "Dark" : "Light"}
  78 | 		</button>
  79 | 	);
  80 | }

```

`components/demo.FormComponents.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { useStore } from "@tanstack/react-form";
   2 |
   3 | import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
   4 | import { Input } from "#/components/ui/input";
   5 | import { Label } from "#/components/ui/label";
   6 | import * as ShadcnSelect from "#/components/ui/select";
   7 | import { Slider as ShadcnSlider } from "#/components/ui/slider";
   8 | import { Switch as ShadcnSwitch } from "#/components/ui/switch";
   9 | import { Textarea as ShadcnTextarea } from "#/components/ui/textarea";
  10 | import { useFieldContext, useFormContext } from "#/hooks/demo.form-context";
  11 |
  12 | export function SubscribeButton({ label }: { label: string }) {
  13 | 	const form = useFormContext();
  14 | 	return (
  15 | 		<form.Subscribe selector={state => state.isSubmitting}>
  16 | 			{isSubmitting => (
  17 | 				<Button
  18 | 					disabled={isSubmitting}
  19 | 					type='submit'
  20 | 				>
  21 | 					{label}
  22 | 				</Button>
  23 | 			)}
  24 | 		</form.Subscribe>
  25 | 	);
  26 | }
  27 |
  28 | function ErrorMessages({ errors }: { errors: Array<string | { message: string }> }) {
  29 | 	return (
  30 | 		<>
  31 | 			{errors.map(error => (
  32 | 				<div
  33 | 					className='mt-1 font-bold text-red-500'
  34 | 					key={typeof error === "string" ? error : error.message}
  35 | 				>
  36 | 					{typeof error === "string" ? error : error.message}
  37 | 				</div>
  38 | 			))}
  39 | 		</>
  40 | 	);
  41 | }
  42 |
  43 | export function TextField({ label, placeholder }: { label: string; placeholder?: string }) {
  44 | 	const field = useFieldContext<string>();
  45 | 	const errors = useStore(field.store, state => state.meta.errors);
  46 |
  47 | 	return (
  48 | 		<div>
  49 | 			<Label
  50 | 				className='mb-2 font-bold text-xl'
  51 | 				htmlFor={label}
  52 | 			>
  53 | 				{label}
  54 | 			</Label>
  55 | 			<Input
  56 | 				onBlur={field.handleBlur}
  57 | 				onChange={e => field.handleChange(e.target.value)}
  58 | 				placeholder={placeholder}
  59 | 				value={field.state.value}
  60 | 			/>
  61 | 			{field.state.meta.isTouched && <ErrorMessages errors={errors} />}
  62 | 		</div>
  63 | 	);
  64 | }
  65 |
  66 | export function TextArea({ label, rows = 3 }: { label: string; rows?: number }) {
  67 | 	const field = useFieldContext<string>();
  68 | 	const errors = useStore(field.store, state => state.meta.errors);
  69 |
  70 | 	return (
  71 | 		<div>
  72 | 			<Label
  73 | 				className='mb-2 font-bold text-xl'
  74 | 				htmlFor={label}
  75 | 			>
  76 | 				{label}
  77 | 			</Label>
  78 | 			<ShadcnTextarea
  79 | 				id={label}
  80 | 				onBlur={field.handleBlur}
  81 | 				onChange={e => field.handleChange(e.target.value)}
  82 | 				rows={rows}
  83 | 				value={field.state.value}
  84 | 			/>
  85 | 			{field.state.meta.isTouched && <ErrorMessages errors={errors} />}
  86 | 		</div>
  87 | 	);
  88 | }
  89 |
  90 | export function Select({
  91 | 	label,
  92 | 	values,
  93 | 	placeholder
  94 | }: {
  95 | 	label: string;
  96 | 	values: Array<{ label: string; value: string }>;
  97 | 	placeholder?: string;
  98 | }) {
  99 | 	const field = useFieldContext<string>();
 100 | 	const errors = useStore(field.store, state => state.meta.errors);
 101 |
 102 | 	return (
 103 | 		<div>
 104 | 			<ShadcnSelect.Select
 105 | 				name={field.name}
 106 | 				onValueChange={value => field.handleChange(value)}
 107 | 				value={field.state.value}
 108 | 			>
 109 | 				<ShadcnSelect.SelectTrigger className='w-full'>
 110 | 					<ShadcnSelect.SelectValue placeholder={placeholder} />
 111 | 				</ShadcnSelect.SelectTrigger>
 112 | 				<ShadcnSelect.SelectContent className='bg-background text-foreground'>
 113 | 					<ShadcnSelect.SelectGroup>
 114 | 						<ShadcnSelect.SelectLabel>{label}</ShadcnSelect.SelectLabel>
 115 | 						{values.map(value => (
 116 | 							<ShadcnSelect.SelectItem
 117 | 								className='text-foreground'
 118 | 								key={value.value}
 119 | 								value={value.value}
 120 | 							>
 121 | 								{value.label}
 122 | 							</ShadcnSelect.SelectItem>
 123 | 						))}
 124 | 					</ShadcnSelect.SelectGroup>
 125 | 				</ShadcnSelect.SelectContent>
 126 | 			</ShadcnSelect.Select>
 127 | 			{field.state.meta.isTouched && <ErrorMessages errors={errors} />}
 128 | 		</div>
 129 | 	);
 130 | }
 131 |
 132 | export function Slider({ label }: { label: string }) {
 133 | 	const field = useFieldContext<number>();
 134 | 	const errors = useStore(field.store, state => state.meta.errors);
 135 |
 136 | 	return (
 137 | 		<div>
 138 | 			<Label
 139 | 				className='mb-2 font-bold text-xl'
 140 | 				htmlFor={label}
 141 | 			>
 142 | 				{label}
 143 | 			</Label>
 144 | 			<ShadcnSlider
 145 | 				id={label}
 146 | 				onBlur={field.handleBlur}
 147 | 				onValueChange={value => field.handleChange(value[0])}
 148 | 				value={[field.state.value]}
 149 | 			/>
 150 | 			{field.state.meta.isTouched && <ErrorMessages errors={errors} />}
 151 | 		</div>
 152 | 	);
 153 | }
 154 |
 155 | export function Switch({ label }: { label: string }) {
 156 | 	const field = useFieldContext<boolean>();
 157 | 	const errors = useStore(field.store, state => state.meta.errors);
 158 |
 159 | 	return (
 160 | 		<div>
 161 | 			<div className='flex items-center gap-2'>
 162 | 				<ShadcnSwitch
 163 | 					checked={field.state.value}
 164 | 					id={label}
 165 | 					onBlur={field.handleBlur}
 166 | 					onCheckedChange={checked => field.handleChange(checked)}
 167 | 				/>
 168 | 				<Label htmlFor={label}>{label}</Label>
 169 | 			</div>
 170 | 			{field.state.meta.isTouched && <ErrorMessages errors={errors} />}
 171 | 		</div>
 172 | 	);
 173 | }
```

```tsx
  47 |         <Avatar className='size-9'>

  49 |           <AvatarFallback className='text-xs'>{row.original.avatarFallback}</AvatarFallback>
  50 |         </Avatar>
  51 |         <div className='flex flex-col text-sm'>
  52 |           <span className='text-card-foreground font-medium'>{row.getValue('name')}</span>
  53 |           <span className='text-muted-foreground'>{row.original.email}</span>
  54 |         </div>
  55 |       </div>
  56 |     )
  57 |   },
  58 |   {
  59 |     accessorKey: 'amount',
  60 |     header: 'Amount',
  61 |     cell: ({ row }) => {
  62 |       const amount = parseFloat(row.getValue('amount'))
  63 |
  64 |       const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
  65 |         style: 'currency',
  66 |         currency: 'USD'
  67 |       }).format(amount)
  68 |
  69 |       return <span>{formatted}</span>
  70 |     }
  71 |   },
  72 |   {
  73 |     accessorKey: 'status',
  74 |     header: 'Status',
  75 |     cell: ({ row }) => (
  76 |       <Badge className='bg-primary/10 text-primary rounded-sm px-1.5 capitalize'>{row.getValue('status')}</Badge>
  77 |     )
  78 |   },
  79 |   {
  80 |     accessorKey: 'paidBy',
  81 |     header: () => <span className='w-fit'>Paid by</span>,
  82 |     cell: ({ row }) => (
  83 |       <img
  84 |         src={
  85 |           row.getValue('paidBy') === 'mastercard'
  86 |             ? 'https://cdn.shadcnstudio.com/ss-assets/blocks/data-table/image-1.png'
  87 |             : 'https://cdn.shadcnstudio.com/ss-assets/blocks/data-table/image-2.png'
  88 |         }
  89 |         alt='Payment platform'
  90 |         className='w-10.5'
  91 |       />
  92 |     )
  93 |   },
  94 |   {
  95 |     id: 'actions',
  96 |     header: () => 'Actions',
  97 |     cell: () => <RowActions />,
  98 |     size: 60,
  99 |     enableHiding: false
 100 |   }
 101 | ]
 102 |
 103 | const TransactionDatatable = ({ data }: { data: Item[] }) => {
 104 |   const pageSize = 5
 105 |
 106 |   const [pagination, setPagination] = useState<PaginationState>({
 107 |     pageIndex: 0,
 108 |     pageSize: pageSize
 109 |   })
 110 |
 111 |   const table = useReactTable({
 112 |     data,
 113 |     columns,
 114 |     getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
 115 |     getPaginationRowModel: getPaginationRowModel(),
 116 |     getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel(),
 117 |     getFilteredRowModel: getFilteredRowModel(),
 118 |     onPaginationChange: setPagination,
 119 |     state: {
 120 |       pagination
 121 |     }
 122 |   })
 123 |
 124 |   const { pages, showLeftEllipsis, showRightEllipsis } = usePagination({
 125 |     currentPage: table.getState().pagination.pageIndex + 1,
 126 |     totalPages: table.getPageCount(),
 127 |     paginationItemsToDisplay: 2
 128 |   })
 129 |
 130 |   return (
 131 |     <div className='w-full'>
 132 |       <div className='border-b'>
 133 |         <Table>
 134 |           <TableHeader>
 135 |             {table.getHeaderGroups().map(headerGroup => (
 136 |               <TableRow key={headerGroup.id}>
 137 |                 {headerGroup.headers.map(header => {
 138 |                   return (
 139 |                     <TableHead key={header.id} className='text-muted-foreground h-14 first:pl-4'>
 140 |                       {header.isPlaceholder ? null : flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())}
 141 |                     </TableHead>
 142 |                   )
 143 |                 })}
 144 |               </TableRow>
 145 |             ))}
 146 |           </TableHeader>
 147 |           <TableBody>
 148 |             {table.getRowModel().rows?.length ? (
 149 |               table.getRowModel().rows.map(row => (
 150 |                 <TableRow key={row.id} data-state={row.getIsSelected() && 'selected'}>
 151 |                   {row.getVisibleCells().map(cell => (
 152 |                     <TableCell key={cell.id} className='first:pl-4'>
 153 |                       {flexRender(cell.column.columnDef.cell, cell.getContext())}
 154 |                     </TableCell>
 155 |                   ))}
 156 |                 </TableRow>
 157 |               ))
 158 |             ) : (
 159 |               <TableRow>
 160 |                 <TableCell colSpan={columns.length} className='h-24 text-center'>
 161 |                   No results.
 162 |                 </TableCell>
 163 |               </TableRow>
 164 |             )}
 165 |           </TableBody>
 166 |         </Table>
 167 |       </div>
 168 |
 169 |       <div className='flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-6 py-4 max-sm:flex-col md:max-lg:flex-col'>
 170 |         <p className='text-muted-foreground text-sm whitespace-nowrap' aria-live='polite'>
 171 |           Showing{' '}
 172 |           <span>
 173 |             {table.getState().pagination.pageIndex * table.getState().pagination.pageSize + 1} to{' '}
 174 |             {Math.min(
 175 |               Math.max(
 176 |                 table.getState().pagination.pageIndex * table.getState().pagination.pageSize +
 177 |                   table.getState().pagination.pageSize,
 178 |                 0
 179 |               ),
 180 |               table.getRowCount()
 181 |             )}
 182 |           </span>{' '}
 183 |           of <span>{table.getRowCount().toString()} entries</span>
 184 |         </p>
 185 |
 186 |         <div>
 187 |           <Pagination>
 188 |             <PaginationContent>
 189 |               <PaginationItem>
 190 |                 <Button
 191 |                   className='disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50'
 192 |                   variant={'ghost'}
 193 |                   onClick={() => table.previousPage()}
 194 |                   disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}
 195 |                   aria-label='Go to previous page'
 196 |                 >
 197 |                   <ChevronLeftIcon aria-hidden='true' />
 198 |                   Previous
 199 |                 </Button>
 200 |               </PaginationItem>
 201 |
 202 |               {showLeftEllipsis && (
 203 |                 <PaginationItem>
 204 |                   <PaginationEllipsis />
 205 |                 </PaginationItem>
 206 |               )}
 207 |
 208 |               {pages.map(page => {
 209 |                 const isActive = page === table.getState().pagination.pageIndex + 1
 210 |
 211 |                 return (
 212 |                   <PaginationItem key={page}>
 213 |                     <Button
 214 |                       size='icon'
 215 |                       className={`${!isActive && 'bg-primary/10 text-primary hover:bg-primary/20 focus-visible:ring-primary/20 dark:focus-visible:ring-primary/40'}`}
 216 |                       onClick={() => table.setPageIndex(page - 1)}
 217 |                       aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
 218 |                     >
 219 |                       {page}
 220 |                     </Button>
 221 |                   </PaginationItem>
 222 |                 )
 223 |               })}
 224 |
 225 |               {showRightEllipsis && (
 226 |                 <PaginationItem>
 227 |                   <PaginationEllipsis />
 228 |                 </PaginationItem>
 229 |               )}
 230 |
 231 |               <PaginationItem>
 232 |                 <Button
 233 |                   className='disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50'
 234 |                   variant={'ghost'}
 235 |                   onClick={() => table.nextPage()}
 236 |                   disabled={!table.getCanNextPage()}
 237 |                   aria-label='Go to next page'
 238 |                 >
 239 |                   Next
 240 |                   <ChevronRightIcon aria-hidden='true' />
 241 |                 </Button>
 242 |               </PaginationItem>
 243 |             </PaginationContent>
 244 |           </Pagination>
 245 |         </div>
 246 |       </div>
 247 |     </div>
 248 |   )
 249 | }
 250 |
 251 | export default TransactionDatatable
 252 |
 253 | function RowActions() {
 254 |   return (
 255 |     <DropdownMenu>
 256 |       <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
 257 |         <div className='flex'>
 258 |           <Button size='icon' variant='ghost' className='rounded-full p-2' aria-label='Edit item'>
 259 |             <EllipsisVerticalIcon className='size-5' aria-hidden='true' />
 260 |           </Button>
 261 |         </div>
 262 |       </DropdownMenuTrigger>
 263 |       <DropdownMenuContent align='end'>
 264 |         <DropdownMenuGroup>
 265 |           <DropdownMenuItem>
 266 |             <span>Edit</span>
 267 |           </DropdownMenuItem>
 268 |           <DropdownMenuItem>
 269 |             <span>Duplicate</span>
 270 |           </DropdownMenuItem>
 271 |           <DropdownMenuItem variant='destructive'>
 272 |             <span>Delete</span>
 273 |           </DropdownMenuItem>
 274 |         </DropdownMenuGroup>
 275 |       </DropdownMenuContent>
 276 |     </DropdownMenu>
 277 |   )
 278 | }
```

```tsx
  77 |                 </div>
  78 |               </div>
  79 |               <div className='space-y-2'>
  80 |                 <p className='text-sm'>{earning.earnings}</p>
  81 |                 <Progress value={earning.progressPercentage} className='w-36' />
  82 |               </div>
  83 |             </div>
  84 |           ))}
  85 |         </div>
  86 |       </CardContent>
  87 |     </Card>
  88 |   )
  89 | }
  90 |
  91 | export default TotalEarningCard
```

`components/ui/chart.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | "use client"
   2 |
   3 | import * as React from "react"
   4 | import * as RechartsPrimitive from "recharts"
   5 | import type { TooltipValueType } from "recharts"
   6 |
   7 | import { cn } from "#/lib/utils.ts"
   8 |
   9 | // Format: { THEME_NAME: CSS_SELECTOR }
  10 | const THEMES = { light: "", dark: ".dark" } as const
  11 |
  12 | const INITIAL_DIMENSION = { width: 320, height: 200 } as const
  13 | type TooltipNameType = number | string
  14 |
  15 | export type ChartConfig = Record<
  16 |   string,
  17 |   {
  18 |     label?: React.ReactNode
  19 |     icon?: React.ComponentType
  20 |   } & (
  21 |     | { color?: string; theme?: never }
  22 |     | { color?: never; theme: Record<keyof typeof THEMES, string> }
  23 |   )
  24 | >
  25 |
  26 | type ChartContextProps = {
  27 |   config: ChartConfig
  28 | }
  29 |
  30 | const ChartContext = React.createContext<ChartContextProps | null>(null)
  31 |
  32 | function useChart() {
  33 |   const context = React.useContext(ChartContext)
  34 |
  35 |   if (!context) {
  36 |     throw new Error("useChart must be used within a <ChartContainer />")
  37 |   }
  38 |
  39 |   return context
  40 | }
  41 |
  42 | function ChartContainer({
  43 |   id,
  44 |   className,
  45 |   children,
  46 |   config,
  47 |   initialDimension = INITIAL_DIMENSION,
  48 |   ...props
  49 | }: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
  50 |   config: ChartConfig
  51 |   children: React.ComponentProps<
  52 |     typeof RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer
  53 |   >["children"]
  54 |   initialDimension?: {
  55 |     width: number
  56 |     height: number
  57 |   }
  58 | }) {
  59 |   const uniqueId = React.useId()
  60 |   const chartId = `chart-${id ?? uniqueId.replace(/:/g, "")}`
  61 |
  62 |   return (
  63 |     <ChartContext.Provider value={{ config }}>
  64 |       <div
  65 |         data-slot="chart"
  66 |         data-chart={chartId}
  67 |         className={cn(
  68 |           "flex aspect-video justify-center text-xs [&_.recharts-cartesian-axis-tick_text]:fill-muted-foreground [&_.recharts-cartesian-grid_line[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border/50 [&_.recharts-curve.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-dot[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-layer]:outline-hidden [&_.recharts-polar-grid_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-radial-bar-background-sector]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-rectangle.recharts-tooltip-cursor]:fill-muted [&_.recharts-reference-line_[stroke='#ccc']]:stroke-border [&_.recharts-sector]:outline-hidden [&_.recharts-sector[stroke='#fff']]:stroke-transparent [&_.recharts-surface]:outline-hidden",
  69 |           className
  70 |         )}
  71 |         {...props}
  72 |       >
  73 |         <ChartStyle id={chartId} config={config} />
  74 |         <RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer
  75 |           initialDimension={initialDimension}
  76 |         >
  77 |           {children}
  78 |         </RechartsPrimitive.ResponsiveContainer>
  79 |       </div>
  80 |     </ChartContext.Provider>
  81 |   )
  82 | }
  83 |
  84 | const ChartStyle = ({ id, config }: { id: string; config: ChartConfig }) => {
  85 |   const colorConfig = Object.entries(config).filter(
  86 |     ([, config]) => config.theme ?? config.color
  87 |   )
  88 |
  89 |   if (!colorConfig.length) {
  90 |     return null
  91 |   }
  92 |
  93 |   return (
  94 |     <style
  95 |       dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
  96 |         __html: Object.entries(THEMES)
  97 |           .map(
  98 |             ([theme, prefix]) => `
  99 | ${prefix} [data-chart=${id}] {
 100 | ${colorConfig
 101 |   .map(([key, itemConfig]) => {
 102 |     const color =
 103 |       itemConfig.theme?.[theme as keyof typeof itemConfig.theme] ??
 104 |       itemConfig.color
 105 |     return color ? `  --color-${key}: ${color};` : null
 106 |   })
 107 |   .join("\n")}
 108 | }
 109 | `
 110 |           )
 111 |           .join("\n"),
 112 |       }}
 113 |     />
 114 |   )
 115 | }
 116 |
 117 | const ChartTooltip = RechartsPrimitive.Tooltip
 118 |
 119 | function ChartTooltipContent({
 120 |   active,
 121 |   payload,
 122 |   className,
 123 |   indicator = "dot",
 124 |   hideLabel = false,
 125 |   hideIndicator = false,
 126 |   label,
 127 |   labelFormatter,
 128 |   labelClassName,
 129 |   formatter,
 130 |   color,
 131 |   nameKey,
 132 |   labelKey,
 133 | }: React.ComponentProps<typeof RechartsPrimitive.Tooltip> &
 134 |   React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
 135 |     hideLabel?: boolean
 136 |     hideIndicator?: boolean
 137 |     indicator?: "line" | "dot" | "dashed"
 138 |     nameKey?: string
 139 |     labelKey?: string
 140 |   } & Omit<
 141 |     RechartsPrimitive.DefaultTooltipContentProps<
 142 |       TooltipValueType,
 143 |       TooltipNameType
 144 |     >,
 145 |     "accessibilityLayer"
 146 |   >) {
 147 |   const { config } = useChart()
 148 |
 149 |   const tooltipLabel = React.useMemo(() => {
 150 |     if (hideLabel || !payload?.length) {
 151 |       return null
 152 |     }
 153 |
 154 |     const [item] = payload
 155 |     const key = `${labelKey ?? item?.dataKey ?? item?.name ?? "value"}`
 156 |     const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
 157 |     const value =
 158 |       !labelKey && typeof label === "string"
 159 |         ? (config[label]?.label ?? label)
 160 |         : itemConfig?.label
 161 |
 162 |     if (labelFormatter) {
 163 |       return (
 164 |         <div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>
 165 |           {labelFormatter(value, payload)}
 166 |         </div>
 167 |       )
 168 |     }
 169 |
 170 |     if (!value) {
 171 |       return null
 172 |     }
 173 |
 174 |     return <div className={cn("font-medium", labelClassName)}>{value}</div>
 175 |   }, [
 176 |     label,
 177 |     labelFormatter,
 178 |     payload,
 179 |     hideLabel,
 180 |     labelClassName,
 181 |     config,
 182 |     labelKey,
 183 |   ])
 184 |
 185 |   if (!active || !payload?.length) {
 186 |     return null
 187 |   }
 188 |
 189 |   const nestLabel = payload.length === 1 && indicator !== "dot"
 190 |
 191 |   return (
 192 |     <div
 193 |       className={cn(
 194 |         "grid min-w-[8rem] items-start gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-border/50 bg-background px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs shadow-xl",
 195 |         className
 196 |       )}
 197 |     >
 198 |       {!nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
 199 |       <div className="grid gap-1.5">
 200 |         {payload
 201 |           .filter((item) => item.type !== "none")
 202 |           .map((item, index) => {
 203 |             const key = `${nameKey ?? item.name ?? item.dataKey ?? "value"}`
 204 |             const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
 205 |             const indicatorColor = color ?? item.payload?.fill ?? item.color
 206 |
 207 |             return (
 208 |               <div
 209 |                 key={index}
 210 |                 className={cn(
 211 |                   "flex w-full flex-wrap items-stretch gap-2 [&>svg]:h-2.5 [&>svg]:w-2.5 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground",
 212 |                   indicator === "dot" && "items-center"
 213 |                 )}
 214 |               >
 215 |                 {formatter && item?.value !== undefined && item.name ? (
 216 |                   formatter(item.value, item.name, item, index, item.payload)
 217 |                 ) : (
 218 |                   <>
 219 |                     {itemConfig?.icon ? (
 220 |                       <itemConfig.icon />
 221 |                     ) : (
 222 |                       !hideIndicator && (
 223 |                         <div
 224 |                           className={cn(
 225 |                             "shrink-0 rounded-[2px] border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-bg)",
 226 |                             {
 227 |                               "h-2.5 w-2.5": indicator === "dot",
 228 |                               "w-1": indicator === "line",
 229 |                               "w-0 border-[1.5px] border-dashed bg-transparent":
 230 |                                 indicator === "dashed",
 231 |                               "my-0.5": nestLabel && indicator === "dashed",
 232 |                             }
 233 |                           )}
 234 |                           style={
 235 |                             {
 236 |                               "--color-bg": indicatorColor,
 237 |                               "--color-border": indicatorColor,
 238 |                             } as React.CSSProperties
 239 |                           }
 240 |                         />
 241 |                       )
 242 |                     )}
 243 |                     <div
 244 |                       className={cn(
 245 |                         "flex flex-1 justify-between leading-none",
 246 |                         nestLabel ? "items-end" : "items-center"
 247 |                       )}
 248 |                     >
 249 |                       <div className="grid gap-1.5">
 250 |                         {nestLabel ? tooltipLabel : null}
 251 |                         <span className="text-muted-foreground">
 252 |                           {itemConfig?.label ?? item.name}
 253 |                         </span>
 254 |                       </div>
 255 |                       {item.value != null && (
 256 |                         <span className="font-mono font-medium text-foreground tabular-nums">
 257 |                           {typeof item.value === "number"
 258 |                             ? item.value.toLocaleString()
 259 |                             : String(item.value)}
 260 |                         </span>
 261 |                       )}
 262 |                     </div>
 263 |                   </>
 264 |                 )}
 265 |               </div>
 266 |             )
 267 |           })}
 268 |       </div>
 269 |     </div>
 270 |   )
 271 | }
 272 |
 273 | const ChartLegend = RechartsPrimitive.Legend
 274 |
 275 | function ChartLegendContent({
 276 |   className,
 277 |   hideIcon = false,
 278 |   payload,
 279 |   verticalAlign = "bottom",
 280 |   nameKey,
 281 | }: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
 282 |   hideIcon?: boolean
 283 |   nameKey?: string
 284 | } & RechartsPrimitive.DefaultLegendContentProps) {
 285 |   const { config } = useChart()
 286 |
 287 |   if (!payload?.length) {
 288 |     return null
 289 |   }
 290 |
 291 |   return (
 292 |     <div
 293 |       className={cn(
 294 |         "flex items-center justify-center gap-4",
 295 |         verticalAlign === "top" ? "pb-3" : "pt-3",
 296 |         className
 297 |       )}
 298 |     >
 299 |       {payload
 300 |         .filter((item) => item.type !== "none")
 301 |         .map((item, index) => {
 302 |           const key = `${nameKey ?? item.dataKey ?? "value"}`
 303 |           const itemConfig = getPayloadConfigFromPayload(config, item, key)
 304 |
 305 |           return (
 306 |             <div
 307 |               key={index}
 308 |               className={cn(
 309 |                 "flex items-center gap-1.5 [&>svg]:h-3 [&>svg]:w-3 [&>svg]:text-muted-foreground"
 310 |               )}
 311 |             >
 312 |               {itemConfig?.icon && !hideIcon ? (
 313 |                 <itemConfig.icon />
 314 |               ) : (
 315 |                 <div
 316 |                   className="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-[2px]"
 317 |                   style={{
 318 |                     backgroundColor: item.color,
 319 |                   }}
 320 |                 />
 321 |               )}
 322 |               {itemConfig?.label}
 323 |             </div>
 324 |           )
 325 |         })}
 326 |     </div>
 327 |   )
 328 | }
 329 |
 330 | // Helper to extract item config from a payload.
 331 | function getPayloadConfigFromPayload(
 332 |   config: ChartConfig,
 333 |   payload: unknown,
 334 |   key: string
 335 | ) {
 336 |   if (typeof payload !== "object" || payload === null) {
 337 |     return undefined
 338 |   }
 339 |
 340 |   const payloadPayload =
 341 |     "payload" in payload &&
 342 |     typeof payload.payload === "object" &&
 343 |     payload.payload !== null
 344 |       ? payload.payload
 345 |       : undefined
 346 |
 347 |   let configLabelKey: string = key
 348 |
 349 |   if (
 350 |     key in payload &&
 351 |     typeof payload[key as keyof typeof payload] === "string"
 352 |   ) {
 353 |     configLabelKey = payload[key as keyof typeof payload] as string
 354 |   } else if (
 355 |     payloadPayload &&
 356 |     key in payloadPayload &&
 357 |     typeof payloadPayload[key as keyof typeof payloadPayload] === "string"
 358 |   ) {
 359 |     configLabelKey = payloadPayload[
 360 |       key as keyof typeof payloadPayload
 361 |     ] as string
 362 |   }
 363 |
 364 |   return configLabelKey in config ? config[configLabelKey] : config[key]
 365 | }
 366 |
 367 | export {
 368 |   ChartContainer,
 369 |   ChartTooltip,
 370 |   ChartTooltipContent,
 371 |   ChartLegend,
 372 |   ChartLegendContent,
 373 |   ChartStyle,
 374 | }
```

`components/ui/infobar.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
   2 | import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
   3 | import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator';
   4 | import {
   5 |   Sheet,
   6 |   SheetContent,
   7 |   SheetDescription,
   8 |   SheetHeader,
   9 |   SheetTitle
  10 | } from '@/components/ui/sheet';
  11 | import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton';
  12 | import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipProvider, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip';
  13 | import { useIsMobile } from '@/hooks/use-mobile';
  14 | import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
  15 | import { Slot } from '@radix-ui/react-slot';
  16 | import { VariantProps, cva } from 'class-variance-authority';
  17 | import { Icons } from '@/components/icons';
  18 | import { useLocation } from '@tanstack/react-router';
  19 | import * as React from 'react';
  20 |
  21 | const INFOBAR_WIDTH = '22rem';
  22 | const INFOBAR_WIDTH_MOBILE = '22rem';
  23 | const INFOBAR_WIDTH_ICON = '3rem';
  24 | const INFOBAR_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUT = 'i';
  25 |
  26 | export type HelpfulLink = {
  27 |   title: string;
  28 |   url: string;
  29 | };
  30 |
  31 | export type DescriptiveSection = {
  32 |   title: string;
  33 |   description: string;
  34 |   links?: HelpfulLink[];
  35 | };
  36 |
  37 | export type InfobarContent = {
  38 |   title: string;
  39 |   sections: DescriptiveSection[];
  40 | };
  41 |
  42 | type InfobarContextProps = {
  43 |   state: 'expanded' | 'collapsed';
  44 |   open: boolean;
  45 |   setOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  46 |   openMobile: boolean;
  47 |   setOpenMobile: (open: boolean) => void;
  48 |   isMobile: boolean;
  49 |   toggleInfobar: () => void;
  50 |   content: InfobarContent | null;
  51 |   setContent: (content: InfobarContent | null) => void;
  52 |   isPathnameChanging: boolean;
  53 | };
  54 |
  55 | const InfobarContext = React.createContext<InfobarContextProps | null>(null);
  56 |
  57 | function useInfobar() {
  58 |   const context = React.useContext(InfobarContext);
  59 |   if (!context) {
  60 |     throw new Error('useInfobar must be used within a InfobarProvider.');
  61 |   }
  62 |
  63 |   return context;
  64 | }
  65 |
  66 | function InfobarProvider({
  67 |   defaultOpen = true,
  68 |   open: openProp,
  69 |   onOpenChange: setOpenProp,
  70 |   className,
  71 |   style,
  72 |   children,
  73 |   ...props
  74 | }: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & {
  75 |   defaultOpen?: boolean;
  76 |   open?: boolean;
  77 |   onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
  78 | }) {
  79 |   const isMobile = useIsMobile();
  80 |   const [openMobile, setOpenMobile] = React.useState(false);
  81 |   const [content, setContent] = React.useState<InfobarContent | null>(null);
  82 |   const [contentPathname, setContentPathname] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  83 |   const [isPathnameChanging, setIsPathnameChanging] = React.useState(false);
  84 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
  85 |
  86 |   // This is the internal state of the infobar.
  87 |   // We use openProp and setOpenProp for control from outside the component.
  88 |   const [_open, _setOpen] = React.useState(defaultOpen);
  89 |   const open = openProp ?? _open;
  90 |   const setOpen = React.useCallback(
  91 |     (value: boolean | ((value: boolean) => boolean)) => {
  92 |       const openState = typeof value === 'function' ? value(open) : value;
  93 |
  94 |       // On mobile, also update the mobile state for the Sheet component
  95 |       if (isMobile) {
  96 |         setOpenMobile(openState);
  97 |       }
  98 |
  99 |       // Handle desktop state
 100 |       if (setOpenProp) {
 101 |         setOpenProp(openState);
 102 |       } else {
 103 |         _setOpen(openState);
 104 |       }
 105 |     },
 106 |     [setOpenProp, open, isMobile]
 107 |   );
 108 |
 109 |   // Helper to toggle the infobar.
 110 |   const toggleInfobar = React.useCallback(() => {
 111 |     return isMobile ? setOpenMobile((open) => !open) : setOpen((open) => !open);
 112 |   }, [isMobile, setOpen, setOpenMobile]);
 113 |
 114 |   // Close infobar when switching between mobile and desktop to prevent state desync
 115 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 116 |     setOpenMobile(false);
 117 |     _setOpen(false);
 118 |   }, [isMobile]);
 119 |
 120 |   // Adds a keyboard shortcut to toggle the infobar.
 121 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 122 |     const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
 123 |       if (event.key === INFOBAR_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUT && (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)) {
 124 |         event.preventDefault();
 125 |         toggleInfobar();
 126 |       }
 127 |     };
 128 |
 129 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
 130 |     return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
 131 |   }, [toggleInfobar]);
 132 |
 133 |   // Clear content and close infobar when pathname changes
 134 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 135 |     if (contentPathname !== null && contentPathname !== pathname) {
 136 |       setIsPathnameChanging(true);
 137 |       setContent(null);
 138 |       setContentPathname(null);
 139 |       setOpen(false);
 140 |
 141 |       const timer = setTimeout(() => {
 142 |         setIsPathnameChanging(false);
 143 |       }, 200);
 144 |
 145 |       return () => clearTimeout(timer);
 146 |     }
 147 |     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps -- setOpen is a stable React state setter
 148 |   }, [pathname, contentPathname]);
 149 |
 150 |   // Update setContent to also track pathname
 151 |   const handleSetContent = React.useCallback(
 152 |     (newContent: InfobarContent | null) => {
 153 |       setContent(newContent);
 154 |       setContentPathname(newContent ? pathname : null);
 155 |     },
 156 |     [pathname]
 157 |   );
 158 |
 159 |   // We add a state so that we can do data-state="expanded" or "collapsed".
 160 |   // This makes it easier to style the infobar with Tailwind classes.
 161 |   const state = open ? 'expanded' : 'collapsed';
 162 |
 163 |   // Update context to use handleSetContent instead of setContent
 164 |   const contextValue = React.useMemo<InfobarContextProps>(
 165 |     () => ({
 166 |       state,
 167 |       open,
 168 |       setOpen,
 169 |       isMobile,
 170 |       openMobile,
 171 |       setOpenMobile,
 172 |       toggleInfobar,
 173 |       content,
 174 |       setContent: handleSetContent,
 175 |       isPathnameChanging
 176 |     }),
 177 |     [
 178 |       state,
 179 |       open,
 180 |       setOpen,
 181 |       isMobile,
 182 |       openMobile,
 183 |       setOpenMobile,
 184 |       toggleInfobar,
 185 |       content,
 186 |       handleSetContent,
 187 |       isPathnameChanging
 188 |     ]
 189 |   );
 190 |
 191 |   return (
 192 |     <InfobarContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
 193 |       <TooltipProvider delayDuration={0}>
 194 |         <div
 195 |           data-slot='infobar-wrapper'
 196 |           style={
 197 |             {
 198 |               '--infobar-width': INFOBAR_WIDTH,
 199 |               '--infobar-width-icon': INFOBAR_WIDTH_ICON,
 200 |               ...style
 201 |             } as React.CSSProperties
 202 |           }
 203 |           className={cn('group/infobar-wrapper flex flex-1 w-full', className)}
 204 |           {...props}
 205 |         >
 206 |           {children}
 207 |         </div>
 208 |       </TooltipProvider>
 209 |     </InfobarContext.Provider>
 210 |   );
 211 | }
 212 |
 213 | function Infobar({
 214 |   side = 'left',
 215 |   variant = 'sidebar',
 216 |   collapsible = 'offcanvas',
 217 |   className,
 218 |   children,
 219 |   ...props
 220 | }: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & {
 221 |   side?: 'left' | 'right';
 222 |   variant?: 'sidebar' | 'floating' | 'inset';
 223 |   collapsible?: 'offcanvas' | 'icon' | 'none';
 224 | }) {
 225 |   const { isMobile, state, setOpen, openMobile, setOpenMobile, isPathnameChanging } = useInfobar();
 226 |
 227 |   if (collapsible === 'none') {
 228 |     return (
 229 |       <div
 230 |         data-slot='infobar'
 231 |         className={cn(
 232 |           'bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground flex h-full w-(--infobar-width) flex-col',
 233 |           className
 234 |         )}
 235 |         {...props}
 236 |       >
 237 |         {children}
 238 |       </div>
 239 |     );
 240 |   }
 241 |
 242 |   if (isMobile) {
 243 |     return (
 244 |       <Sheet
 245 |         open={openMobile}
 246 |         onOpenChange={(value) => {
 247 |           setOpenMobile(value);
 248 |           setOpen(value);
 249 |         }}
 250 |         {...props}
 251 |       >
 252 |         <SheetContent
 253 |           data-infobar='infobar'
 254 |           data-slot='infobar'
 255 |           data-mobile='true'
 256 |           className='bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground w-(--infobar-width) p-0 [&>button]:hidden'
 257 |           style={
 258 |             {
 259 |               '--infobar-width': INFOBAR_WIDTH_MOBILE
 260 |             } as React.CSSProperties
 261 |           }
 262 |           side={side}
 263 |         >
 264 |           <SheetHeader className='sr-only'>
 265 |             <SheetTitle>Infobar</SheetTitle>
 266 |             <SheetDescription>Displays the mobile infobar.</SheetDescription>
 267 |           </SheetHeader>
 268 |           <div className='flex h-full w-full flex-col'>{children}</div>
 269 |         </SheetContent>
 270 |       </Sheet>
 271 |     );
 272 |   }
 273 |
 274 |   return (
 275 |     <div
 276 |       className='group peer text-sidebar-foreground hidden md:block'
 277 |       data-state={state}
 278 |       data-collapsible={state === 'collapsed' ? collapsible : ''}
 279 |       data-variant={variant}
 280 |       data-side={side}
 281 |       data-slot='infobar'
 282 |       style={
 283 |         {
 284 |           '--infobar-transition-duration': isPathnameChanging ? '0ms' : '300ms'
 285 |         } as React.CSSProperties
 286 |       }
 287 |     >
 288 |       <div
 289 |         data-slot='infobar-container'
 290 |         className={cn(
 291 |           'sticky top-0 z-30 hidden h-[calc(100dvh-4.5rem)] w-(--infobar-width) shrink-0 p-2 pl-0 transition-[width,padding] duration-300 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.32,0.72,0,1)] md:flex',
 292 |           'group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:w-0 group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:overflow-hidden group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:p-0',
 293 |           className
 294 |         )}
 295 |         {...props}
 296 |       >
 297 |         <div
 298 |           data-infobar='infobar'
 299 |           data-slot='infobar-inner'
 300 |           className='bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground flex h-full w-full flex-col overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-sidebar-border transition-[opacity,transform] duration-300 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.32,0.72,0,1)] group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:scale-95 group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:opacity-0'
 301 |         >
 302 |           {children}
 303 |         </div>
 304 |       </div>
 305 |     </div>
 306 |   );
 307 | }

 538 | function InfobarMenuButton({
 539 |   asChild = false,
 540 |   isActive = false,
 541 |   variant = 'default',
 542 |   size = 'default',
 543 |   tooltip,
 544 |   className,
 545 |   ...props
 546 | }: React.ComponentProps<'button'> & {
 547 |   asChild?: boolean;
 548 |   isActive?: boolean;
 549 |   tooltip?: string | React.ComponentProps<typeof TooltipContent>;
 550 | } & VariantProps<typeof infobarMenuButtonVariants>) {
 551 |   const Comp = asChild ? Slot : 'button';
 552 |   const { isMobile, state } = useInfobar();
 553 |
 554 |   const button = (
 555 |     <Comp
 556 |       data-slot='infobar-menu-button'
 557 |       data-infobar='menu-button'
 558 |       data-size={size}
 559 |       data-active={isActive}
 560 |       className={cn(infobarMenuButtonVariants({ variant, size }), className)}
 561 |       {...props}
 562 |     />
 563 |   );
 564 |
 565 |   if (!tooltip) {
 566 |     return button;
 567 |   }
 568 |
 569 |   if (typeof tooltip === 'string') {
 570 |     tooltip = {
 571 |       children: tooltip
 572 |     };
 573 |   }
 574 |
 575 |   return (
 576 |     <Tooltip>
 577 |       <TooltipTrigger asChild>{button}</TooltipTrigger>
 578 |       <TooltipContent
 579 |         side='right'
 580 |         align='center'
 581 |         hidden={state !== 'collapsed' || isMobile}
 582 |         {...tooltip}
 583 |       />
 584 |     </Tooltip>
 585 |   );
 586 | }
```

`components/ui/sidebar.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import * as React from "react"
   2 | import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
   3 | import { PanelLeftIcon } from "lucide-react"
   4 | import { Slot } from "radix-ui"
   5 |
   6 | import { useIsMobile } from "#/hooks/use-mobile.tsx"
   7 | import { cn } from "#/lib/utils.ts"
   8 | import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button.tsx"
   9 | import { Input } from "#/components/ui/input.tsx"
  10 | import { Separator } from "#/components/ui/separator.tsx"
  11 | import {
  12 |   Sheet,
  13 |   SheetContent,
  14 |   SheetDescription,
  15 |   SheetHeader,
  16 |   SheetTitle,
  17 | } from "#/components/ui/sheet.tsx"
  18 | import { Skeleton } from "#/components/ui/skeleton.tsx"
  19 | import {
  20 |   Tooltip,
  21 |   TooltipContent,
  22 |   TooltipProvider,
  23 |   TooltipTrigger,
  24 | } from "#/components/ui/tooltip.tsx"
  25 |
  26 | const SIDEBAR_COOKIE_NAME = "sidebar_state"
  27 | const SIDEBAR_COOKIE_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 24 * 7
  28 | const SIDEBAR_WIDTH = "16rem"
  29 | const SIDEBAR_WIDTH_MOBILE = "18rem"
  30 | const SIDEBAR_WIDTH_ICON = "3rem"
  31 | const SIDEBAR_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUT = "b"
  32 |
  33 | type SidebarContextProps = {
  34 |   state: "expanded" | "collapsed"
  35 |   open: boolean
  36 |   setOpen: (open: boolean) => void
  37 |   openMobile: boolean
  38 |   setOpenMobile: (open: boolean) => void
  39 |   isMobile: boolean
  40 |   toggleSidebar: () => void
  41 | }
  42 |
  43 | const SidebarContext = React.createContext<SidebarContextProps | null>(null)
  44 |
  45 | function useSidebar() {
  46 |   const context = React.useContext(SidebarContext)
  47 |   if (!context) {
  48 |     throw new Error("useSidebar must be used within a SidebarProvider.")
  49 |   }
  50 |
  51 |   return context
  52 | }
  53 |
  54 | function SidebarProvider({
  55 |   defaultOpen = true,
  56 |   open: openProp,
  57 |   onOpenChange: setOpenProp,
  58 |   className,
  59 |   style,
  60 |   children,
  61 |   ...props
  62 | }: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
  63 |   defaultOpen?: boolean
  64 |   open?: boolean
  65 |   onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void
  66 | }) {
  67 |   const isMobile = useIsMobile()
  68 |   const [openMobile, setOpenMobile] = React.useState(false)
  69 |
  70 |   // This is the internal state of the sidebar.
  71 |   // We use openProp and setOpenProp for control from outside the component.
  72 |   const [_open, _setOpen] = React.useState(defaultOpen)
  73 |   const open = openProp ?? _open
  74 |   const setOpen = React.useCallback(
  75 |     (value: boolean | ((value: boolean) => boolean)) => {
  76 |       const openState = typeof value === "function" ? value(open) : value
  77 |       if (setOpenProp) {
  78 |         setOpenProp(openState)
  79 |       } else {
  80 |         _setOpen(openState)
  81 |       }
  82 |
  83 |       // This sets the cookie to keep the sidebar state.
  84 |       document.cookie = `${SIDEBAR_COOKIE_NAME}=${openState}; path=/; max-age=${SIDEBAR_COOKIE_MAX_AGE}`
  85 |     },
  86 |     [setOpenProp, open]
  87 |   )
  88 |
  89 |   // Helper to toggle the sidebar.
  90 |   const toggleSidebar = React.useCallback(() => {
  91 |     return isMobile ? setOpenMobile((open) => !open) : setOpen((open) => !open)
  92 |   }, [isMobile, setOpen, setOpenMobile])
  93 |
  94 |   // Adds a keyboard shortcut to toggle the sidebar.
  95 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  96 |     const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
  97 |       if (
  98 |         event.key === SIDEBAR_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUT &&
  99 |         (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)
 100 |       ) {
 101 |         event.preventDefault()
 102 |         toggleSidebar()
 103 |       }
 104 |     }
 105 |
 106 |     window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown)
 107 |     return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown)
 108 |   }, [toggleSidebar])
 109 |
 110 |   // We add a state so that we can do data-state="expanded" or "collapsed".
 111 |   // This makes it easier to style the sidebar with Tailwind classes.
 112 |   const state = open ? "expanded" : "collapsed"
 113 |
 114 |   const contextValue = React.useMemo<SidebarContextProps>(
 115 |     () => ({
 116 |       state,
 117 |       open,
 118 |       setOpen,
 119 |       isMobile,
 120 |       openMobile,
 121 |       setOpenMobile,
 122 |       toggleSidebar,
 123 |     }),
 124 |     [state, open, setOpen, isMobile, openMobile, setOpenMobile, toggleSidebar]
 125 |   )
 126 |
 127 |   return (
 128 |     <SidebarContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
 129 |       <TooltipProvider delayDuration={0}>
 130 |         <div
 131 |           data-slot="sidebar-wrapper"
 132 |           style={
 133 |             {
 134 |               "--sidebar-width": SIDEBAR_WIDTH,
 135 |               "--sidebar-width-icon": SIDEBAR_WIDTH_ICON,
 136 |               ...style,
 137 |             } as React.CSSProperties
 138 |           }
 139 |           className={cn(
 140 |             "group/sidebar-wrapper flex min-h-svh w-full has-data-[variant=inset]:bg-sidebar",
 141 |             className
 142 |           )}
 143 |           {...props}
 144 |         >
 145 |           {children}
 146 |         </div>
 147 |       </TooltipProvider>
 148 |     </SidebarContext.Provider>
 149 |   )
 150 | }
 151 |
 152 | function Sidebar({
 153 |   side = "left",
 154 |   variant = "sidebar",
 155 |   collapsible = "offcanvas",
 156 |   className,
 157 |   children,
 158 |   ...props
 159 | }: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
 160 |   side?: "left" | "right"
 161 |   variant?: "sidebar" | "floating" | "inset"
 162 |   collapsible?: "offcanvas" | "icon" | "none"
 163 | }) {
 164 |   const { isMobile, state, openMobile, setOpenMobile } = useSidebar()
 165 |
 166 |   if (collapsible === "none") {
 167 |     return (
 168 |       <div
 169 |         data-slot="sidebar"
 170 |         className={cn(
 171 |           "flex h-full w-(--sidebar-width) flex-col bg-sidebar text-sidebar-foreground",
 172 |           className
 173 |         )}
 174 |         {...props}
 175 |       >
 176 |         {children}
 177 |       </div>
 178 |     )
 179 |   }
 180 |
 181 |   if (isMobile) {
 182 |     return (
 183 |       <Sheet open={openMobile} onOpenChange={setOpenMobile} {...props}>
 184 |         <SheetContent
 185 |           data-sidebar="sidebar"
 186 |           data-slot="sidebar"
 187 |           data-mobile="true"
 188 |           className="w-(--sidebar-width) bg-sidebar p-0 text-sidebar-foreground [&>button]:hidden"
 189 |           style={
 190 |             {
 191 |               "--sidebar-width": SIDEBAR_WIDTH_MOBILE,
 192 |             } as React.CSSProperties
 193 |           }
 194 |           side={side}
 195 |         >
 196 |           <SheetHeader className="sr-only">
 197 |             <SheetTitle>Sidebar</SheetTitle>
 198 |             <SheetDescription>Displays the mobile sidebar.</SheetDescription>
 199 |           </SheetHeader>
 200 |           <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col">{children}</div>
 201 |         </SheetContent>
 202 |       </Sheet>
 203 |     )
 204 |   }
 205 |
 206 |   return (
 207 |     <div
 208 |       className="group peer hidden text-sidebar-foreground md:block"
 209 |       data-state={state}
 210 |       data-collapsible={state === "collapsed" ? collapsible : ""}
 211 |       data-variant={variant}
 212 |       data-side={side}
 213 |       data-slot="sidebar"
 214 |     >
 215 |       {/* This is what handles the sidebar gap on desktop */}
 216 |       <div
 217 |         data-slot="sidebar-gap"
 218 |         className={cn(
 219 |           "relative w-(--sidebar-width) bg-transparent transition-[width] duration-200 ease-linear",
 220 |           "group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:w-0",
 221 |           "group-data-[side=right]:rotate-180",
 222 |           variant === "floating" || variant === "inset"
 223 |             ? "group-data-[collapsible=icon]:w-[calc(var(--sidebar-width-icon)+(--spacing(4)))]"
 224 |             : "group-data-[collapsible=icon]:w-(--sidebar-width-icon)"
 225 |         )}
 226 |       />
 227 |       <div
 228 |         data-slot="sidebar-container"
 229 |         className={cn(
 230 |           "fixed inset-y-0 z-10 hidden h-svh w-(--sidebar-width) transition-[left,right,width] duration-200 ease-linear md:flex",
 231 |           side === "left"
 232 |             ? "left-0 group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:left-[calc(var(--sidebar-width)*-1)]"
 233 |             : "right-0 group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:right-[calc(var(--sidebar-width)*-1)]",
 234 |           // Adjust the padding for floating and inset variants.
 235 |           variant === "floating" || variant === "inset"
 236 |             ? "p-2 group-data-[collapsible=icon]:w-[calc(var(--sidebar-width-icon)+(--spacing(4))+2px)]"
 237 |             : "group-data-[collapsible=icon]:w-(--sidebar-width-icon) group-data-[side=left]:border-r group-data-[side=right]:border-l",
 238 |           className
 239 |         )}
 240 |         {...props}
 241 |       >
 242 |         <div
 243 |           data-sidebar="sidebar"
 244 |           data-slot="sidebar-inner"
 245 |           className="flex h-full w-full flex-col bg-sidebar group-data-[variant=floating]:rounded-lg group-data-[variant=floating]:border group-data-[variant=floating]:border-sidebar-border group-data-[variant=floating]:shadow-sm"
 246 |         >
 247 |           {children}
 248 |         </div>
 249 |       </div>
 250 |     </div>
 251 |   )
 252 | }
 253 |
 254 | function SidebarTrigger({
 255 |   className,
 256 |   onClick,
 257 |   ...props
 258 | }: React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>) {
 259 |   const { toggleSidebar } = useSidebar()
 260 |
 261 |   return (
 262 |     <Button
 263 |       data-sidebar="trigger"
 264 |       data-slot="sidebar-trigger"
 265 |       variant="ghost"
 266 |       size="icon"
 267 |       className={cn("size-7", className)}
 268 |       onClick={(event) => {
 269 |         onClick?.(event)
 270 |         toggleSidebar()
 271 |       }}
 272 |       {...props}
 273 |     >
 274 |       <PanelLeftIcon />
 275 |       <span className="sr-only">Toggle Sidebar</span>
 276 |     </Button>
 277 |   )
 278 | }

 291 |       className={cn(
 292 |         "absolute inset-y-0 z-20 hidden w-4 -translate-x-1/2 transition-all ease-linear group-data-[side=left]:-right-4 group-data-[side=right]:left-0 after:absolute after:inset-y-0 after:left-1/2 after:w-[2px] hover:after:bg-sidebar-border sm:flex",
 293 |         "in-data-[side=left]:cursor-w-resize in-data-[side=right]:cursor-e-resize",
 294 |         "[[data-side=left][data-state=collapsed]_&]:cursor-e-resize [[data-side=right][data-state=collapsed]_&]:cursor-w-resize",
 295 |         "group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:translate-x-0 group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:after:left-full hover:group-data-[collapsible=offcanvas]:bg-sidebar",
 296 |         "[[data-side=left][data-collapsible=offcanvas]_&]:-right-2",

 496 | function SidebarMenuButton({
 497 |   asChild = false,
 498 |   isActive = false,
 499 |   variant = "default",
 500 |   size = "default",
 501 |   tooltip,
 502 |   className,
 503 |   ...props
 504 | }: React.ComponentProps<"button"> & {
 505 |   asChild?: boolean
 506 |   isActive?: boolean
 507 |   tooltip?: string | React.ComponentProps<typeof TooltipContent>
 508 | } & VariantProps<typeof sidebarMenuButtonVariants>) {
 509 |   const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "button"
 510 |   const { isMobile, state } = useSidebar()
 511 |
 512 |   const button = (
 513 |     <Comp
 514 |       data-slot="sidebar-menu-button"
 515 |       data-sidebar="menu-button"
 516 |       data-size={size}
 517 |       data-active={isActive}
 518 |       className={cn(sidebarMenuButtonVariants({ variant, size }), className)}
 519 |       {...props}
 520 |     />
 521 |   )
 522 |
 523 |   if (!tooltip) {
 524 |     return button
 525 |   }
 526 |
 527 |   if (typeof tooltip === "string") {
 528 |     tooltip = {
 529 |       children: tooltip,
 530 |     }
 531 |   }
 532 |
 533 |   return (
 534 |     <Tooltip>
 535 |       <TooltipTrigger asChild>{button}</TooltipTrigger>
 536 |       <TooltipContent
 537 |         side="right"
 538 |         align="center"
 539 |         hidden={state !== "collapsed" || isMobile}
 540 |         {...tooltip}
 541 |       />
 542 |     </Tooltip>
 543 |   )
 544 | }
```

`components/ui/slider.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | "use client";
   2 |
   3 | import { Slider as SliderPrimitive } from "radix-ui";
   4 | import * as React from "react";
   5 |
   6 | import { cn } from "#/lib/utils.ts";
   7 |
   8 | function Slider({
   9 | 	className,
  10 | 	defaultValue,
  11 | 	value,
  12 | 	min = 0,
  13 | 	max = 100,
  14 | 	...props
  15 | }: React.ComponentProps<typeof SliderPrimitive.Root>) {
  16 | 	const _values = React.useMemo(
  17 | 		() => (Array.isArray(value) ? value : Array.isArray(defaultValue) ? defaultValue : [min, max]),
  18 | 		[value, defaultValue, min, max]
  19 | 	);
  20 |
  21 | 	return (
  22 | 		<SliderPrimitive.Root
  23 | 			className={cn(
  24 | 				"relative flex w-full touch-none select-none items-center data-[orientation=vertical]:h-full data-[orientation=vertical]:min-h-44 data-[orientation=vertical]:w-auto data-[orientation=vertical]:flex-col data-[disabled]:opacity-50",
  25 | 				className
  26 | 			)}
  27 | 			data-slot='slider'
  28 | 			defaultValue={defaultValue}
  29 | 			max={max}
  30 | 			min={min}
  31 | 			value={value}
  32 | 			{...props}
  33 | 		>
  34 | 			<SliderPrimitive.Track
  35 | 				className={cn(
  36 | 					"relative grow overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted data-[orientation=horizontal]:h-1.5 data-[orientation=vertical]:h-full data-[orientation=horizontal]:w-full data-[orientation=vertical]:w-1.5"
  37 | 				)}
  38 | 				data-slot='slider-track'
  39 | 			>
  40 | 				<SliderPrimitive.Range
  41 | 					className={cn(
  42 | 						"absolute bg-primary data-[orientation=horizontal]:h-full data-[orientation=vertical]:w-full"
  43 | 					)}
  44 | 					data-slot='slider-range'
  45 | 				/>
  46 | 			</SliderPrimitive.Track>
  47 | 			{Array.from({ length: _values.length }, (_, index) => (
  48 | 				<SliderPrimitive.Thumb
  49 | 					className='block size-4 shrink-0 rounded-full border border-primary bg-white shadow-sm ring-ring/50 transition-[color,box-shadow] hover:ring-4 focus-visible:outline-hidden focus-visible:ring-4 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50'
  50 | 					data-slot='slider-thumb'
  51 | 					key={index}
  52 | 				/>
  53 | 			))}
  54 | 		</SliderPrimitive.Root>
  55 | 	);
  56 | }
  57 |
  58 | export { Slider };

```

`context/theme.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { ScriptOnce } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   2 | import { createContext, use, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
   3 |
   4 | type Theme = "dark" | "light";
   5 |
   6 | type ThemeProviderProps = {
   7 | 	children: React.ReactNode;
   8 | 	defaultTheme?: Theme;
   9 | };
  10 |
  11 | type ThemeProviderState = {
  12 | 	setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
  13 | 	theme: Theme;
  14 | };
  15 |
  16 | const ThemeProviderContext = createContext<ThemeProviderState>({
  17 | 	setTheme: () => null,
  18 | 	theme: "light"
  19 | });
  20 |
  21 | export function ThemeProvider({ children, defaultTheme = "light", ...props }: ThemeProviderProps) {
  22 | 	const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(() => {
  23 | 		if (typeof window === "undefined") return defaultTheme;
  24 |
  25 | 		const stored = localStorage.getItem("theme") as Theme | null;
  26 |
  27 | 		if (stored) {
  28 | 			return stored;
  29 | 		}
  30 |
  31 | 		return defaultTheme;
  32 | 	});
  33 |
  34 | 	const applyTheme = useCallback((theme: Theme) => {
  35 | 		const root = window.document.documentElement;
  36 |
  37 | 		root.classList.remove("light", "dark");
  38 | 		root.classList.add(theme);
  39 | 	}, []);
  40 |
  41 | 	useEffect(() => {
  42 | 		applyTheme(theme);
  43 |
  44 | 		localStorage.setItem("theme", theme);
  45 | 	}, [theme, applyTheme]);
  46 |
  47 | 	useEffect(() => {
  48 | 		const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
  49 |
  50 | 		const handler = (event: MediaQueryListEvent) => {
  51 | 			if (!localStorage.getItem("theme")) {
  52 | 				setTheme(event.matches ? "dark" : "light");
  53 | 			}
  54 | 		};
  55 |
  56 | 		media.addEventListener("change", handler);
  57 |
  58 | 		return () => media.removeEventListener("change", handler);
  59 | 	}, []);
  60 |
  61 | 	const value = useMemo(
  62 | 		() => ({
  63 | 			theme,
  64 | 			setTheme
  65 | 		}),
  66 | 		[theme]
  67 | 	);
  68 |
  69 | 	return (
  70 | 		<ThemeProviderContext
  71 | 			{...props}
  72 | 			value={value}
  73 | 		>
  74 | 			<ScriptOnce>
  75 | 				{`
  76 |           (function() {
  77 |             var stored = localStorage.getItem('theme');
  78 |             var root = document.documentElement;
  79 |             var theme = stored || '${defaultTheme}';
  80 |             root.classList.remove('light', 'dark');
  81 |             root.classList.add(theme);
  82 |           })();
  83 |         `}
  84 | 			</ScriptOnce>
  85 | 			{children}
  86 | 		</ThemeProviderContext>
  87 | 	);
  88 | }
  89 |
  90 | export const useTheme = () => {
  91 | 	const context = use(ThemeProviderContext);
  92 |
  93 | 	if (context === undefined) {
  94 | 		throw new Error("useTheme must be used within a ThemeProvider");
  95 | 	}
  96 |
  97 | 	return context;
  98 | };

```

`data/demo-table-data.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { faker } from "@faker-js/faker";
   2 |
   3 | export type Person = {
   4 | 	id: number;
   5 | 	firstName: string;
   6 | 	lastName: string;
   7 | 	age: number;
   8 | 	visits: number;
   9 | 	progress: number;
  10 | 	status: "relationship" | "complicated" | "single";
  11 | 	subRows?: Person[];
  12 | };
  13 |
  14 | const range = (len: number) => {
  15 | 	const arr: number[] = [];
  16 | 	for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
  17 | 		arr.push(i);
  18 | 	}
  19 | 	return arr;
  20 | };
  21 |
  22 | const newPerson = (num: number): Person => {
  23 | 	return {
  24 | 		id: num,
  25 | 		firstName: faker.person.firstName(),
  26 | 		lastName: faker.person.lastName(),
  27 | 		age: faker.number.int(40),
  28 | 		visits: faker.number.int(1000),
  29 | 		progress: faker.number.int(100),
  30 | 		status: faker.helpers.shuffle<Person["status"]>(["relationship", "complicated", "single"])[0]!
  31 | 	};
  32 | };
  33 |
  34 | export function makeData(...lens: number[]) {
  35 | 	const makeDataLevel = (depth = 0): Person[] => {
  36 | 		const len = lens[depth]!;
  37 | 		return range(len).map((index): Person => {
  38 | 			return {
  39 | 				...newPerson(index),
  40 | 				subRows: lens[depth + 1] ? makeDataLevel(depth + 1) : undefined
  41 | 			};
  42 | 		});
  43 | 	};
  44 |
  45 | 	return makeDataLevel();
  46 | }

```

```ts
   1 | import { faker } from "@faker-js/faker";
   2 | import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   3 |
   4 | import { auth } from "#/lib/auth";
   5 |
   6 | import { db } from "./";
   7 | import * as schema from "./schema";
   8 | import { clinicMembers, clinics, doctor, user } from "./schema";
   9 |
  10 | const ADMIN_EMAIL = process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL || "hazem032012@gmail.com";
  11 | const ADMIN_PASSWORD = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || "HealthF26";
  12 | const ADMIN_NAME = process.env.ADMIN_NAME || "Dr.Hazem Ali";
  13 | const ADMINPHONE = "01003497579";
  14 | const CLINICNAME = "Smart Clinic";
  15 |
  16 | export async function seedAdminUser() {
  17 | 	console.log("🌱 Seeding admin user...");
  18 |
  19 | 	try {
  20 | 		// Check if admin already exists
  21 | 		const existingAdmin = await db.query.user.findFirst({
  22 | 			where: (user, { eq }) => eq(user.email, ADMIN_EMAIL)
  23 | 		});
  24 |
  25 | 		if (existingAdmin) {
  26 | 			console.log("✅ Admin user already exists");
  27 | 			return;
  28 | 		}
  29 |
  30 | 		// 4️⃣ Create or Update Clinic
  31 | 		const clinicResult = await db
  32 | 			.insert(clinics)
  33 | 			.values({
  34 | 				id: faker.string.uuid(),
  35 | 				name: CLINICNAME,
  36 | 				address: "Hurghada, Egypt",
  37 | 				phone: ADMINPHONE,
  38 | 				email: ADMIN_EMAIL,
  39 | 				timezone: "Africa/Cairo",
  40 | 				isDeleted: false
  41 | 			})
  42 | 			.onConflictDoUpdate({
  43 | 				target: clinics.name, // unique column
  44 | 				set: {
  45 | 					address: "Hurghada, Egypt",
  46 | 					phone: ADMINPHONE,
  47 | 					email: ADMIN_EMAIL,
  48 | 					timezone: "Africa/Cairo",
  49 | 					isDeleted: false
  50 | 				}
  51 | 			})
  52 | 			.returning();
  53 |
  54 | 		const clinic = clinicResult[0];
  55 | 		if (!clinic) {
  56 | 			throw new Error("Failed to create or retrieve clinic during seeding.");
  57 | 		}
  58 |
  59 | 		console.log(`🏥 Clinic created/updated: ${clinic.name}`);
  60 |

  62 | 		const result = await auth.api.createUser({
  63 | 			body: {
  64 | 				name: ADMIN_NAME,
  65 | 				email: ADMIN_EMAIL,
  66 | 				password: ADMIN_PASSWORD,
  67 | 				role: "admin",
  68 | 				data: { clinicId: clinic.id }
  69 | 			}
  70 | 		});
  71 |
  72 | 		// 4️⃣ Update user and upsert related data
  73 | 		await Promise.all([
  74 | 			// Update user
  75 | 			db.update(user).set({ emailVerified: true, role: "admin" }).where(eq(user.id, result.user.id)),
  76 |
  77 | 			// Upsert configStore
  78 | 			db
  79 | 				.insert(schema.configStore)
  80 | 				.values({ key: "admin_onboarded", value: "true" })
  81 | 				.onConflictDoUpdate({
  82 | 					target: schema.configStore.key,
  83 | 					set: { value: "true" }
  84 | 				}),
  85 |
  86 | 			// Upsert doctor
  87 | 			db
  88 | 				.insert(doctor)
  89 | 				.values({
  90 | 					id: faker.string.uuid(),
  91 | 					userId: result.user.id,
  92 | 					email: ADMIN_EMAIL,
  93 | 					name: ADMIN_NAME,
  94 | 					specialty: "Pediatrician",
  95 | 					licenseNumber: "SMART-ADM-001",
  96 | 					phone: ADMINPHONE,
  97 | 					address: "Hurghada, Egypt",
  98 | 					department: "Pediatrics",
  99 | 					img: faker.image.avatar(),
 100 | 					colorCode: faker.color.rgb(),
 101 | 					availabilityStatus: "Available",
 102 | 					type: "FULL",
 103 | 					role: "admin",
 104 | 					availableFromWeekDay: 1,
 105 | 					availableToWeekDay: 5,
 106 | 					availableFromTime: "09:00",
 107 | 					availableToTime: "17:00",
 108 | 					appointmentPrice: 0,
 109 | 					clinicId: clinic.id ?? ""
 110 | 				})
 111 | 				.onConflictDoUpdate({
 112 | 					target: doctor.userId, // unique column
 113 | 					set: {
 114 | 						name: ADMIN_NAME,
 115 | 						specialty: "Pediatrician",
 116 | 						licenseNumber: "SMART-ADM-001",
 117 | 						phone: ADMINPHONE,
 118 | 						address: "Hurghada, Egypt",
 119 | 						department: "Pediatrics",
 120 | 						img: faker.image.avatar(),
 121 | 						colorCode: faker.color.rgb(),
 122 | 						availabilityStatus: "Available",
 123 | 						type: "FULL",
 124 | 						role: "admin",
 125 | 						availableFromWeekDay: 1,
 126 | 						availableToWeekDay: 5,
 127 | 						availableFromTime: "09:00",
 128 | 						availableToTime: "17:00",
 129 | 						appointmentPrice: 0,
 130 | 						clinicId: clinic.id
 131 | 					}
 132 | 				}),

 149 | 	} catch (error) {

 152 | 	}
 153 | }

 158 | 		.then(() => process.exit)
 159 | 		.catch(() => process.exit);
```

`db/index.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { Database } from "bun:sqlite";
   2 |
   3 | import type { BunSQLiteDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/bun-sqlite";
   4 | import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/bun-sqlite";
   5 |
   6 | import { env } from "../env";
   7 | import * as schema from "./schema";
   8 |
   9 | export function createDb(): BunSQLiteDatabase<typeof schema> {
  10 | 	const sqlite = new Database(env.DATABASE_URL?.replace(/^file:/, "") ?? "./data.db");
  11 |
  12 | 	// In bun:sqlite, use .exec() for pragmas
  13 | 	sqlite.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;");
  14 | 	sqlite.exec("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;");
  15 | 	sqlite.exec("PRAGMA cache_size = -64000;");
  16 | 	sqlite.exec("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;");
  17 |
  18 | 	return drizzle(sqlite, { schema });
  19 | }
  20 | /**
  21 |  * Global singleton (dev + hot reload safe)
  22 |  */
  23 | declare global {
  24 | 	// eslint-disable-next-line no-var
  25 | 	var __db: BunSQLiteDatabase<typeof schema> | undefined;
  26 | }
  27 |
  28 | export const db =
  29 | 	globalThis.__db ??
  30 | 	(() => {
  31 | 		const instance = createDb();
  32 | 		if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
  33 | 			globalThis.__db = instance;
  34 | 		}
  35 | 		return instance;
  36 | 	})();
  37 |
  38 | export type DbExecutor = BunSQLiteDatabase<typeof schema>;
  39 | export type AppDb = typeof db;

```

```ts
   6 |
   7 | const runMigrate = process.argv.includes("--migrate") || process.argv.includes("migrate");
   8 |
   9 | if (runMigrate) {
  10 | 	try {
  11 | 		// 2. The usage is the same, but now it uses Bun's native driver
  12 | 		migrate(db, { migrationsFolder: "./src/database/migrations" });
  13 | 		console.log("✅ Migrations applied successfully");
  14 | 	} catch (error) {
  15 | 		console.error("❌ Migration failed:", error);
  16 | 		process.exitCode = 1;
  17 | 	}
  18 | }
```

`db/queries.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   2 |
   3 | import { db } from ".";
   4 | import { clinicMembers, doctor, guardian, user } from "./schema";
   5 |
   6 |
   7 | export async function checkVitalSignsAccess(userId: string, patientId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   8 | 	const userData = await db.query.user.findFirst({
   9 | 		where: eq(user.id, userId),
  10 | 		with: {
  11 | 			patients: true,
  12 | 			doctors: true
  13 | 		}
  14 | 	});
  15 |
  16 | 	if (!userData) return false;
  17 |
  18 | 	if (userData.role === "admin" || userData.role === "staff") return true;
  19 | 	if (userData.role === "doctor" && userData.doctors) return true;
  20 | 	if (userData.role === "patient" && Array.isArray(userData.patients)) {
  21 | 		return userData.patients.some(p => p.id === patientId);
  22 | 	}
  23 |
  24 | 	// Check if user is guardian
  25 | 	const guardianRecord = await db.query.guardian.findFirst({
  26 | 		where: and(eq(guardian.userId, userId), eq(guardian.patientId, patientId))
  27 | 	});
  28 |
  29 | 	return !!guardianRecord;
  30 | }
  31 |
  32 | // Helper function to check doctor access
  33 | export async function checkDoctorAccess(doctorId: string, userId: string, clinicId?: string) {
  34 | 	const userRecord = await db.query.user.findFirst({
  35 | 		where: eq(user.id, userId),
  36 | 		with: {
  37 | 			doctors: true,
  38 | 			clinics: true
  39 | 		}
  40 | 	});
  41 |
  42 | 	if (!userRecord) return false;
  43 |
  44 | 	// Admin has full access
  45 | 	if (userRecord.role === "admin" || userRecord.role === "superadmin") return true;
  46 |
  47 | 	// Staff has limited access
  48 | 	if (userRecord.role === "staff") return true;
  49 |
  50 | 	// Doctor access
  51 | 	if (userRecord.role === "doctor") {
  52 | 		const doctorRecord = await db.query.doctor.findFirst({
  53 | 			where: and(eq(doctor.userId, userId), eq(doctor.id, doctorId))
  54 | 		});
  55 | 		return !!doctorRecord;
  56 | 	}
  57 |
  58 | 	// Check clinic membership
  59 | 	if (clinicId) {
  60 | 		const clinicMember = await db.query.clinicMembers.findFirst({
  61 | 			where: and(eq(clinicMembers.userId, userId), eq(clinicMembers.clinicId, clinicId))
  62 | 		});
  63 | 		return !!clinicMember;
  64 | 	}
  65 |
  66 | 	return false;
  67 | }

```

`db/schema.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { eq, relations, type SQL, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
   2 | import {
   3 | 	index,
   4 | 	integer,
   5 | 	primaryKey,
   6 | 	real,
   7 | 	type SQLiteColumn,
   8 | 	sqliteTable,
   9 | 	sqliteView,
  10 | 	text,
  11 | 	uniqueIndex
  12 | } from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";
  13 | export const user = sqliteTable(
  14 | 	"user",
  15 | 	{
  16 | 		id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  17 | 		name: text("name").notNull(),
  18 | 		email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
  19 | 		emailVerified: integer("email_verified", { mode: "boolean" }).default(false).notNull(),
  20 | 		image: text("image"),
  21 | 		createdAt: integer("created_at", { mode: "timestamp_ms" }).notNull(),
  22 | 		updatedAt: integer("updated_at", { mode: "timestamp_ms" })
  23 | 			.$onUpdate(() => new Date())
  24 | 			.notNull(),
  25 | 		role: text("role"),
  26 | 		banned: integer("banned", { mode: "boolean" }).default(false),
  27 | 		banReason: text("ban_reason"),
  28 | 		banExpires: integer("ban_expires", { mode: "timestamp_ms" }),
  29 | 		twoFactorEnabled: integer("two_factor_enabled", { mode: "boolean" }).default(false),
  30 | 		apiKey: text("api_key"),
  31 | 		clinicId: text("clinic_id")
  32 | 	},
  33 | 	table => [index("user_email_idx").on(table.email)]
  34 | );
  35 |
  36 | export const userQuota = sqliteTable("user_quota", {
  37 | 	userId: text("user_id")
  38 | 		.primaryKey()
  39 | 		.references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  40 | 	quota: integer("quota").notNull().default(0),
  41 | 	usedQuota: integer("used_quota").notNull().default(0),

 999 | export const userRelations = relations(user, ({ many, one }) => ({
1000 | 	sessions: many(session),
1001 | 	accounts: many(account),
1002 | 	clinic: one(clinics, {
1003 | 		fields: [user.clinicId],
1004 | 		references: [clinics.id]
1005 | 	}),
1006 | 	files: many(files),
1007 | 	folders: many(folders),
```
