```ts
1438 | // =========== DASHBOARD VIEWS ===========
1439 |
1440 | // Note: Switched to sqliteView as schema implies SQLite. Materialized views are not supported in SQLite.
1441 | export const clinicDashboardMV = sqliteView("clinic_dashboard_mv").as(qb => {
1442 | 	const base = qb
1443 | 		.select({
1444 | 			clinicId: clinics.id,
1445 | 			clinicName: clinics.name,
1446 |
1447 | 			// Appointment stats
1448 | 			totalAppointments: sql<number>`(
1449 |     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${appointment} WHERE ${appointment.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1450 |   )`.as("totalAppointments"),
1451 | 			todayAppointments: sql<number>`(
1452 |     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${appointment}
1453 |     WHERE date(${appointment.appointmentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch') = date('now') AND ${appointment.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1454 |   )`.as("todayAppointments"),
1455 | 			upcomingAppointments: sql<number>`(
1456 |     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${appointment}
1457 |     WHERE date(${appointment.appointmentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch') > date('now') AND ${appointment.status} IN ('SCHEDULED', 'PENDING') AND ${appointment.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1458 |   )`.as("upcomingAppointments"),
1459 | 			completedAppointments: sql<number>`(
1460 |     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${appointment}
1461 |     WHERE ${appointment.status} = 'COMPLETED' AND ${appointment.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1462 |   )`.as("completedAppointments"),
1463 | 			// Patient stats
1464 | 			totalPatients: sql<number>`(
1465 |     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${patient} WHERE ${patient.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1466 |   )`.as("totalPatients"),
1467 | 			activePatients: sql<number>`(
1468 |     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${patient}
1469 |     WHERE ${patient.isActive} = true AND ${patient.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1470 |   )`.as("activePatients"),
1471 | 			newPatientsThisMonth: sql<number>`(
1472 |     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${patient}
1473 |     WHERE date(${patient.createdAt}/1000, 'unixepoch') >= strftime('%Y-%m-01', 'now') AND ${patient.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1474 |   )`.as("newPatientsThisMonth"),
1475 | 			avgWaitingTime: sql<number>`(
1476 |         SELECT 0
1477 |       )`.as("avgWaitingTime"),
1478 | 			// Financial stats
1479 | 			monthlyRevenue: sql<number>`(
1480 |     SELECT COALESCE(SUM(${payment.totalAmount}), 0) FROM ${payment}
1481 |     WHERE date(${payment.paymentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch') >= strftime('%Y-%m-01', 'now') AND ${payment.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1482 |   )`.as("monthlyRevenue"),
1483 | 			totalRevenue: sql<number>`(
1484 |     SELECT COALESCE(SUM(${payment.totalAmount}), 0) FROM ${payment}
1485 |     WHERE ${payment.clinicId} = ${clinics.id}
1486 |   )`.as("totalRevenue"),
1487 |
1488 | 			// Doctor stats
1489 | 			activeDoctors:
1490 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${doctor} WHERE ${doctor.clinicId} = ${clinics.id} AND ${doctor.isActive} = true AND ${doctor.status} = 'ACTIVE')`.as(
1491 | 					"activeDoctors"
1492 | 				),
1493 | 			averageDoctorRating:
1494 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT AVG(${doctor.rating}) FROM ${doctor} WHERE ${doctor.clinicId} = ${clinics.id})`.as(
1495 | 					"averageDoctorRating"
1496 | 				),
1497 |
1498 | 			// Pediatric-specific stats
1499 | 			immunizationsDue:
1500 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${immunization} WHERE ${immunization.clinicId} = ${clinics.id} AND ${immunization.date} <= unixepoch('now', '+7 days') * 1000)`.as(
1501 | 					"immunizationsDue"
1502 | 				),
1503 | 			growthChecksPending:
1504 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${vitalSign} WHERE ${vitalSign.clinicId} = ${clinics.id} AND ${vitalSign.recordedAt} < unixepoch('now', '-3 months') * 1000)`.as(
1505 | 					"growthChecksPending"
1506 | 				),
1507 |
1508 | 			// Staff stats
1509 | 			totalStaff: sql<number>`(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${staff} WHERE ${staff.clinicId} = ${clinics.id})`.as(
1510 | 				"totalStaff"
1511 | 			),
1512 |
1513 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1514 | 		})
1515 | 		.from(clinics)
1516 | 		.leftJoin(patient, eq(patient.clinicId, clinics.id))
1517 | 		.leftJoin(appointment, eq(appointment.clinicId, clinics.id))
1518 | 		.leftJoin(doctor, eq(doctor.clinicId, clinics.id))
1519 | 		.leftJoin(payment, eq(payment.clinicId, clinics.id))
1520 | 		.leftJoin(immunization, eq(immunization.clinicId, clinics.id))
1521 | 		.leftJoin(vitalSign, eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinics.id))
1522 | 		.leftJoin(staff, eq(staff.clinicId, clinics.id))
1523 | 		.groupBy(clinics.id, clinics.name);
1524 |
1525 | 	return base;
1526 | });
1527 |
1528 | // =========== PATIENT OVERVIEW VIEW ===========
1529 |
1530 | export const patientOverviewMV = sqliteView("patient_overview_mv").as(qb => {
1531 | 	return qb
1532 | 		.select({
1533 | 			patientId: patient.id,
1534 | 			fullName: sql<string>`${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName}`.as("fullName"),
1535 | 			dateOfBirth: patient.dateOfBirth,
1536 | 			ageMonths:
1537 | 				sql<number>`(strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', datetime(${patient.dateOfBirth}/1000, 'unixepoch'))) * 12 + (strftime('%m', 'now') - strftime('%m', datetime(${patient.dateOfBirth}/1000, 'unixepoch')))`.as(
1538 | 					"ageMonths"
1539 | 				),
1540 | 			gender: patient.gender,
1541 | 			bloodGroup: patient.bloodGroup,
1542 |
1543 | 			// Contact info
1544 | 			phone: patient.phone,
1545 | 			email: patient.email,
1546 | 			address: patient.address,
1547 |
1548 | 			// Medical info
1549 | 			allergies: patient.allergies,
1550 | 			medicalConditions: patient.medicalConditions,
1551 |
1552 | 			// Appointment stats
1553 | 			totalAppointments: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${appointment.id})`.as("totalAppointments"),
1554 | 			lastAppointmentDate: sql<number>`MAX(${appointment.appointmentDate})`.as("lastAppointmentDate"),
1555 | 			upcomingAppointments:
1556 | 				sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN ${appointment.appointmentDate} > (unixepoch() * 1000) AND ${appointment.status} IN ('SCHEDULED', 'PENDING') THEN ${appointment.id} END)`.as(
1557 | 					"upcomingAppointments"
1558 | 				),
1559 |
1560 | 			// Medical stats
1561 | 			totalEncounters: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${diagnosis.id})`.as("totalEncounters"),
1562 | 			totalPrescriptions: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${prescription.id})`.as("totalPrescriptions"),
1563 | 			activePrescriptions:
1564 | 				sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN ${prescription.status} = 'active' AND (${prescription.endDate} IS NULL OR ${prescription.endDate} > (unixepoch() * 1000)) THEN ${prescription.id} END)`.as(
1565 | 					"activePrescriptions"
1566 | 				),
1567 |
1568 | 			// Immunization stats
1569 | 			totalImmunizations: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${immunization.id})`.as("totalImmunizations"),
1570 |
1571 | 			// Growth stats
1572 | 			lastWeight:
1573 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT ${growthRecord.weight} FROM ${growthRecord} WHERE ${growthRecord.patientId} = ${patient.id} ORDER BY ${growthRecord.recordedAt} DESC LIMIT 1)`.as(
1574 | 					"lastWeight"
1575 | 				),
1576 | 			lastHeight:
1577 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT ${growthRecord.height} FROM ${growthRecord} WHERE ${growthRecord.patientId} = ${patient.id} ORDER BY ${growthRecord.recordedAt} DESC LIMIT 1)`.as(
1578 | 					"lastHeight"
1579 | 				),
1580 | 			lastGrowthCheck:
1581 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT ${growthRecord.recordedAt} FROM ${growthRecord} WHERE ${growthRecord.patientId} = ${patient.id} ORDER BY ${growthRecord.recordedAt} DESC LIMIT 1)`.as(
1582 | 					"lastGrowthCheck"
1583 | 				),
1584 |
1585 | 			// Guardian info
1586 | 			guardianPhone:
1587 | 				sql<string>`(SELECT ${guardian.phone} FROM ${guardian} WHERE ${guardian.patientId} = ${patient.id} AND ${guardian.isPrimary} = true LIMIT 1)`.as(
1588 | 					"guardianPhone"
1589 | 				),
1590 |
1591 | 			clinicId: patient.clinicId,
1592 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1593 | 		})
1594 | 		.from(patient)
1595 | 		.leftJoin(appointment, eq(appointment.patientId, patient.id))
1596 | 		.leftJoin(diagnosis, eq(diagnosis.patientId, patient.id))
1597 | 		.leftJoin(prescription, eq(prescription.patientId, patient.id))
1598 | 		.leftJoin(immunization, eq(immunization.patientId, patient.id))
1599 | 		.leftJoin(vitalSign, eq(vitalSign.patientId, patient.id))
1600 | 		.leftJoin(guardian, eq(guardian.patientId, patient.id))
1601 | 		.groupBy(
1602 | 			patient.id,
1603 | 			patient.firstName,
1604 | 			patient.lastName,
1605 | 			patient.dateOfBirth,
1606 | 			patient.gender,
1607 | 			patient.bloodGroup,
1608 | 			patient.phone,
1609 | 			patient.email,
1610 | 			patient.address,
1611 | 			patient.allergies,
1612 | 			patient.medicalConditions,
1613 | 			patient.clinicId
1614 | 		);
1615 | });
1616 |
1617 | // =========== DOCTOR PERFORMANCE VIEW ===========
1618 |
1619 | export const doctorPerformanceMV = sqliteView("doctor_performance_mv").as(qb => {
1620 | 	return qb
1621 | 		.select({
1622 | 			doctorId: doctor.id,
1623 | 			name: doctor.name,
1624 | 			specialty: doctor.specialty,
1625 | 			email: doctor.email,
1626 | 			phone: doctor.phone,
1627 | 			rating: doctor.rating,
1628 |
1629 | 			// Appointment stats
1630 | 			totalAppointments: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${appointment.id})`.as("totalAppointments"),
1631 | 			appointmentsThisMonth:
1632 | 				sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date(${appointment.appointmentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch') >= strftime('%Y-%m-01', 'now') THEN ${appointment.id} END)`.as(
1633 | 					"appointmentsThisMonth"
1634 | 				),
1635 | 			completedAppointments:
1636 | 				sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN ${appointment.status} = 'COMPLETED' THEN ${appointment.id} END)`.as(
1637 | 					"completedAppointments"
1638 | 				),
1639 | 			cancellationRate:
1640 | 				sql<number>`(COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN ${appointment.status} = 'CANCELLED' THEN ${appointment.id} END) * 100.0 / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT ${appointment.id}), 0))`.as(
1641 | 					"cancellationRate"
1642 | 				),
1643 |
1644 | 			// Patient stats
1645 | 			totalPatients: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${patient.id})`.as("totalPatients"),
1646 | 			newPatientsThisMonth:
1647 | 				sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date(${patient.createdAt}/1000, 'unixepoch') >= strftime('%Y-%m-01', 'now') THEN ${patient.id} END)`.as(
1648 | 					"newPatientsThisMonth"
1649 | 				),
1650 |
1651 | 			// Revenue stats
1652 | 			totalRevenue: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${payment.totalAmount}), 0)`.as("totalRevenue"),
1653 | 			monthlyRevenue:
1654 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN date(${payment.paymentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch') >= strftime('%Y-%m-01', 'now') THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1655 | 					"monthlyRevenue"
1656 | 				),
1657 |
1658 | 			// Prescription stats
1659 | 			totalPrescriptions: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${prescription.id})`.as("totalPrescriptions"),
1660 | 			activePrescriptions:
1661 | 				sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN ${prescription.status} = 'active' THEN ${prescription.id} END)`.as(
1662 | 					"activePrescriptions"
1663 | 				),
1664 |
1665 | 			// Rating stats
1666 | 			averagePatientRating: sql<number>`COALESCE(AVG(${rating.rating}), 0)`.as("averagePatientRating"),
1667 | 			totalRatings: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${rating.id})`.as("totalRatings"),
1668 |
1669 | 			// Schedule stats
1670 | 			averagePatientsPerDay:
1671 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(COUNT(DISTINCT ${appointment.id}) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT date(${appointment.appointmentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch')), 0), 0)`.as(
1672 | 					"averagePatientsPerDay"
1673 | 				),
1674 |
1675 | 			clinicId: doctor.clinicId,
1676 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1677 | 		})
1678 | 		.from(doctor)
1679 | 		.leftJoin(appointment, eq(appointment.doctorId, doctor.id))
1680 | 		.leftJoin(patient, eq(patient.id, appointment.patientId))
1681 | 		.leftJoin(payment, eq(payment.appointmentId, appointment.id))
1682 | 		.leftJoin(prescription, eq(prescription.doctorId, doctor.id))
1683 | 		.leftJoin(rating, eq(rating.staffId, doctor.id))
1684 | 		.groupBy(doctor.id, doctor.name, doctor.specialty, doctor.email, doctor.phone, doctor.rating, doctor.clinicId);
1685 | });

1687 | // =========== FINANCIAL OVERVIEW VIEW ===========
1688 |
1689 | export const financialOverviewMV = sqliteView("financial_overview_mv").as(qb => {
1690 | 	return qb
1691 | 		.select({
1692 | 			clinicId: clinics.id,
1693 | 			clinicName: clinics.name,
1694 |
1695 | 			// Revenue by month (last 12 months)
1696 | 			currentMonthRevenue:
1697 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN strftime('%Y-%m-01', datetime(${payment.paymentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch')) = strftime('%Y-%m-01', 'now') THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1698 | 					"currentMonthRevenue"
1699 | 				),
1700 | 			previousMonthRevenue:
1701 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN strftime('%Y-%m-01', datetime(${payment.paymentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch')) = strftime('%Y-%m-01', 'now', '-1 month') THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1702 | 					"previousMonthRevenue"
1703 | 				),
1704 |
1705 | 			// Revenue by category
1706 | 			consultationRevenue:
1707 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN ${service.category} = 'CONSULTATION' THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1708 | 					"consultationRevenue"
1709 | 				),
1710 | 			procedureRevenue:
1711 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN ${service.category} = 'PROCEDURE' THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1712 | 					"procedureRevenue"
1713 | 				),
1714 | 			labRevenue:
1715 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN ${service.category} = 'LAB_TEST' THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1716 | 					"labRevenue"
1717 | 				),
1718 | 			vaccinationRevenue:
1719 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN ${service.category} = 'VACCINATION' THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1720 | 					"vaccinationRevenue"
1721 | 				),
1722 |
1723 | 			// Payment status
1724 | 			totalRevenue: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${payment.totalAmount}), 0)`.as("totalRevenue"),
1725 | 			paidAmount:
1726 | 				sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN ${payment.status} = 'PAID' THEN ${payment.totalAmount} ELSE 0 END), 0)`.as(
1727 | 					"paidAmount"
1728 | 				),
1729 |
1730 | 			// Top revenue sources
1731 | 			topService:
1732 | 				sql<string>`(SELECT ${service.serviceName} FROM ${service} LEFT JOIN ${payment} ON ${payment.billId} = ${service.id} WHERE ${service.clinicId} = ${clinics.id} GROUP BY ${service.id}, ${service.serviceName} ORDER BY SUM(${payment.totalAmount}) DESC LIMIT 1)`.as(
1733 | 					"topService"
1734 | 				),
1735 | 			topDoctor:
1736 | 				sql<string>`(SELECT ${doctor.name} FROM ${doctor} LEFT JOIN ${appointment} ON ${appointment.doctorId} = ${doctor.id} LEFT JOIN ${payment} ON ${payment.appointmentId} = ${appointment.id} WHERE ${doctor.clinicId} = ${clinics.id} GROUP BY ${doctor.id}, ${doctor.name} ORDER BY SUM(${payment.totalAmount}) DESC LIMIT 1)`.as(
1737 | 					"topDoctor"
1738 | 				),
1739 |
1740 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1741 | 		})
1742 | 		.from(clinics)
1743 | 		.leftJoin(payment, eq(payment.clinicId, clinics.id))
1744 | 		.groupBy(clinics.id, clinics.name);
1745 | });

1747 | // =========== APPOINTMENT SCHEDULE VIEW ===========
1748 |
1749 | export const appointmentScheduleMV = sqliteView("appointment_schedule_mv").as(qb => {
1750 | 	return qb
1751 | 		.select({
1752 | 			appointmentId: appointment.id,
1753 | 			date: appointment.appointmentDate,
1754 | 			startTime: appointment.time,
1755 | 			status: appointment.status,
1756 | 			type: appointment.type,
1757 | 			reason: appointment.reason,
1758 | 			durationMinutes: appointment.durationMinutes,
1759 | 			// Patient info
1760 | 			patientId: patient.id,
1761 | 			patientName: sql<string>`${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName}`.as("patientName"),
1762 | 			patientAgeMonths:
1763 | 				sql<number>`(strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', datetime(${patient.dateOfBirth}/1000, 'unixepoch'))) * 12 + (strftime('%m', 'now') - strftime('%m', datetime(${patient.dateOfBirth}/1000, 'unixepoch')))`.as(
1764 | 					"patientAgeMonths"
1765 | 				),
1766 | 			patientGender: patient.gender,
1767 | 			patientPhone: patient.phone,
1768 |
1769 | 			// Doctor info
1770 | 			doctorId: doctor.id,
1771 | 			doctorName: doctor.name,
1772 | 			doctorSpecialty: doctor.specialty,
1773 | 			doctorColorCode: doctor.colorCode,
1774 |
1775 | 			// Service info
1776 | 			serviceId: service.id,
1777 | 			serviceName: service.serviceName,
1778 | 			serviceCategory: service.category,
1779 | 			servicePrice: service.price,
1780 |
1781 | 			// Billing info
1782 | 			paymentStatus: payment.status,
1783 | 			totalAmount: payment.totalAmount,
1784 | 			amountPaid: payment.amountPaid,
1785 |
1786 | 			clinicId: appointment.clinicId,
1787 | 			searchVector:
1788 | 				sql<string>`(${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName} || ' ' || ${doctor.name} || ' ' || COALESCE(${appointment.reason}, '') || ' ' || COALESCE(${service.serviceName}, '') || ' ' || CAST(${appointment.status} AS TEXT))`.as(
1789 | 					"searchVector"
1790 | 				),
1791 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1792 | 		})
1793 | 		.from(appointment)
1794 | 		.innerJoin(patient, eq(patient.id, appointment.patientId))
1795 | 		.innerJoin(doctor, eq(doctor.id, appointment.doctorId))
1796 | 		.leftJoin(diagnosis, eq(diagnosis.id, appointment.id))
1797 | 		.leftJoin(service, eq(service.id, appointment.serviceId))
1798 | 		.leftJoin(payment, eq(payment.appointmentId, appointment.id));
1799 | });

1801 | // =========== PATIENT GROWTH CHART VIEW ===========
1802 |
1803 | export const patientGrowthChartMV = sqliteView("patient_growth_chart_mv").as(qb => {
1804 | 	return qb
1805 | 		.select({
1806 | 			patientId: patient.id,
1807 | 			fullName: sql<string>`${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName}`.as("fullName"),
1808 | 			gender: patient.gender,
1809 | 			dateOfBirth: patient.dateOfBirth,
1810 |
1811 | 			// Growth records
1812 | 			ageDays: growthRecord.ageDays,
1813 | 			ageMonths: growthRecord.ageMonths,
1814 | 			recordedAt: growthRecord.recordedAt,
1815 |
1816 | 			// Measurements
1817 | 			weight: growthRecord.weight,
1818 | 			height: growthRecord.height,
1819 | 			headCircumference: growthRecord.headCircumference,
1820 | 			bmi: growthRecord.bmi,
1821 |
1822 | 			// WHO Percentiles
1823 | 			weightForAgeZ: growthRecord.weightForAgeZ,
1824 | 			heightForAgeZ: growthRecord.heightForAgeZ,
1825 | 			hcForAgeZ: growthRecord.hcForAgeZ,
1826 |
1827 | 			// WHO Percentile classifications
1828 | 			weightPercentile: sql<string>`CASE
1829 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.weightForAgeZ} < -3 THEN 'Severely Underweight'
1830 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.weightForAgeZ} < -2 THEN 'Underweight'
1831 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.weightForAgeZ} <= 1 THEN 'Normal'
1832 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.weightForAgeZ} <= 2 THEN 'Overweight'
1833 |         ELSE 'Obese'
1834 |       END`.as("weightPercentile"),
1835 | 			heightPercentile: sql<string>`CASE
1836 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.heightForAgeZ} < -3 THEN 'Severely Stunted'
1837 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.heightForAgeZ} < -2 THEN 'Stunted'
1838 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.heightForAgeZ} <= 1 THEN 'Normal'
1839 |         WHEN ${growthRecord.heightForAgeZ} <= 2 THEN 'Tall'
1840 |         ELSE 'Very Tall'
1841 |       END`.as("heightPercentile"),
1842 | 			notes: growthRecord.notes,
1843 |
1844 | 			clinicId: patient.clinicId,
1845 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1846 | 		})
1847 | 		.from(patient)
1848 | 		.innerJoin(growthRecord, eq(growthRecord.patientId, patient.id))
1849 | 		.orderBy(growthRecord.ageDays);
1850 | });

1852 | // =========== IMMUNIZATION SCHEDULE VIEW ===========
1853 |
1854 | export const immunizationScheduleMV = sqliteView("immunization_schedule_mv").as(qb => {
1855 | 	return qb
1856 | 		.select({
1857 | 			patientId: patient.id,
1858 | 			fullName: sql<string>`${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName}`.as("fullName"),
1859 | 			dateOfBirth: patient.dateOfBirth,
1860 | 			ageMonths:
1861 | 				sql<number>`(strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', datetime(${patient.dateOfBirth}/1000, 'unixepoch'))) * 12 + (strftime('%m', 'now') - strftime('%m', datetime(${patient.dateOfBirth}/1000, 'unixepoch')))`.as(
1862 | 					"ageMonths"
1863 | 				),
1864 |
1865 | 			// Immunization info
1866 | 			immunizationId: immunization.id,
1867 | 			vaccineName: immunization.vaccine,
1868 | 			doseNumber: immunization.dose,
1869 | 			administrationDate: immunization.date,
1870 |
1871 | 			// Schedule info
1872 | 			isMandatory: vaccineSchedule.isMandatory,
1873 | 			description: vaccineSchedule.description,
1874 |
1875 | 			notes: immunization.notes,
1876 |
1877 | 			clinicId: patient.clinicId,
1878 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1879 | 		})
1880 | 		.from(patient)
1881 | 		.innerJoin(immunization, eq(immunization.patientId, patient.id))
1882 | 		.leftJoin(
1883 | 			vaccineSchedule,
1884 | 			sql`${vaccineSchedule.vaccineName} = ${immunization.vaccine} AND ${vaccineSchedule.ageInDaysMin} <= (unixepoch('now') - ${patient.dateOfBirth}/1000) / 86400`
1885 | 		);
1886 | });

1888 | // =========== MEDICAL RECORDS VIEW ===========
1889 |
1890 | export const medicalRecordsMV = sqliteView("medical_records_mv").as(qb => {
1891 | 	return qb
1892 | 		.select({
1893 | 			medicalRecordId: medicalRecord.id ?? "",
1894 |
1895 | 			// Patient info
1896 | 			patientId: patient.id,
1897 | 			patientName: sql<string>`${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName}`.as("patientName"),
1898 |
1899 | 			// Doctor info
1900 | 			doctorId: doctor.id,
1901 | 			doctorName: doctor.name,
1902 | 			doctorSpecialty: doctor.specialty,
1903 |
1904 | 			// Encounter info
1905 | 			encounterId: diagnosis.id,
1906 | 			encounterDate: diagnosis.date,
1907 | 			encounterType: diagnosis.type,
1908 | 			diagnosis: diagnosis.diagnosis,
1909 | 			treatment: diagnosis.treatment,
1910 |
1911 | 			// Appointment info
1912 | 			appointmentId: appointment.id,
1913 | 			appointmentDate: appointment.appointmentDate,
1914 | 			appointmentReason: appointment.reason,
1915 |
1916 | 			// Medical data
1917 | 			symptoms: medicalRecord.symptoms,
1918 | 			followUpDate: medicalRecord.followUpDate,
1919 |
1920 | 			// Prescriptions from this encounter
1921 | 			prescriptionCount:
1922 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${prescription} WHERE ${prescription.encounterId} = ${diagnosis.id})`.as(
1923 | 					"prescriptionCount"
1924 | 				),
1925 |
1926 | 			// Lab tests from this encounter
1927 | 			labTestCount:
1928 | 				sql<number>`(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${labTest} WHERE ${labTest.recordId} = ${medicalRecord.id})`.as(
1929 | 					"labTestCount"
1930 | 				),
1931 |
1932 | 			clinicId: medicalRecord.clinicId,
1933 | 			searchVector:
1934 | 				sql<string>`(${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName} || ' ' || ${doctor.name} || ' ' || COALESCE(${medicalRecord.diagnosis}, '') || ' ' || COALESCE(${medicalRecord.symptoms}, ''))`.as(
1935 | 					"searchVector"
1936 | 				),
1937 | 			updatedAt: sql<number>`(unixepoch() * 1000)`.as("updatedAt")
1938 | 		})
1939 | 		.from(medicalRecord)
1940 | 		.innerJoin(patient, eq(patient.id, medicalRecord.patientId))
1941 | 		.innerJoin(doctor, eq(doctor.id, medicalRecord.doctorId))
1942 | 		.leftJoin(diagnosis, eq(diagnosis.medicalId, medicalRecord.id))
1943 | 		.leftJoin(appointment, eq(appointment.id, medicalRecord.appointmentId))
1944 | 		.leftJoin(vitalSign, eq(vitalSign.encounterId, diagnosis.id));
1945 | });

1972 | // =========== SORT COLUMN MAPPING ===========
1973 |
1974 | const appointmentSortMap: Record<string, SQL<unknown>> = {
1975 | 	date: sql`${appointmentScheduleMV.date}`,
1976 | 	patientName: sql`${appointmentScheduleMV.patientName}`,
1977 | 	doctorName: sql`${appointmentScheduleMV.doctorName}`,
1978 | 	status: sql`${appointmentScheduleMV.status}`,
1979 | 	time: sql`${appointmentScheduleMV.startTime}`
1980 | };
1981 |
1982 | const patientSortMap: Record<string, SQL<unknown>> = {
1983 | 	name: sql`${patientOverviewMV.fullName}`,
1984 | 	age: sql`${patientOverviewMV.ageMonths}`,
1985 | 	lastVisit: sql`${patientOverviewMV.lastAppointmentDate}`
1986 | 	// mrn: sql`${patientOverviewMV.medicalRecordNumber}`,
1987 | };
1988 |
1989 | const doctorSortMap: Record<string, SQL<unknown>> = {
1990 | 	name: sql`${doctorPerformanceMV.name}`,
1991 | 	specialty: sql`${doctorPerformanceMV.specialty}`,
1992 | 	rating: sql`${doctorPerformanceMV.rating}`,
1993 | 	appointments: sql`${doctorPerformanceMV.totalAppointments}`,
1994 | 	revenue: sql`${doctorPerformanceMV.totalRevenue}`
1995 | };
1996 |
1997 | export function getAppointmentSortColumn(sortBy: string): SQL<unknown> {
1998 | 	return appointmentSortMap[sortBy] || sql`${appointmentScheduleMV.date}`;
1999 | }
2000 |
2001 | export function getPatientSortColumn(sortBy: string): SQL<unknown> {
2002 | 	return patientSortMap[sortBy] || sql`${patientOverviewMV.fullName}`;
2003 | }
2004 |
2005 | export function getDoctorSortColumn(sortBy: string): SQL<unknown> {
2006 | 	return doctorSortMap[sortBy] || sql`${doctorPerformanceMV.name}`;
2007 | }

2010 | export type NewDatabaseUser = typeof user.$inferInsert;
2011 | export type DatabaseAccount = typeof account.$inferSelect;
2012 | export type NewDatabaseAccount = typeof account.$inferInsert;
2013 | export type DatabaseSession = typeof session.$inferSelect;
2014 | export type NewDatabaseSession = typeof session.$inferInsert;
2015 | export type DatabaseVerification = typeof verification.$inferSelect;
2016 | export type NewDatabaseVerification = typeof verification.$inferInsert;
2017 | export type DatabaseFiles = typeof files.$inferSelect;
2018 | export type NewDatabaseFiles = typeof files.$inferInsert;
2019 | export type DatabaseFolders = typeof folders.$inferSelect;
2020 | export type NewDatabaseFolders = typeof folders.$inferInsert;
```

`db/seed.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import "dotenv/config";
   2 |
   3 | import { type AppDb, db } from "./";
   4 | import { runSeed } from "./seed/seed";
   5 | import drugSeed from "./seed/seed-drugs";
   6 | import wfaSeed from "./seed/seed-wfa";
   7 |
   8 | /**
   9 |  * Master seed runner
  10 |  */
  11 | async function runAllSeeds(db: AppDb) {
  12 | 	console.log("🚀 Starting Master Seeding Orchestration...\n");
  13 |
  14 | 	await runSeed(db);
  15 | 	console.log("✅ Base Data (Clinic / Faker) Seeded");
  16 | 	console.log("--------------------------------------------------");
  17 |
  18 | 	await drugSeed(db);
  19 | 	console.log("✅ NICU Drug Database Seeded");
  20 | 	console.log("--------------------------------------------------");
  21 |
  22 | 	await wfaSeed(db);
  23 | 	console.log("✅ WHO WFA (JSON) Seeded");
  24 | 	console.log("--------------------------------------------------");
  25 |
  26 | 	console.log("🎉 All seeds completed successfully!");
  27 | }
  28 |
  29 | /**
  30 |  * Top-level execution
  31 |  */
  32 | (async () => {
  33 | 	try {
  34 | 		await runAllSeeds(db);
  35 | 	} catch (error) {
  36 | 		console.error("❌ Master Seeding failed:", error);
  37 | 		process.exitCode = 1;
  38 | 	}
  39 | })();
```

`db/seed/seed-drugs.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
   2 | import * as fs from "node:fs";
   3 | import * as path from "node:path";
   4 |
   5 | import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   6 |
   7 | import type { AppDb } from "../";
   8 | import { doseGuideline, drug as drugs } from "../schema";
   9 |
  10 | /* =======================
  11 |    Types (unchanged)
  12 | ======================= */
  13 |
  14 | type DoseGuidelineData = {
  15 | 	ROUTE: string;
  16 | 	CLINICAL_INDICATION: string;
  17 | 	MIN_DOSE_PER_KG: number | string;
  18 | 	MAX_DOSE_PER_KG: number | string;
  19 | 	DOSE_UNIT: string;
  20 | 	FREQUENCY_DAYS: string;
  21 | 	GESTATIONAL_AGE_WEEKS_MIN: number | string;
  22 | 	GESTATIONAL_AGE_WEEKS_MAX: number | string;
  23 | 	POST_NATAL_AGE_DAYS_MIN: number | string;
  24 | 	POST_NATAL_AGE_DAYS_MAX: number | string;
  25 | 	MAX_DOSE_PER_24H: number | string;
  26 | 	STOCK_CONCENTRATION_MG_ML: number | string;
  27 | 	FINAL_CONCENTRATION_MG_ML: number | string;
  28 | 	MIN_INFUSION_TIME_MIN: number | string;
  29 | 	COMPATIBILITY_DILUENT: string;
  30 | };
  31 |
  32 | type DrugDatabase = {
  33 | 	[drugName: string]: DoseGuidelineData[];
  34 | };
  35 |
  36 | /* =======================
  37 |    Helpers (unchanged)
  38 | ======================= */
  39 |
  40 | function parseNumericValue(value: string | number): number | null {
  41 | 	if (value === "N/A" || value === "" || value == null) return null;
  42 | 	if (typeof value === "number") return value;
  43 | 	const parsed = Number.parseFloat(value.trim());
  44 | 	return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
  45 | }
  46 |
  47 | function parseIntegerValue(value: string | number): number | null {
  48 | 	if (value === "N/A" || value === "" || value == null) return null;
  49 | 	if (typeof value === "number") return Math.floor(value);
  50 | 	const parsed = Number.parseInt(value.trim(), 10);
  51 | 	return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
  52 | }
  53 |
  54 | /* =======================
  55 |    Drizzle Seed
  56 | ======================= */
  57 |
  58 | export default async function drugSeed(db: AppDb) {
  59 | 	console.log("🌱 Starting NICU Drug Database seeding...");
  60 |
  61 | 	const filePath = path.resolve("src/db/data/nicu_data.json");
  62 | 	console.log(`📖 Reading data from: ${filePath}`);
  63 |
  64 | 	const drugData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf-8")) as DrugDatabase;
  65 |
  66 | 	const drugNames = Object.keys(drugData);
  67 | 	console.log(`📊 Found ${drugNames.length} drugs`);
  68 |
  69 | 	const guidelinesBuffer: (typeof doseGuideline.$inferInsert)[] = [];
  70 | 	let totalGuidelines = 0;
  71 |
  72 | 	for (const [drugName, guidelines] of Object.entries(drugData)) {
  73 | 		/* ---------- upsert drug ---------- */
  74 | 		let drug = await db.select().from(drugs).where(eq(drugs.name, drugName)).get();
  75 |
  76 | 		if (!drug) {
  77 | 			const id = randomUUID();
  78 | 			await db.insert(drugs).values({ id, name: drugName });
  79 | 			drug = {
  80 | 				id,
  81 | 				name: drugName,
  82 | 				genericName: null,
  83 | 				brandName: null,
  84 | 				description: null,
  85 | 				sideEffects: null,
  86 | 				interactions: null,
  87 | 				contraindications: null,
  88 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
  89 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
  90 | 			};
  91 | 		}
  92 |
  93 | 		/* ---------- collect guidelines ---------- */
  94 | 		for (const g of guidelines) {
  95 | 			guidelinesBuffer.push({
  96 | 				id: randomUUID(),
  97 | 				drugId: drug.id,
  98 | 				route: g.ROUTE,
  99 | 				clinicalIndication: g.CLINICAL_INDICATION,
 100 |
 101 | 				minDosePerKg: parseNumericValue(g.MIN_DOSE_PER_KG),
 102 | 				maxDosePerKg: parseNumericValue(g.MAX_DOSE_PER_KG),
 103 | 				doseUnit: g.DOSE_UNIT === "N/A" ? null : g.DOSE_UNIT,
 104 | 				frequencyDays: g.FREQUENCY_DAYS === "N/A" ? null : g.FREQUENCY_DAYS,
 105 |
 106 | 				gestationalAgeWeeksMin: parseNumericValue(g.GESTATIONAL_AGE_WEEKS_MIN),
 107 | 				gestationalAgeWeeksMax: parseNumericValue(g.GESTATIONAL_AGE_WEEKS_MAX),
 108 |
 109 | 				postNatalAgeDaysMin: parseIntegerValue(g.POST_NATAL_AGE_DAYS_MIN),
 110 | 				postNatalAgeDaysMax: parseIntegerValue(g.POST_NATAL_AGE_DAYS_MAX),
 111 |
 112 | 				maxDosePer24h: parseNumericValue(g.MAX_DOSE_PER_24H),
 113 | 				stockConcentrationMgMl: parseNumericValue(g.STOCK_CONCENTRATION_MG_ML),
 114 | 				finalConcentrationMgMl: parseNumericValue(g.FINAL_CONCENTRATION_MG_ML),
 115 | 				minInfusionTimeMin: parseIntegerValue(g.MIN_INFUSION_TIME_MIN),
 116 |
 117 | 				compatibilityDiluent: g.COMPATIBILITY_DILUENT === "N/A" ? null : g.COMPATIBILITY_DILUENT
 118 | 			});
 119 | 		}
 120 |
 121 | 		totalGuidelines += guidelines.length;
 122 | 		console.log(`✅ ${drugName}: ${guidelines.length} guidelines`);
 123 | 	}
 124 |
 125 | 	/* ---------- batch insert ---------- */
 126 | 	const BATCH_SIZE = 1000;
 127 | 	console.log(`⏳ Inserting ${guidelinesBuffer.length} guidelines...`);
 128 |
 129 | 	for (let i = 0; i < guidelinesBuffer.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
 130 | 		await db.insert(doseGuideline).values(guidelinesBuffer.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE));
 131 | 	}
 132 |
 133 | 	console.log("🎉 NICU Drug Database seeded successfully!");
 134 | 	console.log(`📈 Summary — Drugs: ${drugNames.length}, Guidelines: ${totalGuidelines}`);
 135 | }
```

`db/seed/seed-wfa.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
   2 | import * as path from "node:path";
   3 | import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
   4 |
   5 | import type { AppDb } from "..";
   6 | import { whoGrowthStandard } from "../schema";
   7 | import { Gender, MeasurementType } from "../seed/seed";
   8 |
   9 | const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
  10 | const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
  11 |
  12 | const ZSCORE_DATA_PATH = path.join(__dirname, "..", "data", "zscore-wfa.json");
  13 |
  14 | type ZScoreDataPoint = {
  15 | 	Day: string;
  16 | 	L: string;
  17 | 	M: string;
  18 | 	S: string;
  19 | 	SD4neg: string;
  20 | 	SD3neg: string;
  21 | 	SD2neg: string;
  22 | 	SD1neg: string;
  23 | 	SD0: string;
  24 | 	SD1: string;
  25 | 	SD2: string;
  26 | 	SD3: string;
  27 | 	SD4: string;
  28 | };
  29 |
  30 | type GenderData = {
  31 | 	boys: ZScoreDataPoint[];
  32 | 	girls: ZScoreDataPoint[];
  33 | };
  34 |
  35 | type ZScoreData = {
  36 | 	wfa: GenderData;
  37 | };
  38 |
  39 | type ValidatedGrowthRecord = {
  40 | 	id: string;
  41 | 	ageInMonths: number;
  42 | 	ageDays: number;
  43 | 	gender: string;
  44 | 	measurementType: string;
  45 | 	lValue: number;
  46 | 	mValue: number;
  47 | 	sValue: number;
  48 | 	sd0: number;
  49 | 	sd1neg: number;
  50 | 	sd1pos: number;
  51 | 	sd2neg: number;
  52 | 	sd2pos: number;
  53 | 	sd3neg: number;
  54 | 	sd3pos: number;
  55 | 	sd4neg: number | null;
  56 | 	sd4pos: number | null;
  57 | };
  58 |
  59 | function parseNumericValue(value: string | undefined): number | null {
  60 | 	if (value === undefined || value.trim() === "") {
  61 | 		return null;
  62 | 	}
  63 | 	const parsed = Number.parseFloat(value);
  64 | 	return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
  65 | }
  66 |
  67 | function calculateAgeInMonths(ageDays: number): number {
  68 | 	// Calculate age in months with better precision
  69 | 	return Number((ageDays / 30.4375).toFixed(2)); // Keep 2 decimal places for accuracy
  70 | }
  71 |
  72 | function mapDataPoint(dataPoint: ZScoreDataPoint, measurementType: string, gender: string): ValidatedGrowthRecord {
  73 | 	const ageDaysRaw = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.Day);
  74 | 	if (ageDaysRaw === null) {
  75 | 		throw new Error(`Invalid 'Day' value: ${dataPoint.Day}`);
  76 | 	}
  77 | 	const ageDays = Math.round(ageDaysRaw);
  78 | 	const ageInMonths = calculateAgeInMonths(ageDays);
  79 |
  80 | 	const lValue = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.L);
  81 | 	const mValue = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.M);
  82 | 	const sValue = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.S);
  83 | 	const sd0 = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD0);
  84 | 	const sd1neg = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD1neg);
  85 | 	const sd1pos = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD1);
  86 | 	const sd2neg = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD2neg);
  87 | 	const sd2pos = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD2);
  88 | 	const sd3neg = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD3neg);
  89 | 	const sd3pos = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD3);
  90 | 	const sd4neg = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD4neg);
  91 | 	const sd4pos = parseNumericValue(dataPoint.SD4);
  92 |
  93 | 	const requiredFields = {
  94 | 		lValue,
  95 | 		mValue,
  96 | 		sValue,
  97 | 		sd0,
  98 | 		sd1neg,
  99 | 		sd1pos,
 100 | 		sd2neg,
 101 | 		sd2pos,
 102 | 		sd3neg,
 103 | 		sd3pos
 104 | 	};
 105 |
 106 | 	for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(requiredFields)) {
 107 | 		if (value === null) {
 108 | 			throw new Error(`Missing or invalid required numeric field: ${key}`);
 109 | 		}
 110 | 	}
 111 |
 112 | 	const validatedFields = requiredFields as Record<keyof typeof requiredFields, number>;
 113 |
 114 | 	return {
 115 | 		id: randomUUID(),
 116 | 		ageInMonths,
 117 | 		ageDays,
 118 | 		gender,
 119 | 		measurementType,
 120 | 		lValue: validatedFields.lValue,
 121 | 		mValue: validatedFields.mValue,
 122 | 		sValue: validatedFields.sValue,
 123 | 		sd0: validatedFields.sd0,
 124 | 		sd1neg: validatedFields.sd1neg,
 125 | 		sd1pos: validatedFields.sd1pos,
 126 | 		sd2neg: validatedFields.sd2neg,
 127 | 		sd2pos: validatedFields.sd2pos,
 128 | 		sd3neg: validatedFields.sd3neg,
 129 | 		sd3pos: validatedFields.sd3pos,
 130 | 		sd4neg,
 131 | 		sd4pos
 132 | 	};
 133 | }
 134 |
 135 | export default async function wfaSeed(db: AppDb) {
 136 | 	console.log("--- Starting WHO Growth Standards Seeding ---");
 137 |
 138 | 	let whoJson: ZScoreData;
 139 | 	try {
 140 | 		whoJson = (await import(ZSCORE_DATA_PATH)).default as ZScoreData;
 141 | 		if (!whoJson?.wfa) {
 142 | 			throw new Error("JSON file is missing required 'wfa' data.");
 143 | 		}
 144 | 	} catch (error) {
 145 | 		console.error(`❌ Error reading or parsing WHO data file at ${ZSCORE_DATA_PATH}.`);
 146 | 		console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error");
 147 | 		return;
 148 | 	}
 149 |
 150 | 	const allRecords: ValidatedGrowthRecord[] = [];
 151 | 	let recordsSkipped = 0;
 152 |
 153 | 	console.log("Processing wfa data...");
 154 |
 155 | 	// Process boys data
 156 | 	if (whoJson.wfa.boys && Array.isArray(whoJson.wfa.boys)) {
 157 | 		console.log(`  Processing ${whoJson.wfa.boys.length} boys records...`);
 158 | 		let boysProcessed = 0;
 159 | 		let boysSkipped = 0;
 160 | 		// FIX 1: Replace forEach with for...of
 161 | 		for (const dataPoint of whoJson.wfa.boys) {
 162 | 			try {
 163 | 				const mappedRecord = mapDataPoint(dataPoint, MeasurementType.WFA, Gender.MALE);
 164 | 				allRecords.push(mappedRecord);
 165 | 				boysProcessed += 1; // FIX 2: Replace ++
 166 | 			} catch (error) {
 167 | 				boysSkipped += 1; // FIX 3: Replace ++
 168 | 				recordsSkipped += 1; // FIX 4: Replace ++
 169 | 				console.warn(`Skipping boys record Day ${dataPoint.Day}: ${error}`);
 170 | 			}
 171 | 		}
 172 | 		console.log(`    ✅ Processed: ${boysProcessed}, ❌ Skipped: ${boysSkipped}`);
 173 | 	}
 174 |
 175 | 	// Process girls data
 176 | 	if (whoJson.wfa.girls && Array.isArray(whoJson.wfa.girls)) {
 177 | 		console.log(`  Processing ${whoJson.wfa.girls.length} girls records...`);
 178 | 		let girlsProcessed = 0;
 179 | 		let girlsSkipped = 0;
 180 |
 181 | 		// FIX 5: Replace forEach with for...of
 182 | 		for (const dataPoint of whoJson.wfa.girls) {
 183 | 			try {
 184 | 				const mappedRecord = mapDataPoint(dataPoint, MeasurementType.WFA, Gender.FEMALE);
 185 | 				allRecords.push(mappedRecord);
 186 | 				girlsProcessed += 1; // FIX 6: Replace ++
 187 | 			} catch (error) {
 188 | 				girlsSkipped += 1; // FIX 7: Replace ++
 189 | 				recordsSkipped += 1; // FIX 8: Replace ++
 190 | 				console.warn(`Skipping girls record Day ${dataPoint.Day}: ${error}`);
 191 | 			}
 192 | 		}
 193 | 		console.log(`    ✅ Processed: ${girlsProcessed}, ❌ Skipped: ${girlsSkipped}`);
 194 | 	}
 195 |
 196 | 	console.log(`\nTotal records successfully mapped: ${allRecords.length}`);
 197 |
 198 | 	if (recordsSkipped > 0) {
 199 | 		console.log(`Records skipped due to errors: ${recordsSkipped}`);
 200 | 	}
 201 |
 202 | 	// Check for duplicates using ageDays + gender + measurementType
 203 | 	const uniqueRecordsMap = new Map<string, ValidatedGrowthRecord>();
 204 | 	const duplicates: string[] = [];
 205 |
 206 | 	for (const record of allRecords) {
 207 | 		// Use ageDays as the unique identifier along with gender and measurementType
 208 | 		const key = `${record.gender}:${record.measurementType}:${record.ageDays}`;
 209 |
 210 | 		if (uniqueRecordsMap.has(key)) {
 211 | 			duplicates.push(key);
 212 | 			console.warn(`⚠️ Duplicate found: ${key} (Age: ${record.ageInMonths} months)`);
 213 | 		} else {
 214 | 			uniqueRecordsMap.set(key, record);
 215 | 		}
 216 | 	}
 217 |
 218 | 	const dataToInsert = Array.from(uniqueRecordsMap.values());
 219 |
 220 | 	console.log(`\nUnique records ready for insertion: ${dataToInsert.length}`);
 221 | 	if (duplicates.length > 0) {
 222 | 		console.log(`Actual duplicates found and removed: ${duplicates.length}`);
 223 | 	}
 224 |
 225 | 	// Show sample age calculations
 226 | 	console.log("\n📏 Sample Age Calculations:");
 227 | 	const sampleAges = [0, 30, 365, 1000, 1856]; // Sample days
 228 |
 229 | 	// FIX 9: Replace forEach with for...of
 230 | 	for (const days of sampleAges) {
 231 | 		const months = calculateAgeInMonths(days);
 232 | 		console.log(`  ${days} days = ${months} months`);
 233 | 	}
 234 |
 235 | 	// Clear existing data
 236 |
 237 | 	console.log("\nClearing existing WHO growth standard data...");
 238 |
 239 | 	try {
 240 | 		await db.delete(whoGrowthStandard).execute();
 241 | 		console.log("✅ Existing data cleared.");
 242 | 	} catch (error) {
 243 | 		console.error("❌ Could not clear existing data:", error);
 244 | 		return;
 245 | 	}
 246 | 	// Perform Bulk Insertion
 247 | 	if (dataToInsert.length > 0) {
 248 | 		try {
 249 | 			const batchSize = 1000;
 250 | 			let totalInserted = 0;
 251 |
 252 | 			for (let i = 0; i < dataToInsert.length; i += batchSize) {
 253 | 				const batch = dataToInsert.slice(i, i + batchSize);
 254 | 				const batchNumber = Math.floor(i / batchSize) + 1;
 255 |
 256 | 				console.log(`\nInserting batch ${batchNumber} (${batch.length} records)...`);
 257 |
 258 | 				await db.insert(whoGrowthStandard).values(batch).execute();
 259 |
 260 | 				totalInserted += batch.length;
 261 |
 262 | 				console.log(`  ✅ Inserted ${batch.length} records in batch ${batchNumber}.`);
 263 | 			}
 264 |
 265 | 			console.log(`\n🎉 SUCCESS! Inserted ${totalInserted} total WHO Growth Standard records.`);
 266 |
 267 | 			const summary = {
 268 | 				"Boys records": whoJson.wfa.boys?.length || 0,
 269 | 				"Girls records": whoJson.wfa.girls?.length || 0,
 270 | 				"Total expected": (whoJson.wfa.boys?.length || 0) + (whoJson.wfa.girls?.length || 0),
 271 | 				"Successfully processed": allRecords.length,
 272 | 				"Skipped due to errors": recordsSkipped,
 273 | 				"Actual duplicates removed": duplicates.length,
 274 | 				"Final inserted": totalInserted
 275 | 			};
 276 |
 277 | 			console.log("\n📊 Seeding Summary:");
 278 | 			for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(summary)) {
 279 | 				console.log(`  ${key}: ${value}`);
 280 | 			}
 281 | 		} catch (error) {
 282 | 			console.error("❌ Failed to insert WHO Growth Standard records:", error);
 283 | 		}
 284 | 	} else {
 285 | 		console.log("⚠️ No valid WHO Growth Standard data was mapped for insertion.");
 286 | 	}
 287 | }

```

`db/seed/seed.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { faker } from "@faker-js/faker";
   2 | import type { SQLiteTableWithColumns } from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";
   3 |
   4 | import type { AppDb } from "..";
   5 | // 导入类型
   6 | import type {
   7 | 	DatabaseUser,

 178 | // Helper functions
 179 | const getRandomEnumValue = <T>(enumObj: Record<string, T>): T => {
 180 | 	const enumValues = Object.values(enumObj) as T[];
 181 | 	if (enumValues.length === 0) {
 182 | 		throw new Error("Enum object has no values");
 183 | 	}
 184 | 	const index = Math.floor(Math.random() * enumValues.length);
 185 | 	const value = enumValues[index];
 186 | 	if (value === undefined) {
 187 | 		throw new Error("Failed to pick a random enum value");
 188 | 	}
 189 | 	return value;
 190 | };
 191 |
 192 | const getRandomSubset = <T>(array: T[], count: number): T[] => {
 193 | 	const shuffled = [...array].sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random());
 194 | 	return shuffled.slice(0, Math.min(count, shuffled.length));
 195 | };
 196 |
 197 | const weightedRandom = <T>(items: Array<{ item: T; weight: number }>): T => {
 198 | 	const total = items.reduce((sum, { weight }) => sum + weight, 0);
 199 | 	let random = Math.random() * total;
 200 |
 201 | 	if (items.length === 0) {
 202 | 		throw new Error("weightedRandom called with empty items");
 203 | 	}
 204 |
 205 | 	for (const { item, weight } of items) {
 206 | 		if (random < weight) return item;
 207 | 		random -= weight;
 208 | 	}
 209 |
 210 | 	const fallbackItem = items[0];
 211 | 	if (fallbackItem === undefined) {
 212 | 		throw new Error("weightedRandom: items[0] is undefined");
 213 | 	}
 214 | 	return fallbackItem.item;
 215 | };
 216 |
 217 | // Time helper functions
 218 | const randomTime = (): string => {
 219 | 	const hours = faker.number.int({ min: 8, max: 17 }).toString().padStart(2, "0");
 220 | 	const minutes = faker.helpers.arrayElement(["00", "15", "30", "45"]);
 221 | 	return `${hours}:${minutes}`;
 222 | };
 223 |
 224 | // Generate realistic date of birth based on patient type
 225 | const generateDateOfBirth = (patientType: "adult" | "child" | "infant"): Date => {
 226 | 	switch (patientType) {
 227 | 		case "infant":
 228 | 			return faker.date.birthdate({ min: 0, max: 2, mode: "age" });
 229 | 		case "child":
 230 | 			return faker.date.birthdate({ min: 2, max: 18, mode: "age" });
 231 | 		default: // adult
 232 | 			return faker.date.birthdate({ min: 18, max: 90, mode: "age" });
 233 | 	}
 234 | };
 235 |
 236 | // Helper function to safely delete data
 237 | // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noExplicitAny: <ok>
 238 | const safeDeleteMany = async (db: AppDb, table: SQLiteTableWithColumns<any>): Promise<void> => {
 239 | 	try {
 240 | 		await db.delete(table).execute();
 241 | 	} catch (err: unknown) {
 242 | 		const errMsg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
 243 | 		if (errMsg.includes("no such table") || errMsg.includes("does not exist")) {
 244 | 			return;
 245 | 		}
 246 | 		throw err;
 247 | 	}
 248 | };
 249 |
 250 | // Function to clear all data in proper order (respecting foreign key constraints)
 251 | const clearAllData = async (db: AppDb) => {
 252 | 	console.log("🗑️ Clearing existing data in proper order...");
 253 |
 254 | 	// Clear data in reverse order of dependencies
 255 | 	const clearOperations = [
 256 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, prescribedItem),
 257 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, prescription),
 258 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, doseGuideline),
 259 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, drug),
 260 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, vitalSign),
 261 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, diagnosis),
 262 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, labTest),
 263 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, patientBill),
 264 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, payment),
 265 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, reminder),
 266 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, feedingLog),
 267 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, immunization),
 268 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, developmentalMilestones),
 269 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, developmentalChecks),
 270 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, rating),
 271 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, medicalRecord),
 272 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, appointment),
 273 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, workingDays),
 274 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, service),
 275 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, guardian),
 276 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, growthRecord),
 277 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, whoGrowthStandard),
 278 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, vaccineSchedule),
 279 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, staff),
 280 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, doctor),
 281 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, patient),
 282 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, clinicMembers),
 283 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, clinicSetting),
 284 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, clinics),
 285 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, verification),
 286 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, account),
 287 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, session),
 288 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, userQuota),
 289 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, invites),
 290 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, twoFactor),
 291 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, folders),
 292 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, files),
 293 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, configStore),
 294 | 		() => safeDeleteMany(db, user)
 295 | 	];
 296 |
 297 | 	for (const operation of clearOperations) {
 298 | 		await operation();
 299 | 	}
 300 | };

 302 | // Create realistic clinics
 303 | async function createClinics(db: AppDb) {
 304 | 	console.log("🏥 Creating clinics...");
 305 | 	const clinicsData: DbClinic[] = [];
 306 | 	const clinicNames = [
 307 | 		"City Medical Center",
 308 | 		"Community Health Clinic",
 309 | 		"Family Care Hospital",
 310 | 		"Pediatric Specialists",
 311 | 		"General Healthcare Clinic",
 312 | 		"Metropolitan Hospital",
 313 | 		"Sunrise Medical Center",
 314 | 		"Children's Health Center"
 315 | 	];
 316 |
 317 | 	for (const name of clinicNames) {
 318 | 		const clinicData = {
 319 | 			id: faker.string.uuid(),
 320 | 			name,
 321 | 			email: faker.internet.email({
 322 | 				firstName: (name.split(" ")[0] ?? "clinic").toLowerCase()
 323 | 			}),
 324 | 			timezone: faker.helpers.arrayElement(["UTC", "America/New_York", "Europe/London", "Asia/Tokyo"]),
 325 | 			address: faker.location.streetAddress(),
 326 | 			phone: faker.phone.number(),
 327 | 			deletedAt: null,
 328 | 			isDeleted: false,
 329 | 			createdAt: faker.date.past({ years: 2 }),
 330 | 			updatedAt: faker.date.past({ years: 2 })
 331 | 		};
 332 |
 333 | 		await db.insert(clinics).values(clinicData).execute();
 334 | 		clinicsData.push(clinicData);
 335 |
 336 | 		// Create clinic settings
 337 | 		await db
 338 | 			.insert(clinicSetting)
 339 | 			.values({
 340 | 				id: faker.string.uuid(),
 341 | 				clinicId: clinicData.id,
 342 | 				openingTime: "08:00",
 343 | 				closingTime: "17:00",
 344 | 				workingDays: "MON,TUE,WED,THU,FRI,SAT",
 345 | 				defaultAppointmentDuration: faker.helpers.arrayElement([15, 30, 45, 60]),
 346 | 				requireEmergencyContact: faker.datatype.boolean(),
 347 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 348 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 349 | 			})
 350 | 			.execute();
 351 | 	}
 352 |
 353 | 	return clinicsData;
 354 | }

 474 | // Associate users with clinics
 475 | async function associateUsersWithClinics(db: AppDb, users: DatabaseUser[], clinicsData: DbClinic[]) {
 476 | 	console.log("🔗 Associating users with clinics...");
 477 |
 478 | 	for (const user of users) {
 479 | 		const userClinics = getRandomSubset(clinicsData, 1);
 480 | 		for (const clinic of userClinics) {
 481 | 			await db
 482 | 				.insert(clinicMembers)
 483 | 				.values({
 484 | 					userId: user.id,
 485 | 					clinicId: clinic.id,
 486 | 					role: user.role ?? "USER",
 487 | 					createdAt: faker.date.past({ years: 1 }),
 488 | 					updatedAt: faker.date.past({ years: 1 })
 489 | 				})
 490 | 				.execute();
 491 | 		}
 492 | 	}
 493 | }

 495 | // Create doctors with realistic data
 496 | async function createDoctors(db: AppDb, users: DatabaseUser[], clinicsData: DbClinic[]) {
 497 | 	console.log("👨‍⚕️ Creating doctors...");
 498 | 	const doctors: DbDoctor[] = [];
 499 | 	const doctorUsers = users.filter(user => user.role === "DOCTOR");
 500 |
 501 | 	const specialties = [
 502 | 		"Pediatrics",
 503 | 		"Cardiology",
 504 | 		"Dermatology",
 505 | 		"Neurology",
 506 | 		"Orthopedics",
 507 | 		"Gynecology",
 508 | 		"Psychiatry",
 509 | 		"General Medicine",
 510 | 		"Surgery",
 511 | 		"Oncology",
 512 | 		"Endocrinology",
 513 | 		"Gastroenterology",
 514 | 		"Pulmonology",
 515 | 		"Rheumatology",
 516 | 		"Urology"
 517 | 	];
 518 |
 519 | 	for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(CONFIG.totalDoctors, doctorUsers.length); i++) {
 520 | 		const user = doctorUsers[i];
 521 | 		if (!user) continue;
 522 |
 523 | 		const clinic = faker.helpers.arrayElement(clinicsData);
 524 | 		const specialty = faker.helpers.arrayElement(specialties);
 525 |
 526 | 		const doctorData = {
 527 | 			id: faker.string.uuid(),
 528 | 			email: user.email ?? "",
 529 | 			name: user.name,
 530 | 			userId: user.id,
 531 | 			clinicId: clinic.id,
 532 | 			specialty,
 533 | 			licenseNumber: faker.string.alphanumeric(10).toUpperCase(),
 534 | 			phone: faker.phone.number(),
 535 | 			address: faker.location.streetAddress(),
 536 | 			department: faker.helpers.arrayElement(["Emergency", "Outpatient", "Surgery", "ICU", "Pediatrics"]),
 537 | 			img: faker.image.avatar(),
 538 | 			colorCode: faker.color.rgb(),
 539 | 			availabilityStatus: weightedRandom([
 540 | 				{ item: "Available", weight: 0.6 },
 541 | 				{ item: "Busy", weight: 0.2 },
 542 | 				{ item: "On Leave", weight: 0.1 },
 543 | 				{ item: "Off Duty", weight: 0.1 }
 544 | 			]),
 545 | 			availableFromWeekDay: faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 5 }),
 546 | 			availableToWeekDay: faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 5 }),
 547 | 			availableFromTime: "08:00",
 548 | 			availableToTime: "18:00",
 549 | 			type: getRandomEnumValue(JOBTYPE),
 550 | 			appointmentPrice: faker.number.int({ min: 80, max: 300 }),
 551 | 			status: weightedRandom([
 552 | 				{ item: Status.ACTIVE, weight: 0.85 },
 553 | 				{ item: Status.INACTIVE, weight: 0.1 },
 554 | 				{ item: Status.DORMANT, weight: 0.05 }
 555 | 			]),
 556 | 			isActive: faker.datatype.boolean(0.9),
 557 | 			role: "DOCTOR",
 558 | 			rating: faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 5 }),
 559 | 			isDeleted: false,
 560 | 			deletedAt: null,
 561 | 			createdAt: faker.date.past({ years: 2 }),
 562 | 			updatedAt: faker.date.past({ years: 2 })
 563 | 		};
 564 |
 565 | 		await db.insert(doctor).values(doctorData).execute();
 566 | 		doctors.push(doctorData);
 567 |
 568 | 		// Create working days
 569 | 		const days = ["Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday"];
 570 | 		const workingDayList = getRandomSubset(days, faker.number.int({ min: 4, max: 6 }));
 571 |
 572 | 		for (const day of workingDayList) {
 573 | 			await db
 574 | 				.insert(workingDays)
 575 | 				.values({
 576 | 					id: faker.string.uuid(),
 577 | 					doctorId: doctorData.id,
 578 | 					day,
 579 | 					startTime: faker.helpers.arrayElement(["08:00", "09:00", "10:00"]),
 580 | 					endTime: faker.helpers.arrayElement(["16:00", "17:00", "18:00"]),
 581 | 					createdAt: new Date(),
 582 | 					updatedAt: new Date()
 583 | 				})
 584 | 				.execute();
 585 | 		}
 586 | 	}
 587 |
 588 | 	return doctors;
 589 | }

 591 | // Create staff members
 592 | async function createStaff(db: AppDb, users: DatabaseUser[], clinicsData: DbClinic[]) {
 593 | 	console.log("👨‍💼 Creating staff...");
 594 | 	const staffData: Staff[] = [];
 595 | 	const staffUsers = users.filter(user => user.role === "STAFF");
 596 |
 597 | 	for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(CONFIG.totalStaff, staffUsers.length); i++) {
 598 | 		const user = staffUsers[i];
 599 | 		if (!user) continue;
 600 |
 601 | 		const clinic = faker.helpers.arrayElement(clinicsData);
 602 |
 603 | 		const staffMember = {
 604 | 			id: faker.string.uuid(),
 605 | 			email: user.email ?? "",
 606 | 			name: user.name,
 607 | 			phone: faker.phone.number(),
 608 | 			userId: user.id,
 609 | 			clinicId: clinic.id,
 610 | 			address: faker.location.streetAddress(),
 611 | 			department: faker.helpers.arrayElement([
 612 | 				"Administration",
 613 | 				"Nursing",
 614 | 				"Laboratory",
 615 | 				"Pharmacy",
 616 | 				"Reception"
 617 | 			]),

 837 | // Create appointments
 838 | async function createAppointments(db: AppDb, patients: DbPatient[], doctors: DbDoctor[], services: DbService[]) {
 839 | 	console.log("📅 Creating appointments...");
 840 | 	const appointments: DbAppointment[] = [];
 841 |
 842 | 	for (let i = 0; i < CONFIG.totalAppointments; i++) {
 843 | 		const patient = faker.helpers.arrayElement(patients);
 844 | 		const doctor = faker.helpers.arrayElement(doctors);
 845 | 		const service = faker.helpers.arrayElement(services);
 846 | 		const clinicId = doctor.clinicId
 847 | 			? doctor.clinicId
 848 | 			: (faker.helpers.arrayElement([...new Set(patients.map(p => p.clinicId))]) ?? "");
 849 |
 850 | 		const status = weightedRandom([
 851 | 			{ item: AppointmentStatus.SCHEDULED, weight: 0.4 },
 852 | 			{ item: AppointmentStatus.COMPLETED, weight: 0.4 },
 853 | 			{ item: AppointmentStatus.CANCELLED, weight: 0.15 },
 854 | 			{ item: AppointmentStatus.PENDING, weight: 0.05 }
 855 | 		]);
 856 |
 857 | 		let appointmentDate: Date;
 858 | 		if (status === AppointmentStatus.COMPLETED) {
 859 | 			appointmentDate = faker.date.past({ years: 1 });
 860 | 		} else if (status === AppointmentStatus.SCHEDULED) {
 861 | 			appointmentDate = faker.date.future({ years: 0.5 });
 862 | 		} else {
 863 | 			const fromDate = faker.date.past({ years: 1 });
 864 | 			const toDate = faker.date.future({ years: 0.5 });
 865 | 			appointmentDate = faker.date.between({ from: fromDate, to: toDate });
 866 | 		}
 867 |
 868 | 		const appointmentData = {
 869 | 			id: faker.string.uuid(),
 870 | 			patientId: patient.id,
 871 | 			doctorId: doctor.id,
 872 | 			serviceId: service.id,
 873 | 			doctorSpecialty: doctor.specialty,
 874 | 			appointmentDate,
 875 | 			appointmentPrice: doctor.appointmentPrice,
 876 | 			clinicId,
 877 | 			durationMinutes: faker.number.int({ min: 30, max: 120 }),
 878 | 			time: randomTime(),
 879 | 			status: status as string,
 880 | 			type: weightedRandom([
 881 | 				{ item: "Checkup", weight: 0.5 },
 882 | 				{ item: "Follow-up", weight: 0.3 },
 883 | 				{ item: "Emergency", weight: 0.1 },
 884 | 				{ item: "Consultation", weight: 0.1 }
 885 | 			]),
 886 | 			note: faker.datatype.boolean(0.7) ? faker.lorem.sentence() : null,
 887 | 			reason: faker.datatype.boolean(0.8) ? faker.lorem.sentence() : null,
 888 | 			isDeleted: false,
 889 | 			deletedAt: null,
 890 | 			createdAt: faker.date.past({ years: 1 }),
 891 | 			updatedAt: faker.date.past({ years: 1 })
 892 | 		};
 893 |
 894 | 		await db.insert(appointment).values(appointmentData).execute();
 895 | 		appointments.push(appointmentData);
 896 |
 897 | 		// Create reminders for upcoming appointments
 898 | 		if (status === AppointmentStatus.SCHEDULED && appointmentDate > new Date()) {
 899 | 			await createReminder(db, appointmentData);
 900 | 		}
 901 | 	}
 902 |
 903 | 	return appointments;
 904 | }

 926 | // Create medical records and related data
 927 | async function createMedicalRecords(
 928 | 	db: AppDb,
 929 | 	patients: DbPatient[],
 930 | 	appointments: DbAppointment[],
 931 | 	services: DbService[]
 932 | ) {
 933 | 	console.log("📋 Creating medical records & related data...");
 934 | 	const medicalRecords: DbMedicalRecord[] = [];
 935 |
 936 | 	// Use only completed appointments for medical records
 937 | 	const completedAppointments = appointments.filter(a => a.status === AppointmentStatus.COMPLETED);
 938 |
 939 | 	for (const appointment of getRandomSubset(completedAppointments, CONFIG.totalMedicalRecords)) {
 940 | 		const patient = patients.find(p => p.id === appointment.patientId);
 941 | 		if (!patient) continue;
 942 |
 943 | 		const medicalRecordData = {
 944 | 			id: faker.string.uuid(),
 945 | 			patientId: appointment.patientId ?? "",
 946 | 			appointmentId: appointment.id,
 947 | 			doctorId: appointment.doctorId,
 948 | 			treatmentPlan: faker.datatype.boolean(0.8) ? faker.lorem.paragraph() : null,
 949 | 			labRequest: faker.datatype.boolean(0.5) ? faker.lorem.sentence() : null,
 950 | 			clinicId: appointment.clinicId ?? "",
 951 | 			diagnosis: faker.datatype.boolean(0.7) ? faker.lorem.words(faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 3 })) : null,
 952 | 			symptoms: faker.datatype.boolean(0.8) ? faker.lorem.words(faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 5 })) : null,
 953 | 			notes: faker.datatype.boolean(0.6) ? faker.lorem.paragraph() : null,
 954 | 			attachments: faker.datatype.boolean(0.3) ? faker.system.filePath() : null,
 955 | 			followUpDate: faker.datatype.boolean(0.4) ? faker.date.future({ years: 0.5 }) : null,
 956 | 			isDeleted: false,
 957 | 			deletedAt: null,
 958 | 			status: faker.datatype.boolean(0.8) ? "completed" : "pending",
 959 | 			diagnosisDate: faker.date.past({ years: 0.5 }),
 960 | 			medications: faker.datatype.boolean(0.5) ? faker.lorem.words(faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 3 })) : null,
 961 | 			createdAt: appointment.appointmentDate,
 962 | 			updatedAt: appointment.appointmentDate
 963 | 		};
 964 |
 965 | 		await db.insert(medicalRecord).values(medicalRecordData).execute();
 966 | 		medicalRecords.push(medicalRecordData);
 967 |
 968 | 		try {
 969 | 			// Create encounter
 970 | 			const encounter = await createDiagnosis(db, medicalRecordData, appointment);
 971 | 			// Create vital signs
 972 | 			if (faker.datatype.boolean(0.8)) {
 973 | 				await createVitalSigns(db, patient, medicalRecordData, encounter);
 974 | 			}
 975 |
 976 | 			if (faker.datatype.boolean(0.4)) {
 977 | 				await createLabTest(db, medicalRecordData, services);
 978 | 			}
 979 | 		} catch (error) {
 980 | 			console.error("Failed to create related data for medical record:", medicalRecordData.id, error);
 981 | 			throw error;
 982 | 		}
 983 | 	}
 984 |
 985 | 	return medicalRecords;
 986 | }

 998 | 			{ item: "Initial", weight: 0.4 },
 999 | 			{ item: "Follow-up", weight: 0.4 },
1000 | 			{ item: "Emergency", weight: 0.1 },
1001 | 			{ item: "Consultation", weight: 0.1 }
1002 | 		]),
1003 | 		diagnosis: medicalRecord.diagnosis,
1004 | 		treatment: medicalRecord.treatmentPlan,
1005 | 		notes: medicalRecord.notes,
1006 | 		medicalId: medicalRecord.id,
1007 | 		symptoms: medicalRecord.symptoms ?? "",
1008 | 		prescribedMedications: faker.datatype.boolean(0.5) ? faker.lorem.words(3) : null,
1009 | 		followUpPlan: faker.datatype.boolean(0.6) ? faker.lorem.sentence() : null,
1010 | 		isDeleted: false,
1011 | 		deletedAt: null,

1013 | 		updatedAt: appointment.appointmentDate,
1014 | 		status: null

1020 |
1021 | // Create vital signs
1022 | const createVitalSigns = async (
1023 | 	db: AppDb,
1024 | 	patient: DbPatient,
1025 | 	medicalRecord: DbMedicalRecord,
1026 | 	encounter: DbDiagnosis
1027 | ) => {
1028 | 	const ageDays = Math.floor((Date.now() - patient.dateOfBirth.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
1029 | 	const ageMonths = Math.floor(ageDays / 30);
1030 |
1031 | 	const vitalSignsData = {
1032 | 		id: faker.string.uuid(),
1033 | 		encounterId: encounter.id,
1034 | 		patientId: patient.id,
1035 | 		medicalId: medicalRecord.id,
1036 | 		growthRecordId: null,
1037 | 		recordedAt: encounter.date ?? medicalRecord.createdAt,
1038 | 		gender: patient.gender,
1039 | 		ageDays,
1040 | 		ageMonths,
1041 | 		notes: faker.datatype.boolean(0.3) ? faker.lorem.sentence() : null,
1042 | 		bodyTemperature: null as number | null,
1043 | 		systolic: null as number | null,
1044 | 		diastolic: null as number | null,
1045 | 		heartRate: null as number | null,
1046 | 		respiratoryRate: null as number | null,
1047 | 		oxygenSaturation: null as number | null,
1048 | 		createdAt: new Date(),
1049 | 		updatedAt: new Date()
1050 | 	};
1051 |
1052 | 	const growthRecordData = {
1053 | 		id: faker.string.uuid(),
1054 | 		patientId: patient.id,
1055 | 		recordedAt: encounter.date ?? medicalRecord.createdAt,
1056 | 		height: null as number | null,
1057 | 		weight: null as number | null,
1058 | 		bmi: null as number | null,
1059 | 		date: encounter.date ?? medicalRecord.createdAt,
1060 | 		headCircumference: null as number | null,
1061 | 		notes: null as string | null,
1062 | 		gender: patient.gender,
1063 | 		ageDays,
1064 | 		ageMonths,
1065 | 		weightForAgeZ: null as number | null,
1066 | 		heightForAgeZ: null as number | null,
1067 | 		bmiForAgeZ: null as number | null,
1068 | 		hcForAgeZ: null as number | null,
1069 | 		createdAt: new Date(),
1070 | 		updatedAt: new Date()
1071 | 	};
1072 |
1073 | 	// Add vitals based on patient age
1074 | 	if (ageMonths < 24) {
1075 | 		// Infant/child
1076 | 		vitalSignsData.bodyTemperature = faker.number.float({
1077 | 			min: 36.5,
1078 | 			max: 37.5
1079 | 		});
1080 | 		growthRecordData.height = faker.number.float({
1081 | 			min: 45,
1082 | 			max: 100
1083 | 		});
1084 | 		growthRecordData.weight = faker.number.float({
1085 | 			min: 2.5,
1086 | 			max: 20
1087 | 		});
1088 | 		growthRecordData.headCircumference = faker.number.float({
1089 | 			min: 30,
1090 | 			max: 50
1091 | 		});
1092 | 	} else {
1093 | 		// Adult
1094 | 		vitalSignsData.bodyTemperature = faker.number.float({
1095 | 			min: 36.0,
1096 | 			max: 37.5
1097 | 		});
1098 | 		growthRecordData.height = faker.number.float({
1099 | 			min: 150,
1100 | 			max: 200
1101 | 		});
1102 | 		growthRecordData.weight = faker.number.float({
1103 | 			min: 45,
1104 | 			max: 120
1105 | 		});
1106 | 		vitalSignsData.systolic = faker.number.int({ min: 100, max: 140 });
1107 | 		vitalSignsData.diastolic = faker.number.int({ min: 60, max: 90 });
1108 |
1109 | 		vitalSignsData.heartRate = faker.number.int({ min: 60, max: 100 });
1110 | 		vitalSignsData.respiratoryRate = faker.number.int({ min: 12, max: 20 });
1111 | 		vitalSignsData.oxygenSaturation = faker.number.int({ min: 95, max: 100 });
1112 |
1113 | 		// Calculate BMI
1114 | 		if (growthRecordData.height && growthRecordData.weight) {
1115 | 			const heightInM = growthRecordData.height / 100;
1116 | 			growthRecordData.bmi = growthRecordData.weight / (heightInM * heightInM);
1117 | 		}
1118 |
1119 | 		// Calculate WHO percentiles for children if under 5 years
1120 | 		if (ageMonths < 60 && growthRecordData.height && growthRecordData.weight) {
1121 | 			growthRecordData.weightForAgeZ = faker.number.float({
1122 | 				min: -2,
1123 | 				max: 2
1124 | 			});
1125 | 			growthRecordData.heightForAgeZ = faker.number.float({
1126 | 				min: -2,
1127 | 				max: 2
1128 | 			});
1129 | 			growthRecordData.bmiForAgeZ = faker.number.float({
1130 | 				min: -2,
1131 | 				max: 2
1132 | 			});
1133 | 			growthRecordData.hcForAgeZ = growthRecordData.headCircumference
1134 | 				? faker.number.float({ min: -2, max: 2 })
1135 | 				: null;
1136 | 		}
1137 | 	}
1138 |
1139 | 	await db.insert(vitalSign).values(vitalSignsData).execute();
1140 | 	await db.insert(growthRecord).values(growthRecordData).execute();
1141 | };
1142 |

1287 | // Create payments
1288 | const createPayments = async (
1289 | 	db: AppDb,
1290 | 	appointments: DbAppointment[],
1291 | 	patients: DbPatient[],
1292 | 	services: DbService[]
1293 | ) => {
1294 | 	console.log("💰 Creating payments...");
1295 | 	const payments: DbPayment[] = [];
1296 |
1297 | 	const paymentAppointments = getRandomSubset(appointments, CONFIG.totalPayments);
1298 |
1299 | 	for (const appointment of paymentAppointments) {
1300 | 		const patient = patients.find(p => p.id === appointment.patientId);
1301 | 		if (!patient) continue;
1302 |
1303 | 		const totalAmount = faker.number.int({ min: 50, max: 500 });
1304 | 		const amountPaid = faker.datatype.boolean(0.8) ? totalAmount : faker.number.int({ min: 0, max: totalAmount });
1305 |
1306 | 		const status =
1307 | 			amountPaid >= totalAmount
1308 | 				? PaymentStatus.PAID
1309 | 				: amountPaid > 0
1310 | 					? PaymentStatus.PARTIAL
1311 | 					: PaymentStatus.UNPAID;
1312 |
1313 | 		const paymentData = {
1314 | 			id: faker.string.uuid(),
1315 | 			clinicId: appointment.clinicId,
1316 | 			patientId: appointment.patientId,
1317 | 			appointmentId: appointment.id,
1318 | 			billDate: appointment.appointmentDate,
1319 | 			paymentDate:
1320 | 				status === PaymentStatus.PAID
1321 | 					? faker.date.between({
1322 | 							from:
1323 | 								appointment.appointmentDate > new Date()
1324 | 									? appointment.createdAt
1325 | 									: appointment.appointmentDate,
1326 | 							to: new Date()
1327 | 						})
1328 | 					: null,
1329 | 			discount: faker.datatype.boolean(0.2) ? faker.number.int({ min: 5, max: 50 }) : null,
1330 | 			totalAmount,
1331 | 			amountPaid,
1332 | 			amount: totalAmount,
1333 | 			status: status as string,
1334 | 			insurance: faker.datatype.boolean(0.3) ? faker.company.name() : null,
1335 | 			insuranceId: faker.datatype.boolean(0.2) ? faker.string.alphanumeric(10) : null,
1336 | 			serviceDate: appointment.appointmentDate,
1337 | 			dueDate: faker.date.future({ years: 0.5 }),
1338 | 			paidDate:
1339 | 				status === PaymentStatus.PAID
1340 | 					? faker.date.between({
1341 | 							from:
1342 | 								appointment.appointmentDate > new Date()
1343 | 									? appointment.createdAt
1344 | 									: appointment.appointmentDate,
1345 | 							to: new Date()
1346 | 						})
1347 | 					: null,
1348 | 			notes: faker.datatype.boolean(0.3) ? faker.lorem.sentence() : null,
1349 | 			paymentMethod: getRandomEnumValue(PaymentMethod),
1350 | 			receiptNumber: faker.number.int({ min: 1000, max: 9999 }),
1351 | 			isDeleted: false,
1352 | 			deletedAt: null,
1353 | 			billId: null,
1354 | 			createdAt: new Date(),
1355 | 			updatedAt: new Date()
1356 | 		};
1357 |
1358 | 		await db.insert(payment).values(paymentData).execute();
1359 | 		payments.push(paymentData);
1360 |
1361 | 		// Create patient bills
1362 | 		if (faker.datatype.boolean(0.6)) {
1363 | 			await createPatientBill(db, paymentData, services);
1364 | 		}
1365 | 	}
1366 |
1367 | 	return payments;
1368 | };

1678 | 		const schedule = {
1679 | 			id: faker.string.uuid(),
1680 | 			vaccineName: vaccine.name,
1681 | 			recommendedAge: vaccine.recommendedAge,
1682 | 			dosesRequired: vaccine.dosesRequired,
1683 | 			minimumInterval: faker.number.int({ min: 30, max: 180 }),
1684 | 			isMandatory: faker.datatype.boolean(0.8),
1685 | 			ageInDaysMin: vaccine.ageInDaysMin,
1686 | 			ageInDaysMax: vaccine.ageInDaysMax,
1687 | 			description: faker.lorem.sentence(),
1688 | 			createdAt: new Date(),
1689 | 			updatedAt: new Date()
1690 | 		};
1691 |
1692 | 		try {
1693 | 			await db.insert(vaccineSchedule).values(schedule).execute();
1694 | 			schedules.push(schedule);
1695 | 		} catch (error) {
1696 | 			// Ignore duplicate key errors for upsert-like behavior
1697 | 			if (!(error instanceof Error && error.message.includes("UNIQUE constraint failed"))) {
1698 | 				throw error;
1699 | 			}
1700 | 		}
1701 | 	}
1702 |
1703 | 	return schedules;
1704 | };
1705 |
1706 | // Create guardians
1707 | const createGuardians = async (db: AppDb, patients: DbPatient[], users: DatabaseUser[]) => {

1711 | 	for (const patient of patients) {
1712 | 		if (faker.datatype.boolean(0.7)) {
1713 | 			const guardianUser = faker.helpers.arrayElement(
1714 | 				users.filter(u => u.role !== "DOCTOR" && u.role !== "STAFF")
1715 | 			);
1716 |

1720 | 				userId: guardianUser.id,

1725 | 			};

1728 | 			guardians.push(newGuardian);
1729 | 		}
1730 | 	}

1733 | };
```

`db/seed/wfa.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // scripts/seed-who-growth-data.ts
   2 |
   3 | import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
   4 | import fs from "node:fs";
   5 | import path from "node:path";
   6 |
   7 | import { db } from "../";
   8 | import { whoGrowthStandard } from "../schema";
   9 | import { Gender, MeasurementType } from "../seed/seed";
  10 |
  11 | type WHODayData = {
  12 | 	Day: string;
  13 | 	L: string;
  14 | 	M: string;
  15 | 	S: string;
  16 | 	SD4neg: string;
  17 | 	SD3neg: string;
  18 | 	SD2neg: string;
  19 | 	SD1neg: string;
  20 | 	SD0: string;
  21 | 	SD1: string;
  22 | 	SD2: string;
  23 | 	SD3: string;
  24 | 	SD4: string;
  25 | };
  26 |
  27 | type WHOData = {
  28 | 	wfa: {
  29 | 		boys: WHODayData[];
  30 | 		girls: WHODayData[];
  31 | 	};
  32 | };
  33 |
  34 | export default async function seedWHOGrowthData() {
  35 | 	console.log("🌱 Seeding WHO growth data...");
  36 |
  37 | 	// 1️⃣ Read JSON
  38 | 	const dataPath = path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "wfa.json");
  39 | 	const rawData = fs.readFileSync(dataPath, "utf-8");
  40 | 	const whoData = JSON.parse(rawData) as WHOData;
  41 |
  42 | 	// 2️⃣ Clear existing data (fastest & safest)
  43 | 	console.log("🗑️ Clearing existing WHO growth data...");
  44 | 	await db.delete(whoGrowthStandard).execute();
  45 |
  46 | 	const records: (typeof whoGrowthStandard.$inferInsert)[] = [];
  47 |
  48 | 	// 3️⃣ Boys
  49 | 	for (const dayData of whoData.wfa.boys) {
  50 | 		const ageDays = Number.parseInt(dayData.Day, 10);
  51 |
  52 | 		records.push({
  53 | 			id: randomUUID(),
  54 | 			ageDays,
  55 | 			ageInMonths: Math.floor(ageDays / 30.4375),
  56 | 			gender: Gender.MALE,
  57 | 			measurementType: MeasurementType.WFA,
  58 | 			lValue: Number(dayData.L),
  59 | 			mValue: Number(dayData.M),
  60 | 			sValue: Number(dayData.S),
  61 | 			sd0: Number(dayData.SD0),
  62 | 			sd1neg: Number(dayData.SD1neg),
  63 | 			sd1pos: Number(dayData.SD1),
  64 | 			sd2neg: Number(dayData.SD2neg),
  65 | 			sd2pos: Number(dayData.SD2),
  66 | 			sd3neg: Number(dayData.SD3neg),
  67 | 			sd3pos: Number(dayData.SD3),
  68 | 			sd4neg: Number(dayData.SD4neg),
  69 | 			sd4pos: Number(dayData.SD4)
  70 | 		});
  71 | 	}
  72 |
  73 | 	// 4️⃣ Girls
  74 | 	for (const dayData of whoData.wfa.girls) {
  75 | 		const ageDays = Number.parseInt(dayData.Day, 10);
  76 |
  77 | 		records.push({
  78 | 			id: randomUUID(),
  79 | 			ageDays,
  80 | 			ageInMonths: Math.floor(ageDays / 30.4375),
  81 | 			gender: Gender.FEMALE,
  82 | 			measurementType: MeasurementType.WFA,
  83 | 			lValue: Number(dayData.L),
  84 | 			mValue: Number(dayData.M),
  85 | 			sValue: Number(dayData.S),
  86 | 			sd0: Number(dayData.SD0),
  87 | 			sd1neg: Number(dayData.SD1neg),
  88 | 			sd1pos: Number(dayData.SD1),
  89 | 			sd2neg: Number(dayData.SD2neg),
  90 | 			sd2pos: Number(dayData.SD2),
  91 | 			sd3neg: Number(dayData.SD3neg),
  92 | 			sd3pos: Number(dayData.SD3),
  93 | 			sd4neg: Number(dayData.SD4neg),
  94 | 			sd4pos: Number(dayData.SD4)
  95 | 		});
  96 | 	}
  97 |
  98 | 	// 5️⃣ Batched insert (SQLite-safe)
  99 | 	const BATCH_SIZE = 1000;
 100 | 	let inserted = 0;
 101 |
 102 | 	for (let i = 0; i < records.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
 103 | 		const batch = records.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);
 104 |
 105 | 		await db.insert(whoGrowthStandard).values(batch).execute();
 106 |
 107 | 		inserted += batch.length;
 108 | 		console.log(`  ✅ Inserted ${batch.length} records`);
 109 | 	}
 110 |
 111 | 	console.log(`🎉 Seeded ${inserted} WHO WFA records`);
 112 | }
```

```ts
   1 | import { createEnv } from "@t3-oss/env-core";
   2 | import { z } from "zod";
   3 |
   4 | export const env = createEnv({
   5 | 	server: {
   6 | 		SERVER_URL: z.url().optional(),
   7 | 		DATABASE_URL: z.string().optional(),
   8 | 		BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: z.string().min(32).max(128),
   9 | 		BETTER_AUTH_URL: z.url().optional(),
  10 | 		BETTER_AUTH_BASE_URL: z.url().optional()
  11 | 	},
  12 |
  13 | 	/**
  14 | 	 * The prefix that client-side variables must have. This is enforced both at
  15 | 	 * a type-level and at runtime.
  16 | 	 */
  17 | 	clientPrefix: "VITE_",
  18 |
  19 | 	client: {
  20 | 		VITE_APP_TITLE: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  21 | 		VITE_BASE_URL: z.url().optional()
  22 | 	},
  23 |
  24 | 	/**
  25 | 	 * What object holds the environment variables at runtime. This is usually
  26 | 	 * `process.env` or `import.meta.env`.
  27 | 	 */
  28 | 	runtimeEnv: import.meta.env,
  29 |
  30 | 	/**
  31 | 	 * By default, this library will feed the environment variables directly to
  32 | 	 * the Zod validator.
  33 | 	 *
  34 | 	 * This means that if you have an empty string for a value that is supposed
  35 | 	 * to be a number (e.g. `PORT=` in a ".env" file), Zod will incorrectly flag
  36 | 	 * it as a type mismatch violation. Additionally, if you have an empty string
  37 | 	 * for a value that is supposed to be a string with a default value (e.g.
  38 | 	 * `DOMAIN=` in an ".env" file), the default value will never be applied.
  39 | 	 *
  40 | 	 * In order to solve these issues, we recommend that all new projects
  41 | 	 * explicitly specify this option as true.
  42 | 	 */
  43 | 	emptyStringAsUndefined: true
  44 | });
```
