`hooks/use-breadcrumbs.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { useLocation } from '@tanstack/react-router';
   2 | import { useMemo } from 'react';
   3 |
   4 | type BreadcrumbItem = {
   5 |   title: string;
   6 |   link: string;
   7 | };
   8 |
   9 | // This allows to add custom title as well
  10 | const routeMapping: Record<string, BreadcrumbItem[]> = {
  11 |   '/dashboard': [{ title: 'Dashboard', link: '/dashboard' }],
  12 |   '/dashboard/employee': [
  13 |     { title: 'Dashboard', link: '/dashboard' },
  14 |     { title: 'Employee', link: '/dashboard/employee' }
  15 |   ],
  16 |   '/dashboard/product': [
  17 |     { title: 'Dashboard', link: '/dashboard' },
  18 |     { title: 'Product', link: '/dashboard/product' }
  19 |   ]
  20 |   // Add more custom mappings as needed
  21 | };
  22 |
  23 | export function useBreadcrumbs() {
  24 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
  25 |
  26 |   const breadcrumbs = useMemo(() => {
  27 |     // Check if we have a custom mapping for this exact path
  28 |     if (routeMapping[pathname]) {
  29 |       return routeMapping[pathname];
  30 |     }
  31 |
  32 |     // If no exact match, fall back to generating breadcrumbs from the path
  33 |     const segments = pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
  34 |     return segments.map((segment, index) => {
  35 |       const path = `/${segments.slice(0, index + 1).join('/')}`;
  36 |       return {
  37 |         title: segment.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + segment.slice(1),
  38 |         link: path
  39 |       };
  40 |     });
  41 |   }, [pathname]);
  42 |
  43 |   return breadcrumbs;
  44 | }

```

`hooks/use-callback-ref.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import * as React from 'react';
   2 |
   3 | /**
   4 |  * @see https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/blob/main/packages/react/use-callback-ref/src/useCallbackRef.tsx
   5 |  */
   6 |
   7 | /**
   8 |  * A custom hook that converts a callback to a ref to avoid triggering re-renders when passed as a
   9 |  * prop or avoid re-executing effects when passed as a dependency
  10 |  */
  11 | function useCallbackRef<T extends (...args: never[]) => unknown>(callback: T | undefined): T {
  12 |   const callbackRef = React.useRef(callback);
  13 |
  14 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  15 |     callbackRef.current = callback;
  16 |   });
  17 |
  18 |   // https://github.com/facebook/react/issues/19240
  19 |   return React.useMemo(() => ((...args) => callbackRef.current?.(...args)) as T, []);
  20 | }
  21 |
  22 | export { useCallbackRef };

```

`hooks/use-controllable-state.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import * as React from 'react';
   2 |
   3 | import { useCallbackRef } from '@/hooks/use-callback-ref';
   4 |
   5 | /**
   6 |  * @see https://github.com/radix-ui/primitives/blob/main/packages/react/use-controllable-state/src/useControllableState.tsx
   7 |  */
   8 |
   9 | type UseControllableStateParams<T> = {
  10 |   prop?: T | undefined;
  11 |   defaultProp?: T | undefined;
  12 |   onChange?: (state: T) => void;
  13 | };
  14 |
  15 | type SetStateFn<T> = (prevState?: T) => T;
  16 |
  17 | function useControllableState<T>({
  18 |   prop,
  19 |   defaultProp,
  20 |   onChange = () => {}
  21 | }: UseControllableStateParams<T>) {
  22 |   const [uncontrolledProp, setUncontrolledProp] = useUncontrolledState({
  23 |     defaultProp,
  24 |     onChange
  25 |   });
  26 |   const isControlled = prop !== undefined;
  27 |   const value = isControlled ? prop : uncontrolledProp;
  28 |   const handleChange = useCallbackRef(onChange);
  29 |
  30 |   const setValue: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<T | undefined>> = React.useCallback(
  31 |     (nextValue) => {
  32 |       if (isControlled) {
  33 |         const setter = nextValue as SetStateFn<T>;
  34 |         const value = typeof nextValue === 'function' ? setter(prop) : nextValue;
  35 |         if (value !== prop) handleChange(value as T);
  36 |       } else {
  37 |         setUncontrolledProp(nextValue);
  38 |       }
  39 |     },
  40 |     [isControlled, prop, setUncontrolledProp, handleChange]
  41 |   );
  42 |
  43 |   return [value, setValue] as const;
  44 | }
  45 |
  46 | function useUncontrolledState<T>({
  47 |   defaultProp,
  48 |   onChange
  49 | }: Omit<UseControllableStateParams<T>, 'prop'>) {
  50 |   const uncontrolledState = React.useState<T | undefined>(defaultProp);
  51 |   const [value] = uncontrolledState;
  52 |   const prevValueRef = React.useRef(value);
  53 |   const handleChange = useCallbackRef(onChange);
  54 |
  55 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  56 |     if (prevValueRef.current !== value) {
  57 |       handleChange(value as T);
  58 |       prevValueRef.current = value;
  59 |     }
  60 |   }, [value, prevValueRef, handleChange]);
  61 |
  62 |   return uncontrolledState;
  63 | }
  64 |
  65 | export { useControllableState };

```

`hooks/use-data-table.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import {
   2 |   type ColumnFiltersState,
   3 |   type ColumnPinningState,
   4 |   type PaginationState,
   5 |   type RowSelectionState,
   6 |   type SortingState,
   7 |   type TableOptions,
   8 |   type TableState,
   9 |   type Updater,
  10 |   type VisibilityState,
  11 |   getCoreRowModel,
  12 |   getFacetedMinMaxValues,
  13 |   getFacetedRowModel,
  14 |   getFacetedUniqueValues,
  15 |   getFilteredRowModel,
  16 |   getPaginationRowModel,
  17 |   getSortedRowModel,
  18 |   useReactTable
  19 | } from '@tanstack/react-table';
  20 | import { useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router';
  21 | import * as React from 'react';
  22 |
  23 | import { useDebouncedCallback } from '@/hooks/use-debounced-callback';
  24 | import { parseSortingState, serializeSortingState } from '@/lib/parsers';
  25 | import type { ExtendedColumnSort } from '@/types/data-table';
  26 |
  27 | const ARRAY_SEPARATOR = ',';
  28 | const DEBOUNCE_MS = 300;
  29 |
  30 | interface UseDataTableProps<TData>
  31 |   extends
  32 |     Omit<
  33 |       TableOptions<TData>,
  34 |       | 'state'
  35 |       | 'pageCount'
  36 |       | 'getCoreRowModel'
  37 |       | 'manualFiltering'
  38 |       | 'manualPagination'
  39 |       | 'manualSorting'
  40 |     >,
  41 |     Required<Pick<TableOptions<TData>, 'pageCount'>> {
  42 |   initialState?: Omit<Partial<TableState>, 'sorting'> & {
  43 |     sorting?: ExtendedColumnSort<TData>[];
  44 |   };
  45 |   history?: 'push' | 'replace';
  46 |   debounceMs?: number;
  47 |   throttleMs?: number;
  48 |   clearOnDefault?: boolean;
  49 |   enableAdvancedFilter?: boolean;
  50 |   scroll?: boolean;
  51 |   shallow?: boolean;
  52 |   startTransition?: React.TransitionStartFunction;
  53 | }
  54 |
  55 | export function useDataTable<TData>(props: UseDataTableProps<TData>) {
  56 |   const {
  57 |     columns,
  58 |     pageCount = -1,
  59 |     initialState,
  60 |     history = 'replace',
  61 |     debounceMs = DEBOUNCE_MS,
  62 |     enableAdvancedFilter = false,
  63 |     shallow = true,
  64 |     ...tableProps
  65 |   } = props;
  66 |
  67 |   const search = useSearch({ strict: false }) as Record<string, unknown>;
  68 |   const navigate = useNavigate();
  69 |
  70 |   const [rowSelection, setRowSelection] = React.useState<RowSelectionState>(
  71 |     initialState?.rowSelection ?? {}
  72 |   );
  73 |   const [columnVisibility, setColumnVisibility] = React.useState<VisibilityState>(
  74 |     initialState?.columnVisibility ?? {}
  75 |   );
  76 |   const [columnPinning, setColumnPinning] = React.useState<ColumnPinningState>(
  77 |     initialState?.columnPinning ?? {}
  78 |   );
  79 |
  80 |   // Read pagination from search params
  81 |   const page = (search.page as number) ?? 1;
  82 |   const perPage = (search.perPage as number) ?? initialState?.pagination?.pageSize ?? 10;
  83 |
  84 |   const pagination: PaginationState = React.useMemo(
  85 |     () => ({
  86 |       pageIndex: page - 1,
  87 |       pageSize: perPage
  88 |     }),
  89 |     [page, perPage]
  90 |   );
  91 |
  92 |   const onPaginationChange = React.useCallback(
  93 |     (updaterOrValue: Updater<PaginationState>) => {
  94 |       const newPagination =
  95 |         typeof updaterOrValue === 'function' ? updaterOrValue(pagination) : updaterOrValue;
  96 |       void navigate({
  97 |         search: (prev: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
  98 |           ...prev,
  99 |           page: newPagination.pageIndex + 1,
 100 |           perPage: newPagination.pageSize
 101 |         }),
 102 |         replace: history === 'replace'
 103 |       });
 104 |     },
 105 |     [pagination, navigate, history]
 106 |   );
 107 |
 108 |   // Read sorting from search params
 109 |   const columnIds = React.useMemo(() => {
 110 |     return new Set(columns.map((column) => column.id).filter(Boolean) as string[]);
 111 |   }, [columns]);
 112 |
 113 |   const sorting = React.useMemo(
 114 |     () =>
 115 |       parseSortingState<TData>(search.sort as string | undefined, columnIds) ??
 116 |       initialState?.sorting ??
 117 |       [],
 118 |     [search.sort, columnIds, initialState?.sorting]
 119 |   );
 120 |
 121 |   const onSortingChange = React.useCallback(
 122 |     (updaterOrValue: Updater<SortingState>) => {
 123 |       const newSorting =
 124 |         typeof updaterOrValue === 'function' ? updaterOrValue(sorting) : updaterOrValue;
 125 |       void navigate({
 126 |         search: (prev: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
 127 |           ...prev,
 128 |           sort:
 129 |             newSorting.length > 0
 130 |               ? serializeSortingState(newSorting as ExtendedColumnSort<TData>[])
 131 |               : undefined
 132 |         }),
 133 |         replace: history === 'replace'
 134 |       });
 135 |     },
 136 |     [sorting, navigate, history]
 137 |   );
 138 |
 139 |   // Filter handling
 140 |   const filterableColumns = React.useMemo(() => {
 141 |     if (enableAdvancedFilter) return [];
 142 |     return columns.filter((column) => column.enableColumnFilter);
 143 |   }, [columns, enableAdvancedFilter]);
 144 |
 145 |   // Read filter values from search params
 146 |   const filterValues = React.useMemo(() => {
 147 |     if (enableAdvancedFilter) return {};
 148 |     const values: Record<string, string | string[] | null> = {};
 149 |     for (const column of filterableColumns) {
 150 |       const key = column.id ?? '';
 151 |       const val = search[key];
 152 |       if (val !== undefined && val !== null) {
 153 |         if (column.meta?.options) {
 154 |           // Array filter - stored as comma-separated string
 155 |           values[key] = typeof val === 'string' ? val.split(ARRAY_SEPARATOR) : null;
 156 |         } else {
 157 |           values[key] = typeof val === 'string' ? val : null;
 158 |         }
 159 |       } else {
 160 |         values[key] = null;
 161 |       }
 162 |     }
 163 |     return values;
 164 |   }, [search, filterableColumns, enableAdvancedFilter]);
 165 |
 166 |   const debouncedSetFilterValues = useDebouncedCallback(
 167 |     (values: Record<string, string | string[] | null>) => {
 168 |       void navigate({
 169 |         search: (prev: Record<string, unknown>) => {
 170 |           const next = { ...prev, page: 1 };
 171 |           for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(values)) {
 172 |             if (value === null || value === undefined) {
 173 |               delete next[key];
 174 |             } else if (Array.isArray(value)) {
 175 |               next[key] = value.join(ARRAY_SEPARATOR);
 176 |             } else {
 177 |               next[key] = value;
 178 |             }
 179 |           }
 180 |           return next;
 181 |         },
 182 |         replace: history === 'replace'
 183 |       });
 184 |     },
 185 |     debounceMs
 186 |   );
 187 |
 188 |   const initialColumnFilters: ColumnFiltersState = React.useMemo(() => {
 189 |     if (enableAdvancedFilter) return [];
 190 |
 191 |     return Object.entries(filterValues).reduce<ColumnFiltersState>((filters, [key, value]) => {
 192 |       if (value !== null) {
 193 |         const processedValue = Array.isArray(value)
 194 |           ? value
 195 |           : typeof value === 'string' && /[^a-zA-Z0-9]/.test(value)
 196 |             ? value.split(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]+/).filter(Boolean)
 197 |             : [value];
 198 |
 199 |         filters.push({
 200 |           id: key,
 201 |           value: processedValue
 202 |         });
 203 |       }
 204 |       return filters;
 205 |     }, []);
 206 |   }, [filterValues, enableAdvancedFilter]);
 207 |
 208 |   const [columnFilters, setColumnFilters] =
 209 |     React.useState<ColumnFiltersState>(initialColumnFilters);
 210 |
 211 |   const onColumnFiltersChange = React.useCallback(
 212 |     (updaterOrValue: Updater<ColumnFiltersState>) => {
 213 |       if (enableAdvancedFilter) return;
 214 |
 215 |       setColumnFilters((prev) => {
 216 |         const next = typeof updaterOrValue === 'function' ? updaterOrValue(prev) : updaterOrValue;
 217 |
 218 |         const filterUpdates: Record<string, string | string[] | null> = {};
 219 |         for (const filter of next) {
 220 |           if (filterableColumns.find((column) => column.id === filter.id)) {
 221 |             filterUpdates[filter.id] = filter.value as string | string[];
 222 |           }
 223 |         }
 224 |
 225 |         for (const prevFilter of prev) {
 226 |           if (!next.some((filter) => filter.id === prevFilter.id)) {
 227 |             filterUpdates[prevFilter.id] = null;
 228 |           }
 229 |         }
 230 |
 231 |         debouncedSetFilterValues(filterUpdates);
 232 |         return next;
 233 |       });
 234 |     },
 235 |     [debouncedSetFilterValues, filterableColumns, enableAdvancedFilter]
 236 |   );
 237 |
 238 |   const table = useReactTable({
 239 |     ...tableProps,
 240 |     columns,
 241 |     initialState,
 242 |     pageCount,
 243 |     state: {
 244 |       pagination,
 245 |       sorting,
 246 |       columnVisibility,
 247 |       columnPinning,
 248 |       rowSelection,
 249 |       columnFilters
 250 |     },
 251 |     defaultColumn: {
 252 |       ...tableProps.defaultColumn,
 253 |       enableColumnFilter: false
 254 |     },
 255 |     enableRowSelection: true,
 256 |     onRowSelectionChange: setRowSelection,
 257 |     onPaginationChange,
 258 |     onSortingChange,
 259 |     onColumnFiltersChange,
 260 |     onColumnVisibilityChange: setColumnVisibility,
 261 |     onColumnPinningChange: setColumnPinning,
 262 |     getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
 263 |     getFilteredRowModel: getFilteredRowModel(),
 264 |     getPaginationRowModel: getPaginationRowModel(),
 265 |     getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel(),
 266 |     getFacetedRowModel: getFacetedRowModel(),
 267 |     getFacetedUniqueValues: getFacetedUniqueValues(),
 268 |     getFacetedMinMaxValues: getFacetedMinMaxValues(),
 269 |     manualPagination: true,
 270 |     manualSorting: true,
 271 |     manualFiltering: true
 272 |   });
 273 |
 274 |   return { table, shallow, debounceMs, throttleMs: props.throttleMs };
 275 | }

```

`hooks/use-debounce.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
   2 |
   3 | export function useDebounce<T>(value: T, delay: number): T {
   4 |   const [debouncedValue, setDebouncedValue] = useState<T>(value);
   5 |
   6 |   useEffect(() => {
   7 |     const handler = setTimeout(() => {
   8 |       setDebouncedValue(value);
   9 |     }, delay);
  10 |
  11 |     return () => {
  12 |       clearTimeout(handler);
  13 |     };
  14 |   }, [value, delay]);
  15 |
  16 |   return debouncedValue;
  17 | }

```

`hooks/use-debounced-callback.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import * as React from 'react';
   2 |
   3 | import { useCallbackRef } from '@/hooks/use-callback-ref';
   4 |
   5 | export function useDebouncedCallback<T extends (...args: never[]) => unknown>(
   6 |   callback: T,
   7 |   delay: number
   8 | ) {
   9 |   const handleCallback = useCallbackRef(callback);
  10 |   const debounceTimerRef = React.useRef(0);
  11 |   React.useEffect(() => () => window.clearTimeout(debounceTimerRef.current), []);
  12 |
  13 |   const setValue = React.useCallback(
  14 |     (...args: Parameters<T>) => {
  15 |       window.clearTimeout(debounceTimerRef.current);
  16 |       debounceTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => handleCallback(...args), delay);
  17 |     },
  18 |     [handleCallback, delay]
  19 |   );
  20 |
  21 |   return setValue;
  22 | }

```

`hooks/use-media-query.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
   2 |
   3 | export function useMediaQuery() {
   4 |   const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
   5 |
   6 |   useEffect(() => {
   7 |     const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)');
   8 |     setIsOpen(mediaQuery.matches);
   9 |
  10 |     const handler = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => {
  11 |       setIsOpen(e.matches);
  12 |     };
  13 |
  14 |     mediaQuery.addEventListener('change', handler);
  15 |     return () => mediaQuery.removeEventListener('change', handler);
  16 |   }, []);
  17 |
  18 |   return { isOpen };
  19 | }
```

```ts
   4 |
   5 | export function useIsMobile() {
   6 |   const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined)
   7 |
   8 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   9 |     const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
  10 |     const onChange = () => {
  11 |       setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
  12 |     }
  13 |     mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
  14 |     setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
  15 |     return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange)
  16 |   }, [])
  17 |
  18 |   return !!isMobile
  19 | }
```

`hooks/use-nav.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { useMemo } from 'react';
   2 | import type { NavItem, NavGroup } from '@/types';
   3 |
   4 | /**
   5 |  * Hook to filter navigation items
   6 |  * RBAC has been removed — returns all items as-is
   7 |  */
   8 | export function useFilteredNavItems(items: NavItem[]) {
   9 |   return useMemo(() => {
  10 |     return items.map((item) => {
  11 |       if (item.items && item.items.length > 0) {
  12 |         return { ...item, items: [...item.items] };
  13 |       }
  14 |       return item;
  15 |     });
  16 |   }, [items]);
  17 | }
  18 |
  19 | /**
  20 |  * Hook to filter navigation groups
  21 |  */
  22 | export function useFilteredNavGroups(groups: NavGroup[]) {
  23 |   const allItems = useMemo(() => groups.flatMap((g) => g.items), [groups]);
  24 |   const filteredItems = useFilteredNavItems(allItems);
  25 |
  26 |   return useMemo(() => {
  27 |     const filteredSet = new Set(filteredItems.map((item) => item.title));
  28 |     return groups
  29 |       .map((group) => ({
  30 |         ...group,
  31 |         items: filteredItems.filter((item) =>
  32 |           group.items.some((gi) => gi.title === item.title && filteredSet.has(gi.title))
  33 |         )
  34 |       }))
  35 |       .filter((group) => group.items.length > 0);
  36 |   }, [groups, filteredItems]);
  37 | }
```

`hooks/use-pagination.ts`:

```ts
   1 | type UsePaginationProps = {
   2 |   currentPage: number
   3 |   totalPages: number
   4 |   paginationItemsToDisplay: number
   5 | }
   6 |
   7 | type UsePaginationReturn = {
   8 |   pages: number[]
   9 |   showLeftEllipsis: boolean
  10 |   showRightEllipsis: boolean
  11 | }
  12 |
  13 | export function usePagination({
  14 |   currentPage,
  15 |   totalPages,
  16 |   paginationItemsToDisplay
  17 | }: UsePaginationProps): UsePaginationReturn {
  18 |   function calculatePaginationRange(): number[] {
  19 |     if (totalPages <= paginationItemsToDisplay) {
  20 |       return Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => i + 1)
  21 |     }
  22 |
  23 |     const halfDisplay = Math.floor(paginationItemsToDisplay / 2)
  24 |
  25 |     const initialRange = {
  26 |       start: currentPage - halfDisplay,
  27 |       end: currentPage + halfDisplay
  28 |     }
  29 |
  30 |     const adjustedRange = {
  31 |       start: Math.max(1, initialRange.start),
  32 |       end: Math.min(totalPages, initialRange.end)
  33 |     }
  34 |
  35 |     if (adjustedRange.start === 1) {
  36 |       adjustedRange.end = Math.min(paginationItemsToDisplay, totalPages)
  37 |     }
  38 |
  39 |     if (adjustedRange.end === totalPages) {
  40 |       adjustedRange.start = Math.max(1, totalPages - paginationItemsToDisplay + 1)
  41 |     }
  42 |
  43 |     return Array.from({ length: adjustedRange.end - adjustedRange.start + 1 }, (_, i) => adjustedRange.start + i)
  44 |   }
  45 |
  46 |   const pages = calculatePaginationRange()
  47 |
  48 |   // Determine ellipsis display based on the actual pages shown
  49 |   const showLeftEllipsis = pages.length > 0 && pages[0] > 1 && pages[0] > 2
  50 |
  51 |   const showRightEllipsis =
  52 |     pages.length > 0 && pages[pages.length - 1] < totalPages && pages[pages.length - 1] < totalPages - 1
  53 |
  54 |   return {
  55 |     pages,
  56 |     showLeftEllipsis,
  57 |     showRightEllipsis
  58 |   }
  59 | }

```

`hooks/use-stepper.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import type { AnyFormApi } from '@tanstack/react-form';
   2 | import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
   3 | import type { ZodTypeAny } from 'zod';
   4 |
   5 | /**
   6 |  * Options for handling cancel/back actions
   7 |  */
   8 | type HandleCancelOrBackOpts = {
   9 |   onBack?: VoidFunction;
  10 |   onCancel?: VoidFunction;
  11 | };
  12 |
  13 | /**
  14 |  * State of the current step
  15 |  */
  16 | type StepState = {
  17 |   value: number;
  18 |   count: number;
  19 |   goToNextStep: () => void;
  20 |   goToPrevStep: () => void;
  21 |   isCompleted: boolean;
  22 | };
  23 |
  24 | /**
  25 |  * Hook for managing multi-step form navigation and validation
  26 |  *
  27 |  * @param schemas - Array of Zod schemas for each step
  28 |  * @returns Object with stepper state and methods
  29 |  */
  30 | export function useFormStepper(schemas: ZodTypeAny[]) {
  31 |   const stepCount = schemas.length;
  32 |   const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(1); // Start from 1
  33 |
  34 |   const goToNextStep = useCallback(() => {
  35 |     setCurrentStep((prev) => Math.min(prev + 1, stepCount));
  36 |   }, [stepCount]);
  37 |
  38 |   const goToPrevStep = useCallback(() => {
  39 |     setCurrentStep((prev) => Math.max(prev - 1, 1));
  40 |   }, []);
  41 |
  42 |   const step: StepState = {
  43 |     value: currentStep,
  44 |     count: stepCount,
  45 |     goToNextStep,
  46 |     goToPrevStep,
  47 |     isCompleted: currentStep === stepCount
  48 |   };
  49 |
  50 |   const currentValidator = schemas[currentStep - 1]; // Convert to 0-based for array access
  51 |   const isFirstStep = currentStep === 1;
  52 |
  53 |   const triggerFormGroup = async (form: AnyFormApi) => {
  54 |     const result = currentValidator.safeParse(form.state.values);
  55 |     if (!result.success) {
  56 |       await form.handleSubmit({ step: String(currentStep) });
  57 |       return result;
  58 |     }
  59 |
  60 |     return result;
  61 |   };
  62 |
  63 |   const handleNextStepOrSubmit = async (form: AnyFormApi) => {
  64 |     const result = await triggerFormGroup(form);
  65 |     if (!result.success) {
  66 |       return;
  67 |     }
  68 |
  69 |     if (currentStep < stepCount) {
  70 |       goToNextStep();
  71 |       return;
  72 |     }
  73 |
  74 |     if (currentStep === stepCount) {
  75 |       form.handleSubmit();
  76 |     }
  77 |   };
  78 |
  79 |   const handleCancelOrBack = (opts?: HandleCancelOrBackOpts) => {
  80 |     if (isFirstStep || step.isCompleted) {
  81 |       opts?.onCancel?.();
  82 |       return;
  83 |     }
  84 |
  85 |     if (currentStep > 1) {
  86 |       opts?.onBack?.();
  87 |       goToPrevStep();
  88 |     }
  89 |   };
  90 |
  91 |   return {
  92 |     step, // Current step state
  93 |     currentStep, // Current step number (1-based)
  94 |     isFirstStep, // Whether current step is the first step
  95 |     currentValidator, // Zod schema for current step
  96 |     triggerFormGroup, // Validate current step fields
  97 |     handleNextStepOrSubmit, // Handle next/submit action
  98 |     handleCancelOrBack // Handle back/cancel action
  99 |   };
 100 | }

```

`lib/auth-functions.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
   2 | import { getRequest, setResponseHeader } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";
   3 |
   4 | import { auth } from "./auth";
   5 |
   6 | type GetUserServerQuery = {
   7 | 	disableCookieCache?: boolean | undefined;
   8 | 	disableRefresh?: boolean | undefined;
   9 | };
  10 |
  11 | /**
  12 |  * Shared logic for session retrieval.
  13 |  * Kept as a standard function to avoid TanStack Start compiler collisions.
  14 |  */
  15 | async function fetchSessionInternal(query?: GetUserServerQuery) {
  16 | 	const request = getRequest();
  17 |
  18 | 	const session = await auth.api.getSession({
  19 | 		headers: request.headers,
  20 | 		query,
  21 | 		returnHeaders: true
  22 | 	});
  23 |
  24 | 	// Forward Set-Cookie headers (essential for Better Auth session management)
  25 | 	const cookies = session.headers?.getSetCookie();
  26 | 	if (cookies?.length) {
  27 | 		setResponseHeader("Set-Cookie", cookies);
  28 | 	}
  29 |
  30 | 	return session;
  31 | }
  32 |
  33 | export const $getUser = createServerFn({ method: "GET" }).handler(async () => {
  34 | 	const session = await fetchSessionInternal();
  35 | 	return session.response?.user ?? null;
  36 | });
  37 |
  38 | export const getSession = createServerFn({ method: "GET" }).handler(async () => {
  39 | 	const session = await fetchSessionInternal();
  40 | 	return session.response;
  41 | });
  42 |
  43 | export const ensureSession = createServerFn({ method: "GET" }).handler(async () => {
  44 | 	const session = await fetchSessionInternal();
  45 |
  46 | 	if (!session.response) {
  47 | 		throw new Error("Unauthorized");
  48 | 	}
  49 |
  50 | 	return session.response;
  51 | });
```

```ts
  12 | import { db } from "#/db";

  17 | import { getSettings } from "./settings";

  19 | const options = {
  20 | 	appName: "Smart Clinic",
  21 | 	secret: env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET,
  22 | 	baseURL: env.BETTER_AUTH_URL,
  23 | 	trustedOrigins: env.BETTER_AUTH_BASE_URL ? [env.BETTER_AUTH_BASE_URL] : undefined,

  56 | 							});

  70 | 							});

  76 | 					};
  77 | 				},
  78 | 				after: async (user, ctx) => {
  79 | 					try {
  80 | 						let clinicId: string;
  81 |
  82 | 						// Check if user already has a clinic relationship
  83 | 						const hasClinic = await db.query.clinicMembers.findFirst({
  84 | 							where: eq(schema.clinicMembers.userId, user.id),
  85 | 							columns: { clinicId: true }
  86 | 						});

  88 | 						if (!hasClinic) {

  97 | 										id: "Smart1",
  98 | 										name: clinicData.name,
  99 | 										address: clinicData.address
 100 | 										// other clinic fields
 101 | 									})
 102 | 									.returning({ id: schema.clinics.id });
 103 |
 104 | 								clinicId = newClinic[0].id;
 105 | 							} else {
 106 | 								// Assign to default clinic
 107 | 								const defaultClinic = await db.query.clinics.findFirst({
 108 | 									columns: { id: true }
 109 | 								});
 110 |
 111 | 								if (!defaultClinic) {
 112 | 									// Create a default clinic if none exists
 113 | 									const newDefaultClinic = await db
 114 | 										.insert(schema.clinics)
 115 | 										.values({
 116 | 											id: "Smart1",
 117 | 											name: "Default Clinic",
 118 | 											address: "Default Address"
 119 | 										})
 120 | 										.returning({ id: schema.clinics.id });

 122 | 									clinicId = newDefaultClinic[0].id;
 123 | 								} else {
 124 | 									clinicId = defaultClinic.id;
 125 | 								}
 126 | 							}

 128 | 							// Connect user to clinic
 129 | 							await db.insert(schema.clinicMembers).values({
 130 | 								userId: user.id,
 131 | 								role: user.role === "admin" ? "doctor" : "staff",
 132 | 								clinicId: clinicId
 133 | 							});

 135 | 							// Update user with default clinic ID
 136 | 							await db.update(schema.user).set({ clinicId: clinicId }).where(eq(schema.user.id, user.id));
 137 | 						}
 138 | 					} catch (error) {
 139 | 						console.error(`Post-create error for ${user.email}:`, error);
 140 | 					}

 142 | 					// Additional operations after user creation

 150 | 								.set({ usedBy: user.id, usedAt: new Date() })

 152 | 								.execute()
 153 | 						);
 154 | 					}

 156 | 					if (user.role === "superadmin") {
 157 | 						updates.push(
 158 | 							db
 159 | 								.insert(schema.configStore)
 160 | 								.values({ key: "admin_onboarded", value: "true" })
 161 | 								.onConflictDoNothing()
 162 | 								.execute()
 163 | 						);
 164 | 					}
 165 |
 166 | 					updates.push(
 167 | 						getSettings().then(settings =>
 168 | 							db.insert(schema.userQuota).values({
 169 | 								userId: user.id,
 170 | 								quota: user.role === "superadmin" ? -1 : settings.defaultUserQuota,

 172 | 								inviteQuota: user.role === "superadmin" ? -1 : settings.defaultInvitesQuota
 173 | 							})
 174 | 						)
 175 | 					);
 176 |
 177 | 					await Promise.all(updates);
 178 | 				}
 179 | 			},
 180 | 			delete: {
 181 | 				before: async user => {
 182 | 					const userFiles = await db
 183 | 						.select({ id: schema.files.id })
 184 | 						.from(schema.files)
 185 | 						.where(eq(schema.files.userId, user.id));

 187 | 					setImmediate(async () => {

 189 | 						for (let i = 0; i < userFiles.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
 190 | 							const batch = userFiles.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE);
 191 | 							await Promise.allSettled(
 192 | 								batch.map(file => fs.unlink(`./storage/${file.id}`).catch(() => {}))
 193 | 							);
 194 | 						}
 195 | 					});
 196 | 				}
 197 | 			}
 198 | 		}
 199 | 	},
 200 | 	user: {
 201 | 		deleteUser: { enabled: true },
 202 | 		additionalFields: {
 203 | 			apiKey: { type: "string", required: false, input: false },
 204 | 			clinicId: { type: "string", required: false, input: true }
 205 | 		}
 206 | 	},
 207 | 	plugins: [

 209 | 			ac,
 210 | 			roles: { staff, admin, patient, doctor },
 211 | 			adminRoles: ["doctor", "admin"]
 212 | 		}),
 213 | 		oneTap(),
 214 | 		twoFactor(),
 215 | 		openAPI({
 216 | 			theme: "deepSpace"
 217 | 		})
 218 | 	]
 219 | } satisfies BetterAuthOptions;
 220 |
 221 | export const auth = betterAuth({
 222 | 	...options,
 223 | 	hooks: {
 224 | 		before: createAuthMiddleware(async ({ path, request }) => {
 225 | 			if (path === "/sign-up/email") {
 226 | 				const settings = await getSettings();
 227 | 				if (!settings.signUpEnabled) {
 228 | 					throw new APIError("FORBIDDEN", {
 229 | 						message: "User sign-up is disabled at the moment."
 230 | 					});
 231 | 				}
 232 | 			}
 233 |
 234 | 			if (path.includes("/delete-user")) {
 235 | 				const session = await auth.api.getSession({
 236 | 					headers: request?.headers ?? getRequestHeaders()
 237 | 				});
 238 | 				if (!session) {
 239 | 					throw new APIError("UNAUTHORIZED");
 240 | 				}
 241 | 				if (session.user.role === "admin" || session.user.role === "superadmin") {
 242 | 					const result = db
 243 | 						.select({ count: count(schema.user.id) })
 244 | 						.from(schema.user)
 245 | 						.where(eq(schema.user.role, session.user.role))
 246 | 						.get();
 247 | 					if (result?.count ?? 0 <= 1) {
 248 | 						throw new APIError("FORBIDDEN", {
 249 | 							message: `Cannot delete the only ${session.user.role} user.`
 250 | 						});
 251 | 					}
 252 | 				}
 253 | 			}
 254 | 		})
 255 | 	},
 256 | 	plugins: [
 257 | 		...(options.plugins ?? []),
 258 | 		customSession(async ({ user, session }) => {
 259 | 			const [userQuota, userClinics, primaryClinic] = await Promise.all([
 260 | 				db.query.userQuota.findFirst({
 261 | 					where: (userQuota, { eq }) => eq(userQuota.userId, user.id)
 262 | 				}),
 263 | 				db.query.clinicMembers.findMany({
 264 | 					where: (clinicMembers, { eq }) => eq(clinicMembers.userId, user.id),
 265 | 					with: {
 266 | 						clinic: true
 267 | 					}
 268 | 				}),
 269 | 				user.clinicId
 270 | 					? db.query.clinics.findFirst({
 271 | 							where: (clinics, { eq }) => eq(clinics.id, user.clinicId as string)
 272 | 						})
 273 | 					: undefined
 274 | 			]);
 275 |
 276 | 			return {
 277 | 				user: {
 278 | 					...user,
 279 | 					quota: userQuota,
 280 | 					primaryClinic,
 281 | 					clinics:
 282 | 						userClinics?.map(cm => ({
 283 | 							id: cm.clinic.id,
 284 | 							name: cm.clinic.name,
 285 | 							email: cm.clinic.email,
 286 | 							memberRole: cm.role,
 287 | 							joinedAt: cm.createdAt,
 288 | 							isPrimary: cm.clinic.id === user.clinicId
 289 | 						})) || []
 290 | 				},
 291 | 				session
 292 | 			};
 293 | 		}, options),
 294 | 		tanstackStartCookies()
 295 | 	]
 296 | });
```

`lib/compose-refs.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import * as React from "react";
   2 |
   3 | type PossibleRef<T> = React.Ref<T> | undefined;
   4 |
   5 | /**
   6 |  * Set a given ref to a given value
   7 |  * This utility takes care of different types of refs: callback refs and RefObject(s)
   8 |  */
   9 | function setRef<T>(ref: PossibleRef<T>, value: T) {
  10 | 	if (typeof ref === "function") {
  11 | 		return ref(value);
  12 | 	}
  13 |
  14 | 	if (ref !== null && ref !== undefined) {
  15 | 		ref.current = value;
  16 | 	}
  17 | }
  18 |
  19 | /**
  20 |  * A utility to compose multiple refs together
  21 |  * Accepts callback refs and RefObject(s)
  22 |  */
  23 | function composeRefs<T>(...refs: PossibleRef<T>[]): React.RefCallback<T> {
  24 | 	return node => {
  25 | 		let hasCleanup = false;
  26 | 		const cleanups = refs.map(ref => {
  27 | 			const cleanup = setRef(ref, node);
  28 | 			if (!hasCleanup && typeof cleanup === "function") {
  29 | 				hasCleanup = true;
  30 | 			}
  31 | 			return cleanup;
  32 | 		});
  33 |
  34 | 		// React <19 will log an error to the console if a callback ref returns a
  35 | 		// value. We don't use ref cleanups internally so this will only happen if a
  36 | 		// user's ref callback returns a value, which we only expect if they are
  37 | 		// using the cleanup functionality added in React 19.
  38 | 		if (hasCleanup) {
  39 | 			return () => {
  40 | 				for (let i = 0; i < cleanups.length; i++) {
  41 | 					const cleanup = cleanups[i];
  42 | 					if (typeof cleanup === "function") {
  43 | 						cleanup();
  44 | 					} else {
  45 | 						setRef(refs[i], null);
  46 | 					}
  47 | 				}
  48 | 			};
  49 | 		}
  50 | 	};
  51 | }
  52 |
  53 | /**
  54 |  * A custom hook that composes multiple refs
  55 |  * Accepts callback refs and RefObject(s)
  56 |  */
  57 | function useComposedRefs<T>(...refs: PossibleRef<T>[]): React.RefCallback<T> {
  58 | 	// biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: we want to memoize by all values
  59 | 	// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps -- intentional: memoize by all ref values
  60 | 	return React.useCallback(composeRefs(...refs), refs);
  61 | }
  62 |
  63 | export { composeRefs, useComposedRefs };

```

`lib/data-table.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import type { Column } from "@tanstack/react-table";
   2 |
   3 | import { dataTableConfig } from "#/config/data-table";
   4 | import type { ExtendedColumnFilter, FilterOperator, FilterVariant } from "#/types/data-table";
   5 |
   6 | export function getCommonPinningStyles<TData>({ column }: { column: Column<TData> }): React.CSSProperties {
   7 | 	const isPinned = column.getIsPinned();
   8 | 	const isLastLeftPinnedColumn = isPinned === "left" && column.getIsLastColumn("left");
   9 | 	const isFirstRightPinnedColumn = isPinned === "right" && column.getIsFirstColumn("right");
  10 |
  11 | 	return {
  12 | 		boxShadow: isLastLeftPinnedColumn
  13 | 			? "-5px 0 5px -5px var(--border) inset"
  14 | 			: isFirstRightPinnedColumn
  15 | 				? "5px 0 5px -5px var(--border) inset"
  16 | 				: undefined,
  17 | 		left: isPinned === "left" ? `${column.getStart("left")}px` : undefined,
  18 | 		right: isPinned === "right" ? `${column.getAfter("right")}px` : undefined,
  19 | 		position: isPinned ? "sticky" : "relative",
  20 | 		background: isPinned ? "var(--background)" : undefined,
  21 | 		width: column.getSize(),
  22 | 		zIndex: isPinned ? 1 : 0
  23 | 	};
  24 | }
  25 |
  26 | export function getFilterOperators(filterVariant: FilterVariant) {
  27 | 	const operatorMap: Record<FilterVariant, { label: string; value: FilterOperator }[]> = {
  28 | 		text: dataTableConfig.textOperators,
  29 | 		number: dataTableConfig.numericOperators,
  30 | 		range: dataTableConfig.numericOperators,
  31 | 		date: dataTableConfig.dateOperators,
  32 | 		dateRange: dataTableConfig.dateOperators,
  33 | 		boolean: dataTableConfig.booleanOperators,
  34 | 		select: dataTableConfig.selectOperators,
  35 | 		multiSelect: dataTableConfig.multiSelectOperators
  36 | 	};
  37 |
  38 | 	return operatorMap[filterVariant] ?? dataTableConfig.textOperators;
  39 | }
  40 |
  41 | export function getDefaultFilterOperator(filterVariant: FilterVariant) {
  42 | 	const operators = getFilterOperators(filterVariant);
  43 |
  44 | 	return operators[0]?.value ?? (filterVariant === "text" ? "iLike" : "eq");
  45 | }
  46 |
  47 | export function getValidFilters<TData>(filters: ExtendedColumnFilter<TData>[]): ExtendedColumnFilter<TData>[] {
  48 | 	return filters.filter(
  49 | 		filter =>
  50 | 			filter.operator === "isEmpty" ||
  51 | 			filter.operator === "isNotEmpty" ||
  52 | 			(Array.isArray(filter.value)
  53 | 				? filter.value.length > 0
  54 | 				: filter.value !== "" && filter.value !== null && filter.value !== undefined)
  55 | 	);
  56 | }
```

```ts
   1 | export function formatDate(date: Date | string | number | undefined, opts: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = {}) {
   2 | 	if (!date) return "";
   3 |
   4 | 	try {
   5 | 		return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
   6 | 			month: opts.month ?? "long",
   7 | 			day: opts.day ?? "numeric",
   8 | 			year: opts.year ?? "numeric",
   9 | 			...opts
  10 | 		}).format(new Date(date));
  11 | 	} catch {
  12 | 		return "";
  13 | 	}
  14 | }
```

`lib/id.ts`:

```ts
   1 | const prefixes = {
   2 | 	user: "USR",
   3 | 	patient: "PAT",
   4 | 	record: "REC",
   5 | 	doctor: "DOC",
   6 | 	appointment: "APT"
   7 | } as const;
   8 |
   9 | type Prefix = keyof typeof prefixes;
  10 |
  11 | type GenerateIdOptions = {
  12 | 	separator?: string;
  13 | };
  14 |
  15 | export function generateId(prefixOrOptions?: Prefix | GenerateIdOptions, inputOptions: GenerateIdOptions = {}) {
  16 | 	// Determine if the first argument is options or prefix
  17 | 	const isOptions = typeof prefixOrOptions === "object" && prefixOrOptions !== null;
  18 |
  19 | 	const finalOptions = isOptions ? prefixOrOptions : inputOptions;
  20 | 	const prefix = isOptions ? undefined : prefixOrOptions;
  21 |
  22 | 	const { separator = "_" } = finalOptions;
  23 |
  24 | 	const id = crypto.randomUUID();
  25 |
  26 | 	if (prefix && prefix in prefixes) {
  27 | 		return `${prefixes[prefix]}${separator}${id}`;
  28 | 	}
  29 |
  30 | 	return id;
  31 | }

```

`lib/parsers.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { z } from "zod";
   2 |
   3 | import { dataTableConfig } from "#/config/data-table";
   4 | import type { ExtendedColumnFilter, ExtendedColumnSort } from "#/types/data-table";
   5 |
   6 | const sortingItemSchema = z.object({
   7 | 	id: z.string(),
   8 | 	desc: z.boolean()
   9 | });
  10 |
  11 | export function parseSortingState<TData>(
  12 | 	value: string | undefined,
  13 | 	columnIds?: string[] | Set<string>
  14 | ): ExtendedColumnSort<TData>[] {
  15 | 	if (!value) return [];
  16 | 	const validKeys = columnIds ? (columnIds instanceof Set ? columnIds : new Set(columnIds)) : null;
  17 |
  18 | 	try {
  19 | 		const parsed = JSON.parse(value);
  20 | 		const result = z.array(sortingItemSchema).safeParse(parsed);
  21 | 		if (!result.success) return [];
  22 | 		if (validKeys && result.data.some(item => !validKeys.has(item.id))) return [];
  23 | 		return result.data as ExtendedColumnSort<TData>[];
  24 | 	} catch {
  25 | 		return [];
  26 | 	}
  27 | }
  28 |
  29 | export function serializeSortingState<TData>(value: ExtendedColumnSort<TData>[]): string {
  30 | 	return JSON.stringify(value);
  31 | }
  32 |
  33 | const filterItemSchema = z.object({
  34 | 	id: z.string(),
  35 | 	value: z.union([z.string(), z.array(z.string())]),
  36 | 	variant: z.enum(dataTableConfig.filterVariants),
  37 | 	operator: z.enum(dataTableConfig.operators),
  38 | 	filterId: z.string()
  39 | });
  40 |
  41 | export type FilterItemSchema = z.infer<typeof filterItemSchema>;
  42 |
  43 | export function parseFiltersState<TData>(
  44 | 	value: string | undefined,
  45 | 	columnIds?: string[] | Set<string>
  46 | ): ExtendedColumnFilter<TData>[] {
  47 | 	if (!value) return [];
  48 | 	const validKeys = columnIds ? (columnIds instanceof Set ? columnIds : new Set(columnIds)) : null;
  49 |
  50 | 	try {
  51 | 		const parsed = JSON.parse(value);
  52 | 		const result = z.array(filterItemSchema).safeParse(parsed);
  53 | 		if (!result.success) return [];
  54 | 		if (validKeys && result.data.some(item => !validKeys.has(item.id))) return [];
  55 | 		return result.data as ExtendedColumnFilter<TData>[];
  56 | 	} catch {
  57 | 		return [];
  58 | 	}
  59 | }
  60 |
  61 | export function serializeFiltersState<TData>(value: ExtendedColumnFilter<TData>[]): string {
  62 | 	return JSON.stringify(value);
  63 | }

```

`lib/settings.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import z from "zod";
   2 |
   3 | import { db } from "../db";
   4 | import { configStore } from "../db/schema";
   5 | import type { CamelCaseKeys } from "./types";
   6 | import { snakeToCamel } from "./utils";
   7 |
   8 | // ... (CONFIG_DEF and type definitions remain the same as they are logic-pure)
   9 | const CONFIG_DEF = {
  10 | 	require_invite: z.boolean().default(false),
  11 | 	sign_up_enabled: z.boolean().default(true),
  12 | 	blacklisted_extensions: z.string().default(""),
  13 | 	max_invite_age: z.number().default(1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 7),
  14 | 	allow_user_create_invites: z.boolean().default(true),
  15 | 	default_user_file_count_quota: z.number().default(1000),
  16 | 	default_user_quota: z.number().default(1024 * 1024 * 1024),
  17 | 	default_invites_quota: z.number().default(10),
  18 | 	upload_file_chunk_size: z.number().default(1024 * 1024 * 25),
  19 | 	upload_file_max_size: z.number().default(1024 * 1024 * 1024 * 5),
  20 | 	cdn_url: z.url().or(z.literal("")).default("")
  21 | };
  22 |
  23 | type ConfigDef = typeof CONFIG_DEF;
  24 | export type ConfigKey = keyof ConfigDef;
  25 | type ConfigState = z.infer<z.ZodObject<ConfigDef>>;
  26 | export type AppSettings = CamelCaseKeys<ConfigState> & {
  27 | 	baseUrl: string;
  28 | 	appName: string;
  29 | };
  30 |
  31 | const getDefaults = (): AppSettings => {
  32 | 	const defaults: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  33 | 	for (const [key, schema] of Object.entries(CONFIG_DEF)) {
  34 | 		defaults[snakeToCamel(key)] = schema.parse(undefined);
  35 | 	}
  36 | 	return {
  37 | 		...(defaults as CamelCaseKeys<ConfigState>),
  38 | 		baseUrl: process.env.AUTH_BASE_URL || "",
  39 | 		appName: process.env.APP_NAME || ""
  40 | 	};
  41 | };
  42 |
  43 | // ... (Logic for zShape, keyMap, and defaults remains the same)
  44 |
  45 | // 1. Convert Data Fetching to a Server Function
  46 | export const getSettings = async (): Promise<AppSettings> => {
  47 | 	let rows: { key: string; value: string }[];
  48 | 	try {
  49 | 		rows = await db.select().from(configStore);
  50 | 	} catch {
  51 | 		return getDefaults();
  52 | 	}
  53 |
  54 | 	const finalConfig: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  55 | 	const rawMap = new Map(rows.map(r => [r.key, r.value]));
  56 |
  57 | 	for (const [snakeKey, value] of Object.entries(CONFIG_DEF)) {
  58 | 		const camelKey = snakeToCamel(snakeKey);
  59 | 		const dbValue = rawMap.get(snakeKey);
  60 | 		const schema = value as z.ZodTypeAny;
  61 |
  62 | 		if (dbValue === undefined) {
  63 | 			finalConfig[camelKey] = schema.parse(undefined);
  64 | 		} else {
  65 | 			// Logic for parsing values from string back to their types
  66 | 			if (schema instanceof z.ZodDefault) {
  67 | 				const innerType = (schema as z.ZodDefault<z.ZodTypeAny>).def.innerType;
  68 | 				if (innerType instanceof z.ZodNumber) finalConfig[camelKey] = Number(dbValue);
  69 | 				else if (innerType instanceof z.ZodBoolean) finalConfig[camelKey] = dbValue === "true";
  70 | 				else finalConfig[camelKey] = dbValue;
  71 | 			} else {
  72 | 				finalConfig[camelKey] = dbValue;
  73 | 			}
  74 | 		}
  75 | 	}
  76 |
  77 | 	// Ensure database has all current keys
  78 | 	const missing = Object.keys(CONFIG_DEF).filter(k => !new Set(rows.map(r => r.key)).has(k));
  79 | 	if (missing.length > 0) {
  80 | 		const inserts = missing.map(k => ({
  81 | 			key: k,
  82 | 			value: String((CONFIG_DEF as Record<string, z.ZodTypeAny | undefined>)[k]?.parse(undefined))
  83 | 		}));
  84 | 		await db.insert(configStore).values(inserts).onConflictDoNothing().execute();
  85 | 	}
  86 |
  87 | 	return {
  88 | 		...(finalConfig as AppSettings),
  89 | 		baseUrl: process.env.AUTH_BASE_URL || "",
  90 | 		appName: process.env.APP_NAME || ""
  91 | 	};
  92 | };
```

`lib/utils.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { type ClassValue, clsx } from "clsx";
   2 | import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";
   3 |
   4 | import type { UploadStatus } from "#/lib/types.ts";
   5 |
   6 | export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
   7 | 	return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
   8 | }
   9 |
  10 | export function formatBytes(bytes: number) {
  11 | 	if (bytes === 0) return "0 B";
  12 | 	const k = 1024;
  13 | 	const sizes = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
  14 | 	const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  15 | 	return `${Number.parseFloat((bytes / k ** i).toFixed(2))} ${sizes[i]}`;
  16 | }
  17 |
  18 | export function parseBytes(input: string): number | null {
  19 | 	const units: Record<string, number> = {
  20 | 		b: 1,
  21 | 		kb: 1024,
  22 | 		mb: 1024 ** 2,
  23 | 		gb: 1024 ** 3,
  24 | 		tb: 1024 ** 4
  25 | 	};
  26 | 	const match = input.toLowerCase().match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([a-z]+)?$/);
  27 | 	if (!match) return null;
  28 | 	const value = Number.parseFloat(match[1]);
  29 | 	const unit = match[2] || "b";
  30 | 	if (!units[unit]) return null;
  31 | 	return Math.floor(value * units[unit]);
  32 | }
  33 |
  34 | export function formatDuration(ms: number): string {
  35 | 	if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;
  36 | 	const s = 1000;
  37 | 	const m = s * 60;
  38 | 	const h = m * 60;
  39 | 	const d = h * 24;
  40 |
  41 | 	if (ms >= d) return `${Number.parseFloat((ms / d).toFixed(2))}d`;
  42 | 	if (ms >= h) return `${Number.parseFloat((ms / h).toFixed(2))}h`;
  43 | 	if (ms >= m) return `${Number.parseFloat((ms / m).toFixed(2))}m`;
  44 | 	return `${Number.parseFloat((ms / s).toFixed(2))}s`;
  45 | }
  46 |
  47 | export function parseDuration(input: string): number | null {
  48 | 	const units: Record<string, number> = {
  49 | 		ms: 1,
  50 | 		s: 1000,
  51 | 		m: 1000 * 60,
  52 | 		h: 1000 * 60 * 60,
  53 | 		d: 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24
  54 | 	};
  55 | 	const match = input.toLowerCase().match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([a-z]+)?$/);
  56 | 	if (!match) return null;
  57 | 	const value = Number.parseFloat(match[1]);
  58 | 	const unit = match[2] || "ms";
  59 | 	if (!units[unit]) return null;
  60 | 	return Math.floor(value * units[unit]);
  61 | }
  62 |
  63 | export function getStatusLabel(status: UploadStatus, progress: number): string {
  64 | 	switch (status) {
  65 | 		case "completed":
  66 | 			return "Done";
  67 | 		case "error":
  68 | 			return "Error";
  69 | 		case "pending":
  70 | 			return "Queued";
  71 | 		case "paused":
  72 | 			return "Paused";
  73 | 		default:
  74 | 			return `${progress.toFixed(0)}%`;
  75 | 	}
  76 | }
  77 |
  78 | export function calculateGlobalProgress(
  79 | 	files: Array<{ id: string; file: File }>,
  80 | 	fileStatuses: Map<string, UploadStatus>,
  81 | 	fileProgress: Map<string, number>
  82 | ): {
  83 | 	completedCount: number;
  84 | 	globalProgress: number;
  85 | 	isGlobalComplete: boolean;
  86 | 	isGlobalError: boolean;
  87 | } {
  88 | 	if (files.length === 0) {
  89 | 		return {
  90 | 			completedCount: 0,
  91 | 			globalProgress: 0,
  92 | 			isGlobalComplete: false,
  93 | 			isGlobalError: false
  94 | 		};
  95 | 	}
  96 |
  97 | 	const completedCount = files.filter(f => fileStatuses.get(f.id) === "completed").length;
  98 |
  99 | 	const hasError = files.some(f => fileStatuses.get(f.id) === "error");
 100 |
 101 | 	const totalSize = files.reduce((acc, f) => acc + f.file.size, 0);
 102 | 	const loadedSize = files.reduce((acc, f) => {
 103 | 		const progress = fileProgress.get(f.id) || 0;
 104 | 		const status = fileStatuses.get(f.id);
 105 | 		const effectiveProgress = status === "completed" ? 100 : progress;
 106 | 		return acc + (f.file.size * effectiveProgress) / 100;
 107 | 	}, 0);
 108 |
 109 | 	return {
 110 | 		completedCount,
 111 | 		globalProgress: totalSize > 0 ? (loadedSize / totalSize) * 100 : 0,
 112 | 		isGlobalComplete: completedCount === files.length,
 113 | 		isGlobalError: hasError
 114 | 	};
 115 | }
 116 |
 117 | export function normalize(s: string) {
 118 | 	return String(s)
 119 | 		.toLowerCase()
 120 | 		.replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
 121 | 		.replace(/\s+/g, " ")
 122 | 		.trim();
 123 | }
 124 |
 125 | export function extractError(err: string): {
 126 | 	status_code: number | null;
 127 | 	message: string;
 128 | } {
 129 | 	const status = err.match(/response code:\s*(\d+)/i);
 130 | 	const message = err.match(/response text:\s*(.*?)\s*,\s*request id/i);
 131 |
 132 | 	return {
 133 | 		status_code: status ? Number(status[1]) : null,
 134 | 		message: message ? message[1] : err
 135 | 	};
 136 | }
 137 |
 138 | export const snakeToCamel = (str: string): string => str.replace(/_([a-z])/g, g => g[1].toUpperCase());
 139 |
 140 | export const camelToSnake = (str: string): string => str.replace(/[A-Z]/g, letter => `_${letter.toLowerCase()}`);
 141 |
 142 | const SLUG_CHARS = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
 143 | const SLUG_LENGTH = 6;
 144 | const MAX_SLUG_RETRIES = 10;
 145 |
 146 | export function generateSlug(): string {
 147 | 	let result = "";
 148 | 	const randomValues = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(SLUG_LENGTH));
 149 | 	for (let i = 0; i < SLUG_LENGTH; i++) {
 150 | 		result += SLUG_CHARS[randomValues[i] % SLUG_CHARS.length];
 151 | 	}
 152 | 	return result;
 153 | }
 154 |
 155 | export async function generateUniqueSlug(checkExists: (slug: string) => boolean | Promise<boolean>): Promise<string> {
 156 | 	for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_SLUG_RETRIES; attempt++) {
 157 | 		const slug = generateSlug();
 158 | 		const exists = await checkExists(slug);
 159 | 		if (!exists) {
 160 | 			return slug;
 161 | 		}
 162 | 	}
 163 | 	throw new Error("Failed to generate unique slug after maximum retries");
 164 | }
 165 |
 166 | export function ring(color = "ring-primary") {
 167 | 	return `focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-none ${color}`;
 168 | }
 169 |
 170 | export function getInitials(name: string) {
 171 | 	const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/);
 172 | 	if (parts.length === 0) return "";
 173 | 	if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
 174 | 	return (parts[0][0] + parts[parts.length - 1][0]).toUpperCase();
 175 | }
```

`mcp-todos.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import fs from "node:fs";
   2 |
   3 | const todosPath = "./mcp-todos.json";
   4 |
   5 | // In-memory todos storage
   6 | const todos = fs.existsSync(todosPath)
   7 | 	? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(todosPath, "utf8"))
   8 | 	: [
   9 | 			{
  10 | 				id: 1,
  11 | 				title: "Buy groceries"
  12 | 			}
  13 | 		];
  14 |
  15 | // Subscription callbacks per userID
  16 | let subscribers: ((todos: Todo[]) => void)[] = [];
  17 |
  18 | export type Todo = {
  19 | 	id: number;
  20 | 	title: string;
  21 | };
  22 |
  23 | // Get the todos for a user
  24 | export function getTodos(): Todo[] {
  25 | 	return todos;
  26 | }
  27 |
  28 | // Add an item to the todos
  29 | export function addTodo(title: string) {
  30 | 	todos.push({ id: todos.length + 1, title });
  31 | 	fs.writeFileSync(todosPath, JSON.stringify(todos, null, 2));
  32 | 	notifySubscribers();
  33 | }
  34 |
  35 | // Subscribe to cart changes for a user
  36 | export function subscribeToTodos(callback: (todos: Todo[]) => void) {
  37 | 	subscribers.push(callback);
  38 | 	callback(todos);
  39 | 	return () => {
  40 | 		subscribers = subscribers.filter(cb => cb !== callback);
  41 | 	};
  42 | }
  43 |
  44 | // Notify all subscribers of a user's cart
  45 | function notifySubscribers() {
  46 | 	for (const cb of subscribers) {
  47 | 		try {
  48 | 			cb(todos);
  49 | 		} catch {}
  50 | 	}
  51 | }
```

`orpc/context.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // src/orpc/context.ts
   2 | import { ORPCError } from "@orpc/server";
   3 | import { getRequestHeaders } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";
   4 |
   5 | import { type AppDb, db } from "#/db";
   6 | import { type AuthSession, auth, type Clinic } from "#/lib/auth";
   7 |
   8 | import type { UserRole } from "../lib/permissions";
   9 |
  10 | export type OrpcContext = {
  11 | 	session: AuthSession["session"] | null;
  12 | 	auth: typeof auth;
  13 | 	user: AuthSession["user"] | null;
  14 | 	clinicId: string;
  15 | 	db: AppDb;
  16 | 	isAuthenticated: boolean;
  17 | 	clinic: AuthSession["user"]["primaryClinic"] | null;
  18 | 	ip?: string;
  19 | 	userAgent?: string;
  20 | 	headers: Headers;
  21 | 	requireRole: (role: UserRole) => void;
  22 | };
  23 |
  24 | // Make this sync by receiving auth data as parameter
  25 | export function createContextSync(data: {
  26 | 	session: AuthSession["session"] | null;
  27 | 	user: AuthSession["user"] | null;
  28 | 	clinic: AuthSession["user"]["primaryClinic"] | null;
  29 | 	headers: Headers;
  30 | }): OrpcContext {
  31 | 	return {
  32 | 		db,
  33 | 		auth,
  34 | 		user: data.user,
  35 | 		session: data.session,
  36 | 		clinic: data.clinic,
  37 | 		clinicId: data.user?.primaryClinic?.id ?? "",
  38 | 		ip: data.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") ?? data.headers.get("x-real-ip") ?? undefined,
  39 | 		userAgent: data.headers.get("user-agent") ?? undefined,
  40 | 		isAuthenticated: !!data.user,
  41 | 		headers: data.headers,
  42 | 		requireRole: (role: UserRole) => {
  43 | 			if (data.user?.role !== role) {
  44 | 				throw new ORPCError("FORBIDDEN");
  45 | 			}
  46 | 		}
  47 | 	};
  48 | }
  49 |
  50 | // Keep async version for compatibility
  51 | export async function createContext(options: CreateContextOptions = {}): Promise<OrpcContext> {
  52 | 	const headers = options.headers ?? getRequestHeaders();
  53 | 	const data = await auth.api.getSession({ headers });
  54 |
  55 | 	return createContextSync({
  56 | 		session: data?.session ?? null,
  57 | 		user: data?.user ?? null,
  58 | 		clinic: data?.user?.primaryClinic ?? null,
  59 | 		headers
  60 | 	});
  61 | }
  62 |
  63 | export type CreateContextOptions = {
  64 | 	request?: Request; // Optional request object for server context
  65 | 	headers?: Headers;
  66 | 	clinic?: Clinic; // Optional override for clinicId, if needed in special cases
  67 | };
  68 |
  69 | export type Context = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof createContext>>;

```

`orpc/init.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { ORPCError, os } from "@orpc/server";
   2 | import { z } from "zod";
   3 |
   4 | import { db } from "#/db";
   5 | import { auth } from "#/lib/auth";
   6 |
   7 | import type { Context, OrpcContext } from "./context";
   8 |
   9 | // Define base with proper Context type
  10 | export const base = os.$context<Context>().errors({
  11 | 	UNAUTHORIZED: {
  12 | 		message: "Unauthorized access",
  13 | 		statusCode: 401
  14 | 	},
  15 | 	RATE_LIMITED: {
  16 | 		data: z.object({ retryAfter: z.number() }),
  17 | 		message: "You are being rate limited",
  18 | 		statusCode: 429
  19 | 	}
  20 | });
  21 |
  22 | // Helper to get session data consistently
  23 | async function getSessionData(headers: Headers) {
  24 | 	const sessionData = await auth.api.getSession({ headers });
  25 |
  26 | 	if (!sessionData?.session || !sessionData?.user) {
  27 | 		throw new ORPCError("UNAUTHORIZED");
  28 | 	}
  29 |
  30 | 	return sessionData;
  31 | }
  32 |
  33 | // Public DB middleware (no auth required)
  34 | export const dbMiddleware = base.middleware(async ({ next, context }) => {
  35 | 	return next({
  36 | 		context: {
  37 | 			...context,
  38 | 			db
  39 | 		}
  40 | 	});
  41 | });
  42 |
  43 | // Enhanced auth middleware with better error handling
  44 | export const authMiddleware = base.middleware(async ({ context, next }) => {
  45 | 	// Ensure headers exist (works with both server and client contexts)
  46 | 	const headers = context.headers ?? new Headers();
  47 | 	const sessionData = await getSessionData(headers);
  48 |
  49 | 	const requireRole = (role: string) => {
  50 | 		if (sessionData.user.role !== role) {
  51 | 			throw new ORPCError("FORBIDDEN", {
  52 | 				message: `Role ${role} required, got ${sessionData.user.role}`
  53 | 			});
  54 | 		}
  55 | 	};
  56 |
  57 | 	// Build enriched context
  58 | 	const enrichedContext: OrpcContext = {
  59 | 		db: context.db ?? db,
  60 | 		auth,
  61 | 		session: sessionData.session,
  62 | 		user: sessionData.user,
  63 | 		clinic: sessionData.user.primaryClinic ?? null,
  64 | 		clinicId: sessionData.user.primaryClinic?.id ?? "",
  65 | 		isAuthenticated: true,
  66 | 		ip: headers.get("x-forwarded-for") ?? headers.get("x-real-ip") ?? undefined,
  67 | 		userAgent: headers.get("user-agent") ?? undefined,
  68 | 		headers,
  69 | 		requireRole
  70 | 	};
  71 |
  72 | 	return next({
  73 | 		context: enrichedContext
  74 | 	});
  75 | });
  76 |
  77 | // Fixed: Role-specific middleware using base directly
  78 | // Updated role middleware to support multiple roles
  79 | export function requireRoleMiddleware(requiredRoles: string[]) {
  80 | 	return base.middleware(async ({ context, next }) => {
  81 | 		const ctx = context as OrpcContext;
  82 | 		if (!ctx.user || !requiredRoles.includes(ctx.user.role ?? "")) {
  83 | 			throw new ORPCError("FORBIDDEN", {
  84 | 				message: `One of roles [${requiredRoles.join(", ")}] required`
  85 | 			});
  86 | 		}
  87 | 		return next({ context });
  88 | 	});
  89 | }
  90 |
  91 | // Export pre‑configured bases
  92 | export const publicProcedure = base.use(dbMiddleware);
  93 | export const authorizedProcedure = base.use(authMiddleware);
  94 | export const protectedProcedure = authorizedProcedure.use(dbMiddleware);
  95 |
  96 | // Fixed: Create admin and staff procedures properly
  97 | // These should use authMiddleware first, then check roles
  98 | export const adminProcedure = base.use(authMiddleware).use(requireRoleMiddleware(["admin"]));
  99 |
 100 | export const clinicProcedure = base.use(authMiddleware).use(requireRoleMiddleware(["doctor", "staff", "admin"]));
 101 |
 102 | export const rateLimit = base.middleware(async ({ next }) => {
 103 | 	throw new ORPCError("RATE_LIMITED", {
 104 | 		message: "You are being rate limited",
 105 | 		data: { retryAfter: 60 }
 106 | 	});
 107 | 	return next();
 108 | });
 109 |
 110 | export function retry(options: { times: number }) {
 111 | 	/**
 112 | 	 * Best practices for dedupe-middlewares
 113 | 	 * {@link https://orpc.dev/docs/best-practices/dedupe-middleware}
 114 | 	 */
 115 | 	return os.$context<{ canRetry?: boolean }>().middleware(({ context, next }) => {
 116 | 		const canRetry = context.canRetry ?? true;
 117 |
 118 | 		if (!canRetry) {
 119 | 			return next();
 120 | 		}
 121 |
 122 | 		let times = 0;
 123 | 		while (true) {
 124 | 			try {
 125 | 				return next({
 126 | 					context: {
 127 | 						canRetry: false
 128 | 					}
 129 | 				});
 130 | 			} catch (e) {
 131 | 				if (times >= options.times) {
 132 | 					throw e;
 133 | 				}
 134 |
 135 | 				times++;
 136 | 			}
 137 | 		}
 138 | 	});
 139 | }
 140 | /* example for rate limit middleware
 141 | export const rateLimit = base
 142 | .use(rateLimit)
 143 | .errors({
 144 |   NOT_FOUND: {
 145 |     message: "Resource not found",
 146 |     statusCode: 404,
 147 |   },
 148 | })
 149 | .middleware(async ({ next }) => {
 150 |   // Implement your rate limiting logic here (e.g., using Redis or in-memory store)
 151 |   const isRateLimited = false; // Replace with actual check
 152 |
 153 |   if (isRateLimited) {
 154 |     throw new ORPCError("RATE_LIMITED", {
 155 |       message: "You are being rate limited",
 156 |       data: { retryAfter: 60 }, // Example: retry after 60 seconds
 157 |     });
 158 |   }
 159 |   return next();
 160 | })
 161 | */

```

```ts
   1 | import z from "zod";
   2 |
   3 | import { auth } from "#/lib/auth";
   4 |
   5 | import { adminProcedure, publicProcedure } from "../init";
   6 | import { UserSchema } from "../schema";
   7 |
   8 | export const UserAuthSchema = UserSchema.extend({
   9 | 	role: z.enum(["admin", "doctor", "patient", "staff"]),
  10 | 	password: z.string().min(6)
  11 | });
  12 | export const listUsers = adminProcedure.handler(async () => {
  13 | 	const result = await auth.api.listUsers();
  14 | 	return result.users;
  15 | });
  16 |
  17 | export const updateUserRole = adminProcedure
  18 | 	.input(
  19 | 		z.object({
  20 | 			userId: z.string(),
  21 | 			role: z.enum(["admin", "doctor", "patient", "staff"])
  22 | 		})
  23 | 	)

  28 | 			body: {
  29 | 				userId,

  33 | 		});
  34 |

  36 | 	});

  52 | 				email,

  57 | 					// Additional metadata

  62 | 		});
```

`orpc/router/appointment.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { and, between, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   2 | import { z } from "zod";
   3 |
   4 | import { db } from "../../db";
   5 | import { appointment } from "../../db/schema";
   6 | import { protectedProcedure } from "../init";
   7 | import { AppointmentsCreateSchema, AppointmentsUpdateSchema } from "../schema";
   8 |
   9 | // Create appointment
  10 | export const createAppointment = protectedProcedure
  11 | 	.input(AppointmentsCreateSchema)
  12 | 	.output(AppointmentsUpdateSchema)
  13 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  14 | 		// Check for conflicts
  15 | 		const existing = await db.query.appointment.findFirst({
  16 | 			where: and(
  17 | 				eq(appointment.doctorId, input.doctorId),
  18 | 				eq(appointment.appointmentDate, input.appointmentDate),
  19 | 				eq(appointment.status, "PENDING")
  20 | 			)
  21 | 		});
  22 |
  23 | 		if (existing) {
  24 | 			throw new Error("Doctor is already booked at this time");
  25 | 		}
  26 | 		if (!context.clinicId) {
  27 | 			throw new Error("Missing clinicId in context");
  28 | 		}
  29 |
  30 | 		const { clinicId, ...appointmentInput } = input;
  31 | 		const [newAppointment] = await db
  32 | 			.insert(appointment)
  33 | 			.values({
  34 | 				...appointmentInput,
  35 | 				clinicId: context.clinicId,
  36 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID()
  37 | 			})
  38 | 			.returning();
  39 | 		return newAppointment;
  40 | 	});
  41 |
  42 | export const getAppointments = protectedProcedure
  43 | 	.input(
  44 | 		z.object({
  45 | 			page: z.number().default(1),
  46 | 			limit: z.number().default(10),
  47 | 			status: z.string().optional(),
  48 | 			doctorId: z.string().optional(),
  49 | 			patientId: z.string().optional()
  50 | 		})
  51 | 	)
  52 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  53 | 		const { page, limit, status, doctorId, patientId } = input;
  54 | 		const offset = (page - 1) * limit;
  55 |
  56 | 		const whereClause = and(
  57 | 			eq(appointment.clinicId, context.clinicId),
  58 | 			eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
  59 | 			status ? eq(appointment.status, status) : undefined,
  60 | 			doctorId ? eq(appointment.doctorId, doctorId) : undefined,
  61 | 			patientId ? eq(appointment.patientId, patientId) : undefined
  62 | 		);
  63 |
  64 | 		const items = await db.query.appointment.findMany({
  65 | 			where: whereClause,
  66 | 			limit,
  67 | 			offset,
  68 | 			orderBy: desc(appointment.appointmentDate),
  69 | 			with: { patient: true, doctor: true }
  70 | 		});
  71 |
  72 | 		return { items, total: await db.$count(appointment, whereClause) };
  73 | 	});
  74 |
  75 | // Get appointments by date range
  76 | export const getAppointmentsByDateRange = protectedProcedure
  77 | 	.input(
  78 | 		z.object({
  79 | 			startDate: z.date(),
  80 | 			endDate: z.date(),
  81 | 			doctorId: z.string().optional(),
  82 | 			status: z.string().optional()
  83 | 		})
  84 | 	)
  85 | 	.output(z.array(AppointmentsUpdateSchema))
  86 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  87 | 		let whereClause = and(
  88 | 			eq(appointment.clinicId, context.clinicId),
  89 | 			between(appointment.appointmentDate, input.startDate, input.endDate),
  90 | 			eq(appointment.isDeleted, false)
  91 | 		);
  92 |
  93 | 		if (input.doctorId) {
  94 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(appointment.doctorId, input.doctorId));
  95 | 		}
  96 |
  97 | 		if (input.status) {
  98 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(appointment.status, input.status));
  99 | 		}
 100 |
 101 | 		return await db.query.appointment.findMany({
 102 | 			where: whereClause,
 103 | 			with: {
 104 | 				patient: true,
 105 | 				doctor: true
 106 | 			},
 107 | 			orderBy: desc(appointment.appointmentDate)
 108 | 		});
 109 | 	});
 110 |
 111 | export const updateAppointmentStatus = protectedProcedure
 112 | 	.input(
 113 | 		z.object({
 114 | 			id: z.string(),
 115 | 			status: z.enum(["PENDING", "CONFIRMED", "COMPLETED", "CANCELLED", "NO_SHOW"])
 116 | 		})
 117 | 	)
 118 | 	.output(AppointmentsUpdateSchema)
 119 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 120 | 		const [updated] = await db
 121 | 			.update(appointment)
 122 | 			.set({ status: input.status })
 123 | 			.where(and(eq(appointment.id, input.id), eq(appointment.clinicId, context.clinicId)))
 124 | 			.returning();
 125 | 		return updated;
 126 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/auth.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { ORPCError } from "@orpc/client";
   2 | import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   3 | import z from "zod";
   4 |
   5 | import { user } from "../../db/schema";
   6 | import { auth } from "../../lib/auth";
   7 | import { adminProcedure, protectedProcedure, publicProcedure } from "../init";
   8 | import { UserCreateSchema } from "../schema";
   9 |
  10 | const signUpSchema = UserCreateSchema.pick({ email: true }).extend({
  11 | 	role: z.enum(["patient", "doctor"]),
  12 | 	password: z.string().min(6),
  13 | 	name: z.string()
  14 | });
  15 |
  16 | export const signup = publicProcedure
  17 | 	.route({
  18 | 		method: "POST",
  19 | 		path: "/auth/signup",
  20 | 		summary: "User signup",
  21 | 		tags: ["Auth"]
  22 | 	})
  23 | 	.input(signUpSchema)
  24 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  25 | 		const { email, password, role, name } = input;
  26 |
  27 | 		// Use signUpEmail for public registration (no admin required)
  28 | 		const result = await auth.api.signUpEmail({
  29 | 			body: {
  30 | 				email,
  31 | 				password,
  32 | 				name
  33 | 			}
  34 | 		});
  35 |
  36 | 		// Handle error response
  37 | 		if (!result || "error" in result) {
  38 | 			throw new ORPCError("RATE_LIMITED", {
  39 | 				message: "You are being rate limited",
  40 | 				data: { retryAfter: 60 }
  41 | 			});
  42 | 		}
  43 |
  44 | 		// Store custom role in separate table or metadata
  45 | 		// Option 1: Update user with additional data (if you have a users table)
  46 | 		await context.db.update(user).set({ role }).where(eq(user.id, result.user.id));
  47 |
  48 | 		return {
  49 | 			id: result.user.id,
  50 | 			email: result.user.email,
  51 | 			name: result.user.name,
  52 | 			role: role
  53 | 		};
  54 | 	});
  55 |
  56 | export const signIn = publicProcedure
  57 | 	.route({
  58 | 		method: "POST",
  59 | 		path: "/auth/signin",
  60 | 		summary: "User signin",
  61 | 		tags: ["Auth"]
  62 | 	})
  63 | 	.input(
  64 | 		z.object({
  65 | 			email: z.string().email(),
  66 | 			password: z.string()
  67 | 		})
  68 | 	)
  69 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  70 | 		const { email, password } = input;
  71 |
  72 | 		const result = await auth.api.signInEmail({
  73 | 			body: {
  74 | 				email,
  75 | 				password
  76 | 			}
  77 | 		});
  78 |
  79 | 		if (!result || "error" in result) {
  80 | 			throw new ORPCError("UNAUTHORIZED", {
  81 | 				message: "Invalid email or password"
  82 | 			});
  83 | 		}
  84 |
  85 | 		return {
  86 | 			id: result.user.id,
  87 | 			email: result.user.email,
  88 | 			name: result.user.name,
  89 | 			role: result.user.role // Assuming role is included in the response
  90 | 		};
  91 | 	});
  92 |
  93 | export const signOut = protectedProcedure
  94 | 	.route({
  95 | 		method: "POST",
  96 | 		path: "/auth/signout",
  97 | 		summary: "User signout",
  98 | 		tags: ["Auth"]
  99 | 	})
 100 | 	.handler(async ({ context }) => {
 101 | 		await auth.api.signOut({
 102 | 			headers: context.headers
 103 | 		});
 104 |
 105 | 		return { success: true };
 106 | 	});
 107 |
 108 | export const revokeAllUserSessions = adminProcedure
 109 | 	.input(z.object({ userId: z.string() }))
 110 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 111 | 		await auth.api.revokeUserSessions({
 112 | 			body: { userId: input.userId },
 113 | 			headers: context.headers
 114 | 		});
 115 | 		return { success: true };
 116 | 	});
```

`orpc/router/clinic.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // routers/clinic.router.ts
   2 | import { and, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
   3 | import { z } from "zod";
   4 |
   5 | import { db } from "../../db";
   6 | import { appointment, clinicMembers, clinicSetting, clinics, doctor, patient, user, vitalSign } from "../../db/schema";
   7 | import { adminProcedure } from "../init";
   8 |
   9 | // Create clinic (admin only)
  10 | export const createClinic = adminProcedure
  11 | 	.input(
  12 | 		z.object({
  13 | 			name: z.string(),
  14 | 			email: z.email().optional(),
  15 | 			timezone: z.string().default("UTC"),
  16 | 			address: z.string().optional(),
  17 | 			phone: z.string().optional()
  18 | 		})
  19 | 	)
  20 | 	.output(z.any())
  21 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  22 | 		const [newClinic] = await db
  23 | 			.insert(clinics)
  24 | 			.values({
  25 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  26 | 				...input
  27 | 			})
  28 | 			.returning();
  29 |
  30 | 		// Create default clinic settings
  31 | 		await db.insert(clinicSetting).values({
  32 | 			id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  33 | 			clinicId: newClinic.id,
  34 | 			openingTime: "09:00",
  35 | 			closingTime: "17:00",
  36 | 			workingDays: JSON.stringify(["MONDAY", "TUESDAY", "WEDNESDAY", "THURSDAY", "FRIDAY"])
  37 | 		});
  38 |
  39 | 		return newClinic;
  40 | 	});
  41 |
  42 | // Add member to clinic
  43 | export const addClinicMember = adminProcedure
  44 | 	.input(
  45 | 		z.object({
  46 | 			clinicId: z.string(),
  47 | 			userId: z.string(),
  48 | 			role: z.enum(["ADMIN", "DOCTOR", "STAFF"])
  49 | 		})
  50 | 	)
  51 | 	.output(z.any())
  52 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  53 | 		const [member] = await db
  54 | 			.insert(clinicMembers)
  55 | 			.values({
  56 | 				userId: input.userId,
  57 | 				clinicId: input.clinicId,
  58 | 				role: input.role
  59 | 			})
  60 | 			.returning();
  61 |
  62 | 		// Update user's clinicId
  63 | 		await db.update(user).set({ clinicId: input.clinicId }).where(eq(user.id, input.userId));
  64 |
  65 | 		return member;
  66 | 	});
  67 |
  68 | export const getVitalSignsHistory = adminProcedure
  69 | 	.input(
  70 | 		z.object({
  71 | 			patientId: z.string(),
  72 | 			startDate: z.date().optional(),
  73 | 			endDate: z.date().optional()
  74 | 		})
  75 | 	)
  76 | 	.output(z.array(z.any()))
  77 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  78 | 		const conditions = [eq(vitalSign.patientId, input.patientId)];
  79 | 		if (input.startDate) {
  80 | 			conditions.push(sql`${vitalSign.createdAt} >= ${input.startDate.toISOString()}`);
  81 | 		}
  82 | 		if (input.endDate) {
  83 | 			conditions.push(sql`${vitalSign.createdAt} <= ${input.endDate.toISOString()}`);
  84 | 		}
  85 | 		const results = await db
  86 | 			.select()
  87 | 			.from(vitalSign)
  88 | 			.where(and(...conditions))
  89 | 			.orderBy(sql`${vitalSign.createdAt} DESC`);
  90 | 		return results;
  91 | 	});
  92 |
  93 | // Get clinic dashboard stats
  94 | export const getClinicStats = adminProcedure
  95 | 	.input(z.object({ clinicId: z.string() }))
  96 | 	.output(
  97 | 		z.object({
  98 | 			totalPatients: z.number(),
  99 | 			totalDoctors: z.number(),
 100 | 			totalAppointments: z.number(),
 101 | 			pendingAppointments: z.number(),
 102 | 			completedAppointments: z.number(),
 103 | 			revenue: z.number().optional()
 104 | 		})
 105 | 	)
 106 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 107 | 		// Check if user has access to this clinic
 108 | 		const member = await db.query.clinicMembers.findFirst({
 109 | 			where: and(eq(clinicMembers.clinicId, input.clinicId), eq(clinicMembers.userId, context.user?.id ?? ""))
 110 | 		});
 111 |
 112 | 		if (!member && context.user?.role !== "admin") {
 113 | 			throw new Error("Access denied");
 114 | 		}
 115 |
 116 | 		const stats = await db.transaction(async tx => {
 117 | 			const totalPatients = await tx.$count(
 118 | 				patient,
 119 | 				and(eq(patient.clinicId, input.clinicId), eq(patient.isDeleted, false))
 120 | 			);
 121 |
 122 | 			const totalDoctors = await tx.$count(
 123 | 				doctor,
 124 | 				and(eq(doctor.clinicId, input.clinicId), eq(doctor.isDeleted, false))
 125 | 			);
 126 |
 127 | 			const totalAppointments = await tx.$count(appointment, eq(appointment.clinicId, input.clinicId));
 128 |
 129 | 			const pendingAppointments = await tx.$count(
 130 | 				appointment,
 131 | 				and(eq(appointment.clinicId, input.clinicId), eq(appointment.status, "PENDING"))
 132 | 			);
 133 |
 134 | 			const completedAppointments = await tx.$count(
 135 | 				appointment,
 136 | 				and(eq(appointment.clinicId, input.clinicId), eq(appointment.status, "COMPLETED"))
 137 | 			);
 138 |
 139 | 			return {
 140 | 				totalPatients,
 141 | 				totalDoctors,
 142 | 				totalAppointments,
 143 | 				pendingAppointments,
 144 | 				completedAppointments
 145 | 			};
 146 | 		});
 147 |
 148 | 		return stats;
 149 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/dashboard.ts`:

```ts

   3 | import { z } from "zod";
   4 |

  95 | 		description: string;
  96 | 		user: string;
  97 | 		timestamp: string;
  98 | 	}>;
  99 | }

 101 | // Base dashboard procedure (extends auth)
 102 | export const dashboardProcedure = protectedProcedure;
 103 |
 104 | // ============================================
 105 | // Main Dashboard Stats

 108 | export const getDashboardStats = dashboardProcedure.handler(async ({ context }): Promise<DashboardStats> => {
 109 | 	const { db, user: ctxUser, clinicId } = context;
 110 | 	if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic ID is required");
 111 |
 112 | 	const today = new Date();
 113 | 	today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
 114 |
 115 | 	const startOfMonth = new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth(), 1);
 116 | 	const startOfWeek = new Date(today);
 117 | 	startOfWeek.setDate(today.getDate() - today.getDay());
 118 | 	startOfWeek.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
 119 |
 120 | 	const endOfWeek = new Date(startOfWeek);
 121 | 	endOfWeek.setDate(startOfWeek.getDate() + 6);
 122 | 	endOfWeek.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
 123 |
 124 | 	console.debug("Fetching dashboard stats", {
 125 | 		clinicId,
 126 | 		userId: ctxUser?.id,
 127 | 		dateRange: { startOfWeek, endOfWeek, startOfMonth }
 128 | 	});
 129 |
 130 | 	try {
 131 | 		// Run all queries in parallel with proper error handling
 132 | 		const [
 133 | 			// Overview stats
 134 | 			totalPatients,
 135 | 			totalAppointments,
 136 | 			activeDoctors,
 137 | 			activeStaff,
 138 |
 139 | 			// Today's stats
 140 | 			todayAppointments,
 141 | 			todayAppointmentsList,
 142 | 			todayRevenue,
 143 | 			todayCompletedAppointments,
 144 | 			todayCancelledAppointments,
 145 |
 146 | 			// Weekly stats
 147 | 			weeklyAppointments,
 148 | 			weeklyNewPatients,
 149 | 			weeklyRevenue,
 150 | 			weeklyAppointmentsByDay,
 151 | 			weeklyTopDoctors,
 152 |
 153 | 			// Monthly stats
 154 | 			monthlyAppointments,
 155 | 			monthlyNewPatients,
 156 | 			monthlyRevenue,
 157 | 			monthlyRevenueByWeek,
 158 | 			monthlyPopularServices,
 159 | 			monthlyAvgWaitTime,
```
