 161 | 			// Alerts & activity
 162 | 			pendingPrescriptions,
 163 | 			dueImmunizations,
 164 | 			lowStockItems,
 165 | 			expiringLicenses,
 166 | 			recentActivity
 167 | 		] = await Promise.all([
 168 | 			// Overview
 169 | 			db.$count(patient, and(eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId), eq(patient.isDeleted, false))),
 170 | 			db.$count(appointment, and(eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId), eq(appointment.isDeleted, false))),
 171 | 			db.$count(doctor, and(eq(doctor.clinicId, clinicId), eq(doctor.isDeleted, false))),
 172 | 			db.$count(staff, and(eq(staff.clinicId, clinicId), eq(staff.isActive, true))),
 173 |
 174 | 			// Today's appointments count
 175 | 			db.$count(
 176 | 				appointment,
 177 | 				and(
 178 | 					eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 179 | 					eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 180 | 					gte(appointment.appointmentDate, today),
 181 | 					lt(appointment.appointmentDate, new Date(today.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)),
 182 | 					notInArray(appointment.status, ["CANCELLED", "COMPLETED", "NO_SHOW"])
 183 | 				)
 184 | 			),
 185 |
 186 | 			// Today's appointments list
 187 | 			db.query.appointment.findMany({
 188 | 				where: and(
 189 | 					eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 190 | 					eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 191 | 					gte(appointment.appointmentDate, today),
 192 | 					lt(appointment.appointmentDate, new Date(today.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)),
 193 | 					notInArray(appointment.status, ["CANCELLED", "COMPLETED", "NO_SHOW"])
 194 | 				),
 195 | 				with: {
 196 | 					patient: {
 197 | 						with: { user: true }
 198 | 					},
 199 | 					doctor: {
 200 | 						with: { user: true }
 201 | 					}
 202 | 				},
 203 | 				orderBy: [asc(appointment.appointmentDate)],
 204 | 				limit: 20
 205 | 			}),
 206 |
 207 | 			// Today's revenue
 208 | 			db
 209 | 				.select({ sum: sql<number>`sum(${payment.amountPaid})` })
 210 | 				.from(payment)
 211 | 				.where(
 212 | 					and(
 213 | 						eq(payment.clinicId, clinicId),
 214 | 						eq(payment.status, "PAID"),
 215 | 						gte(payment.paymentDate, today),
 216 | 						lt(payment.paymentDate, new Date(today.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000))
 217 | 					)
 218 | 				),
 219 |
 220 | 			// Today's completed appointments
 221 | 			db.$count(
 222 | 				appointment,
 223 | 				and(
 224 | 					eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 225 | 					eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 226 | 					gte(appointment.appointmentDate, today),
 227 | 					lt(appointment.appointmentDate, new Date(today.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)),
 228 | 					eq(appointment.status, "COMPLETED")
 229 | 				)
 230 | 			),
 231 |
 232 | 			// Today's cancelled appointments
 233 | 			db.$count(
 234 | 				appointment,
 235 | 				and(
 236 | 					eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 237 | 					eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 238 | 					gte(appointment.appointmentDate, today),
 239 | 					lt(appointment.appointmentDate, new Date(today.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)),
 240 | 					eq(appointment.status, "CANCELLED")
 241 | 				)
 242 | 			),
 243 |
 244 | 			// Weekly appointments count
 245 | 			db.$count(
 246 | 				appointment,
 247 | 				and(
 248 | 					eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 249 | 					eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 250 | 					gte(appointment.appointmentDate, startOfWeek),
 251 | 					lte(appointment.appointmentDate, endOfWeek)
 252 | 				)
 253 | 			),
 254 |
 255 | 			// Weekly new patients
 256 | 			db.$count(
 257 | 				patient,
 258 | 				and(
 259 | 					eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId),
 260 | 					eq(patient.isDeleted, false),
 261 | 					gte(patient.createdAt, startOfWeek),
 262 | 					lte(patient.createdAt, endOfWeek)
 263 | 				)
 264 | 			),
 265 |
 266 | 			// Weekly revenue
 267 | 			db
 268 | 				.select({ sum: sql<number>`sum(${payment.amountPaid})` })
 269 | 				.from(payment)
 270 | 				.where(
 271 | 					and(
 272 | 						eq(payment.clinicId, clinicId),
 273 | 						eq(payment.status, "PAID"),
 274 | 						gte(payment.paymentDate, startOfWeek),
 275 | 						lte(payment.paymentDate, endOfWeek)
 276 | 					)
 277 | 				),
 278 |
 279 | 			// Weekly appointments by day
 280 | 			db
 281 | 				.select({
 282 | 					day_of_week: sql<number>`strftime('%w', ${appointment.appointmentDate})`,
 283 | 					date: sql<string>`date(${appointment.appointmentDate})`,
 284 | 					count: sql<number>`count(*)`
 285 | 				})
 286 | 				.from(appointment)
 287 | 				.where(
 288 | 					and(
 289 | 						eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 290 | 						eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 291 | 						between(appointment.appointmentDate, startOfWeek, endOfWeek)
 292 | 					)
 293 | 				)
 294 | 				.groupBy(sql`date(${appointment.appointmentDate})`),
 295 |
 296 | 			// Weekly top doctors
 297 | 			db.query.doctor.findMany({
 298 | 				where: and(eq(doctor.clinicId, clinicId), eq(doctor.isDeleted, false)),
 299 | 				with: {
 300 | 					appointments: true
 301 | 					// Note: Complex aggregations like top doctors are better handled with custom SQL in Drizzle
 302 | 				},
 303 | 				limit: 5
 304 | 			}),
 305 |
 306 | 			// Monthly appointments count
 307 | 			db.$count(
 308 | 				appointment,
 309 | 				and(
 310 | 					eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 311 | 					eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 312 | 					gte(appointment.createdAt, startOfMonth)
 313 | 				)
 314 | 			),
 315 |
 316 | 			// Monthly new patients
 317 | 			db.$count(
 318 | 				patient,
 319 | 				and(eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId), eq(patient.isDeleted, false), gte(patient.createdAt, startOfMonth))
 320 | 			),
 321 |
 322 | 			// Monthly revenue
 323 | 			db
 324 | 				.select({ sum: sql<number>`sum(${payment.amountPaid})` })
 325 | 				.from(payment)
 326 | 				.where(
 327 | 					and(
 328 | 						eq(payment.clinicId, clinicId),
 329 | 						eq(payment.status, "PAID"),
 330 | 						gte(payment.paymentDate, startOfMonth)
 331 | 					)
 332 | 				),
 333 |
 334 | 			// Monthly revenue by week
 335 | 			db
 336 | 				.select({
 337 | 					week: sql<number>`strftime('%W', ${payment.paymentDate})`,
 338 | 					revenue: sql<number>`sum(${payment.amountPaid})`,
 339 | 					payment_count: sql<number>`count(*)`
 340 | 				})
 341 | 				.from(payment)
 342 | 				.where(
 343 | 					and(
 344 | 						eq(payment.clinicId, clinicId),
 345 | 						eq(payment.status, "PAID"),
 346 | 						gte(payment.paymentDate, startOfMonth)
 347 | 					)
 348 | 				)
 349 | 				.groupBy(sql`strftime('%W', ${payment.paymentDate})`)
 350 | 				.orderBy(asc(sql`strftime('%W', ${payment.paymentDate})`)),
 351 |
 352 | 			// Monthly popular services
 353 | 			db.query.service.findMany({
 354 | 				where: and(eq(service.clinicId, clinicId), eq(service.isDeleted, false)),
 355 | 				limit: 5
 356 | 			}),
 357 |
 358 | 			// Average wait time (in minutes) - using appointment data
 359 | 			db
 360 | 				.select({
 361 | 					avg_wait_time: sql<number>`avg((julianday(${appointment.updatedAt}/1000, 'unixepoch') - julianday(${appointment.appointmentDate}/1000, 'unixepoch')) * 1440)`
 362 | 				})
 363 | 				.from(appointment)
 364 | 				.where(
 365 | 					and(
 366 | 						eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId),
 367 | 						eq(appointment.status, "COMPLETED"),
 368 | 						gte(appointment.createdAt, startOfMonth)
 369 | 					)
 370 | 				),
 371 |
 372 | 			// Alerts
 373 | 			db.$count(
 374 | 				prescription,
 375 | 				and(
 376 | 					eq(prescription.clinicId, clinicId),
 377 | 					eq(prescription.status, "active"),
 378 | 					lt(prescription.endDate, new Date())
 379 | 				)
 380 | 			),
 381 |
 382 | 			db.$count(
 383 | 				immunization,
 384 | 				and(
 385 | 					eq(immunization.clinicId, clinicId),
 386 | 					eq(immunization.status, "PENDING"),
 387 | 					eq(immunization.isOverDue, true)
 388 | 				)
 389 | 			),
 390 |
 391 | 			db.$count(
 392 | 				prescribedItem,
 393 | 				and(eq(prescribedItem.clinicId, clinicId), sql`${prescribedItem.frequency} <= "Three Times"`)
 394 | 			), // Example logic
 395 |
 396 | 			db.$count(
 397 | 				clinics,
 398 | 				and(eq(clinics.id, clinicId), lt(clinics.updatedAt, new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)))
 399 | 			),
 400 |
 401 | 			// Recent activity (simplified)
 402 | 			db.query.appointment.findMany({
 403 | 				where: and(eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId), eq(appointment.isDeleted, false)),
 404 | 				with: {
 405 | 					patient: {
 406 | 						with: { user: true }
 407 | 					},
 408 | 					doctor: {
 409 | 						with: { user: true }
 410 | 					}
 411 | 				},
 412 | 				orderBy: [desc(appointment.createdAt)],
 413 | 				limit: 10
 414 | 			})
 415 | 		]);
 416 |
 417 | 		// Process data into structured format
 418 | 		const occupancyRate =
 419 | 			totalAppointments > 0 && activeDoctors > 0
 420 | 				? Math.min(100, Math.round((todayAppointments / (activeDoctors * 8)) * 100))
 421 | 				: 0;
 422 |
 423 | 		// Format today's appointments
 424 | 		const formattedTodayAppointments = todayAppointmentsList.map(apt => ({
 425 | 			id: apt.id,
 426 | 			time: apt.appointmentDate.toLocaleTimeString(),
 427 | 			patientName:
 428 | 				apt.patient?.user?.name ||
 429 | 				(apt.patient ? `${apt.patient.firstName} ${apt.patient.lastName}` : "Unknown"),
 430 | 			doctorName: apt.doctor?.user?.name || apt.doctor?.name || "Unknown",
 431 | 			status: apt.status || "SCHEDULED"
 432 | 		}));
 433 |
 434 | 		// Process weekly appointments by day
 435 | 		const weeklyAppointmentsByDayFormatted = (
 436 | 			weeklyAppointmentsByDay as Array<{ day_of_week: number; count: number; date: string }>
 437 | 		).map(item => ({
 438 | 			day: ["Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat"][item.day_of_week],
 439 | 			count: Number(item.count),
 440 | 			date: item.date
 441 | 		}));
 442 |
 443 | 		// Process weekly top doctors
 444 | 		const formattedTopDoctors = weeklyTopDoctors.map(doctor => ({
 445 | 			id: doctor.id,
 446 | 			name: doctor.name,
 447 | 			appointmentCount: doctor.appointments.length,
 448 | 			revenue: 0 // Simplified for migration
 449 | 		}));
 450 |
 451 | 		// Process monthly revenue trend
 452 | 		const revenueTrend = (
 453 | 			monthlyRevenueByWeek as unknown as Array<{ week: number; revenue: number; payment_count: number }>
 454 | 		).map(item => ({
 455 | 			week: Number(item.week),
 456 | 			revenue: Number(item.revenue),
 457 | 			appointments: Number(item.payment_count)
 458 | 		}));
 459 |
 460 | 		// Process monthly popular services
 461 | 		const formattedPopularServices = monthlyPopularServices.map(service => ({
 462 | 			id: service.id,
 463 | 			name: service.serviceName,
 464 | 			count: 0,
 465 | 			revenue: 0
 466 | 		}));
 467 |
 468 | 		// Create alerts
 469 | 		const alerts = [];
 470 |
 471 | 		if (pendingPrescriptions > 0) {
 472 | 			alerts.push({
 473 | 				id: "pending-prescriptions",
 474 | 				type: "warning" as const,
 475 | 				title: "Expiring Prescriptions",
 476 | 				message: `${pendingPrescriptions} prescriptions are about to expire or have already expired.`,
 477 | 				timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
 478 | 				action: {
 479 | 					label: "Review Prescriptions",
 480 | 					href: "/prescriptions/pending"
 481 | 				}
 482 | 			});
 483 | 		}
 484 |
 485 | 		if (dueImmunizations > 0) {
 486 | 			alerts.push({
 487 | 				id: "due-immunizations",
 488 | 				type: "error" as const,
 489 | 				title: "Overdue Immunizations",
 490 | 				message: `${dueImmunizations} immunizations are overdue.`,
 491 | 				timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
 492 | 				action: {
 493 | 					label: "View Immunizations",
 494 | 					href: "/immunizations/due"
 495 | 				}
 496 | 			});
 497 | 		}
 498 |
 499 | 		if (lowStockItems > 0) {
 500 | 			alerts.push({
 501 | 				id: "low-stock",
 502 | 				type: "warning" as const,
 503 | 				title: "Low Stock Alert",
 504 | 				message: `${lowStockItems} inventory items are below reorder point.`,
 505 | 				timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
 506 | 				action: {
 507 | 					label: "Review Inventory",
 508 | 					href: "/inventory/low-stock"
 509 | 				}
 510 | 			});
 511 | 		}
 512 |
 513 | 		if (expiringLicenses > 0) {
 514 | 			alerts.push({
 515 | 				id: "expiring-licenses",
 516 | 				type: "info" as const,
 517 | 				title: "License Expiring Soon",
 518 | 				message: `${expiringLicenses} clinic licenses will expire within 30 days.`,
 519 | 				timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
 520 | 				action: {
 521 | 					label: "Manage Licenses",
 522 | 					href: "/settings/licenses"
 523 | 				}
 524 | 			});
 525 | 		}
 526 |
 527 | 		// Process recent activity
 528 | 		const formattedRecentActivity = recentActivity.map(activity => ({
 529 | 			id: activity.id,
 530 | 			type: "appointment" as const,
 531 | 			action: mapActionToActivity(activity.status || "updated"),
 532 | 			description: `Appointment for ${activity.patient?.user?.name || "Patient"} with Dr. ${activity.doctor?.user?.name || "Doctor"}`,
 533 | 			user: activity.doctor?.user?.name || "System",
 534 | 			timestamp: activity.updatedAt.toISOString()
 535 | 		}));
 536 |
 537 | 		console.info("Dashboard stats fetched successfully", {
 538 | 			clinicId,
 539 | 			totalPatients,
 540 | 			todayAppointments,
 541 | 			monthlyRevenue: monthlyRevenue[0]?.sum || 0
 542 | 		});
 543 |
 544 | 		return {
 545 | 			overview: {
 546 | 				totalPatients,
 547 | 				totalAppointments,
 548 | 				activeDoctors,
 549 | 				activeStaff,
 550 | 				occupancyRate,
 551 | 				// Add these helper fields to make frontend access cleaner
 552 | 				pendingPrescriptionsCount: pendingPrescriptions,
 553 | 				lowStockItemsCount: lowStockItems
 554 | 			},
 555 | 			today: {
 556 | 				appointments: todayAppointments,
 557 | 				appointmentsList: formattedTodayAppointments,
 558 | 				revenue: toNumber(todayRevenue[0]?.sum) || 0,
 559 | 				completedAppointments: todayCompletedAppointments,
 560 | 				cancelledAppointments: todayCancelledAppointments
 561 | 			},
 562 | 			weekly: {
 563 | 				appointmentsCount: weeklyAppointments,
 564 | 				appointmentsByDay: weeklyAppointmentsByDayFormatted,
 565 | 				newPatientsCount: weeklyNewPatients,
 566 | 				revenue: toNumber(weeklyRevenue[0]?.sum) || 0,
 567 | 				topDoctors: formattedTopDoctors
 568 | 			},
 569 | 			monthly: {
 570 | 				appointmentsCount: monthlyAppointments,
 571 | 				newPatientsCount: monthlyNewPatients,
 572 | 				revenue: toNumber(monthlyRevenue[0]?.sum) || 0,
 573 | 				revenueTrend,
 574 | 				averageWaitTime: Math.round(
 575 | 					(monthlyAvgWaitTime as unknown as Array<{ avg_wait_time: number }>)[0]?.avg_wait_time || 0
 576 | 				),
 577 | 				patientSatisfaction: calculatePatientSatisfaction(), // Implement based on your survey data
 578 | 				popularServices: formattedPopularServices
 579 | 			},
 580 | 			alerts,
 581 | 			recentActivity: formattedRecentActivity
 582 | 		};
 583 | 	} catch (error) {
 584 | 		console.error("Failed to fetch dashboard stats", { error });
 585 | 		throw error;
 586 | 	}
 587 | });
 588 |
 589 | // ============================================
 590 | // Helper Functions
 591 | // ============================================
 592 |
 593 | // ============================================
 594 | // Specialized Dashboard Procedures
 595 | // ============================================
 596 |
 597 | export const getRecentAppointments = dashboardProcedure
 598 | 	.input(
 599 | 		z.object({
 600 | 			limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(10),
 601 | 			status: z.enum(["SCHEDULED", "COMPLETED", "CANCELLED", "NO_SHOW"]).optional()
 602 | 		})
 603 | 	)
 604 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 605 | 		const { db, clinicId } = context;
 606 |
 607 | 		const appointments = await db.query.appointment.findMany({
 608 | 			where: and(
 609 | 				eq(appointment.clinicId, clinicId ?? ""),
 610 | 				eq(appointment.isDeleted, false),
 611 | 				input.status ? eq(appointment.status, input.status) : undefined
 612 | 			),
 613 | 			with: {
 614 | 				patient: {
 615 | 					with: { user: true }
 616 | 				},
 617 | 				doctor: {
 618 | 					with: { user: true }
 619 | 				}
 620 | 			} as const,
 621 | 			orderBy: [desc(appointment.appointmentDate)],
 622 | 			limit: input.limit
 623 | 		});
 624 |
 625 | 		return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(appointments)) as typeof appointments;
 626 | 	});
 627 |
 628 | export const getRevenueOverview = dashboardProcedure
 629 | 	.input(
 630 | 		z.object({
 631 | 			startDate: z.coerce.date(),
 632 | 			endDate: z.coerce.date(),
 633 | 			groupBy: z.enum(["day", "week", "month"]).default("day")
 634 | 		})
 635 | 	)
 636 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 637 | 		const { db, clinicId } = context;
 638 | 		const { startDate, endDate, groupBy } = input;
 639 |
 640 | 		let groupByClause: string;
 641 | 		switch (groupBy) {
 642 | 			case "day":
 643 | 				groupByClause = "date(payment_date/1000, 'unixepoch')";
 644 | 				break;
 645 | 			case "week":
 646 | 				groupByClause = "strftime('%W', payment_date/1000, 'unixepoch')";
 647 | 				break;
 648 | 			case "month":
 649 | 				groupByClause = "strftime('%m', payment_date/1000, 'unixepoch')";
 650 | 				break;
 651 | 		}
 652 |
 653 | 		const revenue = await db
 654 | 			.select({
 655 | 				period: sql.raw(groupByClause),
 656 | 				total: sql<number>`sum(${payment.amountPaid})`,
 657 | 				count: sql<number>`count(*)`,
 658 | 				average: sql<number>`avg(${payment.amountPaid})`
 659 | 			})
 660 | 			.from(payment)
 661 | 			.where(
 662 | 				and(
 663 | 					eq(payment.clinicId, clinicId ?? ""),
 664 | 					eq(payment.status, "PAID"),
 665 | 					between(payment.paymentDate, startDate, endDate)
 666 | 				)
 667 | 			)
 668 | 			.groupBy(sql.raw(groupByClause));
 669 |
 670 | 		const totalRevenue = await db
 671 | 			.select({
 672 | 				sum: sql<number>`sum(${payment.amountPaid})`,
 673 | 				count: sql<number>`count(*)`
 674 | 			})
 675 | 			.from(payment)
 676 | 			.where(
 677 | 				and(
 678 | 					eq(payment.clinicId, clinicId ?? ""),
 679 | 					eq(payment.status, "PAID"),
 680 | 					gte(payment.paymentDate, startDate),
 681 | 					lte(payment.paymentDate, endDate)
 682 | 				)
 683 | 			);
 684 |
 685 | 		return {
 686 | 			revenue: revenue,
 687 | 			summary: {
 688 | 				total: totalRevenue[0]?.sum || 0,
 689 | 				transactionCount: totalRevenue[0]?.count || 0,
 690 | 				averageTransaction:
 691 | 					(totalRevenue[0]?.count || 0) > 0 ? (totalRevenue[0]?.sum || 0) / totalRevenue[0]?.count : 0
 692 | 			}
 693 | 		};
 694 | 	});
 695 |
 696 | export const getPatientDemographics = dashboardProcedure.handler(async ({ context }) => {
 697 | 	const { db, clinicId } = context;
 698 |
 699 | 	const [ageGroups, genderDistribution, locations] = await Promise.all([
 700 | 		db
 701 | 			.select({
 702 | 				age_group: sql<string>`CASE
 703 | 				WHEN (strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', ${patient.dateOfBirth})) < 18 THEN '0-17'
 704 | 				WHEN (strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', ${patient.dateOfBirth})) BETWEEN 18 AND 30 THEN '18-30'
 705 | 				WHEN (strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', ${patient.dateOfBirth})) BETWEEN 31 AND 50 THEN '31-50'
 706 | 				WHEN (strftime('%Y', 'now') - strftime('%Y', ${patient.dateOfBirth})) BETWEEN 51 AND 70 THEN '51-70'
 707 | 				ELSE '70+'
 708 | 			END`,
 709 | 				count: sql<number>`count(*)`
 710 | 			})
 711 | 			.from(patient)
 712 | 			.where(and(eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId ?? ""), eq(patient.isDeleted, false)))
 713 | 			.groupBy(sql`age_group`),
 714 | 		db
 715 | 			.select({ gender: patient.gender, count: sql`count(*)` })
 716 | 			.from(patient)
 717 | 			.where(and(eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId ?? ""), eq(patient.isDeleted, false)))
 718 | 			.groupBy(patient.gender),
 719 | 		db
 720 | 			.select({ address: patient.address, count: sql`count(*)` })
 721 | 			.from(patient)
 722 | 			.where(and(eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId ?? ""), eq(patient.isDeleted, false), ne(patient.address, "")))
 723 | 			.groupBy(patient.address)
 724 | 			.orderBy(desc(sql`count(*)`))
 725 | 			.limit(10)
 726 | 	]);
 727 |
 728 | 	return {
 729 | 		ageGroups: ageGroups,
 730 | 		genderDistribution,
 731 | 		topLocations: locations
 732 | 	};
 733 | });
 734 |
 735 | export const getDoctorPerformance = dashboardProcedure
 736 | 	.input(
 737 | 		z.object({
 738 | 			period: z.enum(["week", "month", "year"]).default("month"),
 739 | 			doctorId: z.string().optional()
 740 | 		})
 741 | 	)
 742 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 743 | 		const { db, clinicId } = context;
 744 |
 745 | 		// Safety check for clinic context
 746 | 		if (!clinicId) {
 747 | 			throw new Error("Clinic context not found");
 748 | 		}
 749 |
 750 | 		const { period, doctorId } = input;
 751 |
 752 | 		const startDate = new Date();
 753 | 		switch (period) {
 754 | 			case "week":
 755 | 				startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - 7);
 756 | 				break;
 757 | 			case "month":
 758 | 				startDate.setMonth(startDate.getMonth() - 1);
 759 | 				break;
 760 | 			case "year":
 761 | 				startDate.setFullYear(startDate.getFullYear() - 1);
 762 | 				break;
 763 | 		}
 764 |
 765 | 		const doctorsList = await db.query.doctor.findMany({
 766 | 			where: and(
 767 | 				eq(doctor.clinicId, clinicId),
 768 | 				eq(doctor.isDeleted, false),
 769 | 				doctorId ? eq(doctor.id, doctorId) : undefined
 770 | 			),
 771 | 			with: {
 772 | 				appointments: {
 773 | 					where: gte(appointment.appointmentDate, startDate)
 774 | 				}
 775 | 			}
 776 | 		});
 777 |
 778 | 		const performance = doctorsList.map(doctor => ({
 779 | 			id: doctor.id,
 780 | 			name: doctor.name,
 781 | 			specialization: doctor.specialty,
 782 | 			totalAppointments: doctor.appointments.length,
 783 | 			totalRevenue: 0, // Simplified
 784 | 			averageRating: 0,
 785 | 			reviewCount: 0,
 786 | 			uniquePatients: new Set(doctor.appointments.map(a => a.patientId)).size,
 787 | 			completionRate: 0
 788 | 		}));
 789 |
 790 | 		return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(performance));
 791 | 	});

 100 | // Procedures
 101 |
 102 | // Create doctor
 103 | export const createDoctor = protectedProcedure
 104 | 	.input(DoctorsCreateSchema)
 105 | 	.output(DoctorSchema)
 106 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 107 | 		const { headers } = context;
 108 |
 109 | 		// Get user from headers (assuming auth middleware sets user)
 110 | 		const userId = headers.get("x-user-id");
 111 | 		if (!userId) {
 112 | 			throw new Error("Unauthorized");
 113 | 		}
 114 |
 115 | 		const userRecord = await db.query.user.findFirst({
 116 | 			where: eq(user.id, userId)
 117 | 		});
 118 |
 119 | 		if (
 120 | 			!userRecord ||
 121 | 			(userRecord.role !== "admin" && userRecord.role !== "superadmin" && userRecord.role !== "staff")
 122 | 		) {
 123 | 			throw new Error("Forbidden: Only admin/staff can create doctors");
 124 | 		}
 125 |
 126 | 		let clinicId = input.clinicId;
 127 | 		if (!clinicId && userRecord.clinicId) {
 128 | 			clinicId = userRecord.clinicId;
 129 | 		}
 130 |
 131 | 		const [newDoctor] = await db
 132 | 			.insert(doctor)
 133 | 			.values({
 134 | 				...input,
 135 | 				clinicId: clinicId || null,
 136 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 137 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 138 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 139 | 			})
 140 | 			.returning();
 141 |
 142 | 		return newDoctor;
 143 | 	});

 223 | export const permanentlyDeleteDoctor = protectedProcedure
 224 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))

 236 | // Get available doctors for a specific date
 237 | export const getAvailableDoctors = protectedProcedure
 238 | 	.input(
 239 | 		z.object({
 240 | 			date: z.date(),

 242 | 			clinicId: z.string().optional()

 250 |
 251 | 		if (input.clinicId) {
 252 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(doctor.clinicId, input.clinicId));
 253 | 		}

 276 | 						name: true
 277 | 					}
 278 | 				}

 298 | // Add working day to doctor schedule
 299 | export const addWorkingDay = protectedProcedure
 300 | 	.input(
 301 | 		z.object({
 302 | 			doctorId: z.string(),
 303 | 			day: z.string(),
 304 | 			startTime: z.string().regex(/^([0-1]?[0-9]|2[0-3]):[0-5][0-9]$/),
 305 | 			endTime: z.string().regex(/^([0-1]?[0-9]|2[0-3]):[0-5][0-9]$/)
 306 | 		})
 307 | 	)
 308 | 	.output(WorkingDaySchema)
 309 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 310 | 		// Check if working day already exists for this doctor and day
 311 | 		const existing = await db.query.workingDays.findFirst({
 312 | 			where: and(eq(workingDays.doctorId, input.doctorId), eq(workingDays.day, input.day))
 313 | 		});
 314 |
 315 | 		if (existing) {
 316 | 			// Update existing
 317 | 			const [updated] = await db
 318 | 				.update(workingDays)
 319 | 				.set({
 320 | 					startTime: input.startTime,
 321 | 					endTime: input.endTime,
 322 | 					updatedAt: new Date()
 323 | 				})
 324 | 				.where(eq(workingDays.id, existing.id))
 325 | 				.returning();
 326 | 			return updated;
 327 | 		}

 330 | 		const [newWorkingDay] = await db
 331 | 			.insert(workingDays)
 332 | 			.values({
 333 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 334 | 				doctorId: input.doctorId,
 335 | 				day: input.day,
 336 | 				startTime: input.startTime,

 338 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 339 | 				updatedAt: new Date()

 355 | // List doctors with pagination and filters
 356 | export const listDoctors = protectedProcedure
 357 | 	.input(
 358 | 		z.object({
 359 | 			page: z.number().min(1).default(1),
 360 | 			limit: z.number().min(1).max(100).default(10),
 361 | 			search: z.string().optional(),
 362 | 			status: z.string().optional(),
 363 | 			isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
 364 | 			specialty: z.string().optional(),
 365 | 			clinicId: z.string().optional(),
 366 | 			sortBy: z.enum(["name", "createdAt", "rating", "specialty"]).default("createdAt"),
 367 | 			sortOrder: z.enum(["asc", "desc"]).default("desc")
 368 | 		})
 369 | 	)
 370 | 	.output(
 371 | 		z.object({
 372 | 			doctors: z.array(DoctorWithScheduleSchema),
 373 | 			total: z.number(),
 374 | 			page: z.number(),
 375 | 			limit: z.number(),
 376 | 			totalPages: z.number()
 377 | 		})
 378 | 	)
 379 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 380 | 		const { page, limit, search, status, isActive, specialty, clinicId, sortBy, sortOrder } = input;
 381 | 		const offset = (page - 1) * limit;
 382 |
 383 | 		let whereClause = and(eq(doctor.isDeleted, false));
 384 |
 385 | 		if (clinicId) {
 386 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(doctor.clinicId, clinicId));
 387 | 		}
 388 |
 389 | 		if (search) {
 390 | 			whereClause = and(
 391 | 				whereClause,
 392 | 				or(
 393 | 					like(doctor.name, `%${search}%`),
 394 | 					like(doctor.specialty, `%${search}%`),
 395 | 					like(doctor.phone, `%${search}%`),
 396 | 					like(doctor.email, `%${search}%`),
 397 | 					like(doctor.department, `%${search}%`)
 398 | 				)
 399 | 			);
 400 | 		}
 401 |
 402 | 		if (status) {
 403 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(doctor.status, status));
 404 | 		}
 405 |
 406 | 		if (isActive !== undefined) {
 407 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(doctor.isActive, isActive));
 408 | 		}
 409 |
 410 | 		if (specialty) {
 411 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(doctor.specialty, specialty));
 412 | 		}
 413 |
 414 | 		const sortColumn =
 415 | 			sortBy === "name"
 416 | 				? doctor.name
 417 | 				: sortBy === "rating"
 418 | 					? doctor.rating
 419 | 					: sortBy === "specialty"
 420 | 						? doctor.specialty
 421 | 						: doctor.createdAt;
 422 |
 423 | 		const orderBy = sortOrder === "asc" ? sortColumn : desc(sortColumn);
 424 |
 425 | 		const doctorsList = await db.query.doctor.findMany({
 426 | 			where: whereClause,
 427 | 			limit,
 428 | 			offset,
 429 | 			orderBy,
 430 | 			with: {
 431 | 				workingDays: true,
 432 | 				user: {
 433 | 					columns: {
 434 | 						id: true,
 435 | 						name: true,
 436 | 						email: true,
 437 | 						image: true
 438 | 					}
 439 | 				},
 440 | 				clinic: {
 441 | 					columns: {
 442 | 						id: true,
 443 | 						name: true
 444 | 					}
 445 | 				}
 446 | 			}
 447 | 		});
 448 |
 449 | 		const total = await db.$count(doctor, whereClause);
 450 |
 451 | 		return {
 452 | 			doctors: doctorsList,
 453 | 			total,
 454 | 			page,
 455 | 			limit,
 456 | 			totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
 457 | 		};
 458 | 	});
 459 |
 460 | // Get doctors by specialty
 461 | export const getDoctorsBySpecialty = protectedProcedure
 462 | 	.input(
 463 | 		z.object({
 464 | 			specialty: z.string(),
 465 | 			clinicId: z.string().optional()
 466 | 		})
 467 | 	)
 468 | 	.output(z.array(DoctorSchema))
 469 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 470 | 		let whereClause = and(
 471 | 			eq(doctor.specialty, input.specialty),
 472 | 			eq(doctor.isActive, true),
 473 | 			eq(doctor.isDeleted, false)
 474 | 		);
 475 |
 476 | 		if (input.clinicId) {
 477 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(doctor.clinicId, input.clinicId));
 478 | 		}
 479 |
 480 | 		const doctorsList = await db.query.doctor.findMany({
 481 | 			where: whereClause,
 482 | 			orderBy: doctor.name
 483 | 		});
 484 |
 485 | 		return doctorsList;
 486 | 	});
 487 |
 488 | // Get doctor statistics
 489 | export const getDoctorStatistics = protectedProcedure
 490 | 	.input(
 491 | 		z.object({
 492 | 			doctorId: z.string(),
 493 | 			startDate: z.date().optional(),
 494 | 			endDate: z.date().optional()
 495 | 		})
 496 | 	)
 497 | 	.output(
 498 | 		z.object({
 499 | 			totalAppointments: z.number(),
 500 | 			completedAppointments: z.number(),
 501 | 			cancelledAppointments: z.number(),
 502 | 			averageRating: z.number().nullable(),
 503 | 			totalPatients: z.number(),
 504 | 			totalRevenue: z.number(),
 505 | 			upcomingAppointments: z.number()
 506 | 		})
 507 | 	)
 508 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 509 | 		const { doctorId, startDate, endDate } = input;
 510 |
 511 | 		let appointmentFilter: SQL | undefined;
 512 |
 513 | 		if (startDate) {
 514 | 			appointmentFilter = and(appointmentFilter, sql`appointment_date >= ${startDate}`);
 515 | 		}
 516 |
 517 | 		if (endDate) {
 518 | 			appointmentFilter = and(appointmentFilter, sql`appointment_date <= ${endDate}`);
 519 | 		}
 520 |
 521 | 		const appointments = await db.query.appointment.findMany({
 522 | 			where: and(eq(sql`doctor_id`, doctorId), appointmentFilter, eq(sql`is_deleted`, false))
 523 | 		});
 524 |
 525 | 		const totalAppointments = appointments.length;
 526 | 		const completedAppointments = appointments.filter(a => a.status === "COMPLETED").length;
 527 | 		const cancelledAppointments = appointments.filter(a => a.status === "CANCELLED").length;
 528 |
 529 | 		const upcomingAppointments = appointments.filter(a => {
 530 | 			const appointmentDate = new Date(a.appointmentDate);
 531 | 			return appointmentDate > new Date() && (a.status === "SCHEDULED" || a.status === "PENDING");
 532 | 		}).length;
 533 |
 534 | 		const uniquePatients = new Set(appointments.map(a => a.patientId)).size;
 535 |
 536 | 		const totalRevenue = appointments
 537 | 			.filter(a => a.status === "COMPLETED" && a.appointmentPrice)
 538 | 			.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.appointmentPrice || 0), 0);
 539 |
 540 | 		const doctorRating = await db.query.doctor.findFirst({
 541 | 			where: eq(doctor.id, doctorId),
 542 | 			columns: { rating: true }
 543 | 		});
 544 |
 545 | 		return {
 546 | 			totalAppointments,
 547 | 			completedAppointments,
 548 | 			cancelledAppointments,
 549 | 			averageRating: doctorRating?.rating || null,
 550 | 			totalPatients: uniquePatients,
 551 | 			totalRevenue,
 552 | 			upcomingAppointments
 553 | 		};
 554 | 	});
 555 |
 556 | // Bulk update doctor status
 557 | export const bulkUpdateDoctorStatus = protectedProcedure
 558 | 	.input(
 559 | 		z.object({
 560 | 			doctorIds: z.array(z.string()),
 561 | 			isActive: z.boolean(),
 562 | 			status: z.enum(["ACTIVE", "INACTIVE", "DORMANT"]).optional()
 563 | 		})
 564 | 	)
 565 | 	.output(z.object({ updatedCount: z.number() }))
 566 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 567 | 		const updateData: Partial<typeof doctor.$inferInsert> = {
 568 | 			isActive: input.isActive,
 569 | 			updatedAt: new Date()
 570 | 		};
 571 |
 572 | 		if (input.status) {
 573 | 			updateData.status = input.status;
 574 | 		}
 575 |
 576 | 		const result = await db
 577 | 			.update(doctor)
 578 | 			.set(updateData)
 579 | 			.where(sql`id IN ${input.doctorIds}`)
 580 | 			.returning({ id: doctor.id });
 581 |
 582 | 		return { updatedCount: result.length };
 583 | 	});
 584 |
 585 | // Get unique specialties
 586 | export const getSpecialties = protectedProcedure
 587 | 	.input(
 588 | 		z.object({
 589 | 			clinicId: z.string().optional()
 590 | 		})
 591 | 	)
 592 | 	.output(z.array(z.string()))
 593 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 594 | 		let whereClause = and(eq(doctor.isDeleted, false));
 595 |
 596 | 		if (input.clinicId) {
 597 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(doctor.clinicId, input.clinicId));
 598 | 		}
 599 |
 600 | 		const result = await db
 601 | 			.selectDistinct({ specialty: doctor.specialty })
 602 | 			.from(doctor)
 603 | 			.where(whereClause)
 604 | 			.orderBy(doctor.specialty);
 605 |
 606 | 		return result.map(r => r.specialty);
 607 | 	});

```

 426 | 			...encounter,
 427 | 			appointment: encounter.appointment ? { ...encounter.appointment, status: encounter.appointment.status ?? "PENDING" } : undefined,
 428 | 			patient: encounter.patient ?? undefined,
 429 | 			doctor: encounter.doctor ?? undefined,
 430 | 			prescriptions: encounter.prescription.map(p => ({ ...p, status: p.status ?? "active" })),
 431 | 			vitalSigns: encounter.vitalSigns,
 432 | 			type: encounter.type ?? null
 433 | 		};
 434 | 	});
 435 |
 436 | // List encounters with filtering and pagination
 437 | export const listEncounters = os
 438 | 	.input(ListEncountersSchema)
 439 | 	.output(
 440 | 		z.object({
 441 | 			encounters: z.array(
 442 | 				DiagnosisSchema.extend({
 443 | 					patientName: z.string().optional(),
 444 | 					doctorName: z.string().optional()
 445 | 				})
 446 | 			),
 447 | 			total: z.number(),
 448 | 			page: z.number(),
 449 | 			pageSize: z.number(),
 450 | 			totalPages: z.number()
 451 | 		})
 452 | 	)
 453 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 454 | 		const conditions = and(
 455 | 			eq(diagnosis.isDeleted, false),
 456 | 			input.patientId ? eq(diagnosis.patientId, input.patientId) : undefined,
 457 | 			input.doctorId ? eq(diagnosis.doctorId, input.doctorId) : undefined,
 458 | 			input.appointmentId ? eq(diagnosis.appointmentId, input.appointmentId) : undefined,
 459 | 			input.status ? eq(diagnosis.status, input.status) : undefined,
 460 | 			input.dateFrom ? sql`${diagnosis.date} >= ${input.dateFrom}` : undefined,
 461 | 			input.dateTo ? sql`${diagnosis.date} <= ${input.dateTo}` : undefined,
 462 | 			input.searchTerm
 463 | 				? sql`(
 464 | 				${diagnosis.diagnosis} LIKE ${`%${input.searchTerm}%`} OR
 465 | 				${diagnosis.symptoms} LIKE ${`%${input.searchTerm}%`} OR
 466 | 				${diagnosis.treatment} LIKE ${`%${input.searchTerm}%`}
 467 | 			)`
 468 | 				: undefined
 469 | 		);
 470 |
 471 | 		const encounters = await db.query.diagnosis.findMany({
 472 | 			where: conditions,
 473 | 			limit: input.pageSize,
 474 | 			offset: (input.page - 1) * input.pageSize,
 475 | 			orderBy: desc(diagnosis.date),
 476 | 			with: {
 477 | 				patient: {
 478 | 					columns: {
 479 | 						firstName: true,
 480 | 						lastName: true
 481 | 					}
 482 | 				},
 483 | 				doctor: {
 484 | 					columns: {
 485 | 						name: true
 486 | 					}
 487 | 				}
 488 | 			}
 489 | 		});
 490 |
 491 | 		const total = await db.$count(diagnosis, conditions);
 492 |
 493 | 		const encountersWithNames = encounters.map(encounter => ({
 494 | 			...encounter,
 495 | 			patientName: encounter.patient ? `${encounter.patient.firstName} ${encounter.patient.lastName}` : undefined,
 496 | 			doctorName: encounter.doctor ? encounter.doctor.name : undefined
 497 | 		}));
 498 |
 499 | 		return {
 500 | 			encounters: encountersWithNames,
 501 | 			total,
 502 | 			page: input.page,
 503 | 			pageSize: input.pageSize,
 504 | 			totalPages: Math.ceil(total / input.pageSize)
 505 | 		};
 506 | 	});
 507 |
 508 | // Delete encounter (soft delete)
 509 | export const deleteEncounter = os
 510 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
 511 | 	.output(z.object({ success: z.boolean() }))
 512 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 513 | 		await db
 514 | 			.update(diagnosis)
 515 | 			.set({
 516 | 				isDeleted: true,
 517 | 				deletedAt: new Date(),
 518 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 519 | 			})
 520 | 			.where(eq(diagnosis.id, input.id));
 521 |
 522 | 		return { success: true };
 523 | 	});
 524 |
 525 | // Get encounters by date range
 526 | export const getEncountersByDateRange = os
 527 | 	.input(
 528 | 		z.object({
 529 | 			startDate: z.date(),
 530 | 			endDate: z.date(),
 531 | 			doctorId: z.string().optional(),
 532 | 			clinicId: z.string().optional()
 533 | 		})
 534 | 	)
 535 | 	.output(z.array(DiagnosisSchema))
 536 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 537 | 		const conditions = and(
 538 | 			eq(diagnosis.isDeleted, false),
 539 | 			sql`${diagnosis.date} BETWEEN ${input.startDate} AND ${input.endDate}`,
 540 | 			input.doctorId ? eq(diagnosis.doctorId, input.doctorId) : undefined,
 541 | 			input.clinicId ? eq(diagnosis.clinicId, input.clinicId) : undefined
 542 | 		);
 543 |
 544 | 		const encounters = await db.query.diagnosis.findMany({
 545 | 			where: conditions,
 546 | 			orderBy: desc(diagnosis.date)
 547 | 		});
 548 |
 549 | 		return encounters;
 550 | 	});
 551 |
 552 | // Get encounter statistics
 553 | export const getEncounterStats = os
 554 | 	.input(
 555 | 		z.object({
 556 | 			clinicId: z.string().optional(),
 557 | 			doctorId: z.string().optional(),
 558 | 			startDate: z.date().optional(),
 559 | 			endDate: z.date().optional()
 560 | 		})
 561 | 	)
 562 | 	.output(
 563 | 		z.object({
 564 | 			totalEncounters: z.number(),
 565 | 			activeEncounters: z.number(),
 566 | 			completedEncounters: z.number(),
 567 | 			avgPerDay: z.number(),
 568 | 			topDiagnosis: z.array(
 569 | 				z.object({
 570 | 					diagnosis: z.string(),
 571 | 					count: z.number()
 572 | 				})
 573 | 			),
 574 | 			encountersByType: z.array(
 575 | 				z.object({
 576 | 					type: z.string(),
 577 | 					count: z.number()
 578 | 				})
 579 | 			)
 580 | 		})
 581 | 	)
 582 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 583 | 		const conditions = and(
 584 | 			eq(diagnosis.isDeleted, false),
 585 | 			input.clinicId ? eq(diagnosis.clinicId, input.clinicId) : undefined,
 586 | 			input.doctorId ? eq(diagnosis.doctorId, input.doctorId) : undefined,
 587 | 			input.startDate ? sql`${diagnosis.date} >= ${input.startDate}` : undefined,
 588 | 			input.endDate ? sql`${diagnosis.date} <= ${input.endDate}` : undefined
 589 | 		);
 590 |
 591 | 		const allEncounters = await db.query.diagnosis.findMany({
 592 | 			where: conditions
 593 | 		});
 594 |
 595 | 		const totalEncounters = allEncounters.length;
 596 | 		const activeEncounters = allEncounters.filter(e => e.status === "ACTIVE").length;
 597 | 		const completedEncounters = allEncounters.filter(e => e.status === "COMPLETED").length;
 598 |
 599 | 		// Calculate average per day
 600 | 		const dates = allEncounters.map(e => e.date.toDateString());
 601 | 		const uniqueDates = new Set(dates);
 602 | 		const avgPerDay = uniqueDates.size > 0 ? totalEncounters / uniqueDates.size : 0;
 603 |
 604 | 		// Get top diagnoses
 605 | 		const diagnosisCounts = new Map<string, number>();
 606 | 		for (const encounter of allEncounters) {
 607 | 			if (encounter.diagnosis) {
 608 | 				diagnosisCounts.set(encounter.diagnosis, (diagnosisCounts.get(encounter.diagnosis) || 0) + 1);
 609 | 			}
 610 | 		}
 611 | 		const topDiagnosis = Array.from(diagnosisCounts.entries())
 612 | 			.map(([diagnosis, count]) => ({ diagnosis, count }))
 613 | 			.sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
 614 | 			.slice(0, 5);
 615 |
 616 | 		// Get encounters by type
 617 | 		const typeCounts = new Map<string, number>();
 618 | 		for (const diagnosis of allEncounters) {
 619 | 			typeCounts.set(diagnosis.type??"", (typeCounts.get(diagnosis.type??"") || 0) + 1);
 620 | 		}
 621 | 		const encountersByType = Array.from(typeCounts.entries()).map(([type, count]) => ({ type, count }));
 622 |
 623 | 		return {
 624 | 			totalEncounters,
 625 | 			activeEncounters,
 626 | 			completedEncounters,
 627 | 			avgPerDay,
 628 | 			topDiagnosis,
 629 | 			encountersByType
 630 | 		};
 631 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/growth.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // routers/growth.router.ts
   2 | import { and, between, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   3 | import { z } from "zod";
   4 |
   5 | import { db } from "../../db";
   6 | import { growthRecord, patient } from "../../db/schema";
   7 | import {
   8 | 	calculateAgeInDays,
   9 | 	calculateAgeInMonths,
  10 | 	calculateZScore,
  11 | 	getMeasurementValue,
  12 | 	getWHOStandard,
  13 | 	getZScoreValue
  14 | } from "../../utils/growth";
  15 | import { clinicProcedure } from "../init";
  16 |
  17 | // Get patient growth history
  18 | export const getPatientGrowthHistory = clinicProcedure
  19 | 	.input(
  20 | 		z.object({
  21 | 			patientId: z.string(),
  22 | 			limit: z.number().optional().default(50),
  23 | 			offset: z.number().optional().default(0)
  24 | 		})
  25 | 	)
  26 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  27 | 		const records = await db.query.growthRecord.findMany({
  28 | 			where: and(eq(growthRecord.patientId, input.patientId), eq(growthRecord.clinicId, context.clinicId)),
  29 | 			orderBy: (growthRecord, { desc }) => [desc(growthRecord.date)],
  30 | 			limit: input.limit,
  31 | 			offset: input.offset
  32 | 		});
  33 |
  34 | 		const total = await db.$count(
  35 | 			growthRecord,
  36 | 			and(eq(growthRecord.patientId, input.patientId), eq(growthRecord.clinicId, context.clinicId))
  37 | 		);
  38 |
  39 | 		return {
  40 | 			records,
  41 | 			total,
  42 | 			limit: input.limit,
  43 | 			offset: input.offset
  44 | 		};
  45 | 	});
  46 |
  47 | // Record growth measurement
  48 | export const recordGrowth = clinicProcedure
  49 | 	.input(
  50 | 		z.object({
  51 | 			patientId: z.string(),
  52 | 			date: z.date(),
  53 | 			weight: z.number().optional(),
  54 | 			height: z.number().optional(),
  55 | 			headCircumference: z.number().optional(),
  56 | 			notes: z.string().optional()
  57 | 		})
  58 | 	)
  59 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  60 | 		const patientData = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
  61 | 			where: eq(patient.id, input.patientId)
  62 | 		});
  63 |
  64 | 		if (!patientData) throw new Error("Patient not found");
  65 |
  66 | 		// Calculate age in months and days
  67 | 		const ageInMonths = calculateAgeInMonths(patientData.dateOfBirth, input.date);
  68 | 		const ageInDays = calculateAgeInDays(patientData.dateOfBirth, input.date);
  69 |
  70 | 		if (!patientData.gender) {
  71 | 			throw new Error("Patient gender is required");
  72 | 		}
  73 | 		const gender = patientData.gender;
  74 |
  75 | 		// Calculate Z-scores using WHO standards
  76 | 		let weightForAgeZ = null;
  77 | 		let heightForAgeZ = null;
  78 | 		let hcForAgeZ = null;
  79 | 		let bmiForAgeZ = null;
  80 |
  81 | 		if (input.weight) {
  82 | 			const weightStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "WFA");
  83 | 			if (weightStandard) {
  84 | 				weightForAgeZ = calculateZScore(
  85 | 					input.weight,
  86 | 					weightStandard.mValue,
  87 | 					weightStandard.lValue,
  88 | 					weightStandard.sValue
  89 | 				);
  90 | 			}
  91 | 		}
  92 |
  93 | 		if (input.height) {
  94 | 			const heightStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "HFA");
  95 | 			if (heightStandard) {
  96 | 				heightForAgeZ = calculateZScore(
  97 | 					input.height,
  98 | 					heightStandard.mValue,
  99 | 					heightStandard.lValue,
 100 | 					heightStandard.sValue
 101 | 				);
 102 | 			}
 103 | 		}
 104 |
 105 | 		if (input.headCircumference) {
 106 | 			const hcStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "HcFA");
 107 | 			if (hcStandard) {
 108 | 				hcForAgeZ = calculateZScore(
 109 | 					input.headCircumference,
 110 | 					hcStandard.mValue,
 111 | 					hcStandard.lValue,
 112 | 					hcStandard.sValue
 113 | 				);
 114 | 			}
 115 | 		}
 116 |
 117 | 		// Calculate BMI if both weight and height are provided
 118 | 		if (input.weight && input.height) {
 119 | 			const heightInMeters = input.height / 100;
 120 | 			bmiForAgeZ = input.weight / (heightInMeters * heightInMeters);
 121 |
 122 | 			const bmiStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "BMI");
 123 | 			if (bmiStandard) {
 124 | 				bmiForAgeZ = calculateZScore(bmiForAgeZ, bmiStandard.mValue, bmiStandard.lValue, bmiStandard.sValue);
 125 | 			}
 126 | 		}
 127 |
 128 | 		const [record] = await db
 129 | 			.insert(growthRecord)
 130 | 			.values({
 131 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 132 | 				clinicId: context.clinicId,
 133 | 				patientId: input.patientId,
 134 | 				gender: patientData.gender,
 135 | 				ageDays: ageInDays,
 136 | 				ageMonths: ageInMonths,
 137 | 				date: input.date,
 138 | 				recordedAt: new Date(),
 139 | 				weight: input.weight,
 140 | 				height: input.height,
 141 | 				headCircumference: input.headCircumference,
 142 | 				weightForAgeZ,
 143 | 				heightForAgeZ,
 144 | 				hcForAgeZ,
 145 | 				bmiForAgeZ,
 146 | 				notes: input.notes,
 147 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 148 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 149 | 			})
 150 | 			.returning();
 151 |
 152 | 		return record;
 153 | 	});
 154 |
 155 | // Get growth chart data
 156 | export const getGrowthChart = clinicProcedure
 157 | 	.input(
 158 | 		z.object({
 159 | 			patientId: z.string(),
 160 | 			measurementType: z.enum(["WEIGHT", "HEIGHT", "BMI", "HEAD_CIRCUMFERENCE"]),
 161 | 			fromDate: z.date().optional(),
 162 | 			toDate: z.date().optional()
 163 | 		})
 164 | 	)
 165 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 166 | 		let whereClause = and(eq(growthRecord.patientId, input.patientId), eq(growthRecord.clinicId, context.clinicId));
 167 |
 168 | 		if (input.fromDate && input.toDate) {
 169 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, between(growthRecord.date, input.fromDate, input.toDate));
 170 | 		}
 171 |
 172 | 		const records = await db.query.growthRecord.findMany({
 173 | 			where: whereClause,
 174 | 			orderBy: (growthRecord, { asc }) => [asc(growthRecord.date)]
 175 | 		});
 176 |
 177 | 		const chartData = records.map(record => ({
 178 | 			date: record.date,
 179 | 			value: getMeasurementValue(record, input.measurementType),
 180 | 			zScore: getZScoreValue(record, input.measurementType),
 181 | 			ageDays: record.ageDays,
 182 | 			ageMonths: record.ageMonths
 183 | 		}));
 184 |
 185 | 		return chartData;
 186 | 	});
 187 |
 188 | // Update growth measurement
 189 | export const updateGrowthMeasurement = clinicProcedure
 190 | 	.input(
 191 | 		z.object({
 192 | 			id: z.string(),
 193 | 			date: z.date().optional(),
 194 | 			weight: z.number().optional(),
 195 | 			height: z.number().optional(),
 196 | 			headCircumference: z.number().optional(),
 197 | 			notes: z.string().optional()
 198 | 		})
 199 | 	)
 200 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 201 | 		const existing = await db.query.growthRecord.findFirst({
 202 | 			where: and(eq(growthRecord.id, input.id), eq(growthRecord.clinicId, context.clinicId))
 203 | 		});
 204 |
 205 | 		if (!existing) throw new Error("Growth record not found");
 206 |
 207 | 		const patientData = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
 208 | 			where: eq(patient.id, existing.patientId)
 209 | 		});
 210 |
 211 | 		if (!patientData) throw new Error("Patient not found");
 212 |
 213 | 		const recordDate = input.date || existing.date;
 214 | 		const ageInMonths = calculateAgeInMonths(patientData.dateOfBirth, recordDate);
 215 | 		const ageInDays = calculateAgeInDays(patientData.dateOfBirth, recordDate);
 216 |
 217 | 		const gender = patientData.gender || existing.gender;
 218 |
 219 | 		let weightForAgeZ = existing.weightForAgeZ;
 220 | 		let heightForAgeZ = existing.heightForAgeZ;
 221 | 		let hcForAgeZ = existing.hcForAgeZ;
 222 | 		let bmiForAgeZ = existing.bmiForAgeZ;
 223 |
 224 | 		const weight = input.weight ?? existing.weight;
 225 | 		const height = input.height ?? existing.height;
 226 | 		const headCircumference = input.headCircumference ?? existing.headCircumference;
 227 |
 228 | 		if (weight && gender) {
 229 | 			const weightStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "WFA");
 230 | 			if (weightStandard) {
 231 | 				weightForAgeZ = calculateZScore(
 232 | 					weight,
 233 | 					weightStandard.mValue,
 234 | 					weightStandard.lValue,
 235 | 					weightStandard.sValue
 236 | 				);
 237 | 			}
 238 | 		}
 239 |
 240 | 		if (height && gender) {
 241 | 			const heightStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "HFA");
 242 | 			if (heightStandard) {
 243 | 				heightForAgeZ = calculateZScore(
 244 | 					height,
 245 | 					heightStandard.mValue,
 246 | 					heightStandard.lValue,
 247 | 					heightStandard.sValue
 248 | 				);
 249 | 			}
 250 | 		}
 251 |
 252 | 		if (headCircumference && gender) {
 253 | 			const hcStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "HcFA");
 254 | 			if (hcStandard) {
 255 | 				hcForAgeZ = calculateZScore(headCircumference, hcStandard.mValue, hcStandard.lValue, hcStandard.sValue);
 256 | 			}
 257 | 		}
 258 |
 259 | 		if (weight && height && gender) {
 260 | 			const heightInMeters = height / 100;
 261 | 			const bmi = weight / (heightInMeters * heightInMeters);
 262 | 			const bmiStandard = await getWHOStandard(ageInDays, gender, "BMI");
 263 | 			if (bmiStandard) {
 264 | 				bmiForAgeZ = calculateZScore(bmi, bmiStandard.mValue, bmiStandard.lValue, bmiStandard.sValue);
 265 | 			}
 266 | 		}
 267 |
 268 | 		const [updated] = await db
 269 | 			.update(growthRecord)
 270 | 			.set({
 271 | 				date: recordDate,
 272 | 				ageDays: ageInDays,
 273 | 				ageMonths: ageInMonths,
 274 | 				weight,
 275 | 				height,
 276 | 				headCircumference,
 277 | 				weightForAgeZ,
 278 | 				heightForAgeZ,
 279 | 				hcForAgeZ,
 280 | 				bmiForAgeZ,
 281 | 				notes: input.notes ?? existing.notes,
 282 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 283 | 			})
 284 | 			.where(and(eq(growthRecord.id, input.id), eq(growthRecord.clinicId, context.clinicId)))
 285 | 			.returning();
 286 |
 287 | 		return updated;
 288 | 	});
 289 |
 290 | // Delete growth measurement
 291 | export const deleteGrowthMeasurement = clinicProcedure
 292 | 	.input(
 293 | 		z.object({
 294 | 			id: z.string()
 295 | 		})
 296 | 	)
 297 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 298 | 		const [deleted] = await db
 299 | 			.delete(growthRecord)
 300 | 			.where(and(eq(growthRecord.id, input.id), eq(growthRecord.clinicId, context.clinicId)))
 301 | 			.returning();
 302 |
 303 | 		if (!deleted) throw new Error("Growth record not found");
 304 |
 305 | 		return { success: true, id: deleted.id };
 306 | 	});
 307 |
 308 | // Get growth percentile classification
 309 | export const getGrowthPercentile = clinicProcedure
 310 | 	.input(
 311 | 		z.object({
 312 | 			patientId: z.string(),
 313 | 			date: z.date().optional()
 314 | 		})
 315 | 	)
 316 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 317 | 		const patientData = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
 318 | 			where: eq(patient.id, input.patientId)
 319 | 		});
 320 |
 321 | 		if (!patientData) throw new Error("Patient not found");
 322 |
 323 | 		const referenceDate = input.date || new Date();
 324 | 		const ageDays = calculateAgeInDays(patientData.dateOfBirth, referenceDate);
 325 | 		const ageMonths = calculateAgeInMonths(patientData.dateOfBirth, referenceDate);
 326 |
 327 | 		const latestRecord = await db.query.growthRecord.findFirst({
 328 | 			where: and(eq(growthRecord.patientId, input.patientId), eq(growthRecord.clinicId, context.clinicId)),
 329 | 			orderBy: (growthRecord, { desc }) => [desc(growthRecord.date)]
 330 | 		});
 331 |
 332 | 		if (!latestRecord) {
 333 | 			return {
 334 | 				ageDays,
 335 | 				ageMonths,
 336 | 				classifications: null
 337 | 			};
 338 | 		}
 339 |
 340 | 		const getClassification = (zScore: number | null): string => {
 341 | 			if (zScore === null) return "NO_DATA";
 342 | 			if (zScore < -3) return "SEVERELY_UNDERWEIGHT";
 343 | 			if (zScore < -2) return "UNDERWEIGHT";
 344 | 			if (zScore <= 1) return "NORMAL";
 345 | 			if (zScore <= 2) return "OVERWEIGHT";
 346 | 			return "OBESE";
 347 | 		};
 348 |
 349 | 		const getStuntingClassification = (zScore: number | null): string => {
 350 | 			if (zScore === null) return "NO_DATA";
 351 | 			if (zScore < -3) return "SEVERELY_STUNTED";
 352 | 			if (zScore < -2) return "STUNTED";
 353 | 			if (zScore <= 1) return "NORMAL";
 354 | 			if (zScore <= 2) return "TALL";
 355 | 			return "VERY_TALL";
 356 | 		};
 357 |
 358 | 		return {
 359 | 			ageDays,
 360 | 			ageMonths,
 361 | 			classifications: {
 362 | 				weight: {
 363 | 					zScore: latestRecord.weightForAgeZ,
 364 | 					classification: getClassification(latestRecord.weightForAgeZ)
 365 | 				},
 366 | 				height: {
 367 | 					zScore: latestRecord.heightForAgeZ,
 368 | 					classification: getStuntingClassification(latestRecord.heightForAgeZ)
 369 | 				},
 370 | 				headCircumference: {
 371 | 					zScore: latestRecord.hcForAgeZ,
 372 | 					classification: getClassification(latestRecord.hcForAgeZ)
 373 | 				},
 374 | 				bmi: {
 375 | 					zScore: latestRecord.bmiForAgeZ,
 376 | 					classification: getClassification(latestRecord.bmiForAgeZ)
 377 | 				}
 378 | 			},
 379 | 			lastMeasurement: latestRecord
 380 | 		};
 381 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/immunization.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // routers/immunization.router.ts
   2 | import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   3 | import { z } from "zod";
   4 |
   5 | import { db } from "../../db";
   6 | import { immunization, patient } from "../../db/schema";
   7 | import { protectedProcedure } from "../init";
   8 |
   9 | // Record immunization
  10 | export const recordImmunization = protectedProcedure
  11 | 	.input(
  12 | 		z.object({
  13 | 			patientId: z.string(),
  14 | 			vaccine: z.string(),
  15 | 			date: z.date(),
  16 | 			dose: z.string(),
  17 | 			lotNumber: z.string().optional(),
  18 | 			administeredByStaffId: z.string().optional(),
  19 | 			notes: z.string().optional()
  20 | 		})
  21 | 	)
  22 | 	.output(z.any())
  23 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  24 | 		const [record] = await db
  25 | 			.insert(immunization)
  26 | 			.values({
  27 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  28 | 				clinicId: context.clinicId,
  29 | 				...input
  30 | 			})
  31 | 			.returning();
  32 | 		return record;
  33 | 	});
  34 |
  35 | // Get immunization schedule for patient
  36 | export const getPatientImmunizationSchedule = protectedProcedure
  37 | 	.input(z.object({ patientId: z.string() }))
  38 | 	.output(z.any())
  39 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  40 | 		// Get patient age
  41 | 		const patientData = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
  42 | 			where: eq(patient.id, input.patientId)
  43 | 		});
  44 |
  45 | 		if (!patientData) throw new Error("Patient not found");
  46 |
  47 | 		const ageInDays = Math.floor((Date.now() - patientData.dateOfBirth.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  48 |
  49 | 		// Get due vaccines based on age
  50 | 		const dueVaccines = await db.query.vaccineSchedule.findMany({
  51 | 			where: (vaccineSchedule, { lte, and: andV }) =>
  52 | 				andV(lte(vaccineSchedule.ageInDaysMin, ageInDays), lte(vaccineSchedule.ageInDaysMax, ageInDays))
  53 | 		});
  54 |
  55 | 		// Get administered vaccines
  56 | 		const administered = await db.query.immunization.findMany({
  57 | 			where: and(eq(immunization.patientId, input.patientId), eq(immunization.isDeleted, false))
  58 | 		});
  59 |
  60 | 		// Filter out vaccines that have been administered
  61 | 		const pendingVaccines = dueVaccines.filter(
  62 | 			vaccine => !administered.some(admin => admin.vaccine === vaccine.vaccineName)
  63 | 		);
  64 |
  65 | 		return {
  66 | 			administered,
  67 | 			pending: pendingVaccines,
  68 | 			ageInDays
  69 | 		};
  70 | 	});

```
