`orpc/router/medical-record.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // orpc/router/medical-record.router.ts
   2 | import { and, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   3 | import { z } from "zod";
   4 |
   5 | import { db } from "../../db";
   6 | import { appointment, diagnosis, medicalRecord, vitalSign } from "../../db/schema";
   7 | import { generateId } from "../../lib/id";
   8 | import { protectedProcedure } from "../init";
   9 | import {
  10 | 	DiagnosisCreateSchema,
  11 | 	DiagnosisSchema,
  12 | 	MedicalRecordSchema,
  13 | 	MedicalRecordsUpdateSchema,
  14 | 	VitalSignSchema
  15 | } from "../schema";
  16 |
  17 | const VitalSignsCreateSchema = VitalSignSchema.omit({
  18 | 	id: true
  19 | });
  20 | const MedicalRecordCreateSchema = MedicalRecordSchema.omit({
  21 | 	id: true
  22 | });
  23 | // ================ MEDICAL RECORD PROCEDURES ================
  24 |
  25 | // Create complete medical record with diagnosis and vitals
  26 | export const createMedicalRecord = protectedProcedure
  27 | 	.input(
  28 | 		z.object({
  29 | 			record: MedicalRecordCreateSchema,
  30 | 			diagnosis: DiagnosisCreateSchema.optional(),
  31 | 			vitals: VitalSignsCreateSchema.optional()
  32 | 		})
  33 | 	)
  34 | 	.output(MedicalRecordSchema)
  35 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  36 | 		const recordId = crypto.randomUUID();
  37 |
  38 | 		// Create medical record
  39 | 		const [record] = await db
  40 | 			.insert(medicalRecord)
  41 | 			.values({
  42 | 				...input.record,
  43 | 				id: recordId,
  44 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
  45 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
  46 | 			})
  47 | 			.returning();
  48 |
  49 | 		// Create diagnosis if provided
  50 | 		if (input.diagnosis) {
  51 | 			await db.insert(diagnosis).values({
  52 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  53 | 				medicalId: recordId,
  54 | 				patientId: input.record.patientId,
  55 | 				doctorId: input.diagnosis.doctorId,
  56 | 				appointmentId: input.record.appointmentId,
  57 | 				symptoms: input.diagnosis.symptoms,
  58 | 				diagnosis: input.diagnosis.diagnosis,
  59 | 				treatment: input.diagnosis.treatment,
  60 | 				type: input.diagnosis.type,
  61 | 				notes: input.diagnosis.notes,
  62 | 				prescribedMedications: input.diagnosis.prescribedMedications,
  63 | 				followUpPlan: input.diagnosis.followUpPlan,
  64 | 				date: new Date(),
  65 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
  66 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
  67 | 			});
  68 | 		}
  69 |
  70 | 		// Create vitals if provided
  71 | 		if (input.vitals) {
  72 | 			await db.insert(vitalSign).values({
  73 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  74 | 				medicalId: recordId,
  75 | 				patientId: input.vitals.patientId,
  76 | 				encounterId: input.vitals.encounterId,
  77 | 				bodyTemperature: input.vitals.bodyTemperature,
  78 | 				systolic: input.vitals.systolic,
  79 | 				diastolic: input.vitals.diastolic,
  80 | 				heartRate: input.vitals.heartRate,
  81 | 				respiratoryRate: input.vitals.respiratoryRate,
  82 | 				oxygenSaturation: input.vitals.oxygenSaturation,
  83 | 				gender: input.vitals.gender,
  84 | 				notes: input.vitals.notes,
  85 | 				ageDays: input.vitals.ageDays,
  86 | 				ageMonths: input.vitals.ageMonths,
  87 | 				recordedAt: new Date(),
  88 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
  89 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
  90 | 			});
  91 | 		}
  92 |
  93 | 		return record;
  94 | 	});
  95 |
  96 | // Get medical record by ID with full relations
  97 | export const getMedicalRecordById = protectedProcedure
  98 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
  99 | 	.output(
 100 | 		MedicalRecordSchema.extend({
 101 | 			patient: z
 102 | 				.object({
 103 | 					id: z.string(),
 104 | 					firstName: z.string(),
 105 | 					lastName: z.string(),
 106 | 					dateOfBirth: z.date(),
 107 | 					gender: z.string().nullable()
 108 | 				})
 109 | 				.nullable(),
 110 | 			doctor: z
 111 | 				.object({
 112 | 					id: z.string(),
 113 | 					name: z.string(),
 114 | 					specialty: z.string()
 115 | 				})
 116 | 				.nullable(),
 117 | 			appointment: z
 118 | 				.object({
 119 | 					id: z.string(),
 120 | 					appointmentDate: z.date(),
 121 | 					status: z.string().nullable()
 122 | 				})
 123 | 				.nullable(),
 124 | 			encounter: z.array(DiagnosisSchema).nullable(),
 125 | 			vitalSigns: z.array(VitalSignSchema).nullable(),
 126 | 			prescriptions: z.array(z.any()).nullable()
 127 | 		})
 128 | 	)
 129 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 130 | 		const record = await db.query.medicalRecord.findFirst({
 131 | 			where: eq(medicalRecord.id, input.id),
 132 | 			with: {
 133 | 				patient: {
 134 | 					columns: {
 135 | 						id: true,
 136 | 						firstName: true,
 137 | 						lastName: true,
 138 | 						dateOfBirth: true,
 139 | 						gender: true
 140 | 					}
 141 | 				},
 142 | 				doctor: {
 143 | 					columns: {
 144 | 						id: true,
 145 | 						name: true,
 146 | 						specialty: true
 147 | 					}
 148 | 				},
 149 | 				appointment: {
 150 | 					columns: {
 151 | 						id: true,
 152 | 						appointmentDate: true,
 153 | 						status: true
 154 | 					}
 155 | 				},
 156 | 				encounter: true,
 157 | 				vitalSigns: true,
 158 | 				prescriptions: true
 159 | 			}
 160 | 		});
 161 |
 162 | 		if (!record) {
 163 | 			throw new Error("Medical record not found");
 164 | 		}
 165 |
 166 | 		return {
 167 | 			...record,
 168 | 			encounter: record.encounter.map(e => ({
 169 | 				...e,
 170 | 				type: e.type ?? null,
 171 | 				diagnosis: e.diagnosis ?? null,
 172 | 				treatment: e.treatment ?? null
 173 | 			}))
 174 | 		};
 175 | 	});
 176 |
 177 | // Get patient medical history
 178 | export const getPatientMedicalHistory = protectedProcedure
 179 | 	.input(
 180 | 		z.object({
 181 | 			patientId: z.string(),
 182 | 			limit: z.number().default(20),
 183 | 			offset: z.number().default(0),
 184 | 			includeDeleted: z.boolean().default(false)
 185 | 		})
 186 | 	)
 187 | 	.output(
 188 | 		z.object({
 189 | 			records: z.array(
 190 | 				MedicalRecordSchema.extend({
 191 | 					doctor: z
 192 | 						.object({
 193 | 							id: z.string(),
 194 | 							name: z.string(),
 195 | 							specialty: z.string()
 196 | 						})
 197 | 						.nullable(),
 198 | 					appointment: z
 199 | 						.object({
 200 | 							id: z.string(),
 201 | 							appointmentDate: z.date()
 202 | 						})
 203 | 						.nullable(),
 204 | 					diagnosisCount: z.number(),
 205 | 					vitalSignsCount: z.number()
 206 | 				})
 207 | 			),
 208 | 			total: z.number(),
 209 | 			hasMore: z.boolean()
 210 | 		})
 211 | 	)
 212 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 213 | 		const { patientId, limit, offset, includeDeleted } = input;
 214 |
 215 | 		const whereConditions = and(
 216 | 			eq(medicalRecord.patientId, patientId),
 217 | 			includeDeleted ? undefined : eq(medicalRecord.isDeleted, false)
 218 | 		);
 219 |
 220 | 		const records = await db.query.medicalRecord.findMany({
 221 | 			where: whereConditions,
 222 | 			with: {
 223 | 				doctor: {
 224 | 					columns: {
 225 | 						id: true,
 226 | 						name: true,
 227 | 						specialty: true
 228 | 					}
 229 | 				},
 230 | 				appointment: {
 231 | 					columns: {
 232 | 						id: true,
 233 | 						appointmentDate: true
 234 | 					}
 235 | 				}
 236 | 			},
 237 | 			limit,
 238 | 			offset,
 239 | 			orderBy: desc(medicalRecord.createdAt)
 240 | 		});
 241 |
 242 | 		// Get counts for diagnosis and vitals per record
 243 | 		const enrichedRecords = await Promise.all(
 244 | 			records.map(async record => {
 245 | 				const diagnosisCount = await db.$count(diagnosis, eq(diagnosis.medicalId, record.id));
 246 | 				const vitalSignsCount = await db.$count(vitalSign, eq(vitalSign.medicalId, record.id));
 247 |
 248 | 				return {
 249 | 					...record,
 250 | 					diagnosisCount,
 251 | 					vitalSignsCount
 252 | 				};
 253 | 			})
 254 | 		);
 255 |
 256 | 		const total = await db.$count(medicalRecord, whereConditions);
 257 | 		const hasMore = offset + limit < total;
 258 |
 259 | 		return { records: enrichedRecords, total, hasMore };
 260 | 	});
 261 |
 262 | // Update medical record
 263 | export const updateMedicalRecord = protectedProcedure
 264 | 	.input(
 265 | 		z.object({
 266 | 			id: z.string(),
 267 | 			data: MedicalRecordsUpdateSchema
 268 | 		})
 269 | 	)
 270 | 	.output(MedicalRecordSchema)
 271 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 272 | 		const [updated] = await db
 273 | 			.update(medicalRecord)
 274 | 			.set({
 275 | 				...input.data,
 276 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 277 | 			})
 278 | 			.where(eq(medicalRecord.id, input.id))
 279 | 			.returning();
 280 |
 281 | 		if (!updated) {
 282 | 			throw new Error("Medical record not found");
 283 | 		}
 284 |
 285 | 		return updated;
 286 | 	});
 287 |
 288 | // Delete (soft delete) medical record
 289 | export const deleteMedicalRecord = protectedProcedure
 290 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
 291 | 	.output(z.object({ success: z.boolean(), id: z.string() }))
 292 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 293 | 		await db
 294 | 			.update(medicalRecord)
 295 | 			.set({
 296 | 				isDeleted: true,
 297 | 				deletedAt: new Date(),
 298 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 299 | 			})
 300 | 			.where(eq(medicalRecord.id, input.id));
 301 |
 302 | 		return { success: true, id: input.id };
 303 | 	});
 304 |
 305 | // ================ DIAGNOSIS PROCEDURES ================
 306 |
 307 | // Get diagnosis by medical record ID
 308 | export const getDiagnosesByMedicalRecord = protectedProcedure
 309 | 	.input(z.object({ medicalId: z.string() }))
 310 | 	.output(z.array(DiagnosisSchema))
 311 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 312 | 		const diagnoses = await db.query.diagnosis.findMany({
 313 | 			where: eq(diagnosis.medicalId, input.medicalId),
 314 | 			orderBy: desc(diagnosis.date)
 315 | 		});
 316 |
 317 | 		return diagnoses;
 318 | 	});
 319 |
 320 | // Create diagnosis for medical record
 321 | export const createDiagnosis = protectedProcedure
 322 | 	.input(DiagnosisSchema.omit({ id: true }))
 323 | 	.output(DiagnosisSchema)
 324 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 325 | 		const [diagnosisRecord] = await db
 326 | 			.insert(diagnosis)
 327 | 			.values({
 328 | 				id: generateId(),
 329 | 				...input,
 330 | 				date: new Date(),
 331 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 332 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 333 | 			})
 334 | 			.returning();
 335 |
 336 | 		return diagnosisRecord;
 337 | 	});
 338 |
 339 | // Update diagnosis
 340 | export const updateDiagnosis = protectedProcedure
 341 | 	.input(
 342 | 		z.object({
 343 | 			id: z.string(),
 344 | 			data: DiagnosisCreateSchema.partial()
 345 | 		})
 346 | 	)
 347 | 	.output(DiagnosisSchema)
 348 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 349 | 		const [updated] = await db
 350 | 			.update(diagnosis)
 351 | 			.set({
 352 | 				...input.data,
 353 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 354 | 			})
 355 | 			.where(eq(diagnosis.id, input.id))
 356 | 			.returning();
 357 |
 358 | 		if (!updated) {
 359 | 			throw new Error("Diagnosis not found");
 360 | 		}
 361 |
 362 | 		return updated;
 363 | 	});
 364 |
 365 | // ================ VITAL SIGNS PROCEDURES ================
 366 |
 367 | // Get vital signs by medical record ID
 368 | export const getVitalSignsByMedicalRecord = protectedProcedure
 369 | 	.input(z.object({ medicalId: z.string() }))
 370 | 	.output(z.array(VitalSignSchema))
 371 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 372 | 		const vitals = await db.query.vitalSign.findMany({
 373 | 			where: eq(vitalSign.medicalId, input.medicalId),
 374 | 			orderBy: desc(vitalSign.recordedAt)
 375 | 		});
 376 |
 377 | 		return vitals;
 378 | 	});
 379 |
 380 | // Create vital sign record
 381 | export const createVitalSign = protectedProcedure
 382 | 	.input(VitalSignsCreateSchema)
 383 | 	.output(VitalSignSchema)
 384 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 385 | 		const id = crypto.randomUUID();
 386 |
 387 | 		const [vital] = await db
 388 | 			.insert(vitalSign)
 389 | 			.values({
 390 | 				id,
 391 | 				...input,
 392 | 				recordedAt: new Date(),
 393 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 394 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 395 | 			})
 396 | 			.returning();
 397 |
 398 | 		return vital;
 399 | 	});
 400 |
 401 | // Get latest vital signs for a patient
 402 | export const getLatestVitalSigns = protectedProcedure
 403 | 	.input(z.object({ patientId: z.string(), limit: z.number().default(5) }))
 404 | 	.output(z.array(VitalSignSchema))
 405 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 406 | 		const vitals = await db.query.vitalSign.findMany({
 407 | 			where: eq(vitalSign.patientId, input.patientId),
 408 | 			limit: input.limit,
 409 | 			orderBy: desc(vitalSign.recordedAt)
 410 | 		});
 411 |
 412 | 		return vitals;
 413 | 	});
 414 |
 415 | // ================ COMPLETE MEDICAL ENCOUNTER ================
 416 |
 417 | // Create a complete medical encounter (record + diagnosis + vitals)
 418 | export const createCompleteEncounter = protectedProcedure
 419 | 	.input(
 420 | 		z.object({
 421 | 			patientId: z.string(),
 422 | 			appointmentId: z.string(),
 423 | 			doctorId: z.string(),
 424 | 			diagnosis: z.object({
 425 | 				symptoms: z.string(),
 426 | 				diagnosis: z.string().optional(),
 427 | 				treatment: z.string().optional(),
 428 | 				type: z.string().optional(),
 429 | 				prescribedMedications: z.string().optional(),
 430 | 				followUpPlan: z.string().optional()
 431 | 			}),
 432 | 			vitals: z
 433 | 				.object({
 434 | 					bodyTemperature: z.number().optional(),
 435 | 					systolic: z.number().optional(),
 436 | 					diastolic: z.number().optional(),
 437 | 					heartRate: z.number().optional(),
 438 | 					respiratoryRate: z.number().optional(),
 439 | 					oxygenSaturation: z.number().optional(),
 440 | 					notes: z.string().optional()
 441 | 				})
 442 | 				.optional(),
 443 | 			medicalRecord: z
 444 | 				.object({
 445 | 					notes: z.string().optional(),
 446 | 					treatmentPlan: z.string().optional(),
 447 | 					labRequest: z.string().optional(),
 448 | 					medications: z.string().optional(),
 449 | 					followUpDate: z.date().optional()
 450 | 				})
 451 | 				.optional()
 452 | 		})
 453 | 	)
 454 | 	.output(
 455 | 		z.object({
 456 | 			medicalRecord: MedicalRecordSchema,
 457 | 			diagnosis: DiagnosisSchema,
 458 | 			vitalSigns: VitalSignSchema.optional()
 459 | 		})
 460 | 	)
 461 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
 462 | 		const recordId = generateId();
 463 |
 464 | 		// Get appointment to fetch clinicId
 465 | 		const appointmentData = await db.query.appointment.findFirst({
 466 | 			where: eq(appointment.id, input.appointmentId),
 467 | 			columns: { clinicId: true }
 468 | 		});
 469 |
 470 | 		if (!appointmentData?.clinicId) {
 471 | 			throw new Error("Appointment not found or clinic not assigned");
 472 | 		}
 473 |
 474 | 		// Update appointment status to completed
 475 | 		await db
 476 | 			.update(appointment)
 477 | 			.set({ status: "COMPLETED", updatedAt: new Date() })
 478 | 			.where(eq(appointment.id, input.appointmentId));
 479 |
 480 | 		// Create medical record
 481 | 		const [record] = await db
 482 | 			.insert(medicalRecord)
 483 | 			.values({
 484 | 				id: recordId,
 485 | 				clinicId: appointmentData.clinicId,
 486 | 				patientId: input.patientId,
 487 | 				appointmentId: input.appointmentId,
 488 | 				doctorId: input.doctorId,
 489 | 				notes: input.medicalRecord?.notes,
 490 | 				treatmentPlan: input.medicalRecord?.treatmentPlan,
 491 | 				labRequest: input.medicalRecord?.labRequest,
 492 | 				medications: input.medicalRecord?.medications,
 493 | 				followUpDate: input.medicalRecord?.followUpDate,
 494 | 				diagnosisDate: new Date(),
 495 | 				status: "ACTIVE",
 496 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 497 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 498 | 			})
 499 | 			.returning();
 500 |
 501 | 		// Create diagnosis
 502 | 		const [diagnosisRecord] = await db
 503 | 			.insert(diagnosis)
 504 | 			.values({
 505 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 506 | 				medicalId: recordId,
 507 | 				patientId: input.patientId,
 508 | 				doctorId: input.doctorId,
 509 | 				appointmentId: input.appointmentId,
 510 | 				symptoms: input.diagnosis.symptoms,
 511 | 				diagnosis: input.diagnosis.diagnosis,
 512 | 				treatment: input.diagnosis.treatment,
 513 | 				type: input.diagnosis.type,
 514 | 				prescribedMedications: input.diagnosis.prescribedMedications,
 515 | 				followUpPlan: input.diagnosis.followUpPlan,
 516 | 				date: new Date(),
 517 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 518 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 519 | 			})
 520 | 			.returning();
 521 |
 522 | 		let vitalRecord: z.infer<typeof VitalSignSchema> | undefined;
 523 |
 524 | 		// Create vitals if provided
 525 | 		if (input.vitals) {
 526 | 			const [vital] = await db
 527 | 				.insert(vitalSign)
 528 | 				.values({
 529 | 					id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 530 | 					medicalId: recordId,
 531 | 					patientId: input.patientId,
 532 | 					encounterId: diagnosisRecord.id,
 533 | 					bodyTemperature: input.vitals.bodyTemperature,
 534 | 					systolic: input.vitals.systolic,
 535 | 					diastolic: input.vitals.diastolic,
 536 | 					heartRate: input.vitals.heartRate,
 537 | 					respiratoryRate: input.vitals.respiratoryRate,
 538 | 					oxygenSaturation: input.vitals.oxygenSaturation,
 539 | 					notes: input.vitals.notes,
 540 | 					recordedAt: new Date(),
 541 | 					createdAt: new Date(),
 542 | 					updatedAt: new Date()
 543 | 				})
 544 | 				.returning();
 545 |
 546 | 			vitalRecord = vital;
 547 | 		}
 548 |
 549 | 		return {
 550 | 			medicalRecord: record,
 551 | 			diagnosis: diagnosisRecord,
 552 | 			vitalSigns: vitalRecord
 553 | 		};
 554 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/patient.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // routes/demo/patient-router.ts
   2 |
   3 | import { and, asc, desc, eq, like, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
   4 | import { z } from "zod";
   5 |
   6 | import { db } from "#/db";
   7 | import {
   8 | 	appointment,
   9 | 	diagnosis,
  10 | 	growthRecord,
  11 | 	immunization,
  12 | 	medicalRecord,
  13 | 	patient,
  14 | 	payment,
  15 | 	prescription,
  16 | 	vitalSign
  17 | } from "#/db/schema";
  18 |
  19 | import { protectedProcedure } from "../init";
  20 |
  21 | // ================ SCHEMAS ================
  22 |
  23 | export const PatientSchema = z.object({
  24 | 	id: z.string(),
  25 | 	clinicId: z.string(),
  26 | 	userId: z.string(),
  27 | 	email: z.string().nullable(),
  28 | 	phone: z.string().nullable(),
  29 | 	emergencyContactNumber: z.string().nullable(),
  30 | 	firstName: z.string(),
  31 | 	lastName: z.string(),
  32 | 	dateOfBirth: z.date(),
  33 | 	gender: z.string().nullable(),
  34 | 	maritalStatus: z.string().nullable(),
  35 | 	nutritionalStatus: z.string().nullable(),
  36 | 	address: z.string().nullable(),
  37 | 	emergencyContactName: z.string().nullable(),
  38 | 	relation: z.string().nullable(),
  39 | 	allergies: z.string().nullable(),
  40 | 	medicalConditions: z.string().nullable(),
  41 | 	medicalHistory: z.string().nullable(),
  42 | 	image: z.string().nullable(),
  43 | 	colorCode: z.string().nullable(),
  44 | 	role: z.string().nullable(),
  45 | 	status: z.string().nullable(),
  46 | 	isActive: z.boolean().nullable(),
  47 | 	deletedAt: z.date().nullable(),
  48 | 	isDeleted: z.boolean().nullable(),
  49 | 	createdById: z.string().nullable(),
  50 | 	updatedById: z.string().nullable(),
  51 | 	bloodGroup: z.string().nullable(),
  52 | 	createdAt: z.date(),
  53 | 	updatedAt: z.date()
  54 | });
  55 |
  56 | export const PatientCreateSchema = PatientSchema.omit({
  57 | 	id: true,
  58 | 	clinicId: true,
  59 | 	createdAt: true,
  60 | 	updatedAt: true,
  61 | 	deletedAt: true,
  62 | 	isDeleted: true,
  63 | 	createdById: true,
  64 | 	updatedById: true
  65 | });
  66 |
  67 | export const PatientUpdateSchema = PatientCreateSchema.partial();
  68 |
  69 | export const PatientWithRelationsSchema = PatientSchema.extend({
  70 | 	appointments: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  71 | 	medicalRecords: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  72 | 	encounters: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  73 | 	immunizations: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  74 | 	vitalSigns: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  75 | 	prescriptions: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  76 | 	growthRecords: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  77 | 	payments: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  78 | 	guardians: z.array(z.any()).optional()
  79 | });
  80 |
  81 | // ================ PATIENT PROCEDURES ================
  82 |
  83 | // Create patient
  84 | export const createPatient = protectedProcedure
  85 | 	.input(PatientCreateSchema)
  86 | 	.output(PatientSchema)
  87 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  88 | 		const [newPatient] = await db
  89 | 			.insert(patient)
  90 | 			.values({
  91 | 				...input,
  92 | 				clinicId: context.clinicId || "default-clinic-id",
  93 | 				createdById: context.user?.id ?? "",
  94 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  95 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
  96 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
  97 | 			})
  98 | 			.returning();
  99 |
 100 | 		return newPatient;
 101 | 	});
 102 |
 103 | // Get patient by ID
 104 | export const getPatient = protectedProcedure
 105 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
 106 | 	.output(PatientSchema.nullable())
 107 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 108 | 		const result = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
 109 | 			where: and(
 110 | 				eq(patient.id, input.id),
 111 | 				eq(patient.clinicId, context.clinicId || ""),
 112 | 				eq(patient.isDeleted, false)
 113 | 			)
 114 | 		});
 115 | 		return result ?? null;
 116 | 	});
 117 |
 118 | // Get patient with full details (appointments, medical records, etc.)
 119 | export const getPatientWithDetails = protectedProcedure
 120 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
 121 | 	.output(PatientWithRelationsSchema.nullable())
 122 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 123 | 		const result = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
 124 | 			where: and(
 125 | 				eq(patient.id, input.id),
 126 | 				eq(patient.clinicId, context.clinicId || ""),
 127 | 				eq(patient.isDeleted, false)
 128 | 			),
 129 | 			with: {
 130 | 				appointments: {
 131 | 					with: {
 132 | 						doctor: true,
 133 | 						service: true
 134 | 					},
 135 | 					orderBy: desc(appointment.appointmentDate)
 136 | 				},
 137 | 				medicalRecords: {
 138 | 					with: {
 139 | 						doctor: true,
 140 | 						encounter: true
 141 | 					},
 142 | 					orderBy: desc(medicalRecord.createdAt)
 143 | 				},
 144 | 				encounters: {
 145 | 					with: {
 146 | 						doctor: true
 147 | 					},
 148 | 					orderBy: desc(diagnosis.date)
 149 | 				},
 150 | 				immunizations: {
 151 | 					orderBy: desc(immunization.date)
 152 | 				},
 153 | 				vitalSigns: {
 154 | 					orderBy: desc(vitalSign.recordedAt)
 155 | 				},
 156 | 				prescriptions: {
 157 | 					with: {
 158 | 						doctor: true,
 159 | 						prescribedItems: true
 160 | 					},
 161 | 					orderBy: desc(prescription.issuedDate)
 162 | 				},
 163 | 				growthRecords: {
 164 | 					orderBy: desc(growthRecord.recordedAt)
 165 | 				},
 166 | 				payments: {
 167 | 					orderBy: desc(payment.paymentDate)
 168 | 				},
 169 | 				guardians: {
 170 | 					with: {
 171 | 						user: true
 172 | 					}
 173 | 				}
 174 | 			}
 175 | 		});
 176 | 		return result ?? null;
 177 | 	});
 178 |
 179 | // List patients with pagination and filters
 180 | export const listPatients = protectedProcedure
 181 | 	.input(
 182 | 		z.object({
 183 | 			page: z.number().default(1),
 184 | 			limit: z.number().default(10),
 185 | 			search: z.string().optional(),
 186 | 			status: z.string().optional(),
 187 | 			isActive: z.boolean().optional(),
 188 | 			gender: z.string().optional(),
 189 | 			bloodGroup: z.string().optional()
 190 | 		})
 191 | 	)
 192 | 	.output(
 193 | 		z.object({
 194 | 			patients: z.array(PatientSchema),
 195 | 			total: z.number(),
 196 | 			page: z.number(),
 197 | 			limit: z.number(),
 198 | 			totalPages: z.number()
 199 | 		})
 200 | 	)
 201 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 202 | 		const { page, limit, search, status, isActive, gender, bloodGroup } = input;
 203 | 		const offset = (page - 1) * limit;
 204 |
 205 | 		let whereClause = and(eq(patient.clinicId, context.clinicId || ""), eq(patient.isDeleted, false));
 206 |
 207 | 		if (search) {
 208 | 			whereClause = and(
 209 | 				whereClause,
 210 | 				or(
 211 | 					like(patient.firstName, `%${search}%`),
 212 | 					like(patient.lastName, `%${search}%`),
 213 | 					like(patient.email, `%${search}%`),
 214 | 					like(patient.phone, `%${search}%`)
 215 | 				)
 216 | 			);
 217 | 		}
 218 |
 219 | 		if (status) {
 220 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(patient.status, status));
 221 | 		}
 222 |
 223 | 		if (isActive !== undefined) {
 224 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(patient.isActive, isActive));
 225 | 		}
 226 |
 227 | 		if (gender) {
 228 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(patient.gender, gender));
 229 | 		}
 230 |
 231 | 		if (bloodGroup) {
 232 | 			whereClause = and(whereClause, eq(patient.bloodGroup, bloodGroup));
 233 | 		}
 234 |
 235 | 		const patientsList = await db.query.patient.findMany({
 236 | 			where: whereClause,
 237 | 			limit,
 238 | 			offset,
 239 | 			orderBy: desc(patient.createdAt)
 240 | 		});
 241 |
 242 | 		const totalResult = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` }).from(patient).where(whereClause).get();
 243 |
 244 | 		const total = totalResult?.count ?? 0;
 245 |
 246 | 		return {
 247 | 			patients: patientsList,
 248 | 			total,
 249 | 			page,
 250 | 			limit,
 251 | 			totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
 252 | 		};
 253 | 	});
 254 |
 255 | // Update patient
 256 | export const updatePatient = protectedProcedure
 257 | 	.input(
 258 | 		z.object({
 259 | 			id: z.string(),
 260 | 			data: PatientUpdateSchema
 261 | 		})
 262 | 	)
 263 | 	.output(PatientSchema)
 264 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 265 | 		const [updated] = await db
 266 | 			.update(patient)
 267 | 			.set({
 268 | 				...input.data,
 269 | 				updatedById: context.user?.id,
 270 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 271 | 			})
 272 | 			.where(and(eq(patient.id, input.id), eq(patient.clinicId, context.clinicId || "")))
 273 | 			.returning();
 274 |
 275 | 		if (!updated) {
 276 | 			throw new Error("Patient not found");
 277 | 		}
 278 |
 279 | 		return updated;
 280 | 	});
 281 |
 282 | // Soft delete patient
 283 | export const deletePatient = protectedProcedure
 284 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
 285 | 	.output(z.boolean())
 286 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 287 | 		await db
 288 | 			.update(patient)
 289 | 			.set({
 290 | 				isDeleted: true,
 291 | 				deletedAt: new Date(),
 292 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 293 | 			})
 294 | 			.where(and(eq(patient.id, input.id), eq(patient.clinicId, context.clinicId || "")));
 295 | 		return true;
 296 | 	});
 297 |
 298 | // Hard delete patient (permanent)
 299 | export const hardDeletePatient = protectedProcedure
 300 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
 301 | 	.output(z.boolean())
 302 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 303 | 		await db.delete(patient).where(and(eq(patient.id, input.id), eq(patient.clinicId, context.clinicId || "")));
 304 | 		return true;
 305 | 	});
 306 |
 307 | // Get patient statistics
 308 | export const getPatientStats = protectedProcedure
 309 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
 310 | 	.output(
 311 | 		z.object({
 312 | 			totalAppointments: z.number(),
 313 | 			completedAppointments: z.number(),
 314 | 			cancelledAppointments: z.number(),
 315 | 			totalPayments: z.number(),
 316 | 			paidAmount: z.number(),
 317 | 			dueAmount: z.number(),
 318 | 			totalPrescriptions: z.number(),
 319 | 			activePrescriptions: z.number(),
 320 | 			totalImmunizations: z.number(),
 321 | 			lastVisit: z.date().nullable(),
 322 | 			nextAppointment: z.date().nullable()
 323 | 		})
 324 | 	)
 325 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 326 | 		const patientId = input.id;
 327 |
 328 | 		// Get appointment stats
 329 | 		const appointmentStats = context.db
 330 | 			.select({
 331 | 				total: sql<number>`count(*)`,
 332 | 				completed: sql<number>`sum(case when ${appointment.status} = 'COMPLETED' then 1 else 0 end)`,
 333 | 				cancelled: sql<number>`sum(case when ${appointment.status} = 'CANCELLED' then 1 else 0 end)`,
 334 | 				lastVisit: sql<Date>`max(case when ${appointment.status} = 'COMPLETED' then ${appointment.appointmentDate} else null end)`,
 335 | 				nextAppointment: sql<Date>`min(case when ${appointment.appointmentDate} > datetime('now') and ${appointment.status} not in ('CANCELLED', 'COMPLETED') then ${appointment.appointmentDate} else null end)`
 336 | 			})
 337 | 			.from(appointment)
 338 | 			.where(and(eq(appointment.patientId, patientId), eq(appointment.isDeleted, false)))
 339 | 			.get();
 340 |
 341 | 		// Get payment stats
 342 | 		const paymentStats = context.db
 343 | 			.select({
 344 | 				total: sql<number>`count(*)`,
 345 | 				paidAmount: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${payment.amountPaid}), 0)`,
 346 | 				totalAmount: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${payment.totalAmount}), 0)`
 347 | 			})
 348 | 			.from(payment)
 349 | 			.where(and(eq(payment.patientId, patientId), eq(payment.isDeleted, false)))
 350 | 			.get();
 351 |
 352 | 		// Get prescription stats
 353 | 		const prescriptionStats = context.db
 354 | 			.select({
 355 | 				total: sql<number>`count(*)`,
 356 | 				active: sql<number>`sum(case when ${prescription.status} = 'active' then 1 else 0 end)`
 357 | 			})
 358 | 			.from(prescription)
 359 | 			.where(eq(prescription.patientId, patientId))
 360 | 			.get();
 361 |
 362 | 		// Get immunization count
 363 | 		const immunizationCount = context.db
 364 | 			.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
 365 | 			.from(immunization)
 366 | 			.where(and(eq(immunization.patientId, patientId), eq(immunization.isDeleted, false)))
 367 | 			.get();
 368 |
 369 | 		return {
 370 | 			totalAppointments: appointmentStats?.total ?? 0,
 371 | 			completedAppointments: appointmentStats?.completed ?? 0,
 372 | 			cancelledAppointments: appointmentStats?.cancelled ?? 0,
 373 | 			totalPayments: paymentStats?.total ?? 0,
 374 | 			paidAmount: paymentStats?.paidAmount ?? 0,
 375 | 			dueAmount: (paymentStats?.totalAmount ?? 0) - (paymentStats?.paidAmount ?? 0),
 376 | 			totalPrescriptions: prescriptionStats?.total ?? 0,
 377 | 			activePrescriptions: prescriptionStats?.active ?? 0,
 378 | 			totalImmunizations: immunizationCount?.count ?? 0,
 379 | 			lastVisit: appointmentStats?.lastVisit ?? null,
 380 | 			nextAppointment: appointmentStats?.nextAppointment ?? null
 381 | 		};
 382 | 	});
 383 |
 384 | // Search patients with advanced filters
 385 | export const searchPatients = protectedProcedure
 386 | 	.input(
 387 | 		z.object({
 388 | 			query: z.string(),
 389 | 			limit: z.number().default(20),
 390 | 			includeDeleted: z.boolean().default(false)
 391 | 		})
 392 | 	)
 393 | 	.output(z.array(PatientSchema))
 394 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 395 | 		const { query, limit, includeDeleted } = input;
 396 |
 397 | 		const whereClause = and(
 398 | 			eq(patient.clinicId, context.clinicId || ""),
 399 | 			includeDeleted ? undefined : eq(patient.isDeleted, false),
 400 | 			or(
 401 | 				like(patient.firstName, `%${query}%`),
 402 | 				like(patient.lastName, `%${query}%`),
 403 | 				like(patient.email, `%${query}%`),
 404 | 				like(patient.phone, `%${query}%`),
 405 | 				sql`${patient.firstName} || ' ' || ${patient.lastName} LIKE ${`%${query}%`}`
 406 | 			)
 407 | 		);
 408 |
 409 | 		const results = await db.query.patient.findMany({
 410 | 			where: whereClause,
 411 | 			limit,
 412 | 			orderBy: [asc(patient.firstName), asc(patient.lastName)]
 413 | 		});
 414 |
 415 | 		return results;
 416 | 	});
 417 |
 418 | // Bulk create patients
 419 | export const bulkCreatePatients = protectedProcedure
 420 | 	.input(z.array(PatientCreateSchema))
 421 | 	.output(z.array(PatientSchema))
 422 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 423 | 		const patientsToInsert = input.map(patientData => ({
 424 | 			...patientData,
 425 | 			id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 426 | 			clinicId: context.clinicId || "",
 427 | 			createdById: context.user?.id ?? "",
 428 | 			createdAt: new Date(),
 429 | 			updatedAt: new Date()
 430 | 		}));
 431 |
 432 | 		const results = await db.insert(patient).values(patientsToInsert).returning();
 433 | 		return results;
 434 | 	});
 435 |
 436 | // Export all procedures
 437 | export const patientRouter = {
 438 | 	createPatient,
 439 | 	getPatient,
 440 | 	getPatientWithDetails,
 441 | 	listPatients,
 442 | 	updatePatient,
 443 | 	deletePatient,
 444 | 	hardDeletePatient,
 445 | 	getPatientStats,
 446 | 	searchPatients,
 447 | 	bulkCreatePatients
 448 | };

```

`orpc/router/prescription.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // routers/prescription.router.ts
   2 | import { and, eq, like, or } from "drizzle-orm";
   3 | import { z } from "zod";
   4 |
   5 | import { db } from "../../db";
   6 | import { drug, prescribedItem, prescription } from "../../db/schema";
   7 | import { protectedProcedure } from "../init";
   8 |
   9 | // Create prescription with items
  10 | export const createPrescription = protectedProcedure
  11 | 	.input(
  12 | 		z.object({
  13 | 			medicalRecordId: z.string(),
  14 | 			patientId: z.string(),
  15 | 			doctorId: z.string(),
  16 | 			encounterId: z.string(),
  17 | 			items: z.array(
  18 | 				z.object({
  19 | 					drugId: z.string(),
  20 | 					dosageValue: z.number(),
  21 | 					dosageUnit: z.string(),
  22 | 					frequency: z.string(),
  23 | 					duration: z.string(),
  24 | 					instructions: z.string().optional()
  25 | 				})
  26 | 			)
  27 | 		})
  28 | 	)
  29 | 	.output(z.any())
  30 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  31 | 		const prescriptionId = crypto.randomUUID();
  32 |
  33 | 		// Create prescription
  34 | 		const [newPrescription] = await db
  35 | 			.insert(prescription)
  36 | 			.values({
  37 | 				id: prescriptionId,
  38 | 				medicalRecordId: input.medicalRecordId,
  39 | 				patientId: input.patientId,
  40 | 				doctorId: input.doctorId,
  41 | 				encounterId: input.encounterId,
  42 | 				clinicId: context.clinicId
  43 | 			})
  44 | 			.returning();
  45 |
  46 | 		// Create prescribed items
  47 | 		const prescribedItems = await Promise.all(
  48 | 			input.items.map(async item => {
  49 | 				const [prescribed] = await db
  50 | 					.insert(prescribedItem)
  51 | 					.values({
  52 | 						id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  53 | 						prescriptionId,
  54 | 						...item
  55 | 					})
  56 | 					.returning();
  57 | 				return prescribed;
  58 | 			})
  59 | 		);
  60 |
  61 | 		return { ...newPrescription, items: prescribedItems };
  62 | 	});
  63 |
  64 | // Get active prescriptions for patient
  65 | export const getPatientActivePrescriptions = protectedProcedure
  66 | 	.input(z.object({ patientId: z.string() }))
  67 | 	.output(z.array(z.any()))
  68 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  69 | 		return await db.query.prescription.findMany({
  70 | 			where: and(
  71 | 				eq(prescription.patientId, input.patientId),
  72 | 				eq(prescription.clinicId, context.clinicId),
  73 | 				eq(prescription.status, "active")
  74 | 			),
  75 | 			with: {
  76 | 				prescribedItems: {
  77 | 					with: {
  78 | 						drug: true
  79 | 					}
  80 | 				},
  81 | 				doctor: true
  82 | 			},
  83 | 			orderBy: (prescriptions, { desc }) => [desc(prescriptions.issuedDate)]
  84 | 		});
  85 | 	});
  86 |
  87 | export const listDrugs = protectedProcedure
  88 | 	.input(
  89 | 		z.object({
  90 | 			search: z.string().optional(),
  91 | 			limit: z.number().default(50)
  92 | 		})
  93 | 	)
  94 | 	.output(z.array(z.any()))
  95 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  96 | 		const { search, limit } = input;
  97 |
  98 | 		let whereClause;
  99 |
 100 | 		if (search) {
 101 | 			whereClause = or(
 102 | 				like(drug.name, `%${search}%`),
 103 | 				like(drug.genericName, `%${search}%`),
 104 | 				like(drug.brandName, `%${search}%`)
 105 | 			);
 106 | 		}
 107 |
 108 | 		const drugs = await db.query.drug.findMany({
 109 | 			where: whereClause,
 110 | 			limit,
 111 | 			orderBy: (drug, { asc }) => [asc(drug.name)]
 112 | 		});
 113 |
 114 | 		return drugs;
 115 | 	});
 116 |
 117 | export const updatePrescription = protectedProcedure
 118 | 	.input(
 119 | 		z.object({
 120 | 			prescriptionId: z.string(),
 121 | 			status: z.enum(["active", "completed", "cancelled"])
 122 | 		})
 123 | 	)
 124 | 	.output(z.any())
 125 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
 126 | 		const { prescriptionId, status } = input;
 127 | 		const updated = await db
 128 | 			.update(prescription)
 129 | 			.set({ status })
 130 | 			.where(and(eq(prescription.id, prescriptionId), eq(prescription.clinicId, context.clinicId)))
 131 | 			.returning();
 132 |
 133 | 		return updated[0];
 134 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/service.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { and, desc, eq, like } from "drizzle-orm";
   2 | import { z } from "zod";
   3 |
   4 | import { db } from "../../db";
   5 | import { service } from "../../db/schema";
   6 | import { protectedProcedure } from "../init";
   7 |
   8 | export const listServices = protectedProcedure
   9 | 	.input(
  10 | 		z.object({
  11 | 			search: z.string().optional(),
  12 | 			category: z.string().optional(),
  13 | 			page: z.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  14 | 			pageSize: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(50)
  15 | 		})
  16 | 	)
  17 | 	.output(
  18 | 		z.object({
  19 | 			services: z.array(z.any()),
  20 | 			total: z.number(),
  21 | 			page: z.number(),
  22 | 			totalPages: z.number()
  23 | 		})
  24 | 	)
  25 | 	.handler(async ({ input, context }) => {
  26 | 		const { search, category, page, pageSize } = input;
  27 | 		const clinicId = context.clinicId;
  28 |
  29 | 		const conditions = and(
  30 | 			eq(service.isDeleted, false),
  31 | 			clinicId ? eq(service.clinicId, clinicId) : undefined,
  32 | 			category ? eq(service.category, category) : undefined,
  33 | 			search ? like(service.serviceName, `%${search}%`) : undefined
  34 | 		);
  35 |
  36 | 		const services = await db.query.service.findMany({
  37 | 			where: conditions,
  38 | 			limit: pageSize,
  39 | 			offset: (page - 1) * pageSize,
  40 | 			orderBy: desc(service.createdAt)
  41 | 		});
  42 |
  43 | 		const total = await db.$count(service, conditions);
  44 |
  45 | 		return {
  46 | 			services,
  47 | 			total,
  48 | 			page,
  49 | 			totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageSize)
  50 | 		};
  51 | 	});
  52 |
  53 | export const getService = protectedProcedure
  54 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string() }))
  55 | 	.output(z.any())
  56 | 	.handler(async ({ input }) => {
  57 | 		const found = await db.query.service.findFirst({
  58 | 			where: and(eq(service.id, input.id), eq(service.isDeleted, false))
  59 | 		});
  60 | 		if (!found) throw new Error("Service not found");
  61 | 		return found;
  62 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/sse.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { eventIterator, os } from "@orpc/server";
   2 | import * as z from "zod";
   3 |
   4 | const MAX_EVENTS = 5;
   5 |
   6 | export const sse = os
   7 | 	.route({
   8 | 		method: "GET",
   9 | 		path: "/sse",
  10 | 		tags: ["SSE"],
  11 | 		summary: "Server-Sent Events"
  12 | 	})
  13 | 	.output(eventIterator(z.object({ time: z.date() })))
  14 | 	.handler(async function* () {
  15 | 		let count = 0;
  16 |
  17 | 		while (count < MAX_EVENTS) {
  18 | 			count++;
  19 | 			yield { time: new Date() };
  20 | 			await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1000));
  21 | 		}
  22 | 	});

```

`orpc/router/todos.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { os } from "@orpc/server";
   2 | import * as z from "zod";
   3 |
   4 | const todos = [
   5 | 	{ id: 1, name: "Get groceries" },
   6 | 	{ id: 2, name: "Buy a new phone" },
   7 | 	{ id: 3, name: "Finish the project" }
   8 | ];
   9 |
  10 | export const listTodos = os.input(z.object({})).handler(() => {
  11 | 	return todos;
  12 | });
  13 |
  14 | export const addTodo = os.input(z.object({ name: z.string() })).handler(({ input }) => {
  15 | 	const newTodo = { id: todos.length + 1, name: input.name };
  16 | 	todos.push(newTodo);
  17 | 	return newTodo;
  18 | });
```

`orpc/router/vital-sign.ts`:

```ts
   1 | // routers/vital-signs.router.ts
   2 |
   3 | import type {} from "better-auth";
   4 | import { startOfDay, subDays } from "date-fns";
   5 | import { and, between, desc, eq, gte, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
   6 | import { z } from "zod";
   7 |
   8 | import { checkVitalSignsAccess } from "../../db/queries";
   9 | import { diagnosis, doctor, medicalRecord, patient, vitalSign } from "../../db/schema";
  10 | import { clinicProcedure, protectedProcedure } from "../init";
  11 |
  12 | // ============================================
  13 | // Schemas
  14 | // ============================================
  15 |
  16 | const CreateVitalSignsSchema = z.object({
  17 | 	patientId: z.string(),
  18 | 	medicalId: z.string(),
  19 | 	encounterId: z.string().optional(),
  20 | 	recordedAt: z.date().default(() => new Date()),
  21 | 	bodyTemperature: z.number().optional(),
  22 | 	systolic: z.number().optional(),
  23 | 	diastolic: z.number().optional(),
  24 | 	heartRate: z.number().optional(),
  25 | 	respiratoryRate: z.number().optional(),
  26 | 	oxygenSaturation: z.number().optional(),
  27 | 	gender: z.string().optional(),
  28 | 	notes: z.string().optional()
  29 | });
  30 |
  31 | const UpdateVitalSignsSchema = CreateVitalSignsSchema.partial().extend({
  32 | 	id: z.string()
  33 | });
  34 |
  35 | const ListVitalSignsSchema = z.object({
  36 | 	patientId: z.string(),
  37 | 	page: z.number().int().min(1).default(1),
  38 | 	pageSize: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(20),
  39 | 	fromDate: z.date().optional(),
  40 | 	toDate: z.date().optional(),
  41 | 	encounterId: z.string().optional()
  42 | });
  43 |
  44 | const VitalSignsTrendSchema = z.object({
  45 | 	patientId: z.string(),
  46 | 	days: z.number().int().min(1).max(365).default(30),
  47 | 	metrics: z
  48 | 		.array(z.enum(["temperature", "bloodPressure", "heartRate", "oxygen"]))
  49 | 		.default(["temperature", "heartRate", "oxygen"])
  50 | });
  51 |
  52 | // ============================================
  53 | // Vital Signs Procedures
  54 | // ============================================
  55 |
  56 | export const createVitalSigns = protectedProcedure.input(CreateVitalSignsSchema).handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
  57 | 	const { clinicId, db } = context;
  58 |
  59 | 	if (!clinicId) {
  60 | 		throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
  61 | 	}
  62 |
  63 | 	console.info("Recording vital signs", {
  64 | 		patientId: input.patientId,
  65 | 		medicalId: input.medicalId
  66 | 	});
  67 |
  68 | 	// Verify medical record exists
  69 | 	const medicalRecordExists = await db.query.medicalRecord.findFirst({
  70 | 		where: and(
  71 | 			eq(medicalRecord.id, input.medicalId),
  72 | 			eq(medicalRecord.clinicId, clinicId),
  73 | 			eq(medicalRecord.isDeleted, false)
  74 | 		)
  75 | 	});
  76 |
  77 | 	if (!medicalRecordExists) {
  78 | 		throw new Error("Medical record not found");
  79 | 	}
  80 |
  81 | 	// Verify patient exists
  82 | 	const patientData = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
  83 | 		where: and(eq(patient.id, input.patientId), eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId), eq(patient.isDeleted, false))
  84 | 	});
  85 |
  86 | 	if (!patientData) {
  87 | 		throw new Error("Patient not found");
  88 | 	}
  89 |
  90 | 	// Check if vital signs already exist for this medical record
  91 | 	const existing = await db.query.vitalSign.findFirst({
  92 | 		where: and(eq(vitalSign.medicalId, input.medicalId), eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId))
  93 | 	});
  94 |
  95 | 	if (existing) {
  96 | 		throw new Error("Vital signs already recorded for this medical record");
  97 | 	}
  98 |
  99 | 	// Calculate age
 100 | 	const today = new Date();
 101 | 	const ageDays = Math.floor((today.getTime() - patientData.dateOfBirth.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
 102 | 	const ageMonths = Math.floor(ageDays / 30.44);
 103 |
 104 | 	const [vitalSignsRecord] = await db
 105 | 		.insert(vitalSign)
 106 | 		.values({
 107 | 			id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 108 | 			clinicId: clinicId ?? "",
 109 | 			patientId: input.patientId,
 110 | 			medicalId: input.medicalId,
 111 | 			encounterId: input.encounterId,
 112 | 			recordedAt: input.recordedAt,
 113 | 			bodyTemperature: input.bodyTemperature,
 114 | 			systolic: input.systolic,
 115 | 			diastolic: input.diastolic,
 116 | 			heartRate: input.heartRate,
 117 | 			respiratoryRate: input.respiratoryRate,
 118 | 			oxygenSaturation: input.oxygenSaturation,
 119 | 			gender: input.gender ?? patientData.gender,
 120 | 			notes: input.notes,
 121 | 			ageDays,
 122 | 			ageMonths,
 123 | 			createdAt: new Date(),
 124 | 			updatedAt: new Date()
 125 | 		})
 126 | 		.returning();
 127 |
 128 | 	// Update medical record with vital signs reference
 129 | 	await db.update(medicalRecord).set({ updatedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(medicalRecord.id, input.medicalId));
 130 |
 131 | 	console.info("Vital signs recorded successfully", { vitalSignsId: vitalSignsRecord.id });
 132 |
 133 | 	// Fetch the complete record with relations
 134 | 	const completeRecord = await db.query.vitalSign.findFirst({
 135 | 		where: eq(vitalSign.id, vitalSignsRecord.id),
 136 | 		with: {
 137 | 			patient: {
 138 | 				with: {
 139 | 					user: {
 140 | 						columns: {
 141 | 							name: true,
 142 | 							email: true
 143 | 						}
 144 | 					}
 145 | 				}
 146 | 			},
 147 | 			medical: true,
 148 | 			encounter: true
 149 | 		}
 150 | 	});
 151 |
 152 | 	return completeRecord;
 153 | });
 154 |
 155 | export const getPatientHistory = protectedProcedure
 156 | 	.input(z.object({ patientId: z.string() }))
 157 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 158 | 		const { clinicId, db } = context;
 159 | 		const { patientId } = input;
 160 |
 161 | 		if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");

 164 | 			db.query.vitalSign.findMany({

 166 | 				with: {
 167 | 					medical: {
 168 | 						columns: {
 169 | 							id: true,
 170 | 							createdAt: true
 171 | 						}
 172 | 					},

 174 | 						columns: {
 175 | 							id: true,

 180 | 				orderBy: [desc(vitalSign.recordedAt)]
 181 | 			}),

 183 | 				where: and(eq(vitalSign.patientId, patientId), eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId)),
 184 | 				orderBy: [vitalSign.recordedAt],
 185 | 				limit: 50
 186 | 			})
 187 | 		]);

 190 | 	});

 192 | export const updateVitalSigns = protectedProcedure.input(UpdateVitalSignsSchema).handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 193 | 	const { clinicId, db } = context;
 194 | 	const { id, ...updateData } = input;
 195 |
 196 | 	if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 197 |
 198 | 	console.info("Updating vital signs", { vitalSignsId: id });
 199 |
 200 | 	const existing = await db.query.vitalSign.findFirst({
 201 | 		where: and(eq(vitalSign.id, id), eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId))
 202 | 	});
 203 |
 204 | 	if (!existing) {
 205 | 		throw new Error("Vital signs not found");
 206 | 	}
 207 |
 208 | 	const [updated] = await db
 209 | 		.update(vitalSign)
 210 | 		.set({
 211 | 			...updateData,
 212 | 			updatedAt: new Date()
 213 | 		})
 214 | 		.where(eq(vitalSign.id, id))
 215 | 		.returning();

 217 | 	console.info("Vital signs updated", { vitalSignsId: id });
 218 | 	return updated;
 219 | });

 221 | export const getVitalSignsById = protectedProcedure

 223 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 224 | 		const { clinicId, db } = context;
 225 | 		const { id } = input;
 226 |
 227 | 		if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 228 |
 229 | 		const vitalSignsRecord = await db.query.vitalSign.findFirst({
 230 | 			where: and(eq(vitalSign.id, id), eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId)),
 231 | 			with: {
 232 | 				patient: {
 233 | 					with: {

 239 | 						}
 240 | 					}

 245 | 							with: {

 270 | export const listVitalSignsByPatient = protectedProcedure
 271 | 	.input(ListVitalSignsSchema)
 272 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 273 | 		const { clinicId, db, user } = context;
 274 | 		const { patientId, page, pageSize, fromDate, toDate, encounterId } = input;
 275 |
 276 | 		if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 277 |
 278 | 		console.debug("Listing vital signs", { patientId, page, pageSize });
 279 |
 280 | 		// Check access permissions
 281 | 		const hasAccess = await checkVitalSignsAccess(user?.id ?? "", patientId);
 282 | 		if (!hasAccess && user?.role !== "admin" && user?.role !== "staff") {
 283 | 			throw new Error("You don't have access to this patient's vital signs");
 284 | 		}
 285 |
 286 | 		const conditions = and(
 287 | 			eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId ?? ""),
 288 | 			eq(vitalSign.patientId, patientId),
 289 | 			fromDate && toDate ? between(vitalSign.recordedAt, fromDate, toDate) : undefined,
 290 | 			encounterId ? eq(vitalSign.encounterId, encounterId) : undefined
 291 | 		);
 292 |
 293 | 		// Fetch vital signs
 294 |
 295 | 		const vitalSignsList = await db.query.vitalSign.findMany({
 296 | 			where: conditions,
 297 | 			with: {
 298 | 				medical: {
 299 | 					columns: {
 300 | 						id: true,
 301 | 						createdAt: true
 302 | 					}
 303 | 				},
 304 | 				encounter: {
 305 | 					columns: {
 306 | 						id: true,
 307 | 						diagnosis: true
 308 | 					}
 309 | 				}
 310 | 			},
 311 | 			limit: pageSize,
 312 | 			offset: (page - 1) * pageSize,
 313 | 			orderBy: [desc(vitalSign.recordedAt)]
 314 | 		});
 315 |
 316 | 		const [totalResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` }).from(vitalSign).where(conditions);
 317 |
 318 | 		const total = totalResult?.count ?? 0;
 319 |
 320 | 		return {
 321 | 			vitalSigns: vitalSignsList,
 322 | 			pagination: {
 323 | 				page,
 324 | 				pageSize,
 325 | 				total,
 326 | 				totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageSize)
 327 | 			}
 328 | 		};
 329 | 	});

 331 | export const getLatestVitalSigns = protectedProcedure
 332 | 	.input(z.object({ patientId: z.string() }))
 333 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 334 | 		const { clinicId, db } = context;
 335 | 		const { patientId } = input;
 336 |
 337 | 		if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 338 |
 339 | 		const latestVitalSigns = await db.query.vitalSign.findFirst({
 340 | 			where: and(eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId), eq(vitalSign.patientId, patientId)),
 341 | 			orderBy: [desc(vitalSign.recordedAt)]
 342 | 		});
 343 |
 344 | 		return latestVitalSigns ?? null;
 345 | 	});
 346 |
 347 | export const getVitalSignsTrend = protectedProcedure
 348 | 	.input(VitalSignsTrendSchema)
 349 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 350 | 		const { clinicId, db } = context;
 351 | 		const { patientId, days, metrics } = input;
 352 |
 353 | 		const startDate = startOfDay(subDays(new Date(), days));
 354 |
 355 | 		if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 356 |
 357 | 		console.debug("Getting vital signs trend", { patientId, days, metrics });
 358 |
 359 | 		const vitalSignsList = await db.query.vitalSign.findMany({
 360 | 			where: and(
 361 | 				eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId),
 362 | 				eq(vitalSign.patientId, patientId),
 363 | 				gte(vitalSign.recordedAt, startDate)
 364 | 			),
 365 | 			orderBy: [vitalSign.recordedAt]
 366 | 		});
 367 |
 368 | 		const trend: {
 369 | 			dates: string[];
 370 | 			temperature: Array<{ value: number; date: string }>;
 371 | 			bloodPressure: Array<{ systolic: number; diastolic: number; date: string }>;
 372 | 			heartRate: Array<{ value: number; date: string }>;
 373 | 			oxygen: Array<{ value: number; date: string }>;
 374 | 		} = {
 375 | 			dates: [],
 376 | 			temperature: [],
 377 | 			bloodPressure: [],
 378 | 			heartRate: [],
 379 | 			oxygen: []
 380 | 		};
 381 |
 382 | 		vitalSignsList.forEach(vs => {
 383 | 			const date = vs.recordedAt.toISOString();
 384 | 			trend.dates.push(date);
 385 |
 386 | 			if (metrics.includes("temperature") && vs.bodyTemperature) {
 387 | 				trend.temperature.push({ value: vs.bodyTemperature, date });
 388 | 			}
 389 |
 390 | 			if (metrics.includes("bloodPressure") && vs.systolic && vs.diastolic) {
 391 | 				trend.bloodPressure.push({ systolic: vs.systolic, diastolic: vs.diastolic, date });
 392 | 			}
 393 |
 394 | 			if (metrics.includes("heartRate") && vs.heartRate) {
 395 | 				trend.heartRate.push({ value: vs.heartRate, date });
 396 | 			}
 397 |
 398 | 			if (metrics.includes("oxygen") && vs.oxygenSaturation) {
 399 | 				trend.oxygen.push({ value: vs.oxygenSaturation, date });
 400 | 			}
 401 | 		});
 402 |
 403 | 		// Calculate summary statistics
 404 | 		const summary = {
 405 | 			temperature: calculateSummary(trend.temperature.map(t => t.value)),
 406 | 			heartRate: calculateSummary(trend.heartRate.map(h => h.value)),
 407 | 			oxygenSaturation: calculateSummary(trend.oxygen.map(o => o.value))
 408 | 		};
 409 |
 410 | 		return {
 411 | 			patientId,
 412 | 			period: { days, startDate, endDate: new Date() },
 413 | 			trend,
 414 | 			summary,
 415 | 			totalReadings: vitalSignsList.length
 416 | 		};
 417 | 	});
 418 |
 419 | export const deleteVitalSigns = protectedProcedure
 420 | 	.input(z.object({ id: z.string(), reason: z.string().optional() }))
 421 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 422 | 		const { clinicId, db } = context;
 423 | 		const { id, reason } = input;
 424 |
 425 | 		if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 426 |
 427 | 		console.warn("Deleting vital signs", { vitalSignsId: id, reason });
 428 |
 429 | 		const existing = await db.query.vitalSign.findFirst({
 430 | 			where: and(eq(vitalSign.id, id), eq(vitalSign.clinicId, clinicId)),
 431 | 			with: {
 432 | 				patient: {
 433 | 					with: {
 434 | 						user: true
 435 | 					}
 436 | 				}
 437 | 			}
 438 | 		});
 439 |
 440 | 		if (!existing) {
 441 | 			throw new Error("Vital signs not found");
 442 | 		}
 443 |
 444 | 		await db.delete(vitalSign).where(eq(vitalSign.id, id));
 445 |
 446 | 		return { success: true, message: "Vital signs deleted successfully" };
 447 | 	});

 494 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {

 498 | 			throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 499 | 		}

 501 | 		console.info("Creating diagnosis", {
 502 | 			patientId: input.patientId,
 503 | 			doctorId: input.doctorId,
 504 | 			diagnosis: input.diagnosis
 505 | 		});
 506 |
 507 | 		// Verify medical record exists
 508 | 		const medicalRecordExists = await db.query.medicalRecord.findFirst({
 509 | 			where: and(
 510 | 				eq(medicalRecord.id, input.medicalId),
 511 | 				eq(medicalRecord.clinicId, clinicId),
 512 | 				eq(medicalRecord.isDeleted, false)
 513 | 			)
 514 | 		});
 515 |

 517 | 			throw new Error("Medical record not found");
 518 | 		}

 521 | 		const patientData = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
 522 | 			where: and(eq(patient.id, input.patientId), eq(patient.clinicId, clinicId), eq(patient.isDeleted, false))
 523 | 		});
 524 |
 525 | 		if (!patientData) {
 526 | 			throw new Error("Patient not found");
 527 | 		}
 528 |
 529 | 		// Verify doctor exists
 530 | 		const doctorData = await db.query.doctor.findFirst({
 531 | 			where: and(eq(doctor.id, input.doctorId), eq(doctor.clinicId, clinicId), eq(doctor.isDeleted, false))
 532 | 		});
 533 |
 534 | 		if (!doctorData) {
 535 | 			throw new Error("Doctor not found");
 536 | 		}
 537 |
 538 | 		// Check if diagnosis already exists for this medical record
 539 | 		const existing = await db.query.diagnosis.findFirst({
 540 | 			where: and(
 541 | 				eq(diagnosis.medicalId, input.medicalId),
 542 | 				eq(diagnosis.clinicId, clinicId),
 543 | 				eq(diagnosis.isDeleted, false)
 544 | 			)
 545 | 		});
 546 |
 547 | 		if (existing) {
 548 | 			throw new Error("Diagnosis already exists for this medical record");
 549 | 		}

 552 | 			.insert(diagnosis)
 553 | 			.values({
 554 | 				id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 555 | 				clinicId,
 556 | 				patientId: input.patientId,
 557 | 				doctorId: input.doctorId,
 558 | 				appointmentId: input.appointmentId,
 559 | 				medicalId: input.medicalId,
 560 | 				date: input.date,
 561 | 				type: input.type ?? input.typeOfEncounter,
 562 | 				diagnosis: input.diagnosis,
 563 | 				treatment: input.treatment,
 564 | 				notes: input.notes,
 565 | 				symptoms: input.symptoms ?? "",
 566 | 				prescribedMedications: input.prescribedMedications,
 567 | 				followUpPlan: input.followUpPlan,
 568 | 				status: input.status,
 569 | 				// Removed direct typeOfEncounter insert as it's not in the DB schema
 570 | 				createdAt: new Date(),
 571 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 572 | 			})
 573 | 			.returning();
 574 |
 575 | 		// Update medical record with diagnosis reference
 576 | 		await db.update(medicalRecord).set({ updatedAt: new Date() }).where(eq(medicalRecord.id, input.medicalId));
 577 |
 578 | 		// Update appointment status if appointment exists
 579 | 		if (input.appointmentId) {
 580 | 			await db
 581 | 				.update(medicalRecord) // Assuming appointment updates through medical record
 582 | 				.set({ updatedAt: new Date() })
 583 | 				.where(eq(medicalRecord.id, input.medicalId));
 584 | 		}
 585 |
 586 | 		console.info("Diagnosis created successfully", { diagnosisId: diagnosisRecord.id });
 587 |
 588 | 		// Fetch complete record with relations
 589 | 		const completeRecord = await db.query.diagnosis.findFirst({
 590 | 			where: eq(diagnosis.id, diagnosisRecord.id),
 591 | 			with: {
 592 | 				patient: {
 593 | 					with: {
 594 | 						user: {
 595 | 							columns: {
 596 | 								name: true,
 597 | 								email: true
 598 | 							}
 599 | 						}
 600 | 					}
 601 | 				},
 602 | 				doctor: {
 603 | 					with: {
 604 | 						user: {
 605 | 							columns: {
 606 | 								name: true,
 607 | 								email: true
 608 | 							}
 609 | 						}
 610 | 					}
 611 | 				},
 612 | 				appointment: true,
 613 | 				medical: true,
 614 | 				vitalSigns: true
 615 | 			}
 616 | 		});
 617 |
 618 | 		return completeRecord;
 619 | 	});

 702 | export const listDiagnoses = diagnosisProcedure.input(ListDiagnosesFullSchema).handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 703 | 	const { clinicId, db, user } = context;
 704 | 	const { patientId, doctorId, page, pageSize, fromDate, toDate, status, typeOfEncounter, search } = input;
 705 |
 706 | 	if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 707 |
 708 | 	console.debug("Listing diagnoses", { patientId, doctorId, page, pageSize });
 709 |
 710 | 	let conditions = and(
 711 | 		eq(diagnosis.clinicId, clinicId),
 712 | 		eq(diagnosis.isDeleted, false),
 713 | 		patientId ? eq(diagnosis.patientId, patientId) : undefined,
 714 | 		doctorId ? eq(diagnosis.doctorId, doctorId) : undefined,
 715 | 		fromDate && toDate ? between(diagnosis.date, fromDate, toDate) : undefined,
 716 | 		status ? eq(diagnosis.status, status) : undefined,
 717 | 		typeOfEncounter ? eq(diagnosis.type, typeOfEncounter) : undefined
 718 | 	);
 719 |
 720 | 	// Role-based filtering
 721 | 	if (user?.role === "patient") {
 722 | 		const patientData = await db.query.patient.findFirst({
 723 | 			where: eq(patient.userId, user.id)
 724 | 		});
 725 | 		if (patientData) {
 726 | 			conditions = and(conditions, eq(diagnosis.patientId, patientData.id));
 727 | 		}
 728 | 	} else if (user?.role === "doctor") {
 729 | 		const doctorData = await db.query.doctor.findFirst({
 730 | 			where: eq(doctor.userId, user.id)
 731 | 		});
 732 | 		if (doctorData) {
 733 | 			conditions = and(conditions, eq(diagnosis.doctorId, doctorData.id));
 734 | 		}
 735 | 	}
 736 |
 737 | 	let diagnosesList = await db.query.diagnosis.findMany({
 738 | 		where: conditions,
 739 | 		with: {
 740 | 			patient: {
 741 | 				with: {
 742 | 					user: {
 743 | 						columns: {
 744 | 							name: true
 745 | 						}
 746 | 					}
 747 | 				}
 748 | 			},
 749 | 			doctor: {
 750 | 				with: {
 751 | 					user: {
 752 | 						columns: {
 753 | 							name: true
 754 | 						}
 755 | 					}
 756 | 				}
 757 | 			},
 758 | 			appointment: true,
 759 | 			medical: true,
 760 | 			vitalSigns: true
 761 | 		},
 762 | 		limit: pageSize,
 763 | 		offset: (page - 1) * pageSize,
 764 | 		orderBy: [desc(diagnosis.date)]
 765 | 	});
 766 |
 767 | 	// Apply search filter
 768 | 	if (search) {
 769 | 		const searchLower = search.toLowerCase();
 770 | 		diagnosesList = diagnosesList.filter(
 771 | 			d =>
 772 | 				d.diagnosis?.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower) ||
 773 | 				d.treatment?.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower) ||
 774 | 				d.symptoms?.toLowerCase().includes(searchLower)
 775 | 		);
 776 | 	}
 777 |
 778 | 	const [totalResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` }).from(diagnosis).where(conditions);
 779 |
 780 | 	const total = totalResult?.count ?? 0;
 781 |
 782 | 	return {
 783 | 		diagnoses: diagnosesList,
 784 | 		pagination: {
 785 | 			page,
 786 | 			pageSize,
 787 | 			total,
 788 | 			totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pageSize)
 789 | 		}
 790 | 	};
 791 | });

 802 |
 803 | 		const existing = await db.query.diagnosis.findFirst({
 804 | 			where: and(eq(diagnosis.id, id), eq(diagnosis.clinicId, clinicId), eq(diagnosis.isDeleted, false))
 805 | 		});
 806 |
 807 | 		if (!existing) {
 808 | 			throw new Error("Diagnosis not found");
 809 | 		}
 810 |

 812 | 			.update(diagnosis)
 813 | 			.set({
 814 | 				isDeleted: true,
 815 | 				deletedAt: new Date(),
 816 | 				updatedAt: new Date()
 817 | 			})

 825 | export const getDiagnosisStatistics = protectedProcedure
 826 | 	.input(
 827 | 		z.object({
 828 | 			startDate: z.coerce.date(),
 829 | 			endDate: z.coerce.date(),
 830 | 			groupBy: z.enum(["doctor", "type", "status"]).default("doctor")
 831 | 		})
 832 | 	)
 833 | 	.handler(async ({ context, input }) => {
 834 | 		const { clinicId, db } = context;
 835 | 		const { startDate, endDate, groupBy } = input;
 836 |
 837 | 		if (!clinicId) throw new Error("Clinic context missing");
 838 |
 839 | 		console.info("Generating diagnosis statistics", { startDate, endDate, groupBy });
 840 |
 841 | 		const whereConditions = and(
 842 | 			eq(diagnosis.clinicId, clinicId),
 843 | 			eq(diagnosis.isDeleted, false),
 844 | 			between(diagnosis.date, startDate, endDate)
 845 | 		);
 846 |
 847 | 		const [totalResult] = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` }).from(diagnosis).where(whereConditions);
 848 |
 849 | 		const byType = await db
 850 | 			.select({
 851 | 				type: diagnosis.type,
 852 | 				count: sql<number>`count(*)`
 853 | 			})
 854 | 			.from(diagnosis)
 855 | 			.where(whereConditions)
 856 | 			.groupBy(diagnosis.type);
 857 |
 858 | 		const byStatus = await db
 859 | 			.select({
 860 | 				status: diagnosis.status,
 861 | 				count: sql<number>`count(*)`
 862 | 			})
 863 | 			.from(diagnosis)
 864 | 			.where(whereConditions)
 865 | 			.groupBy(diagnosis.status);
 866 |
 867 | 		const byDoctor = await db
 868 | 			.select({
 869 | 				doctorId: diagnosis.doctorId,
 870 | 				count: sql<number>`count(*)`
 871 | 			})
 872 | 			.from(diagnosis)
 873 | 			.where(whereConditions)
 874 | 			.groupBy(diagnosis.doctorId)
 875 | 			.orderBy(sql`count(*) DESC`)
 876 | 			.limit(10);
 877 |
 878 | 		// Get doctor names for the top doctors
 879 | 		const doctorIds = byDoctor.map(d => d.doctorId).filter(Boolean);
 880 | 		const doctorsList =
 881 | 			doctorIds.length > 0
 882 | 				? await db.query.doctor.findMany({
 883 | 						where: and(eq(doctor.clinicId, clinicId), inArray(doctor.id, doctorIds as string[])),
 884 | 						with: {
 885 | 							user: {
 886 | 								columns: {
 887 | 									name: true
 888 | 								}
 889 | 							}
 890 | 						}
 891 | 					})
 892 | 				: [];
 893 |
 894 | 		const doctorMap = new Map(doctorsList.map(d => [d.id, d.user?.name || d.name]));
 895 |
 896 | 		const statistics = {
 897 | 			period: { startDate, endDate },
 898 | 			total: totalResult?.count ?? 0,
 899 | 			distribution: {
 900 | 				byType: byType.map(t => ({ type: t.type, count: Number(t.count) })),
 901 | 				byStatus: byStatus.map(s => ({ status: s.status, count: Number(s.count) }))
 902 | 			},
 903 | 			topDoctors: byDoctor.map(d => ({
 904 | 				doctorId: d.doctorId,
 905 | 				doctorName: doctorMap.get(d.doctorId) || "Unknown",
 906 | 				count: Number(d.count)
 907 | 			}))
 908 | 		};
 909 |
 910 | 		console.info("Diagnosis statistics generated", statistics);
 911 | 		return statistics;
 912 | 	});
 913 |
 914 | // ============================================
 915 | // Helper Functions
 916 | // ============================================
 917 |
 918 | function calculateSummary(values: number[]): {
 919 | 	min: number | null;
 920 | 	max: number | null;
 921 | 	avg: number | null;
 922 | 	latest: number | null;
 923 | } {
 924 | 	if (values.length === 0) {
 925 | 		return { min: null, max: null, avg: null, latest: null };
 926 | 	}
 927 | 	return {
 928 | 		min: Math.min(...values),
 929 | 		max: Math.max(...values),
 930 | 		avg: values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length,
 931 | 		latest: values[values.length - 1]
 932 | 	};
 933 | }

```

`orpc/schema.ts`:


`polyfill.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { File } from "node:buffer";
   2 |
   3 | /**
   4 |  * This file aims to polyfill missing APIs in Node.js 18 that oRPC depends on.
   5 |  *
   6 |  * Since Stackblitz runs on Node.js 18, these polyfills ensure oRPC works in that environment.
   7 |  * If you're running oRPC locally, please use Node.js 20 or later for full compatibility.
   8 |  */
   9 |
  10 | /**
  11 |  * Note: Stackblitz provides an emulated Node.js environment with inherent limitations.
  12 |  * If you encounter issues, please test on a local setup with Node.js 20 or later before reporting them.
  13 |  */
  14 |
  15 | /**
  16 |  * The `oz.file()` schema depends on the `File` API.
  17 |  * If you're not using `oz.file()`, you can safely remove this polyfill.
  18 |  */
  19 | if (typeof globalThis.File === "undefined") {
  20 | 	globalThis.File = File as any;
  21 | }
```

```tsx
   2 | import { setupRouterSsrQueryIntegration } from "@tanstack/react-router-ssr-query";
   3 |
   4 | import { getContext } from "./integrations/tanstack-query/root-provider";
   5 | import { routeTree } from "./routeTree.gen";
   6 |
   7 | export function getRouter() {
   8 | 	const context = getContext();
   9 |
  10 | 	const router = createTanStackRouter({
  11 | 		routeTree,
  12 | 		context,
  13 | 		scrollRestoration: true,
  14 | 		defaultPreload: "intent",
  15 | 		defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0
  16 | 	});
  17 |
  18 | 	setupRouterSsrQueryIntegration({ router, queryClient: context.queryClient });
  19 |
  20 | 	return router;
  21 | }
  22 |
  23 | declare module "@tanstack/react-router" {
  24 | 	interface Register {
  25 | 		router: ReturnType<typeof getRouter>;
  26 | 	}
  27 | }

```

```tsx
   1 | import { TanStackDevtools } from "@tanstack/react-devtools";
   2 | import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
   3 | import { createRootRouteWithContext, HeadContent, Scripts } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   4 | import { TanStackRouterDevtoolsPanel } from "@tanstack/react-router-devtools";
   5 |
   6 | import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
   7 |
   8 | import Footer from "../components/Footer";
   9 | import Header from "../components/Header";
  10 | import TanStackQueryDevtools from "../integrations/tanstack-query/devtools";
  11 | import appCss from "../styles.css?url";
  12 |
  13 | interface MyRouterContext {
  14 | 	queryClient: QueryClient;
  15 | }
  16 |
  17 | const THEME_INIT_SCRIPT = `(function(){try{var stored=window.localStorage.getItem('theme');var mode=(stored==='light'||stored==='dark'||stored==='auto')?stored:'auto';var prefersDark=window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;var resolved=mode==='auto'?(prefersDark?'dark':'light'):mode;var root=document.documentElement;root.classList.remove('light','dark');root.classList.add(resolved);if(mode==='auto'){root.removeAttribute('data-theme')}else{root.setAttribute('data-theme',mode)}root.style.colorScheme=resolved;}catch(e){}})();`;
  18 |
  19 | export const Route = createRootRouteWithContext<MyRouterContext>()({
  20 | 	head: () => ({

  52 | 			</head>
```

`routes/api.$.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import "#/polyfill";
   2 |
   3 | import { SmartCoercionPlugin } from "@orpc/json-schema";
   4 | import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
   5 | import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
   6 | import { onError } from "@orpc/server";
   7 | import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";
   8 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   9 | import { getRequestHeaders } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";
  10 |
  11 | import router from "#/orpc/router";
  12 | import { TodoSchema } from "#/orpc/schema";
  13 |
  14 | import { createContext } from "../orpc/context";
  15 |
  16 | const handler = new OpenAPIHandler(router, {
  17 | 	interceptors: [
  18 | 		onError(error => {
  19 | 			console.error(error);
  20 | 		})
  21 | 	],
  22 | 	plugins: [
  23 | 		new SmartCoercionPlugin({
  24 | 			schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()]
  25 | 		}),
  26 | 		new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
  27 | 			schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
  28 | 			specGenerateOptions: {
  29 | 				info: {
  30 | 					title: "TanStack ORPC Playground",
  31 | 					version: "1.0.0"
  32 | 				},
  33 | 				commonSchemas: {
  34 | 					Todo: { schema: TodoSchema },
  35 | 					UndefinedError: { error: "UndefinedError" }
  36 | 				},
  37 | 				security: [{ bearerAuth: [] }],
  38 | 				components: {
  39 | 					securitySchemes: {
  40 | 						bearerAuth: {
  41 | 							type: "http",
  42 | 							scheme: "bearer"
  43 | 						}
  44 | 					}
  45 | 				}
  46 | 			},
  47 | 			docsConfig: {
  48 | 				authentication: {
  49 | 					securitySchemes: {
  50 | 						bearerAuth: {
  51 | 							token: "default-token"
  52 | 						}
  53 | 					}
  54 | 				}
  55 | 			}
  56 | 		})
  57 | 	]
  58 | });
  59 |
  60 | async function handle({ request }: { request: Request }) {
  61 | 	const { response } = await handler.handle(request, {
  62 | 		prefix: "/api",
  63 | 		context: await createContext({ headers: getRequestHeaders() })
  64 | 	});
  65 |
  66 | 	return response ?? new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
  67 | }
  68 |
  69 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/api/$")({
  70 | 	server: {
  71 | 		handlers: {
  72 | 			HEAD: handle,
  73 | 			GET: handle,
  74 | 			POST: handle,
  75 | 			PUT: handle,
  76 | 			PATCH: handle,
  77 | 			DELETE: handle
  78 | 		}
  79 | 	}
  80 | });
```
