`routes/demo/api.mcp-todos.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   2 |
   3 | import { addTodo, getTodos, subscribeToTodos } from "#/mcp-todos";
   4 |
   5 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/demo/api/mcp-todos")({
   6 | 	server: {
   7 | 		handlers: {
   8 | 			GET: () => {
   9 | 				const stream = new ReadableStream({
  10 | 					start(controller) {
  11 | 						function ping() {
  12 | 							try {
  13 | 								controller.enqueue("event: ping\n\n");
  14 | 								setTimeout(ping, 1000);
  15 | 							} catch {}
  16 | 						}
  17 | 						ping();
  18 | 						const unsubscribe = subscribeToTodos(todos => {
  19 | 							controller.enqueue(`data: ${JSON.stringify(todos)}\n\n`);
  20 | 						});
  21 | 						const todos = getTodos();
  22 | 						controller.enqueue(`data: ${JSON.stringify(todos)}\n\n`);
  23 | 						return () => unsubscribe();
  24 | 					}
  25 | 				});
  26 | 				return new Response(stream, {
  27 | 					headers: { "Content-Type": "text/event-stream" }
  28 | 				});
  29 | 			},
  30 | 			POST: async ({ request }) => {
  31 | 				const { title } = await request.json();
  32 | 				addTodo(title);
  33 | 				return Response.json(getTodos());
  34 | 			}
  35 | 		}
  36 | 	}
  37 | });

```

`routes/demo/better-auth.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   2 | import { useState } from "react";
   3 |
   4 | import { authClient } from "#/lib/auth-client";
   5 |
   6 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/demo/better-auth")({
   7 | 	component: BetterAuthDemo
   8 | });
   9 |
  10 | function BetterAuthDemo() {
  11 | 	const { data: session, isPending } = authClient.useSession();
  12 | 	const [isSignUp, setIsSignUp] = useState(false);
  13 | 	const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  14 | 	const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  15 | 	const [name, setName] = useState("");
  16 | 	const [error, setError] = useState("");
  17 | 	const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  18 |
  19 | 	if (isPending) {
  20 | 		return (
  21 | 			<div className='flex items-center justify-center py-10'>
  22 | 				<div className='h-5 w-5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-neutral-200 border-t-neutral-900 dark:border-neutral-800 dark:border-t-neutral-100' />
  23 | 			</div>
  24 | 		);
  25 | 	}
  26 |
  27 | 	if (session?.user) {
  28 | 		return (
  29 | 			<div className='flex justify-center px-4 py-10'>
  30 | 				<div className='w-full max-w-md space-y-6 p-6'>
  31 | 					<div className='space-y-1.5'>
  32 | 						<h1 className='font-semibold text-lg leading-none tracking-tight'>Welcome back</h1>
  33 | 						<p className='text-neutral-500 text-sm dark:text-neutral-400'>
  34 | 							You're signed in as {session.user.email}
  35 | 						</p>
  36 | 					</div>
  37 |
  38 | 					<div className='flex items-center gap-3'>
  39 | 						{session.user.image ? (
  40 | 							<img
  41 | 								alt=''
  42 | 								className='h-10 w-10'
  43 | 								src={session.user.image}
  44 | 							/>
  45 | 						) : (
  46 | 							<div className='flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center bg-neutral-200 dark:bg-neutral-800'>
  47 | 								<span className='font-medium text-neutral-600 text-sm dark:text-neutral-400'>
  48 | 									{session.user.name?.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "U"}
  49 | 								</span>
  50 | 							</div>
  51 | 						)}
  52 | 						<div className='min-w-0 flex-1'>
  53 | 							<p className='truncate font-medium text-sm'>{session.user.name}</p>
  54 | 							<p className='truncate text-neutral-500 text-xs dark:text-neutral-400'>
  55 | 								{session.user.email}
  56 | 							</p>
  57 | 						</div>
  58 | 					</div>
  59 |
  60 | 					<button
  61 | 						className='h-9 w-full border border-neutral-300 px-4 font-medium text-sm transition-colors hover:bg-neutral-100 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:hover:bg-neutral-800'
  62 | 						onClick={() => {
  63 | 							void authClient.signOut();
  64 | 						}}
  65 | 					>
  66 | 						Sign out
  67 | 					</button>
  68 |
  69 | 					<p className='text-center text-neutral-400 text-xs dark:text-neutral-500'>
  70 | 						Built with{" "}
  71 | 						<a
  72 | 							className='font-medium hover:text-neutral-600 dark:hover:text-neutral-300'
  73 | 							href='https://better-auth.com'
  74 | 							rel='noopener noreferrer'
  75 | 							target='_blank'
  76 | 						>
  77 | 							BETTER-AUTH
  78 | 						</a>
  79 | 						.
  80 | 					</p>
  81 | 				</div>
  82 | 			</div>
  83 | 		);
  84 | 	}
  85 |
  86 | 	const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
  87 | 		e.preventDefault();
  88 | 		setError("");
  89 | 		setLoading(true);
  90 |
  91 | 		try {
  92 | 			if (isSignUp) {
  93 | 				const result = await authClient.signUp.email({
  94 | 					email,
  95 | 					password,
  96 | 					name
  97 | 				});
  98 | 				if (result.error) {
  99 | 					setError(result.error.message || "Sign up failed");
 100 | 				}
 101 | 			} else {
 102 | 				const result = await authClient.signIn.email({
 103 | 					email,
 104 | 					password
 105 | 				});
 106 | 				if (result.error) {
 107 | 					setError(result.error.message || "Sign in failed");
 108 | 				}
 109 | 			}
 110 | 		} catch (_err) {
 111 | 			setError("An unexpected error occurred");
 112 | 		} finally {
 113 | 			setLoading(false);
 114 | 		}
 115 | 	};
 116 |
 117 | 	return (
 118 | 		<div className='flex justify-center px-4 py-10'>
 119 | 			<div className='w-full max-w-md p-6'>
 120 | 				<h1 className='font-semibold text-lg leading-none tracking-tight'>
 121 | 					{isSignUp ? "Create an account" : "Sign in"}
 122 | 				</h1>
 123 | 				<p className='mt-2 mb-6 text-neutral-500 text-sm dark:text-neutral-400'>
 124 | 					{isSignUp
 125 | 						? "Enter your information to create an account"
 126 | 						: "Enter your email below to login to your account"}
 127 | 				</p>
 128 |
 129 | 				<form
 130 | 					className='grid gap-4'
 131 | 					onSubmit={handleSubmit}
 132 | 				>
 133 | 					{isSignUp && (
 134 | 						<div className='grid gap-2'>
 135 | 							<label
 136 | 								className='font-medium text-sm leading-none'
 137 | 								htmlFor='name'
 138 | 							>
 139 | 								Name
 140 | 							</label>
 141 | 							<input
 142 | 								className='flex h-9 w-full border border-neutral-300 bg-transparent px-3 text-sm focus:border-neutral-900 focus:outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:focus:border-neutral-100'
 143 | 								id='name'
 144 | 								onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
 145 | 								required
 146 | 								type='text'
 147 | 								value={name}
 148 | 							/>
 149 | 						</div>
 150 | 					)}
 151 |
 152 | 					<div className='grid gap-2'>
 153 | 						<label
 154 | 							className='font-medium text-sm leading-none'
 155 | 							htmlFor='email'
 156 | 						>
 157 | 							Email
 158 | 						</label>
 159 | 						<input
 160 | 							className='flex h-9 w-full border border-neutral-300 bg-transparent px-3 text-sm focus:border-neutral-900 focus:outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:focus:border-neutral-100'
 161 | 							id='email'
 162 | 							onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
 163 | 							required
 164 | 							type='email'
 165 | 							value={email}
 166 | 						/>
 167 | 					</div>
 168 |
 169 | 					<div className='grid gap-2'>
 170 | 						<label
 171 | 							className='font-medium text-sm leading-none'
 172 | 							htmlFor='password'
 173 | 						>
 174 | 							Password
 175 | 						</label>
 176 | 						<input
 177 | 							className='flex h-9 w-full border border-neutral-300 bg-transparent px-3 text-sm focus:border-neutral-900 focus:outline-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:focus:border-neutral-100'
 178 | 							id='password'
 179 | 							minLength={8}
 180 | 							onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
 181 | 							required
 182 | 							type='password'
 183 | 							value={password}
 184 | 						/>
 185 | 					</div>
 186 |
 187 | 					{error && (
 188 | 						<div className='border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 dark:border-red-800 dark:bg-red-900/20'>
 189 | 							<p className='text-red-600 text-sm dark:text-red-400'>{error}</p>
 190 | 						</div>
 191 | 					)}
 192 |
 193 | 					<button
 194 | 						className='h-9 w-full bg-neutral-900 px-4 font-medium text-sm text-white transition-colors hover:bg-neutral-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 dark:bg-neutral-50 dark:text-neutral-900 dark:hover:bg-neutral-200'
 195 | 						disabled={loading}
 196 | 						type='submit'
 197 | 					>
 198 | 						{loading ? (
 199 | 							<span className='flex items-center justify-center gap-2'>
 200 | 								<span className='h-4 w-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-neutral-400 border-t-white dark:border-neutral-600 dark:border-t-neutral-900' />
 201 | 								<span>Please wait</span>
 202 | 							</span>
 203 | 						) : isSignUp ? (
 204 | 							"Create account"
 205 | 						) : (
 206 | 							"Sign in"
 207 | 						)}
 208 | 					</button>
 209 | 				</form>
 210 |
 211 | 				<div className='mt-4 text-center'>
 212 | 					<button
 213 | 						className='text-neutral-500 text-sm transition-colors hover:text-neutral-900 dark:text-neutral-400 dark:hover:text-neutral-100'
 214 | 						onClick={() => {
 215 | 							setIsSignUp(!isSignUp);
 216 | 							setError("");
 217 | 						}}
 218 | 						type='button'
 219 | 					>
 220 | 						{isSignUp ? "Already have an account? Sign in" : "Don't have an account? Sign up"}
 221 | 					</button>
 222 | 				</div>
 223 |
 224 | 				<p className='mt-6 text-center text-neutral-400 text-xs dark:text-neutral-500'>
```

`routes/demo/drizzle.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { createFileRoute, useRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   2 | import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
   3 | import { desc } from "drizzle-orm";
   4 |
   5 | import { db } from "#/db/index";
   6 | import { todos } from "#/db/schema";
   7 |
   8 | const getTodos = createServerFn({
   9 | 	method: "GET"
  10 | }).handler(async () => {
  11 | 	return await db.query.todos.findMany({
  12 | 		orderBy: [desc(todos.createdAt)]
  13 | 	});
  14 | });
  15 |
  16 | const createTodo = createServerFn({
  17 | 	method: "POST"
  18 | })
  19 | 	.inputValidator((data: { title: string }) => data)
  20 | 	.handler(async ({ data }) => {
  21 | 		await db.insert(todos).values({ title: data.title });
  22 | 		return { success: true };
  23 | 	});
  24 |
  25 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/demo/drizzle")({
  26 | 	component: DemoDrizzle,
  27 | 	loader: async () => await getTodos()
  28 | });
  29 |
  30 | function DemoDrizzle() {
  31 | 	const router = useRouter();
  32 | 	const todos = Route.useLoaderData();
  33 |
  34 | 	const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
  35 | 		e.preventDefault();
  36 | 		const formData = new FormData(e.target as HTMLFormElement);
  37 | 		const title = formData.get("title") as string;
  38 |
  39 | 		if (!title) return;
  40 |
  41 | 		try {
  42 | 			await createTodo({ data: { title } });
  43 | 			router.invalidate();
  44 | 			(e.target as HTMLFormElement).reset();
  45 | 		} catch (error) {
  46 | 			console.error("Failed to create todo:", error);
  47 | 		}
  48 | 	};
  49 |
  50 | 	return (
```

```tsx
   9 | function AddressForm() {

  11 | 		defaultValues: {

  26 | 					fields: {}
  27 | 				} as {
  28 | 					fields: Record<string, string>;
  29 | 				};
  30 | 				if (value.fullName.trim().length === 0) {
  31 | 					errors.fields.fullName = "Full name is required";
  32 | 				}

  52 | 					className='space-y-6'
  53 | 					onSubmit={e => {
  54 | 						e.preventDefault();
  55 | 						e.stopPropagation();
  56 | 						form.handleSubmit();
  57 | 					}}
  58 | 				>
  59 | 					<form.AppField name='fullName'>{field => <field.TextField label='Full Name' />}</form.AppField>
  60 |
  61 | 					<form.AppField
  62 | 						name='email'
  63 | 						validators={{
  64 | 							onBlur: ({ value }) => {
  65 | 								if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) {
  66 | 									return "Email is required";
  67 | 								}
  68 | 								if (!/^[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}$/i.test(value)) {
  69 | 									return "Invalid email address";
  70 | 								}
  71 | 								return undefined;
  72 | 							}
  73 | 						}}
  74 | 					>
  75 | 						{field => <field.TextField label='Email' />}
  76 | 					</form.AppField>
  77 |

  79 | 						name='address.street'

 112 | 									}
 113 | 									return undefined;
 114 | 								}

 124 | 										return "Zip code is required";
 125 | 									}
```

```tsx
   1 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";

   4 | import { useAppForm } from "#/hooks/demo.form";
   5 |
   6 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/demo/form/simple")({
   7 | 	component: SimpleForm
   8 | });
   9 |
  10 | const schema = z.object({
  11 | 	title: z.string().min(1, "Title is required"),
  12 | 	description: z.string().min(1, "Description is required")
  13 | });

  23 | 		},

  27 | 			alert("Form submitted successfully!");
  28 | 		}
  29 | 	});
  30 |
  31 | 	return (
  32 | 		<div

  34 | 			style={{
```

`routes/demo/mcp-todos.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   2 | import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
   3 |
   4 | type Todo = {
   5 | 	id: number;
   6 | 	title: string;
   7 | };
   8 |
   9 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/demo/mcp-todos")({
  10 | 	component: ORPCTodos
  11 | });
  12 |
  13 | function ORPCTodos() {
  14 | 	const [todos, setTodos] = useState<Todo[]>([]);
  15 |
  16 | 	useEffect(() => {
  17 | 		const eventSource = new EventSource("/demo/api/mcp-todos");
  18 | 		eventSource.onmessage = event => {
  19 | 			setTodos(JSON.parse(event.data));
  20 | 		};
  21 | 		return () => eventSource.close();
  22 | 	}, []);
  23 |
  24 | 	const [todo, setTodo] = useState("");
  25 |
  26 | 	const submitTodo = useCallback(async () => {
  27 | 		await fetch("/demo/api/mcp-todos", {
  28 | 			method: "POST",
  29 | 			body: JSON.stringify({ title: todo })
  30 | 		});
  31 | 		setTodo("");
  32 | 	}, [todo]);
  33 |
  34 | 	return (
  35 | 		<div
  36 | 			className='flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-200 to-emerald-900 p-4 text-white'
  37 | 			style={{
  38 | 				backgroundImage: "radial-gradient(70% 70% at 20% 20%, #07A798 0%, #045C4B 60%, #01251F 100%)"
  39 | 			}}
  40 | 		>
  41 | 			<div className='w-full max-w-2xl rounded-xl border-8 border-black/10 bg-black/50 p-8 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-md'>
  42 | 				<h1 className='mb-4 text-2xl'>MCP Todos list</h1>
  43 | 				<ul className='mb-4 space-y-2'>
  44 | 					{todos?.map(t => (
  45 | 						<li
  46 | 							className='rounded-lg border border-white/20 bg-white/10 p-3 shadow-md backdrop-blur-sm'
  47 | 							key={t.id}
  48 | 						>
  49 | 							<span className='text-lg text-white'>{t.title}</span>
  50 | 						</li>
  51 | 					))}
  52 | 				</ul>
  53 | 				<div className='flex flex-col gap-2'>
  54 | 					<input
  55 | 						className='w-full rounded-lg border border-white/20 bg-white/10 px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder-white/60 backdrop-blur-sm focus:border-transparent focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-400'
  56 | 						onChange={e => setTodo(e.target.value)}
  57 | 						onKeyDown={e => {
  58 | 							if (e.key === "Enter") {
  59 | 								submitTodo();
  60 | 							}
  61 | 						}}
  62 | 						placeholder='Enter a new todo...'
  63 | 						type='text'
  64 | 						value={todo}
  65 | 					/>
  66 | 					<button
  67 | 						className='rounded-lg bg-blue-500 px-4 py-3 font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-blue-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-blue-500/50'
  68 | 						disabled={todo.trim().length === 0}
  69 | 						onClick={submitTodo}
  70 | 					>
  71 | 						Add todo
  72 | 					</button>
  73 | 				</div>
  74 | 			</div>
  75 | 		</div>
  76 | 	);
  77 | }

```

`routes/demo/orpc-todo.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
   2 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   3 | import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
   4 |
   5 | import { orpc } from "#/orpc/client";
   6 |
   7 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/demo/orpc-todo")({
   8 | 	component: ORPCTodos,
   9 | 	loader: async ({ context }) => {
  10 | 		await context.queryClient.prefetchQuery(
  11 | 			orpc.listTodos.queryOptions({
  12 | 				input: {}
  13 | 			})
  14 | 		);
  15 | 	}
  16 | });
  17 |
  18 | function ORPCTodos() {
  19 | 	const { data, refetch } = useQuery(
  20 | 		orpc.listTodos.queryOptions({
  21 | 			input: {}
  22 | 		})
  23 | 	);
  24 |
  25 | 	const [todo, setTodo] = useState("");
  26 | 	const { mutate: addTodo } = useMutation({
  27 | 		mutationFn: orpc.addTodo.call,
  28 | 		onSuccess: () => {
  29 | 			refetch();
  30 | 			setTodo("");
  31 | 		}
  32 | 	});
  33 |
  34 | 	const submitTodo = useCallback(() => {
  35 | 		addTodo({ name: todo });
  36 | 	}, [addTodo, todo]);
  37 |
  38 | 	return (
  39 | 		<div
  40 | 			className='flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-100 to-blue-100 p-4 text-white'
  41 | 			style={{
  42 | 				backgroundImage: "radial-gradient(50% 50% at 50% 50%, #D2149D 0%, #8E1066 50%, #2D0A1F 100%)"
  43 | 			}}
  44 | 		>
  45 | 			<div className='w-full max-w-2xl rounded-xl border-8 border-black/10 bg-black/50 p-8 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-md'>
  46 | 				<h1 className='mb-4 text-2xl'>oRPC Todos list</h1>
  47 | 				<ul className='mb-4 space-y-2'>
  48 | 					{data?.map(t => (
  49 | 						<li
  50 | 							className='rounded-lg border border-white/20 bg-white/10 p-3 shadow-md backdrop-blur-sm'
  51 | 							key={t.id}
  52 | 						>
  53 | 							<span className='text-lg text-white'>{t.name}</span>
  54 | 						</li>
  55 | 					))}
  56 | 				</ul>
  57 | 				<div className='flex flex-col gap-2'>
  58 | 					<input
  59 | 						className='w-full rounded-lg border border-white/20 bg-white/10 px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder-white/60 backdrop-blur-sm focus:border-transparent focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-400'
  60 | 						onChange={e => setTodo(e.target.value)}
  61 | 						onKeyDown={e => {
  62 | 							if (e.key === "Enter") {
  63 | 								submitTodo();
  64 | 							}
  65 | 						}}
  66 | 						placeholder='Enter a new todo...'
  67 | 						type='text'
  68 | 						value={todo}
  69 | 					/>
  70 | 					<button
  71 | 						className='rounded-lg bg-blue-500 px-4 py-3 font-bold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-blue-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-blue-500/50'
  72 | 						disabled={todo.trim().length === 0}
  73 | 						onClick={submitTodo}
  74 | 					>
  75 | 						Add todo
  76 | 					</button>
  77 | 				</div>
  78 | 			</div>
  79 | 		</div>
  80 | 	);
  81 | }

```

```tsx
 162 | // Result Badge Component
 163 | function ResultBadge({ type, spanOp, onCopy }: { type: "success" | "error"; spanOp: string; onCopy: () => void }) {
 164 | 	const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
 165 |
 166 | 	const handleCopy = () => {
 167 | 		navigator.clipboard.writeText(spanOp);
 168 | 		setCopied(true);
 169 | 		onCopy();
 170 | 		setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
 171 | 	};
 172 |
 173 | 	return (
 174 | 		<div className='mt-4 space-y-3'>
 175 | 			{type === "error" && (
 176 | 				<div className='flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#E50045]/30 bg-[#E50045]/10 px-4 py-3'>
 177 | 					<svg
 178 | 						className='h-5 w-5 text-[#FF1A5C]'
 179 | 						fill='none'
 180 | 						stroke='currentColor'
 181 | 						strokeLinecap='round'
 182 | 						strokeLinejoin='round'
 183 | 						strokeWidth='2'
 184 | 						viewBox='0 0 24 24'
 185 | 					>
 186 | 						<title>Error captured</title>
 187 | 						<path d='M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z' />
 188 | 					</svg>
 189 | 					<span className='font-medium text-[#FF1A5C] text-sm'>Error captured and sent to Sentry</span>
 190 | 				</div>
 191 | 			)}
 192 |
 193 | 			{type === "success" && (
 194 | 				<div className='flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#00BF4D]/30 bg-[#00F261]/10 px-4 py-3'>
 195 | 					<svg
 196 | 						className='h-5 w-5 text-[#00F261]'
 197 | 						fill='none'
 198 | 						stroke='currentColor'
 199 | 						strokeLinecap='round'
 200 | 						strokeLinejoin='round'
 201 | 						strokeWidth='2'
 202 | 						viewBox='0 0 24 24'
 203 | 					>
 204 | 						<title>Trace complete</title>
 205 | 						<path d='M5 13l4 4L19 7' />
 206 | 					</svg>
 207 | 					<span className='font-medium text-[#00F261] text-sm'>Trace completed successfully</span>
 208 | 				</div>
 209 | 			)}
 210 |
 211 | 			<button
 212 | 				className='relative flex w-full cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#7553FF]/30 bg-[#7553FF]/10 px-4 py-2 transition-all hover:bg-[#7553FF]/20'
 213 | 				onClick={handleCopy}
 214 | 				type='button'
 215 | 			>
 216 | 				<span className='text-[#B3A1FF] text-sm'>span.op:</span>
 217 | 				<code className='font-mono text-[#7553FF] text-sm'>{spanOp}</code>
 218 | 				<svg

 222 | 					strokeLinecap='round'

 229 | 				</svg>

 258 | function RouteComponent() {
 259 | 	const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
 260 | 	const [results, setResults] = useState<Record<string, { type: "success" | "error"; spanOp: string }>>({});
 261 | 	const [sentryConfigured, setSentryConfigured] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
 262 |
 263 | 	useEffect(() => {
 264 | 		// Check if Sentry DSN environment variable is set
 265 | 		const hasDsn = !!import.meta.env.VITE_SENTRY_DSN;
 266 | 		setSentryConfigured(hasDsn);
 267 | 	}, []);
 268 |
 269 | 	// Don't show warning until we've checked on the client
 270 | 	const showWarning = sentryConfigured === false;
 271 |
 272 | 	const handleClientError = async () => {
 273 | 		setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, clientError: true }));
 274 | 		try {
 275 | 			await Sentry.startSpan({ name: "Client Error Flow Demo", op: "demo.client-error" }, async () => {
 276 | 				Sentry.setContext("demo", {
 277 | 					feature: "client-error-demo",
 278 | 					triggered_at: new Date().toISOString()
 279 | 				});
 280 | 				throw new Error("Client-side error demonstration");
 281 | 			});
 282 | 		} catch (error) {
 283 | 			Sentry.captureException(error);
 284 | 			setResults(prev => ({
 285 | 				...prev,
 286 | 				clientError: { type: "error", spanOp: "demo.client-error" }
 287 | 			}));
 288 | 		} finally {
 289 | 			setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, clientError: false }));
 290 | 		}
 291 | 	};
 292 |
 293 | 	const handleServerError = async () => {
 294 | 		setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, serverError: true }));
 295 | 		try {
 296 | 			await Sentry.startSpan({ name: "Server Error Flow Demo", op: "demo.server-error" }, async () => {
 297 | 				Sentry.setContext("demo", {
 298 | 					feature: "server-error-demo",
 299 | 					triggered_at: new Date().toISOString()
 300 | 				});
 301 | 				await badServerFunc();
 302 | 			});
 303 | 		} catch (error) {
 304 | 			Sentry.captureException(error);
 305 | 			setResults(prev => ({
 306 | 				...prev,
 307 | 				serverError: { type: "error", spanOp: "demo.server-error" }
 308 | 			}));
 309 | 		} finally {
 310 | 			setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, serverError: false }));
 311 | 		}
 312 | 	};
 313 |
 314 | 	const handleClientTrace = async () => {
 315 | 		setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, clientTrace: true }));
 316 | 		await Sentry.startSpan({ name: "Client Operation", op: "demo.client-trace" }, async () => {
 317 | 			await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1000));
 318 | 		});
 319 | 		setResults(prev => ({
 320 | 			...prev,
 321 | 			clientTrace: { type: "success", spanOp: "demo.client-trace" }
 322 | 		}));
 323 | 		setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, clientTrace: false }));
 324 | 	};
 325 |
 326 | 	const handleServerTrace = async () => {
 327 | 		setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, serverTrace: true }));
 328 | 		try {
 329 | 			await Sentry.startSpan({ name: "Server Operation", op: "demo.server-trace" }, async () => {
 330 | 				await goodServerFunc();
 331 | 			});
 332 | 			setResults(prev => ({
 333 | 				...prev,
 334 | 				serverTrace: { type: "success", spanOp: "demo.server-trace" }
 335 | 			}));
 336 | 		} finally {
 337 | 			setIsLoading(prev => ({ ...prev, serverTrace: false }));
 338 | 		}
 339 | 	};
 340 |
 341 | 	return (
 342 | 		<div
 343 | 			className='min-h-screen text-white'
 344 | 			style={{
 345 | 				fontFamily:
 346 | 					'system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", sans-serif',
 347 | 				background: "linear-gradient(180deg, #181423 0%, #1C1825 50%, #181423 100%)"
 348 | 			}}
 349 | 		>
 350 | 			<div className='mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-16'>
 351 | 				{/* Header */}
 352 | 				<div className='mb-16 text-center'>
 353 | 					<div className='mb-8 inline-flex items-center gap-4'>
 354 | 						<div className='text-[#7553FF]'>
 355 | 							<SentryLogo size={56} />
 356 | 						</div>
 357 | 						<div className='text-left'>
 358 | 							<h1 className='font-bold text-3xl text-white tracking-tight'>Sentry Demo</h1>
 359 | 							<p className='text-[#A49FB5] text-sm'>Error monitoring & performance tracing</p>
 360 | 						</div>
 361 | 					</div>
 362 | 					<p className='mx-auto max-w-xl text-[#A49FB5] text-lg leading-relaxed'>
 363 | 						Click the buttons below to trigger errors and traces, then view them in your{" "}
 364 | 						<a
 365 | 							className='text-[#7553FF] underline transition-colors hover:text-[#B3A1FF]'

 367 | 							rel='noopener noreferrer'
 368 | 							target='_blank'
 369 | 						>
 370 | 							Sentry dashboard

 372 | 						.
 373 | 					</p>
 374 | 				</div>
 375 |
 376 | 				{/* Sentry Not Initialized Warning */}
 377 | 				{showWarning && (
 378 | 					<div className='mb-8 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[#E5A000]/30 bg-[#E5A000]/10 px-6 py-4'>

 390 | 						</svg>
 391 | 						<div>
 392 | 							<p className='font-medium text-[#E5A000]'>Sentry is not initialized</p>
 393 | 							<p className='mt-1 text-[#A49FB5] text-sm'>
 394 | 								Set the{" "}
 395 | 								<code className='rounded bg-[#1C1825] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[#B3A1FF]'>
 396 | 									VITE_SENTRY_DSN
 397 | 								</code>{" "}
 398 | 								environment variable to enable error tracking and performance monitoring.
 399 | 							</p>
 400 | 						</div>
 401 | 					</div>
 402 | 				)}
 403 |
 404 | 				{/* Features Grid */}
 405 | 				<div className='mb-12 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4'>
 406 | 					<FeatureCard

 408 | 						icon={

 411 | 								fill='currentColor'
 412 | 								viewBox='0 0 24 24'

 415 | 							</svg>
 416 | 						}
```

`routes/demo/table.tsx`:

```tsx
   1 | import type { RankingInfo } from "@tanstack/match-sorter-utils";
   2 | import { compareItems, rankItem } from "@tanstack/match-sorter-utils";
   3 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   4 | import type { Column, ColumnDef, ColumnFiltersState, FilterFn, SortingFn } from "@tanstack/react-table";
   5 | import {
   6 | 	flexRender,
   7 | 	getCoreRowModel,
   8 | 	getFilteredRowModel,
   9 | 	getPaginationRowModel,
  10 | 	getSortedRowModel,
  11 | 	sortingFns,
  12 | 	useReactTable
  13 | } from "@tanstack/react-table";
  14 | import React from "react";
  15 |
  16 | import type { Person } from "#/data/demo-table-data";
  17 | import { makeData } from "#/data/demo-table-data";
  18 |
  19 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/demo/table")({
  20 | 	component: TableDemo
  21 | });
  22 |
  23 | declare module "@tanstack/react-table" {
  24 | 	interface FilterFns {
  25 | 		fuzzy: FilterFn<unknown>;
  26 | 	}
  27 | 	interface FilterMeta {
  28 | 		itemRank: RankingInfo;
  29 | 	}
  30 | }
  31 |
  32 | // Define a custom fuzzy filter function that will apply ranking info to rows (using match-sorter utils)
  33 | const fuzzyFilter: FilterFn<any> = (row, columnId, value, addMeta) => {
  34 | 	// Rank the item
  35 | 	const itemRank = rankItem(row.getValue(columnId), value);
  36 |
  37 | 	// Store the itemRank info
  38 | 	addMeta({
  39 | 		itemRank
  40 | 	});
  41 |
  42 | 	// Return if the item should be filtered in/out
  43 | 	return itemRank.passed;
  44 | };
  45 |
  46 | // Define a custom fuzzy sort function that will sort by rank if the row has ranking information
  47 | const fuzzySort: SortingFn<any> = (rowA, rowB, columnId) => {
  48 | 	let dir = 0;
  49 |
  50 | 	// Only sort by rank if the column has ranking information
  51 | 	if (rowA.columnFiltersMeta[columnId]) {
  52 | 		dir = compareItems(rowA.columnFiltersMeta[columnId]?.itemRank!, rowB.columnFiltersMeta[columnId]?.itemRank!);
  53 | 	}
  54 |
  55 | 	// Provide an alphanumeric fallback for when the item ranks are equal
  56 | 	return dir === 0 ? sortingFns.alphanumeric(rowA, rowB, columnId) : dir;
  57 | };
  58 |
  59 | function TableDemo() {
  60 | 	const rerender = React.useReducer(() => ({}), {})[1];
  61 |
  62 | 	const [columnFilters, setColumnFilters] = React.useState<ColumnFiltersState>([]);
  63 | 	const [globalFilter, setGlobalFilter] = React.useState("");
  64 |
  65 | 	const columns = React.useMemo<ColumnDef<Person, any>[]>(
  66 | 		() => [
  67 | 			{
  68 | 				accessorKey: "id",
  69 | 				filterFn: "equalsString" //note: normal non-fuzzy filter column - exact match required
  70 | 			},
  71 | 			{
  72 | 				accessorKey: "firstName",
  73 | 				cell: info => info.getValue(),
  74 | 				filterFn: "includesStringSensitive" //note: normal non-fuzzy filter column - case sensitive
  75 | 			},
  76 | 			{
  77 | 				accessorFn: row => row.lastName,
  78 | 				id: "lastName",
  79 | 				cell: info => info.getValue(),
  80 | 				header: () => <span>Last Name</span>,
  81 | 				filterFn: "includesString" //note: normal non-fuzzy filter column - case insensitive
  82 | 			},
  83 | 			{
  84 | 				accessorFn: row => `${row.firstName} ${row.lastName}`,
  85 | 				id: "fullName",
  86 | 				header: "Full Name",
  87 | 				cell: info => info.getValue(),
  88 | 				filterFn: "fuzzy", //using our custom fuzzy filter function
  89 | 				// filterFn: fuzzyFilter, //or just define with the function
  90 | 				sortingFn: fuzzySort //sort by fuzzy rank (falls back to alphanumeric)
  91 | 			}
  92 | 		],
  93 | 		[]
  94 | 	);
  95 |
  96 | 	const [data, setData] = React.useState<Person[]>(() => makeData(5_000));
  97 | 	const refreshData = () => setData(_old => makeData(50_000)); //stress test
  98 |
  99 | 	const table = useReactTable({
 100 | 		data,
 101 | 		columns,
 102 | 		filterFns: {
 103 | 			fuzzy: fuzzyFilter //define as a filter function that can be used in column definitions
 104 | 		},
 105 | 		state: {
 106 | 			columnFilters,
 107 | 			globalFilter
 108 | 		},
 109 | 		onColumnFiltersChange: setColumnFilters,
 110 | 		onGlobalFilterChange: setGlobalFilter,
 111 | 		globalFilterFn: "fuzzy", //apply fuzzy filter to the global filter (most common use case for fuzzy filter)
 112 | 		getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
 113 | 		getFilteredRowModel: getFilteredRowModel(), //client side filtering
 114 | 		getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel(),
 115 | 		getPaginationRowModel: getPaginationRowModel(),
 116 | 		debugTable: true,
 117 | 		debugHeaders: true,
 118 | 		debugColumns: false
 119 | 	});
 120 |
 121 | 	//apply the fuzzy sort if the fullName column is being filtered
 122 | 	React.useEffect(() => {
 123 | 		if (table.getState().columnFilters[0]?.id === "fullName") {
 124 | 			if (table.getState().sorting[0]?.id !== "fullName") {
 125 | 				table.setSorting([{ id: "fullName", desc: false }]);
 126 | 			}
 127 | 		}
 128 | 	}, [table.setSorting, table.getState]);
 129 |
 130 | 	return (
 131 | 		<div className='min-h-screen bg-gray-900 p-6'>
 132 | 			<div>
 133 | 				<DebouncedInput
 134 | 					className='w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-700 bg-gray-800 p-3 text-white outline-none focus:border-transparent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500'
 135 | 					onChange={value => setGlobalFilter(String(value))}
 136 | 					placeholder='Search all columns...'
 137 | 					value={globalFilter ?? ""}
 138 | 				/>
 139 | 			</div>
 140 | 			<div className='h-4' />
 141 | 			<div className='overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-gray-700'>
 142 | 				<table className='w-full text-gray-200 text-sm'>
 143 | 					<thead className='bg-gray-800 text-gray-100'>
 144 | 						{table.getHeaderGroups().map(headerGroup => (
 145 | 							<tr key={headerGroup.id}>
 146 | 								{headerGroup.headers.map(header => {
 147 | 									return (
 148 | 										<th
 149 | 											className='px-4 py-3 text-left'
 150 | 											colSpan={header.colSpan}
 151 | 											key={header.id}
 152 | 										>
 153 | 											{header.isPlaceholder ? null : (
 154 | 												<>
 155 | 													<div
 156 | 														{...{
 157 | 															className: header.column.getCanSort()
 158 | 																? "cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors"
 159 | 																: "",
 160 | 															onClick: header.column.getToggleSortingHandler()
 161 | 														}}
 162 | 													>
 163 | 														{flexRender(
 164 | 															header.column.columnDef.header,
 165 | 															header.getContext()
 166 | 														)}
 167 | 														{{
 168 | 															asc: " 🔼",
 169 | 															desc: " 🔽"
 170 | 														}[header.column.getIsSorted() as string] ?? null}
 171 | 													</div>
 172 | 													{header.column.getCanFilter() ? (
 173 | 														<div className='mt-2'>
 174 | 															<Filter column={header.column} />
 175 | 														</div>
 176 | 													) : null}
 177 | 												</>
 178 | 											)}
 179 | 										</th>
 180 | 									);
 181 | 								})}
 182 | 							</tr>
 183 | 						))}
 184 | 					</thead>
 185 | 					<tbody className='divide-y divide-gray-700'>
 186 | 						{table.getRowModel().rows.map(row => {
 187 | 							return (
 188 | 								<tr
 189 | 									className='transition-colors hover:bg-gray-800'
 190 | 									key={row.id}
 191 | 								>
 192 | 									{row.getVisibleCells().map(cell => {
 193 | 										return (
 194 | 											<td
 195 | 												className='px-4 py-3'

 200 | 										);
 201 | 									})}
 202 | 								</tr>
 203 | 							);
 204 | 						})}
 205 | 					</tbody>
 206 | 				</table>
 207 | 			</div>
 208 | 			<div className='h-4' />
 209 | 			<div className='flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-gray-200'>
 210 | 				<button
 211 | 					className='rounded-md bg-gray-800 px-3 py-1 hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50'
 212 | 					disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}
 213 | 					onClick={() => table.setPageIndex(0)}
 214 | 				>
 215 | 					{"<<"}
 216 | 				</button>
 217 | 				<button
 218 | 					className='rounded-md bg-gray-800 px-3 py-1 hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50'
 219 | 					disabled={!table.getCanPreviousPage()}
 220 | 					onClick={() => table.previousPage()}
 221 | 				>
 222 | 					{"<"}

 300 | }
 301 |
 302 | function Filter({ column }: { column: Column<any, unknown> }) {
 303 | 	const columnFilterValue = column.getFilterValue();
 304 |
 305 | 	return (
 306 | 		<DebouncedInput
 307 | 			className='w-full rounded-md border border-gray-600 bg-gray-700 px-2 py-1 text-white outline-none focus:border-transparent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500'
 308 | 			onChange={value => column.setFilterValue(value)}
 309 | 			placeholder={"Search..."}
 310 | 			type='text'
 311 | 			value={(columnFilterValue ?? "") as string}
 312 | 		/>
 313 | 	);
 314 | }
 315 |
 316 | // A typical debounced input react component
 317 | function DebouncedInput({
 318 | 	value: initialValue,
 319 | 	onChange,
 320 | 	debounce = 500,
 321 | 	...props
 322 | }: {
 323 | 	value: string | number;
 324 | 	onChange: (value: string | number) => void;
 325 | 	debounce?: number;
 326 | } & Omit<React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, "onChange">) {
 327 | 	const [value, setValue] = React.useState(initialValue);
 328 |
 329 | 	React.useEffect(() => {
 330 | 		setValue(initialValue);
 331 | 	}, [initialValue]);
 332 |
 333 | 	React.useEffect(() => {
 334 | 		const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
 335 | 			onChange(value);
 336 | 		}, debounce);
 337 |
 338 | 		return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
 339 | 	}, [value, onChange, debounce]);
 340 |
 341 | 	return (
 342 | 		<input
 343 | 			{...props}
 344 | 			onChange={e => setValue(e.target.value)}
 345 | 			value={value}
 346 | 		/>
 347 | 	);
 348 | }

```

```tsx
  23 | 					>

  25 | 					</a>
  26 | 					<a
  27 | 						className='rounded-full border border-[rgba(23,58,64,0.2)] bg-white/50 px-5 py-2.5 font-semibold text-[var(--sea-ink)] text-sm no-underline transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(23,58,64,0.35)]'
  28 | 						href='https://tanstack.com/router'
  29 | 						rel='noopener noreferrer'
  30 | 						target='_blank'
  31 | 					>
  32 | 						Router Guide
  33 | 					</a>
  34 | 				</div>
  35 | 			</section>
  36 |
  37 | 			<section className='mt-8 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4'>
  38 | 				{[
  39 | 					["Type-Safe Routing", "Routes and links stay in sync across every page."],
  40 | 					["Server Functions", "Call server code from your UI without creating API boilerplate."],
  41 | 					["Streaming by Default", "Ship progressively rendered responses for faster experiences."],
  42 | 					["Tailwind Native", "Design quickly with utility-first styling and reusable tokens."]
  43 | 				].map(([title, desc], index) => (
  44 | 					<article
  45 | 						className='island-shell feature-card rise-in rounded-2xl p-5'

  51 | 					</article>
  52 | 				))}
  53 | 			</section>

  55 | 			<section className='island-shell mt-8 rounded-2xl p-6'>
```

```ts
   1 | import { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js";
   2 | import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
   3 | import z from "zod";
   4 |
   5 | import { addTodo } from "#/mcp-todos";
   6 | import { handleMcpRequest } from "#/utils/mcp-handler";
   7 |
   8 | const server = new McpServer({
   9 | 	name: "start-server",
  10 | 	version: "1.0.0"
  11 | });
  12 |
  13 | server.registerTool(
  14 | 	"addTodo",
  15 | 	{
  16 | 		title: "Tool to add a todo to a list of todos",
  17 | 		description: "Add a todo to a list of todos",
  18 | 		inputSchema: {
  19 | 			title: z.string().describe("The title of the todo")
  20 | 		}
  21 | 	},
  22 | 	({ title }) => ({
  23 | 		content: [{ type: "text", text: String(addTodo(title)) }]
  24 | 	})
  25 | );
  26 |

  28 | //   "counter-value",
  29 | //   "count://",
  30 | //   {
  31 | //     title: "Counter Resource",
  32 | //     description: "Returns the current value of the counter",
  33 | //   },

  36 | //       contents: [
  37 | //         {

  39 | //           text: `The counter is at 20!`,
  40 | //         },
  41 | //       ],
  42 | //     };
  43 | //   }
  44 | // );
  45 |
  46 | export const Route = createFileRoute("/mcp")({
  47 | 	server: {
  48 | 		handlers: {
  49 | 			POST: async ({ request }) => handleMcpRequest(request, server)
  50 | 		}
  51 | 	}
  52 | });
```

`types/data-grid.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import type { RowData } from "@tanstack/react-table";
   2 |
   3 | export type RowHeightValue = "short" | "medium" | "tall" | "extra-tall";
   4 |
   5 | export interface CellSelectOption {
   6 | 	label: string;
   7 | 	value: string;
   8 | }
   9 |
  10 | export type Cell =
  11 | 	| {
  12 | 			variant: "short-text";
  13 | 	  }
  14 | 	| {
  15 | 			variant: "long-text";
  16 | 	  }
  17 | 	| {
  18 | 			variant: "number";
  19 | 			min?: number;
  20 | 			max?: number;
  21 | 			step?: number;
  22 | 	  }
  23 | 	| {
  24 | 			variant: "select";
  25 | 			options: CellSelectOption[];
  26 | 	  }
  27 | 	| {
  28 | 			variant: "multi-select";
  29 | 			options: CellSelectOption[];
  30 | 	  }
  31 | 	| {
  32 | 			variant: "checkbox";
  33 | 	  }
  34 | 	| {
  35 | 			variant: "date";
  36 | 	  };
  37 |
  38 | export interface UpdateCell {
  39 | 	rowIndex: number;
  40 | 	columnId: string;
  41 | 	value: unknown;
  42 | }
  43 |
  44 | declare module "@tanstack/react-table" {
  45 | 	interface ColumnMeta<TData extends RowData, TValue> {
  46 | 		label?: string;
  47 | 		cell?: Cell;
  48 | 	}
  49 |
  50 | 	interface TableMeta<TData extends RowData> {
  51 | 		dataGridRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
  52 | 		focusedCell?: CellPosition | null;
  53 | 		editingCell?: CellPosition | null;
  54 | 		selectionState?: SelectionState;
  55 | 		searchOpen?: boolean;
  56 | 		isScrolling?: boolean;
  57 | 		getIsCellSelected?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string) => boolean;
  58 | 		getIsSearchMatch?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string) => boolean;
  59 | 		getIsActiveSearchMatch?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string) => boolean;
  60 | 		onDataUpdate?: (props: UpdateCell | Array<UpdateCell>) => void;
  61 | 		onRowsDelete?: (rowIndices: number[]) => void | Promise<void>;
  62 | 		onColumnClick?: (columnId: string) => void;
  63 | 		onCellClick?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string, event?: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  64 | 		onCellDoubleClick?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string) => void;
  65 | 		onCellMouseDown?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string, event: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  66 | 		onCellMouseEnter?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string, event: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  67 | 		onCellMouseUp?: () => void;
  68 | 		onCellContextMenu?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string, event: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  69 | 		onCellEditingStart?: (rowIndex: number, columnId: string) => void;
  70 | 		onCellEditingStop?: (opts?: { direction?: NavigationDirection; moveToNextRow?: boolean }) => void;
  71 | 		contextMenu?: ContextMenuState;
  72 | 		onContextMenuOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
  73 | 		rowHeight?: RowHeightValue;
  74 | 		onRowHeightChange?: (value: RowHeightValue) => void;
  75 | 		onRowSelect?: (rowIndex: number, checked: boolean, shiftKey: boolean) => void;
  76 | 	}
  77 | }
  78 |
```

`utils/decimal.ts`:

```ts
   1 | /**
   2 |  * Client-safe decimal utilities
   3 |  * These functions work with Decimal.js types without importing server dependencies
   4 |  */
   5 |
   6 | interface DecimalLike {
   7 | 	toNumber: () => number;
   8 | }
   9 |
  10 | function isDecimalLike(value: unknown): value is DecimalLike {
  11 | 	return (
  12 | 		typeof value === "object" &&
  13 | 		value !== null &&
  14 | 		"toNumber" in value &&
  15 | 		typeof (value as DecimalLike).toNumber === "function"
  16 | 	);
  17 | }
  18 |
  19 | /**
  20 |  * Convert any valid decimal input to a JS number
  21 |  * Supports: number, string, Decimal-like objects
  22 |  */
  23 | export function toNumber(value: unknown): number {
  24 | 	if (typeof value === "number") {
  25 | 		return value;
  26 | 	}
  27 | 	if (typeof value === "string") {
  28 | 		const num = Number(value);
  29 | 		if (Number.isNaN(num)) {
  30 | 			throw new Error(`Invalid number string: ${value}`);
  31 | 		}
  32 | 		return num;
  33 | 	}
  34 | 	if (isDecimalLike(value)) {
  35 | 		return value.toNumber();
  36 | 	}
  37 | 	throw new Error("Invalid decimal input");
  38 | }
  39 |
  40 | /**
  41 |  * Convert decimal to number with fallback
  42 |  */
  43 | export function decimalToNumber(value?: unknown): number {
  44 | 	if (value === null || value === undefined) {
  45 | 		return 0;
  46 | 	}
  47 | 	if (typeof value === "number") {
  48 | 		return value;
  49 | 	}
  50 | 	if (isDecimalLike(value)) {
  51 | 		return value.toNumber();
  52 | 	}
  53 | 	return 0;
  54 | }

```

`utils/growth.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
   2 |
   3 | import { db } from "../db";
   4 | import   { whoGrowthStandard } from "../db/schema";
   5 |
   6 | // Helper functions
   7 | export function calculateAgeInMonths(birthDate: Date, date: Date): number {
   8 | 	const months = (date.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear()) * 12;
   9 | 	return months + (date.getMonth() - birthDate.getMonth());
  10 | }
  11 |
  12 | export function calculateAgeInDays(birthDate: Date, date: Date): number {
  13 | 	return Math.floor((date.getTime() - birthDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  14 | }
  15 |
  16 | export async function getWHOStandard(ageDays: number, gender: string, type: string) {
  17 | 	return await db.query.whoGrowthStandard.findFirst({
  18 | 		where: and(
  19 | 			eq(whoGrowthStandard.ageDays, ageDays),
  20 | 			eq(whoGrowthStandard.gender, gender),
  21 | 			eq(whoGrowthStandard.measurementType, type)
  22 | 		)
  23 | 	});
  24 | }
  25 |
  26 | export function calculateZScore(value: number, m: number, l: number, s: number): number {
  27 | 	if (l === 0) {
  28 | 		return Math.log(value / m) / s;
  29 | 	}
  30 | 	return ((value / m) ** l - 1) / (l * s);
  31 | }
  32 |
  33 | export type GrowthRecordMeasurement = {
  34 | 	weight?: number | null;
  35 | 	height?: number | null;
  36 | 	bmi?: number | null;
  37 | 	headCircumference?: number | null;
  38 | 	weightForAgeZ?: number | null;
  39 | 	heightForAgeZ?: number | null;
  40 | 	bmiForAgeZ?: number | null;
  41 | };
  42 |
  43 | export function getMeasurementValue(record: GrowthRecordMeasurement, type: string): number | null {
  44 | 	switch (type) {
  45 | 		case "WEIGHT":
  46 | 			return record.weight ?? null;
  47 | 		case "HEIGHT":
  48 | 			return record.height ?? null;
  49 | 		case "BMI":
  50 | 			return record.bmi ?? null;
  51 | 		case "HEAD_CIRCUMFERENCE":
  52 | 			return record.headCircumference ?? null;
  53 | 		default:
  54 | 			return null;
  55 | 	}
  56 | }
  57 |
  58 | export function getZScoreValue(record: GrowthRecordMeasurement, type: string): number | null {
  59 | 	switch (type) {
  60 | 		case "WEIGHT":
  61 | 			return record.weightForAgeZ ?? null;
  62 | 		case "HEIGHT":
  63 | 			return record.heightForAgeZ ?? null;
  64 | 		case "BMI":
  65 | 			return record.bmiForAgeZ ?? null;
  66 | 		default:
  67 | 			return null;
  68 | 	}
  69 | }
```

`utils/index.ts`:

```ts
   1 | export function mapActionToType(action: string): "appointment" | "patient" | "payment" | "prescription" {
   2 | 	const lowerAction = action.toLowerCase();
   3 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("appointment")) return "appointment";
   4 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("patient")) return "patient";
   5 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("payment")) return "payment";
   6 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("prescription")) return "prescription";
   7 | 	return "appointment";
   8 | }
   9 |
  10 | export function mapActionToActivity(action: string): "created" | "updated" | "cancelled" | "completed" {
  11 | 	const lowerAction = action.toLowerCase();
  12 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("create")) return "created";
  13 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("update")) return "updated";
  14 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("cancel")) return "cancelled";
  15 | 	if (lowerAction.includes("complete")) return "completed";
  16 | 	return "updated";
  17 | }
  18 |

  20 | 	// Implement based on your survey/review data
  21 | 	// For now, return a default or calculate from database
  22 | 	return 4.5;
  23 | }
```

`utils/mcp-handler.ts`:

```ts
   1 | import { InMemoryTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/inMemory.js";
   2 | import type { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js";
   3 | import type { JSONRPCMessage } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js";
   4 |
   5 | export async function handleMcpRequest(request: Request, server: McpServer): Promise<Response> {
   6 | 	try {
   7 | 		const jsonRpcRequest = (await request.json()) as JSONRPCMessage;
   8 |
   9 | 		const [clientTransport, serverTransport] = InMemoryTransport.createLinkedPair();
  10 |
  11 | 		let responseData: JSONRPCMessage | null = null;
  12 |
  13 | 		clientTransport.onmessage = (message: JSONRPCMessage) => {
  14 | 			responseData = message;
  15 | 		};
  16 |
  17 | 		await server.connect(serverTransport);
  18 |
  19 | 		await clientTransport.start();
  20 | 		await serverTransport.start();
  21 |
  22 | 		await clientTransport.send(jsonRpcRequest);
  23 |
  24 | 		await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
  25 |
  26 | 		await clientTransport.close();
  27 | 		await serverTransport.close();
  28 |
  29 | 		return Response.json(responseData, {
  30 | 			headers: {
  31 | 				"Content-Type": "application/json"
  32 | 			}
  33 | 		});
  34 | 	} catch (error) {
  35 | 		console.error("MCP handler error:", error);
  36 |
  37 | 		// Return a JSON-RPC error response
  38 | 		return Response.json(
  39 | 			{
  40 | 				jsonrpc: "2.0",
  41 | 				error: {
  42 | 					code: -32603,
  43 | 					message: "Internal server error",
  44 | 					data: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
  45 | 				},
  46 | 				id: null
  47 | 			},
  48 | 			{
  49 | 				status: 500,
  50 | 				headers: {
  51 | 					"Content-Type": "application/json"
  52 | 				}
  53 | 			}
  54 | 		);
  55 | 	}
  56 | }

```
